```
1→"""Display and dashboard rendering for plan triage."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.issue_id_display import short_issue_id
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     9→from desloppify.base.output.user_message import print_user_message
    10→

    12→    print_action_guidance,
    13→    print_dashboard_header,
    14→    print_issues_by_dimension,
    15→    print_prior_stage_reports,
    16→    show_plan_summary,
    17→)

    26→
    27→def _cluster_tags(cluster: dict) -> str:
    28→    """Build display tags for cluster metadata."""
    29→    desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    30→    steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
    31→    auto = cluster.get("auto", False)
    32→    tags: list[str] = []
    33→    if auto:
    34→        tags.append("auto")
    35→    if desc:
    36→        tags.append("desc")
    37→    else:
    38→        tags.append("no desc")
    39→    if steps:
    40→        tags.append(f"{len(steps)} steps")
    41→    elif not auto:
    42→        tags.append("no steps")
    43→    return f" [{', '.join(tags)}]"
    44→
    45→
    46→def _print_active_clusters(active_clusters: dict[str, dict]) -> None:
    47→    """Render currently active clusters with tags and descriptions."""
    48→    if not active_clusters:
    49→        return
    50→    print(colorize("\n  Current clusters:", "cyan"))
    51→    for name, cluster in active_clusters.items():
    52→        count = len(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
    53→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    54→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    55→        print(f"    {name}: {count} items{_cluster_tags(cluster)}{desc_str}")
    56→
    57→
    58→def _unclustered_issue_ids(clusters: dict, open_issues: dict) -> list[str]:
    59→    """Return issue IDs not currently assigned to any cluster."""
    60→    all_clustered: set[str] = set()
    61→    for cluster in clusters.values():
    62→        all_clustered.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
    63→    return [fid for fid in open_issues if fid not in all_clustered]
    64→
    65→
    66→def _print_unclustered_issues(unclustered: list[str], open_issues: dict) -> None:
    67→    """Render unclustered issue list with compact IDs."""
    68→    if not unclustered:
    69→        return
    70→    print(colorize(f"\n  {len(unclustered)} issues not yet in a cluster:", "yellow"))
    71→    for fid in unclustered[:10]:
    72→        issue = open_issues[fid]
    73→        detail = issue.get("detail")
    74→        dim = (detail or {}).get("dimension", "") if isinstance(detail, dict) else ""
    75→        short = short_issue_id(fid)
    76→        print(f"    [{short}] [{dim}] {issue.get('summary', '')}")
    77→    if len(unclustered) > 10:
    78→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 10} more", "dim"))
    79→
    80→
    81→def print_progress(plan: dict, open_issues: dict) -> None:
    82→    """Show cluster state and unclustered issues."""
    83→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    84→    active_clusters = {name: c for name, c in clusters.items() if cluster_issue_ids(c)}
    85→    _print_active_clusters(active_clusters)
    86→
    87→    unclustered = _unclustered_issue_ids(clusters, open_issues)
    88→    if unclustered:
    89→        _print_unclustered_issues(unclustered, open_issues)
    90→    elif open_issues:
    91→        organized, total, _ = triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=set(open_issues.keys()))
    92→        print(colorize(f"\n  All {organized}/{total} issues are in clusters.", "green"))
    93→
    94→
    95→def print_reflect_result(
    96→    *,
    97→    issue_count: int,
    98→    recurring_dims: list[str],
    99→    recurring: dict,
   100→    report: str,
   101→    is_reuse: bool,
   102→    cleared: list,
   103→    stages: dict,
   104→) -> None:
   105→    """Print reflect stage output including briefing box and next steps."""
   106→    print(colorize(f"  Reflect stage recorded: {issue_count} issues, {len(recurring_dims)} recurring dimension(s).", "green"))
   107→    if is_reuse:
   108→        print(colorize("  Reflect data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   109→        if cleared:
   110→            print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   111→    else:
   112→        print(colorize("  Now confirm your strategy.", "yellow"))
   113→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm reflect", "dim"))
   114→    if recurring_dims:
   115→        for dim in recurring_dims:
   116→            info = recurring[dim]
   117→            print(colorize(f"    {dim}: {len(info['resolved'])} resolved, {len(info['open'])} still open", "dim"))
   118→
   119→    print()
   120→    print(colorize("  ┌─ Strategic briefing (share with user before organizing) ─┐", "cyan"))
   121→    for line in report.strip().splitlines():
   122→        print(colorize(f"  │ {line}", "cyan"))
   123→    print(colorize("  └" + "─" * 57 + "┘", "cyan"))
   124→    print_user_message(
   125→        "Reflect recorded. Before confirming — check the"
   126→        " subagent's report. Is it a strategy or just observe"
   127→        " restated? It should include a concrete cluster"
   128→        " blueprint: which clusters, which issues, what to skip"
   129→        " (with per-issue reasons). Confirm when the blueprint"
   130→        " is specific enough for organize to execute mechanically."
   131→    )
   132→
   133→
   134→def print_organize_result(
   135→    *,
   136→    manual_clusters: list[str],
   137→    plan: dict,
   138→    report: str,
   139→    is_reuse: bool,
   140→    cleared: list,
   141→    stages: dict,
   142→) -> None:
   143→    """Print organize stage output including cluster summary and next steps."""
   144→    print(colorize(f"  Organize stage recorded: {len(manual_clusters)} enriched cluster(s).", "green"))
   145→    if is_reuse:
   146→        print(colorize("  Organize data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   147→        if cleared:
   148→            print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   149→    else:
   150→        print(colorize("  Now confirm the plan.", "yellow"))
   151→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm organize", "dim"))
   152→    for name in manual_clusters:
   153→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(name, {})
   154→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
   155→        desc = cluster.get("description", "")
   156→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   157→        print(colorize(f"    {name}: {len(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))} issues, {len(steps)} steps{desc_str}", "dim"))
   158→
   159→    print()
   160→    print(colorize("  ┌─ Prioritized organization (share with user) ────────────┐", "cyan"))
   161→    for line in report.strip().splitlines():
   162→        print(colorize(f"  │ {line}", "cyan"))
   163→    print(colorize("  └" + "─" * 57 + "┘", "cyan"))
   164→    print_user_message(
   165→        "Organize recorded. Before confirming — does the"
   166→        " organize output match the reflect blueprint? Clusters"
   167→        " by file area (same PR), not by theme? Step count <"

   169→        " locations, not problem types? This should read like"

   173→
   174→def print_reflect_dashboard(
   175→    si: object,
   176→    plan: dict,
   177→    *,
   178→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   179→) -> None:
   180→    """Show completed clusters, resolved issues, and recurring patterns."""
   181→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   182→    completed = getattr(si, "completed_clusters", [])
   183→    resolved = getattr(si, "resolved_issues", {})
   184→    open_issues = getattr(si, "open_issues", {})
   185→
   186→    _print_completed_clusters(completed)
   187→    _print_resolved_issue_deltas(resolved)
   188→    _print_recurring_or_first_triage(
   189→        recurring=resolved_services.detect_recurring_patterns(open_issues, resolved),
   190→        completed=completed,
   191→        resolved=resolved,
   192→    )
   193→
   194→
   195→def _print_completed_clusters(completed: list[dict]) -> None:
   196→    """Render previously completed cluster snapshots."""
   197→    if not completed:
   198→        return
   199→    print(colorize("\n  Previously completed clusters:", "cyan"))
   200→    for cluster in completed[:10]:
   201→        name = cluster.get("name", "?")
   202→        count = len(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   203→        thesis = cluster.get("thesis", "")
   204→        print(f"    {name}: {count} issues")
   205→        if thesis:
   206→            print(colorize(f"      {thesis}", "dim"))
   207→        for step in cluster.get("action_steps", [])[:3]:
   208→            print(colorize(f"      - {step}", "dim"))
   209→    if len(completed) > 10:
   210→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(completed) - 10} more", "dim"))
   211→
   212→
   213→def _print_resolved_issue_deltas(resolved: dict[str, dict]) -> None:
   214→    """Render issues resolved since the previous triage cycle."""
   215→    if not resolved:
   216→        return
   217→    print(colorize(f"\n  Resolved issues since last triage: {len(resolved)}", "cyan"))
   218→    for fid, issue in sorted(resolved.items())[:10]:
   219→        status = issue.get("status", "")
   220→        summary = issue.get("summary", "")
   221→        detail = issue.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(issue.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   222→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "")
   223→        print(f"    [{status}] [{dim}] {summary}")
   224→        print(colorize(f"      {fid}", "dim"))
   225→    if len(resolved) > 10:
   226→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(resolved) - 10} more", "dim"))
   227→
   228→
   229→def _print_recurring_or_first_triage(
   230→    *,
   231→    recurring: dict,
   232→    completed: list[dict],
   233→    resolved: dict,
   234→) -> None:
   235→    """Render recurring-pattern warnings or first-triage guidance."""
   236→    if recurring:
   237→        print(colorize("\n  Recurring patterns detected:", "yellow"))
   238→        for dim, info in sorted(recurring.items()):
   239→            resolved_count = len(info["resolved"])
   240→            open_count = len(info["open"])
   241→            label = "potential loop" if open_count >= resolved_count else "root cause unaddressed"
   242→            print(colorize(f"    {dim}: {resolved_count} resolved, {open_count} still open — {label}", "yellow"))
   243→        return
   244→    if completed or resolved:
   245→        return
   246→    print(colorize("\n  First triage — no prior work to compare against.", "dim"))
   247→    print(colorize("  Focus your reflect report on your strategy:", "yellow"))
   248→    print(colorize("  - How will you resolve contradictions you identified in observe?", "dim"))
   249→    print(colorize("  - Which issues will you cluster together vs defer?", "dim"))
   250→    print(colorize("  - What's the overall arc of work and why?", "dim"))
   251→
   252→
   253→def cmd_triage_dashboard(
   254→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   255→    *,
   256→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   257→) -> None:
   258→    """Default view: show issues, stage progress, and next command."""
   259→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   260→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   261→    state = runtime.state
   262→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   263→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   264→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   265→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   266→
   267→    print_dashboard_header(si, stages, meta, plan)
   268→    print_action_guidance(stages, meta, si, plan)
   269→    print_prior_stage_reports(stages)
   270→    print_issues_by_dimension(si.open_issues)
   271→
   272→    if "observe" in stages and "reflect" not in stages:
   273→        print_reflect_dashboard(si, plan, services=resolved_services)
   274→
   275→    print_progress(plan, si.open_issues)
   276→
   277→
   278→__all__ = [
```

```
1→"""Triage workflow orchestration for command routing and stage dispatch."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from collections.abc import Callable
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE
    12→
    13→from .confirmations.router import cmd_confirm_stage
    14→from .display.dashboard import cmd_triage_dashboard
    15→from .lifecycle import ensure_triage_started
    16→from .review_coverage import ensure_active_triage_issue_ids
    17→from .runner.orchestrator_claude import run_claude_orchestrator
    18→from .runner.orchestrator_codex_pipeline import run_codex_pipeline
    19→from .runner.orchestrator_common import parse_only_stages
    20→from .runner.stage_prompts import cmd_stage_prompt
    21→from .services import TriageServices
    22→from .stage_queue import has_triage_in_queue, inject_triage_stages
    23→from .stages.completion import cmd_confirm_existing, cmd_triage_complete
    24→from .stages.commands import run_stage_command
    25→
    26→
    27→def _cmd_triage_start(
    28→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    29→    *,
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    services: TriageServices,
    32→) -> None:
    33→    """Manually inject triage stage IDs into the queue and clear prior stages."""
    34→    plan = services.load_plan()
    35→
    36→    if has_triage_in_queue(plan):
    37→        print(colorize("  Planning mode stages are already in the queue.", "yellow"))
    38→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    39→        stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    40→        if stages:
    41→            print(
    42→                colorize(
    43→                    f"  {len(stages)} stage(s) in progress - clearing to restart.",
    44→                    "yellow",
    45→                )
    46→            )
    47→            meta["triage_stages"] = {}
    48→            ensure_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
    49→            inject_triage_stages(plan)
    50→            services.save_plan(plan)
    51→            services.append_log_entry(
    52→                plan,
    53→                "triage_start",
    54→                actor="user",
    55→                detail={"action": "restart", "cleared_stages": list(stages.keys())},
    56→            )
    57→            services.save_plan(plan)
    58→            print(colorize("  Stages cleared. Begin with observe:", "green"))
    59→        else:
    60→            ensure_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
    61→            inject_triage_stages(plan)
    62→            services.save_plan(plan)
    63→            print(colorize("  Begin with observe:", "green"))
    64→        print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}", "dim"))
    65→        return
    66→
    67→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
    68→    start_outcome = ensure_triage_started(
    69→        plan,
    70→        services=services,
    71→        state=state,
    72→        attestation=attestation,
    73→        log_action="triage_start",
    74→        log_actor="user",
    75→        log_detail={"action": "start"},
    76→        start_message="  Planning mode started (6 stages queued).",
    77→        start_message_style="green",
    78→    )
    79→    if start_outcome.status == "blocked":
    80→        return
    81→
    82→    si = services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    83→    print(f"  Open review issues: {len(si.open_issues)}")
    84→    print(colorize("  Begin with observe:", "dim"))
    85→    print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}", "dim"))
    86→
    87→
    88→def _run_staged_runner(
    89→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    90→    *,
    91→    services: TriageServices,
    92→) -> None:
    93→    runner = str(getattr(args, "runner", "codex")).strip().lower()
    94→    try:
    95→        stages_to_run = parse_only_stages(getattr(args, "only_stages", None))
    96→    except ValueError as exc:
    97→        raise CommandError(str(exc), exit_code=1) from exc
    98→    if runner == "claude":
    99→        run_claude_orchestrator(args, services=services)
   100→        return
   101→    if runner == "codex":
   102→        run_codex_pipeline(
   103→            args,
   104→            stages_to_run=stages_to_run,
   105→            services=services,
   106→        )
   107→        return
   108→    raise CommandError(
   109→        f"Unknown runner: {runner}. Use 'codex' or 'claude'.",
   110→        exit_code=1,
   111→    )
   112→
   113→
   114→def _run_dry_run(
   115→    *,
   116→    services: TriageServices,
   117→    state: dict,
   118→) -> None:
   119→    plan = services.load_plan()
   120→    si = services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   121→    prompt = services.build_triage_prompt(si)
   122→    existing_clusters = si.existing_clusters
   123→    print(colorize("  Cluster triage - dry run", "bold"))
   124→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   125→    print(f"  Open review issues: {len(si.open_issues)}")
   126→    print(f"  Existing clusters: {len(existing_clusters)}")
   127→    print(f"  New since last: {len(si.new_since_last)}")
   128→    print(f"  Resolved since last: {len(si.resolved_since_last)}")
   129→    print(colorize("\n  Prompt that would be sent to LLM:", "dim"))
   130→    print()
   131→    print(prompt)
   132→
   133→
   134→def _read_report_file(report_file: str) -> str:
   135→    """Read report text from a file path or raise ``CommandError``."""
   136→    path = Path(report_file)
   137→    if not path.is_file():
   138→        raise CommandError(f"--report-file not found: {report_file}", exit_code=1)
   139→    try:
   140→        return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
   141→    except OSError as exc:
   142→        raise CommandError(f"Cannot read --report-file: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   143→
   144→
   145→def run_triage_workflow(
   146→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   147→    *,
   148→    services: TriageServices,
   149→    require_issue_inventory_fn: Callable[[dict], bool],
   150→) -> None:
   151→    """Route `plan triage` args through one orchestration seam."""
   152→    # Resolve --report-file to --report (--report takes precedence)
   153→    if not getattr(args, "report", None):
   154→        report_file = getattr(args, "report_file", None)
   155→        if report_file:
   156→            args.report = _read_report_file(report_file)
   157→
   158→    runtime = services.command_runtime(args)
   159→    state = runtime.state
   160→    if not require_issue_inventory_fn(state):
   161→        return
   162→
   163→    if getattr(args, "stage_prompt", None):
   164→        cmd_stage_prompt(args, services=services)
   165→        return
   166→    if getattr(args, "run_stages", False):
   167→        _run_staged_runner(args, services=services)
   168→        return
   169→    if getattr(args, "start", False):
   170→        _cmd_triage_start(args, state=state, services=services)
   171→        return
   172→    if getattr(args, "confirm", None):
   173→        cmd_confirm_stage(args, services=services)
   174→        return
   175→    if getattr(args, "complete", False):
   176→        cmd_triage_complete(args, services=services)
   177→        return
   178→    if getattr(args, "confirm_existing", False):
   179→        cmd_confirm_existing(args, services=services)
   180→        return
   181→
   182→    stage = getattr(args, "stage", None)
   183→    if run_stage_command(stage, args, services=services):
   184→        return
   185→
   186→    if getattr(args, "dry_run", False):
   187→        _run_dry_run(services=services, state=state)
   188→        return
   189→
   190→    cmd_triage_dashboard(args, services=services)
   191→
   192→
   193→__all__ = ["run_triage_workflow"]
   194→
```

```
1→"""Public triage stage-policy operations shared by commands and runners."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.command_runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import save_plan
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import collect_triage_input, detect_recurring_patterns
    12→from desloppify.state_io import utc_now
    13→
    14→from ..confirmations.basic import MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN, validate_attestation
    15→from ..observe_batches import observe_dimension_breakdown
    16→from .reflect_accounting import validate_reflect_accounting
    17→
    18→
    19→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    20→class AutoConfirmStageRequest:
    21→    """Configuration for one fold-confirm stage auto-confirmation."""
    22→
    23→    stage_name: str
    24→    stage_label: str
    25→    blocked_heading: str
    26→    confirm_cmd: str
    27→    inline_hint: str
    28→    dimensions: list[str] | None = None
    29→    cluster_names: list[str] | None = None
    30→
    31→
    32→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    33→class StagePrerequisite:
    34→    """One required upstream stage for a triage workflow seam."""
    35→
    36→    stage_name: str
    37→    require_confirmation: bool = False
    38→
    39→
    40→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    41→class ReflectAutoConfirmDeps:
    42→    """Dependency bundle for reflect auto-confirm flows."""
    43→
    44→    triage_input: object | None = None
    45→    command_runtime_fn: object | None = None
    46→    collect_triage_input_fn: object = collect_triage_input
    47→    detect_recurring_patterns_fn: object = detect_recurring_patterns
    48→    save_plan_fn: object | None = None
    49→
    50→
    51→_STAGE_PREREQUISITES = {
    52→    "organize": (
    53→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
    54→        StagePrerequisite("reflect"),
    55→    ),
    56→    "enrich": (
    57→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
    58→        StagePrerequisite("reflect"),
    59→        StagePrerequisite("organize"),
    60→    ),
    61→    "sense-check": (
    62→        StagePrerequisite("enrich", require_confirmation=True),
    63→    ),
    64→    "complete:organize": (
    65→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
    66→        StagePrerequisite("organize"),
    67→    ),
    68→    "complete:enrich": (
    69→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
    70→        StagePrerequisite("organize"),
    71→        StagePrerequisite("enrich"),
    72→    ),
    73→    "complete:sense-check": (
    74→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
    75→        StagePrerequisite("organize"),
    76→        StagePrerequisite("enrich"),
    77→        StagePrerequisite("sense-check"),
    78→    ),
    79→}
    80→
    81→
    82→def missing_stage_prerequisite(
    83→    stages: dict,
    84→    *,
    85→    flow: str,
    86→) -> StagePrerequisite | None:
    87→    """Return the first missing upstream stage required by a triage flow."""
    88→    for prerequisite in _STAGE_PREREQUISITES.get(flow, ()):
    89→        stage_record = stages.get(prerequisite.stage_name)
    90→        if stage_record is None:
    91→            return prerequisite
    92→        if prerequisite.require_confirmation and not stage_record.get("confirmed_at"):
    93→            return prerequisite
    94→    return None
    95→
    96→
    97→def require_prerequisite(
    98→    stages: dict,
    99→    *,
   100→    flow: str,
   101→    messages: dict[str, tuple[str, str]],
   102→) -> bool:
   103→    """Print consistent prerequisite guidance for one triage flow."""
   104→    missing = missing_stage_prerequisite(stages, flow=flow)
   105→    if missing is None:
   106→        return True
   107→    blocked_heading, command_hint = messages[missing.stage_name]
   108→    print(colorize(blocked_heading, "red"))
   109→    print(colorize(command_hint, "dim"))
   110→    return False
   111→
   112→
   113→def confirm_stage(
   114→    *,
   115→    plan: dict,
   116→    stage_record: dict,
   117→    attestation: str | None,
   118→    request: AutoConfirmStageRequest,
   119→    save_plan_fn=None,
   120→    utc_now_fn=None,
   121→) -> bool:
   122→    """Auto-confirm one recorded stage with a validated attestation."""
   123→    resolved_save_plan = save_plan_fn or save_plan
   124→    resolved_utc_now = utc_now_fn or utc_now
   125→    if stage_record.get("confirmed_at"):
   126→        return True
   127→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   128→        print(colorize(f"  {request.blocked_heading}", "red"))
   129→        print(colorize(f"  Run: {request.confirm_cmd}", "dim"))
   130→        print(colorize(f"  {request.inline_hint}", "dim"))
   131→        return False
   132→
   133→    confirmed_text = attestation.strip()
   134→    validation_err = validate_attestation(
   135→        confirmed_text,
   136→        request.stage_name,
   137→        dimensions=request.dimensions,
   138→        cluster_names=request.cluster_names,
   139→    )
   140→    if validation_err:
   141→        print(colorize(f"  {validation_err}", "red"))
   142→        return False
   143→
   144→    stage_record["confirmed_at"] = resolved_utc_now()
   145→    stage_record["confirmed_text"] = confirmed_text
   146→    resolved_save_plan(plan)
   147→    print(colorize(f"  ✓ {request.stage_label} auto-confirmed via --attestation.", "green"))
   148→    return True
   149→
   150→
   151→def auto_confirm_observe_if_attested(
   152→    *,
   153→    plan: dict,
   154→    stages: dict,
   155→    attestation: str | None,
   156→    triage_input,
   157→    save_plan_fn=None,
   158→    utc_now_fn=None,
   159→) -> bool:
   160→    """Auto-confirm observe inline when the report already exists."""
   161→    observe_stage = stages.get("observe")
   162→    if observe_stage is None:
   163→        return False
   164→    _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   165→    return confirm_stage(
   166→        plan=plan,
   167→        stage_record=observe_stage,
   168→        attestation=attestation,
   169→        request=AutoConfirmStageRequest(
   170→            stage_name="observe",
   171→            stage_label="Observe",
   172→            blocked_heading="Cannot reflect: observe stage not confirmed.",
   173→            confirm_cmd="desloppify plan triage --confirm observe",
   174→            inline_hint="Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm observe inline.",
   175→            dimensions=dim_names,
   176→        ),
   177→        save_plan_fn=save_plan_fn,
   178→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   179→    )
   180→
   181→
   182→def auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize(
   183→    *,
   184→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   185→    plan: dict,
   186→    stages: dict,
   187→    attestation: str | None,
   188→    deps: ReflectAutoConfirmDeps | None = None,
   189→    utc_now_fn=None,
   190→) -> bool:
   191→    """Auto-confirm reflect after validating its issue accounting."""
   192→    reflect_stage = stages.get("reflect")
   193→    if reflect_stage is None:
   194→        return False
   195→
   196→    resolved_deps = deps or ReflectAutoConfirmDeps()
   197→    triage_input = resolved_deps.triage_input
   198→    if triage_input is None:
   199→        runtime_factory = resolved_deps.command_runtime_fn or command_runtime
   200→        runtime = runtime_factory(args)
   201→        triage_input = resolved_deps.collect_triage_input_fn(plan, runtime.state)
   202→
   203→    valid_ids = set(triage_input.open_issues.keys())
   204→    accounting_ok, cited_ids, missing_ids, duplicate_ids = validate_reflect_accounting(
   205→        report=str(reflect_stage.get("report", "")),
   206→        valid_ids=valid_ids,
   207→    )
   208→    if not accounting_ok:
   209→        return False
   210→    reflect_stage["cited_ids"] = sorted(cited_ids)
   211→    reflect_stage["missing_issue_ids"] = missing_ids
   212→    reflect_stage["duplicate_issue_ids"] = duplicate_ids
   213→
   214→    recurring = resolved_deps.detect_recurring_patterns_fn(
   215→        triage_input.open_issues,
   216→        triage_input.resolved_issues,
   217→    )
   218→    _by_dim, observe_dims = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   219→    reflect_dims = sorted(set((list(recurring.keys()) if recurring else []) + observe_dims))
   220→    reflect_clusters = [
   221→        name for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items() if not cluster.get("auto")
   222→    ]
   223→    return confirm_stage(
   224→        plan=plan,
   225→        stage_record=reflect_stage,
   226→        attestation=attestation,
   227→        request=AutoConfirmStageRequest(
   228→            stage_name="reflect",
   229→            stage_label="Reflect",
   230→            blocked_heading="Cannot organize: reflect stage not confirmed.",
   231→            confirm_cmd="desloppify plan triage --confirm reflect",
   232→            inline_hint="Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm reflect inline.",
   233→            dimensions=reflect_dims,
   234→            cluster_names=reflect_clusters,
   235→        ),
   236→        save_plan_fn=resolved_deps.save_plan_fn,
   237→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   238→    )
   239→
   240→
   241→__all__ = [
   242→    "AutoConfirmStageRequest",
   243→    "ReflectAutoConfirmDeps",
   244→    "StagePrerequisite",
   245→    "auto_confirm_observe_if_attested",
   246→    "auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize",
   247→    "confirm_stage",
   248→    "missing_stage_prerequisite",
   249→    "require_prerequisite",
   250→]
   251→
```

```
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE
     7→

    10→from ..stages.helpers import unenriched_clusters
    11→from .stage_policy import (
    12→    AutoConfirmStageRequest,
    13→    confirm_stage,

    17→
    18→_COMPLETE_AUTO_CONFIRM_STAGE_CONFIG = {
    19→    "organize": {
    20→        "label": "Organize",
    21→        "blocked_heading": "Cannot complete: organize stage not confirmed.",
    22→        "confirm_cmd": "desloppify plan triage --confirm organize",
    23→        "inline_hint": "Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm organize inline.",
    24→    },
    25→    "enrich": {
    26→        "label": "Enrich",
    27→        "blocked_heading": "Cannot complete: enrich stage not confirmed.",
    28→        "confirm_cmd": "desloppify plan triage --confirm enrich",
    29→        "inline_hint": "Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm enrich inline.",
    30→    },
    31→    "sense-check": {
    32→        "label": "Sense-check",
    33→        "blocked_heading": "Cannot complete: sense-check stage not confirmed.",
    34→        "confirm_cmd": "desloppify plan triage --confirm sense-check",
    35→        "inline_hint": "Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm sense-check inline.",
    36→    },
    37→}
    38→
    39→
    40→def _manual_cluster_names(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
    41→    return [name for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items() if not cluster.get("auto")]
    42→
    43→
    44→def _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete(
    45→    *,
    46→    plan: dict,
    47→    stages: dict,
    48→    stage: str,
    49→    attestation: str | None,
    50→    save_plan_fn=None,
    51→) -> bool:
    52→    stage_record = stages.get(stage)
    53→    if stage_record is None:
    54→        return False
    55→
    56→    config = _COMPLETE_AUTO_CONFIRM_STAGE_CONFIG[stage]
    57→    return confirm_stage(
    58→        plan=plan,
    59→        stage_record=stage_record,
    60→        attestation=attestation,
    61→        request=AutoConfirmStageRequest(
    62→            stage_name=stage,
    63→            stage_label=config["label"],
    64→            blocked_heading=config["blocked_heading"],
    65→            confirm_cmd=config["confirm_cmd"],
    66→            inline_hint=config["inline_hint"],
    67→            cluster_names=_manual_cluster_names(plan),
    68→        ),
    69→        save_plan_fn=save_plan_fn,
    70→    )
    71→
    72→
    73→def _require_enrich_stage_for_complete(
    74→    *,
    75→    plan: dict,
    76→    meta: dict,
    77→    stages: dict,
    78→    underspec: list[tuple[str, int, int]] | None = None,
    79→) -> bool:
    80→    missing = missing_stage_prerequisite(stages, flow="complete:enrich")
    81→    if missing is None:
    82→        return True
    83→    if missing.stage_name != "enrich":
    84→        return _require_organize_stage_for_complete(plan=plan, meta=meta, stages=stages)
    85→
    86→    if underspec is None:
    87→        underspec = _underspecified_steps(plan)
    88→    if underspec:
    89→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: enrich stage not done.", "red"))
    90→        print(colorize(f"  {len(underspec)} cluster(s) have underspecified steps (missing detail or issue_refs):", "yellow"))
    91→        for name, bare, total in underspec[:5]:
    92→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps need enrichment", "yellow"))
    93→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"', "dim"))
    94→        print(colorize('  Then: desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "..."', "dim"))
    95→    else:
    96→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: enrich stage not recorded.", "red"))
    97→        print(colorize("  Steps look enriched. Record the stage:", "dim"))
    98→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "..."', "dim"))
    99→    return False
   100→
   101→
   102→def _auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete(
   103→    *,
   104→    plan: dict,
   105→    stages: dict,
   106→    attestation: str | None,
   107→    save_plan_fn=None,
   108→    underspec: list[tuple[str, int, int]] | None = None,
   109→) -> bool:
   110→    if "enrich" not in stages:
   111→        return False
   112→
   113→    if underspec is None:
   114→        underspec = _underspecified_steps(plan)
   115→    if underspec:
   116→        total_bare = sum(n for _, n, _ in underspec)
   117→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot auto-confirm enrich: {total_bare} step(s) still lack detail or issue_refs.", "red"))
   118→        for name, bare, total in underspec[:5]:
   119→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps", "yellow"))
   120→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"', "dim"))
   121→        return False
   122→
   123→    return _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete(
   124→        plan=plan,
   125→        stages=stages,
   126→        stage="enrich",
   127→        attestation=attestation,
   128→        save_plan_fn=save_plan_fn,
   129→    )
   130→
   131→
   132→def _require_sense_check_stage_for_complete(
   133→    *,
   134→    plan: dict,
   135→    meta: dict,
   136→    stages: dict,
   137→) -> bool:
   138→    missing = missing_stage_prerequisite(stages, flow="complete:sense-check")
   139→    if missing is None:
   140→        return True
   141→    if missing.stage_name != "sense-check":
   142→        return _require_enrich_stage_for_complete(plan=plan, meta=meta, stages=stages)
   143→
   144→    print(colorize("  Cannot complete: sense-check stage not recorded.", "red"))
   145→    print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage sense-check --report "..."', "dim"))
   146→    return False
   147→
   148→
   149→def _require_organize_stage_for_complete(
   150→    *,
   151→    plan: dict,
   152→    meta: dict,
   153→    stages: dict,
   154→) -> bool:
   155→    missing = missing_stage_prerequisite(stages, flow="complete:organize")
   156→    if missing is None:
   157→        return True
   158→    if missing.stage_name == "observe":
   159→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no stages done yet.", "red"))
   160→        print(colorize('  Start with: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   161→        return False
   162→
   163→    print(colorize("  Cannot complete: organize stage not done.", "red"))
   164→    gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
   165→    if gaps:
   166→        print(colorize(f"  {len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment:", "yellow"))
   167→        for name, missing in gaps:
   168→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   169→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "..." --steps "step1" "step2"', "dim"))
   170→        print(colorize(f"  Then: {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   171→    else:
   172→        manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   173→        if manual:
   174→            print(colorize("  Clusters are enriched. Record the organize stage first:", "dim"))
   175→            print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   176→        else:
   177→            print(colorize("  Create enriched clusters first, then record organize:", "dim"))
   178→            print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   179→    if meta.get("strategy_summary"):
   180→        print(colorize('  Or fast-track: --confirm-existing --note "why plan is still valid" --strategy "..."', "dim"))
   181→    return False
   182→
   183→
   184→__all__ = [
   185→    "_auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete",
   186→    "_auto_confirm_stage_for_complete",
   187→    "_require_enrich_stage_for_complete",
   188→    "_require_organize_stage_for_complete",
   189→    "_require_sense_check_stage_for_complete",
   190→]
   191→
```

```
1→"""Review-coverage helpers for triage planning."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.shared.cluster_membership import cluster_issue_ids
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.stale import open_review_ids
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     8→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import Cluster, PlanModel
     9→
    10→from .plan_state_access import (
    11→    ensure_cluster_map,
    12→    ensure_queue_order,
    13→    ensure_skipped_map,
    14→    ensure_triage_meta,
    15→    normalized_issue_id_list,
    16→)
    17→
    18→[REDACTED]
    19→[REDACTED]
    20→[REDACTED]
    21→
    22→
    23→def open_review_ids_from_state(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    24→    """Return open review IDs from the current state snapshot."""
    25→    return open_review_ids(state)
    26→
    27→
    28→def has_open_review_issues(state: StateModel | dict | None) -> bool:
    29→    """Return True when any open review issues exist."""
    30→    return bool(open_review_ids_from_state(state or {}))
    31→
    32→
    33→def plan_review_ids(plan: PlanModel) -> list[str]:
    34→    """Return review/concerns IDs currently represented in queue_order."""
    35→    return [
    36→        fid
    37→        for fid in ensure_queue_order(plan)
    38→        if not fid.startswith("triage::")
    39→        and not fid.startswith("workflow::")
    40→        and (fid.startswith("review::") or fid.startswith("concerns::"))
    41→    ]
    42→
    43→
    44→def coverage_open_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    45→    """Return the frozen or live open review IDs covered by this triage run."""
    46→    active_ids = normalized_issue_id_list(
    47→        ensure_triage_meta(plan).get(_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY)
    48→    )
    49→    if active_ids:
    50→        return set(active_ids)
    51→    has_completed_scan = bool(state.get("last_scan"))
    52→    review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
    53→    if not has_completed_scan and not review_ids:
    54→        return set(plan_review_ids(plan))
    55→    return review_ids
    56→
    57→
    58→def active_triage_issue_ids(
    59→    plan: PlanModel,
    60→    state: StateModel | None = None,
    61→) -> set[str]:
    62→    """Return the frozen review issue set for the current triage run."""
    63→    meta = ensure_triage_meta(plan)
    64→    active_ids = normalized_issue_id_list(meta.get(_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY))
    65→    if active_ids:
    66→        return set(active_ids)
    67→    if state is None:
    68→        return set()
    69→    return coverage_open_ids(plan, state)
    70→
    71→
    72→def live_active_triage_issue_ids(
    73→    plan: PlanModel,
    74→    state: StateModel | None = None,
    75→) -> set[str]:
    76→    """Return frozen triage IDs that are still open review issues in state."""
    77→    frozen_ids = active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
    78→    if state is None or not frozen_ids:
    79→        return frozen_ids
    80→    return frozen_ids & open_review_ids(state)
    81→
    82→
    83→def ensure_active_triage_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> list[str]:
    84→    """Freeze the current triage issue set for validation across stage reruns."""
    85→    meta = ensure_triage_meta(plan)
    86→    active_ids = sorted(coverage_open_ids(plan, state))
    87→    meta[_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY] = active_ids
    88→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY, None)
    89→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY, None)
    90→    return active_ids
    91→
    92→
    93→def clear_active_triage_issue_tracking(meta: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    94→    """Clear frozen triage coverage metadata after successful completion."""
    95→    meta.pop(_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY, None)
    96→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY, None)
    97→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY, None)
    98→
    99→
   100→def undispositioned_triage_issue_ids(
   101→    plan: PlanModel,
   102→    state: StateModel | None = None,
   103→) -> list[str]:
   104→    """Return frozen triage issues still lacking cluster/skip/dismiss coverage."""
   105→    target_ids = live_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   106→    if not target_ids:
   107→        return []
   108→    covered_ids: set[str] = set()
   109→    for cluster in ensure_cluster_map(plan).values():
   110→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   111→            continue
   112→        covered_ids.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   113→    covered_ids.update(
   114→        issue_id for issue_id in ensure_skipped_map(plan) if isinstance(issue_id, str)
   115→    )
   116→    covered_ids.update(
   117→        normalized_issue_id_list(ensure_triage_meta(plan).get("dismissed_ids"))
   118→    )
   119→    # Also check disposition map for auto-skipped entries that have matching
   120→    # skip entries in plan state (belt-and-suspenders with the skipped map check)
   121→    dispositions = ensure_triage_meta(plan).get("issue_dispositions", {})
   122→    for issue_id, disp in dispositions.items():
   123→        if disp.get("decision_source") == "observe_auto" and issue_id in ensure_skipped_map(plan):
   124→            covered_ids.add(issue_id)
   125→    return sorted(issue_id for issue_id in target_ids if issue_id not in covered_ids)
   126→
   127→
   128→def sync_undispositioned_triage_meta(
   129→    plan: PlanModel,
   130→    state: StateModel | None = None,
   131→) -> list[str]:
   132→    """Persist the current undispositioned triage issue set for recovery UX."""
   133→    meta = ensure_triage_meta(plan)
   134→    missing = undispositioned_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   135→    if missing:
   136→        meta[_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY] = missing
   137→        meta[_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY] = len(missing)
   138→    else:
   139→        meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY, None)
   140→        meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY, None)
   141→    return missing
   142→
   143→
   144→def triage_coverage(
   145→    plan: PlanModel,
   146→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   147→) -> tuple[int, int, dict[str, Cluster]]:
   148→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for review issues in triage."""
   149→    clusters = ensure_cluster_map(plan)
   150→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
   151→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   152→        all_cluster_ids.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   153→    review_ids = list(open_review_ids) if open_review_ids is not None else plan_review_ids(plan)
   154→    organized = sum(1 for fid in review_ids if fid in all_cluster_ids)
   155→    return organized, len(review_ids), clusters
   156→
   157→
   158→def manual_clusters_with_issues(plan: PlanModel) -> list[str]:
   159→    """Return manual clusters that currently own at least one issue."""
   160→    return [
   161→        name
   162→        for name, cluster in ensure_cluster_map(plan).items()
   163→        if cluster_issue_ids(cluster) and not cluster.get("auto")
   164→    ]
   165→
   166→
   167→def find_cluster_for(fid: str, clusters: dict[str, Cluster]) -> str | None:
   168→    """Return the owning cluster name for an issue ID, if any."""
   169→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   170→        if fid in cluster_issue_ids(cluster):
   171→            return name
   172→    return None
   173→
   174→
   175→__all__ = [
   176→    "active_triage_issue_ids",
   177→    "clear_active_triage_issue_tracking",
   178→    "cluster_issue_ids",
   179→    "coverage_open_ids",
   180→    "ensure_active_triage_issue_ids",
   181→    "find_cluster_for",
   182→    "has_open_review_issues",
   183→    "live_active_triage_issue_ids",
   184→    "manual_clusters_with_issues",
   185→    "open_review_ids_from_state",
   186→    "plan_review_ids",
   187→    "sync_undispositioned_triage_meta",
   188→    "triage_coverage",
   189→    "undispositioned_triage_issue_ids",
   190→]
   191→
```

```
    36→def _print_runner_paths(
    37→    *,
    38→    only_stages: str | None = None,
    39→    manual_fallback: str | None = None,
    40→    intro: str = "  Preferred runners:",
    41→) -> None:
    42→    print(colorize(intro, "yellow"))
    43→    for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages=only_stages):
    44→        print(f"    {label}:  {command}")
    45→    if manual_fallback:
    46→        print(colorize(f"  Manual fallback: {manual_fallback}", "dim"))
    47→
    48→
    49→def print_dashboard_header(si: object, stages: dict, meta: dict, plan: dict) -> None:
    50→    """Print the header section: title, open issues count, stage progress, overall status."""
    51→    print(colorize("  Cluster triage", "bold"))
    52→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
    53→    print(f"  Open review issues: {len(si.open_issues)}")
    54→    print(colorize("  Goal: identify contradictions, resolve them, then group the coherent", "cyan"))
    55→    print(colorize("  remainder into clusters by root cause with action steps and priorities.", "cyan"))
    56→    print(colorize("  Preferred: staged runner workflow (Codex or Claude).", "cyan"))
    57→    print(colorize(f"    Codex:  {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX}", "dim"))
    58→    print(colorize(f"    Claude: {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE}", "dim"))
    59→    print(colorize("  Manual stage commands below are fallback/debug paths.", "dim"))
    60→    existing_clusters = si.existing_clusters
    61→    if existing_clusters:
    62→        print(f"  Existing clusters: {len(existing_clusters)}")
    63→    if si.new_since_last:
    64→        print(colorize(f"  New since last triage: {len(si.new_since_last)}", "yellow"))
    65→        for fid in sorted(si.new_since_last):
    66→            issue = si.open_issues.get(fid, {})
    67→            dim = ""
    68→            detail = issue.get("detail")
    69→            if isinstance(detail, dict):
    70→                dim = detail.get("dimension", "")
    71→            dim_tag = f" ({dim})" if dim else ""
    72→            print(colorize(f"    * [{short_issue_id(fid)}] {issue.get('summary', '')}{dim_tag}", "yellow"))
    73→    if si.resolved_since_last:
    74→        print(f"  Resolved since last triage: {len(si.resolved_since_last)}")
    75→
    76→    print()
    77→    print_stage_progress(stages, plan)
    78→    if meta.get("stage_refresh_required"):
    79→        print(
    80→            colorize(
    81→                "  Note: review issues changed since stage progress started; "
    82→                "refresh stage reports before completion.",
    83→                "yellow",
    84→            )
    85→        )
    86→
    87→
    88→def _print_completed_guidance(si: object) -> None:
    89→    """Triage completed, no new issues — point to execution."""
    90→    print(colorize("  Triage complete. Executing current plan.", "green"))
    91→    print(colorize("    desloppify next", "dim"))
    92→    if si.resolved_since_last:
    93→        print(colorize(f"  {len(si.resolved_since_last)} issue(s) resolved since last triage.", "dim"))
    94→
    95→
    96→def _print_retriage_guidance(si: object, meta: dict) -> None:
    97→    """Triage completed but new issues arrived — offer re-triage paths."""
    98→    has_only_additions = bool(si.new_since_last) and not si.resolved_since_last
    99→    if has_only_additions and meta.get("strategy_summary"):
   100→        print(colorize("  Two paths available:", "yellow"))
   101→        print()
   102→        print(colorize("  To reuse the existing enriched cluster plan (without rewriting clusters):", "cyan"))
   103→        print('    desloppify plan triage --confirm-existing --note "..." --strategy "same" --confirmed "I have reviewed..."')
   104→        print()
   105→        print(colorize("  To re-prioritize and restructure:", "cyan"))
   106→        print(f"    Codex:  {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX}")
   107→        print(f"    Claude: {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE}")
   108→        print(colorize(f"    Manual fallback: {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}", "dim"))
   109→    else:
   110→        _print_runner_paths(
   111→            only_stages="observe",
   112→            manual_fallback=TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE,
   113→            intro="  Next step:",
   114→        )
   115→        print(colorize("    (themes, root causes, contradictions between issues — NOT a list of IDs)", "dim"))
   116→
   117→
   118→def _print_in_progress_guidance(stages: dict, meta: dict, plan: dict) -> None:
   119→    """Triage stages are active — guide through the stage chain."""
   120→    if "reflect" not in stages:
   121→        _print_runner_paths(
   122→            only_stages="reflect",
   123→            manual_fallback=TRIAGE_CMD_REFLECT,
   124→            intro="  Next step: use the completed work and patterns below to write your reflect report.",
   125→        )
   126→        print(colorize("    (Contradictions, recurring patterns, which direction to take, what to defer)", "dim"))
   127→    elif "organize" not in stages:
   128→        gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
   129→        manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   130→
   131→        if not manual:
   132→            _print_runner_paths(
   133→                only_stages="organize",
   134→                manual_fallback=TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE,
   135→                intro="  Next steps:",
   136→            )
   137→            print("    0. Defer contradictory issues: `desloppify plan skip <hash>`")
   138→            print(f"    1. Create clusters:  {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_CREATE}")
   139→            print(f"    2. Add issues:     {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ADD}")
   140→            print(f"    3. Enrich clusters:  {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_STEPS}")
   141→            print(f"    4. Record stage:     {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}")
   142→        elif gaps:
   143→            print(colorize("  Enrich these clusters before recording organize:", "yellow"))
   144→            for name, missing in gaps:
   145→                print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   146→            print(colorize(f"    Fix: {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT}", "dim"))
   147→            print(colorize(f"    Then: {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   148→        else:
   149→            print(colorize("  All clusters enriched! Record the organize stage:", "green"))
   150→            _print_runner_paths(
   151→                only_stages="organize",
   152→                manual_fallback=TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE,
   153→                intro="  Preferred runner paths:",
   154→            )
   155→
   156→        if meta.get("strategy_summary"):
   157→            print()
   158→            print(colorize("  Or fast-track by reusing the current enriched cluster plan:", "dim"))
   159→            print(colorize("    (This confirms the existing manual clusters; it does not materialize a new reflect blueprint.)", "dim"))
   160→            print(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_CONFIRM_EXISTING}")
   161→    elif "enrich" not in stages:
   162→        shallow = unenriched_clusters(plan)
   163→        if shallow:
   164→            print(colorize("  Next step: enrich steps with detail and issue_refs.", "yellow"))
   165→            print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"', "dim"))
   166→        else:
   167→            print(colorize("  Steps look enriched. Record the enrich stage:", "green"))
   168→        _print_runner_paths(
   169→            only_stages="enrich",
   170→            manual_fallback=TRIAGE_CMD_ENRICH,
   171→            intro="  Preferred runner paths:",
   172→        )
   173→        print(colorize("  You can still reorganize: add/remove clusters, reorder items.", "dim"))
   174→    else:
   175→        print(colorize("  Ready to complete:", "green"))
   176→        _print_runner_paths(
   177→            manual_fallback=TRIAGE_CMD_COMPLETE_VERBOSE,
   178→            intro="  Preferred runner paths:",
   179→        )
   180→        print(colorize('    (use --strategy "same" to keep existing strategy)', "dim"))
   181→
   182→
   183→def print_action_guidance(stages: dict, meta: dict, si: object, plan: dict) -> None:
   184→    """Print the 'What to do' action guidance section based on current stage.
   185→
   186→    Three states, matching the engine's canonical triage lifecycle:
   187→      1. Completed & current — triaged_ids present, no new issues
   188→      2. Needs (re-)triage — observe not done yet (fresh start or new issues)
   189→      3. In progress — observe done, working through remaining stages
   190→    """
   191→    print()
   192→    triage_has_run = bool(meta.get("triaged_ids"))
   193→    has_new_issues = bool(si.new_since_last)
   194→
   195→    if "observe" not in stages:
   196→        if triage_has_run and not has_new_issues:
   197→            _print_completed_guidance(si)
   198→        else:
   199→            _print_retriage_guidance(si, meta)
   200→    else:
   201→        _print_in_progress_guidance(stages, meta, plan)
   202→
   203→
   204→def print_prior_stage_reports(stages: dict) -> None:
   205→    """Print prior stage reports (observe/reflect) as context for current action."""
   206→    if "observe" in stages:
   207→        obs_report = stages["observe"].get("report", "")
   208→        if obs_report:
   209→            print(colorize("\n  Your observe analysis:", "dim"))
   210→            for line in obs_report.strip().splitlines()[:8]:
   211→                print(colorize(f"    {line}", "dim"))
   212→            if len(obs_report.strip().splitlines()) > 8:
   213→                print(colorize("    ...", "dim"))
   214→    if "reflect" in stages:
   215→        ref_report = stages["reflect"].get("report", "")
   216→        if ref_report:
   217→            print(colorize("\n  Your reflect strategy:", "dim"))
   218→            for line in ref_report.strip().splitlines()[:8]:
   219→                print(colorize(f"    {line}", "dim"))
   220→            if len(ref_report.strip().splitlines()) > 8:
   221→                print(colorize("    ...", "dim"))
   222→
   223→
   224→def print_issues_by_dimension(open_issues: dict) -> None:
   225→    """Print issues grouped by dimension with suggestions to surface contradictions."""
   226→    by_dim: dict[str, list[tuple[str, dict]]] = defaultdict(list)
   227→    for fid, issue in open_issues.items():
   228→        detail = issue.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(issue.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   229→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
   230→        by_dim[dim].append((fid, issue))
   231→
   232→    print(colorize("\n  Review issues by dimension:", "cyan"))
   233→    print(colorize("  (Look for contradictions: issues in the same dimension that", "dim"))
   234→    print(colorize("  recommend opposite changes. These must be resolved before clustering.)", "dim"))
   235→    max_per_dim = 5
   236→    for dim in sorted(by_dim, key=lambda d: (-len(by_dim[d]), d)):
   237→        items = by_dim[dim]
   238→        print(colorize(f"\n    {dim} ({len(items)}):", "bold"))
   239→        for fid, issue in items[:max_per_dim]:
   240→            summary = issue.get("summary", "")
   241→            short = short_issue_id(fid)
   242→            detail = issue.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(issue.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   243→            suggestion = (detail.get("suggestion") or "")[:120]
   244→            print(f"      [{short}] {summary}")
   245→            if suggestion:
   246→                print(colorize(f"        → {suggestion}", "dim"))
   247→        if len(items) > max_per_dim:
   248→            print(colorize(f"      ... and {len(items) - max_per_dim} more", "dim"))
   249→    print(colorize("\n  Use hash in commands: desloppify plan skip <hash>  |  desloppify show <hash>", "dim"))
   250→
   251→
   252→def show_plan_summary(plan: dict, state: dict) -> None:
   253→    """Print a compact plan rendering: clusters + queue order + coverage."""
   254→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   255→    active = {
   256→        name: cluster
   257→        for name, cluster in clusters.items()
   258→        if cluster_issue_ids(cluster) and not cluster.get("auto")
   259→    }
   260→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   261→
   262→    if active:
   263→        print(colorize(f"\n  Clusters ({len(active)}):", "bold"))
   264→        for name, cluster in active.items():
   265→            count = len(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   266→            steps = len(cluster.get("action_steps", []))
   267→            desc = (cluster.get("description") or "")[:60]
   268→            print(f"    {name}: {count} items, {steps} steps — {desc}")
   269→
   270→    queue_order = [
   271→        fid
   272→        for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   273→        if not fid.startswith("triage::") and not fid.startswith("workflow::")
   274→    ]
   275→    if queue_order:
   276→        show = min(15, len(queue_order))
   277→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue order (first {show} of {len(queue_order)}):", "bold"))
   278→        for i, fid in enumerate(queue_order[:show]):
   279→            issue = issues.get(fid, {})
   280→            summary = (issue.get("summary") or fid)[:60]
   281→            detector = issue.get("detector", "?")
   282→            cluster_name = find_cluster_for(fid, active)
   283→            print(f"    {i + 1}. [{detector}] {summary}{f' ({cluster_name})' if cluster_name else ''}")
   284→
   285→    organized, total, _ = triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=open_review_ids_from_state(state))
   286→    pct = int(organized / total * 100) if total else 0
   287→    print(colorize(f"\n  Coverage: {organized}/{total} in clusters ({pct}%)", "bold"))
   288→
   289→
   290→__all__ = [
   291→    "print_action_guidance",
   292→    "print_dashboard_header",
   293→    "print_issues_by_dimension",
   294→    "print_prior_stage_reports",
   295→    "show_plan_summary",
   296→]
   297→
```

```
1→"""Progress rendering helpers for triage command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.issue_id_display import short_issue_id
     6→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage.review_coverage import (
     7→    cluster_issue_ids,
     8→    triage_coverage,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→
    12→
    13→def _print_progress(plan: dict, open_issues: dict) -> None:
    14→    """Show cluster state and unclustered issues."""
    15→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    16→    _print_active_clusters(clusters)
    17→    unclustered = _collect_unclustered_issues(clusters, open_issues)
    18→    _print_unclustered_issues(plan, open_issues, unclustered)
    19→
    20→
    21→def _print_active_clusters(clusters: dict[str, dict]) -> None:
    22→    """Print current clusters that contain issues."""
    23→    active_clusters = {
    24→        name: cluster for name, cluster in clusters.items() if cluster_issue_ids(cluster)
    25→    }
    26→    if not active_clusters:
    27→        return
    28→    print(colorize("\n  Current clusters:", "cyan"))
    29→    for name, cluster in active_clusters.items():
    30→        count = len(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
    31→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    32→        tag_str = _cluster_tag_summary(cluster)
    33→        desc_str = f" - {desc}" if desc else ""
    34→        print(f"    {name}: {count} items{tag_str}{desc_str}")
    35→
    36→
    37→def _cluster_tag_summary(cluster: dict) -> str:
    38→    """Build compact tag summary for one cluster row."""
    39→    steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
    40→    auto = cluster.get("auto", False)
    41→    tags: list[str] = []
    42→    tags.append("auto" if auto else "manual")
    43→    tags.append("desc" if cluster.get("description") else "no desc")
    44→    if steps:
    45→        tags.append(f"{len(steps)} steps")
    46→    elif not auto:
    47→        tags.append("no steps")
    48→    return f" [{', '.join(tags)}]"
    49→
    50→
    51→def _collect_unclustered_issues(clusters: dict[str, dict], open_issues: dict) -> list[str]:
    52→    """Return issue IDs that are not attached to any cluster."""
    53→    all_clustered: set[str] = set()
    54→    for cluster in clusters.values():
    55→        all_clustered.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
    56→    return [issue_id for issue_id in open_issues if issue_id not in all_clustered]
    57→
    58→
    59→def _print_unclustered_issues(
    60→    plan: dict,
    61→    open_issues: dict,
    62→    unclustered: list[str],
    63→) -> None:
    64→    """Print unclustered issues summary or all-clustered confirmation."""
    65→    if unclustered:
    66→        print(colorize(f"\n  {len(unclustered)} issues not yet in a cluster:", "yellow"))
    67→        for issue_id in unclustered[:10]:
    68→            issue = open_issues[issue_id]
    69→            dim = (
    70→                (issue.get("detail", {}) or {}).get("dimension", "")
    71→                if isinstance(issue.get("detail"), dict)
    72→                else ""
    73→            )
    74→            short = short_issue_id(issue_id)
    75→            print(f"    [{short}] [{dim}] {issue.get('summary', '')}")
    76→        if len(unclustered) > 10:
    77→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 10} more", "dim"))
    78→        return
    79→    if open_issues:
    80→        organized, total, _ = triage_coverage(plan)
    81→        print(colorize(f"\n  All {organized}/{total} issues are in clusters.", "green"))
    82→
```

```
1→"""Plan-state accessors for triage workflow helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, cast
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import Cluster, PlanModel
     8→
     9→
    10→def ensure_queue_order(plan: PlanModel) -> list[str]:
    11→    """Return queue order, creating the stored list when missing."""
    12→    order = plan.get("queue_order")
    13→    if isinstance(order, list):
    14→        return order
    15→    normalized: list[str] = []
    16→    plan["queue_order"] = normalized
    17→    return normalized
    18→
    19→
    20→def ensure_skipped_map(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, Any]:
    21→    """Return skipped metadata, creating the stored map when missing."""
    22→    skipped = plan.get("skipped")
    23→    if isinstance(skipped, dict):
    24→        return cast(dict[str, Any], skipped)
    25→    normalized: dict[str, Any] = {}
    26→    plan["skipped"] = normalized
    27→    return normalized
    28→
    29→
    30→def ensure_cluster_map(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
    31→    """Return cluster map, creating the stored map when missing."""
    32→    clusters = plan.get("clusters")
    33→    if isinstance(clusters, dict):
    34→        return cast(dict[str, Cluster], clusters)
    35→    normalized: dict[str, Cluster] = {}
    36→    plan["clusters"] = normalized
    37→    return normalized
    38→
    39→
    40→def ensure_triage_meta(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, Any]:
    41→    """Return triage metadata, creating the stored map when missing."""
    42→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta")
    43→    if isinstance(meta, dict):
    44→        return cast(dict[str, Any], meta)
    45→    normalized: dict[str, Any] = {}
    46→    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = normalized
    47→    return normalized
    48→
    49→
    50→def ensure_execution_log(plan: PlanModel) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    51→    """Return execution log, creating the stored list when missing."""
    52→    log = plan.get("execution_log")
    53→    if isinstance(log, list):
    54→        normalized = [entry for entry in log if isinstance(entry, dict)]
    55→        if normalized is not log:
    56→            plan["execution_log"] = normalized
    57→        return normalized
    58→    normalized: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    59→    plan["execution_log"] = normalized
    60→    return normalized
    61→
    62→
    63→def normalized_issue_id_list(raw_ids: object) -> list[str]:
    64→    """Normalize a raw stored ID collection to strings only."""
    65→    if not isinstance(raw_ids, list):
    66→        return []
    67→    return [issue_id for issue_id in raw_ids if isinstance(issue_id, str)]
    68→
    69→
    70→__all__ = [
    71→    "ensure_cluster_map",
    72→    "ensure_execution_log",
    73→    "ensure_queue_order",
    74→    "ensure_skipped_map",
    75→    "ensure_triage_meta",
    76→    "normalized_issue_id_list",
    77→]
    78→
```

```
    67→
    68→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    69→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    70→
    71→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    72→    if output:
    73→        try:
    74→            from pathlib import Path
    75→            output_path = Path(output)
    76→            if output_path.exists():
    77→                print(colorize(
    78→                    f"  {output} already exists — refusing to overwrite.",
    79→                    "red",
    80→                ))
    81→                print(colorize(
    82→                    f"  Delete it first (`desloppify plan reset` or `rm {output}`), "
    83→                    f"or choose a different filename.",
    84→                    "dim",
    85→                ))
    86→                return
    87→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    88→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    89→            print_agent_plan(
    90→                ["Inspect and execute the generated plan."],
    91→                next_command=next_command,
    92→            )
    93→        except OSError as e:
    94→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    95→    else:
    96→        print(plan_md)
    97→        print()
    98→        print_agent_plan(
    99→            ["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."],
   100→            next_command=next_command,
   101→        )
   102→
   103→
   104→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   105→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
   106→    cmd_plan_output(args)
   107→
   108→
   109→def _plan_queue_line(state: dict, plan: dict) -> str:
   110→    """Build the queue headline with a resilient fallback."""
   111→    try:
   112→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   113→        return format_queue_headline(breakdown)
   114→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   115→        logger.debug("Plan show fell back to raw queue counts.", exc_info=exc)
   116→        ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   117→        return f"Queue: {ordered} items prioritized"
   118→
   119→
   120→def _skip_kind_counts(plan: dict) -> tuple[int, int, int, int]:
   121→    """Return total skipped plus temporary/permanent/false-positive counts."""
   122→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   123→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
   124→    kind_counts = {
   125→        kind: sum(1 for entry in skipped.values() if entry.get("kind") == kind)
   126→        for kind in USER_SKIP_KINDS
   127→    }
   128→    return (
   129→        total_skipped,
   130→        kind_counts["temporary"],
   131→        kind_counts["permanent"],
   132→        kind_counts["false_positive"],
   133→    )
   134→
   135→
   136→def _print_cluster_summary(plan: dict, *, active: str | None) -> None:
   137→    """Print cluster counts and focused cluster details."""
   138→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   139→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
   140→    if not clusters:
   141→        return
   142→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   143→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   144→        member_count = len(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   145→        marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   146→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   147→        print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
   148→
   149→
   150→def _print_commit_tracking(plan: dict) -> None:
   151→    """Print commit-tracking summary when enabled and populated."""
   152→    cfg = load_config()
   153→    if not cfg.get("commit_tracking_enabled", True):
   154→        return
   155→    ct = commit_tracking_summary(plan)
   156→    if ct["total"] <= 0:
   157→        return
   158→    pr_num = cfg.get("commit_pr", 0)
   159→    pr_str = f"  PR: #{pr_num}" if pr_num else ""
   160→    print(
   161→        f"  Commit tracking:  {ct['uncommitted']} uncommitted, "
   162→        f"{ct['committed']} committed ({ct['total']} issues){pr_str}"
   163→    )
   164→
   165→
   166→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   167→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
   168→    plan = load_plan()
   169→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   170→
   171→    queue_line = _plan_queue_line(runtime.state, plan)
   172→    total_skipped, temp_count, perm_count, fp_count = _skip_kind_counts(plan)
   173→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   174→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
   175→
   176→    described, noted = annotation_counts(plan)
   177→
   178→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
   179→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
   180→    print(f"  {queue_line}")
   181→    if total_skipped:
   182→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
   183→    else:
   184→        print("  Skipped:          0")
   185→    _print_cluster_summary(plan, active=active)
   186→    if described or noted:
   187→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
   188→    if active:
   189→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
   190→    if superseded:
   191→        print(f"  Scan drift:       {superseded} (no longer actionable in current state)")
   192→
   193→    _print_commit_tracking(plan)
   194→
   195→
   196→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   197→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
   198→    plan = load_plan()
   199→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   200→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
   201→    reset_plan(plan)
   202→    append_log_entry(
   203→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
   204→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
   205→    )
   206→    save_plan(plan)
   207→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
   208→
   209→
   210→_PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS = {
   211→    "show": _cmd_plan_show,
   212→    "queue": cmd_plan_queue,
   213→    "reset": _cmd_plan_reset,
   214→    "reorder": cmd_plan_reorder,
   215→    "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   216→    "resolve": cmd_plan_resolve,
   217→    "note": cmd_plan_note,
   218→    "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   219→    "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   220→    "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   221→    "backlog": cmd_plan_backlog,
   222→    "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   223→    "cluster": cmd_cluster_dispatch,
   224→    "triage": cmd_plan_triage,
   225→    "scan-gate": cmd_plan_scan_gate,
   226→    "commit-log": cmd_commit_log_dispatch,
   227→    "policy": cmd_policy_dispatch,
   228→    "repair-state": cmd_plan_repair_state,
   229→}
   230→
   231→
   232→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   233→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
   234→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
   235→    if plan_action is None:
   236→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
   237→        return
   238→
   239→    handler = _PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS.get(plan_action)
   240→    if handler is None:
   241→        print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   242→        return
   243→    handler(args)
   244→
   245→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   246→
```

```
    23→build_work_queue = build_execution_queue

    26→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    27→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    28→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    29→    state = runtime.state
    30→    config = runtime.config
    31→    if not require_issue_inventory(state):
    32→        return
    33→
    34→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    35→    if skill_warning:
    36→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    37→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    38→    if config_warning:
    39→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    40→
    41→    if getattr(args, "format", "terminal") == "terminal":
    42→        print_triage_guardrail_info(state=state)
    43→    build_and_render_queue(
    44→        args,
    45→        state,
    46→        config,
    47→        view=EXECUTION_QUEUE_VIEW,
    48→        deps=QueueRenderDeps(
    49→            resolve_lang_fn=resolve_lang,
    50→            load_plan_fn=load_plan,
    51→            build_work_queue_fn=build_work_queue,
    52→            write_query_fn=write_query,
    53→        ),
    54→    )
    55→
    56→
```
