1→"""Public triage stage-policy operations shared by commands and runners."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.command_runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import save_plan
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import collect_triage_input, detect_recurring_patterns
    12→from desloppify.state_io import utc_now
    13→
    14→from ..confirmations.basic import MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN, validate_attestation
    15→from ..observe_batches import observe_dimension_breakdown
    16→from .reflect_accounting import validate_reflect_accounting
    17→
    18→
    19→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    20→class AutoConfirmStageRequest:
    21→    """Configuration for one fold-confirm stage auto-confirmation."""
    22→
    23→    stage_name: str
    24→    stage_label: str
    25→    blocked_heading: str
    26→    confirm_cmd: str
    27→    inline_hint: str
    28→    dimensions: list[str] | None = None
    29→    cluster_names: list[str] | None = None
    30→
    31→
    32→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    33→class StagePrerequisite:
    34→    """One required upstream stage for a triage workflow seam."""
    35→
    36→    stage_name: str
    37→    require_confirmation: bool = False
    38→
    39→
    40→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    41→class ReflectAutoConfirmDeps:
    42→    """Dependency bundle for reflect auto-confirm flows."""
    43→
    44→    triage_input: object | None = None
    45→    command_runtime_fn: object | None = None
    46→    collect_triage_input_fn: object = collect_triage_input
    47→    detect_recurring_patterns_fn: object = detect_recurring_patterns
    48→    save_plan_fn: object | None = None
    49→
    50→
    51→_STAGE_PREREQUISITES = {
    52→    "organize": (
    53→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
    54→        StagePrerequisite("reflect"),
    55→    ),
    56→    "enrich": (
    57→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
    58→        StagePrerequisite("reflect"),
    59→        StagePrerequisite("organize"),
    60→    ),
    61→    "sense-check": (
    62→        StagePrerequisite("enrich", require_confirmation=True),
    63→    ),
    64→    "complete:organize": (
    65→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
    66→        StagePrerequisite("organize"),
    67→    ),
    68→    "complete:enrich": (
    69→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
    70→        StagePrerequisite("organize"),
    71→        StagePrerequisite("enrich"),
    72→    ),
    73→    "complete:sense-check": (
    74→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
    75→        StagePrerequisite("organize"),
    76→        StagePrerequisite("enrich"),
    77→        StagePrerequisite("sense-check"),
    78→    ),
    79→}
    80→
    81→
    82→def missing_stage_prerequisite(
    83→    stages: dict,
    84→    *,
    85→    flow: str,
    86→) -> StagePrerequisite | None:
    87→    """Return the first missing upstream stage required by a triage flow."""
    88→    for prerequisite in _STAGE_PREREQUISITES.get(flow, ()):
    89→        stage_record = stages.get(prerequisite.stage_name)
    90→        if stage_record is None:
    91→            return prerequisite
    92→        if prerequisite.require_confirmation and not stage_record.get("confirmed_at"):
    93→            return prerequisite
    94→    return None
    95→
    96→
    97→def require_prerequisite(
    98→    stages: dict,
    99→    *,
   100→    flow: str,
   101→    messages: dict[str, tuple[str, str]],
   102→) -> bool:
   103→    """Print consistent prerequisite guidance for one triage flow."""
   104→    missing = missing_stage_prerequisite(stages, flow=flow)
   105→    if missing is None:
   106→        return True
   107→    blocked_heading, command_hint = messages[missing.stage_name]
   108→    print(colorize(blocked_heading, "red"))
   109→    print(colorize(command_hint, "dim"))
   110→    return False
   111→
   112→
   113→def confirm_stage(
   114→    *,
   115→    plan: dict,
   116→    stage_record: dict,
   117→    attestation: str | None,
   118→    request: AutoConfirmStageRequest,
   119→    save_plan_fn=None,
   120→    utc_now_fn=None,
   121→) -> bool:
   122→    """Auto-confirm one recorded stage with a validated attestation."""
   123→    resolved_save_plan = save_plan_fn or save_plan
   124→    resolved_utc_now = utc_now_fn or utc_now
   125→    if stage_record.get("confirmed_at"):
   126→        return True
   127→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   128→        print(colorize(f"  {request.blocked_heading}", "red"))
   129→        print(colorize(f"  Run: {request.confirm_cmd}", "dim"))
   130→        print(colorize(f"  {request.inline_hint}", "dim"))
   131→        return False
   132→
   133→    confirmed_text = attestation.strip()
   134→    validation_err = validate_attestation(
   135→        confirmed_text,
   136→        request.stage_name,
   137→        dimensions=request.dimensions,
   138→        cluster_names=request.cluster_names,
   139→    )
   140→    if validation_err:
   141→        print(colorize(f"  {validation_err}", "red"))
   142→        return False
   143→
   144→    stage_record["confirmed_at"] = resolved_utc_now()
   145→    stage_record["confirmed_text"] = confirmed_text
   146→    resolved_save_plan(plan)
   147→    print(colorize(f"  ✓ {request.stage_label} auto-confirmed via --attestation.", "green"))
   148→    return True
   149→
   150→
   151→def auto_confirm_observe_if_attested(
   152→    *,
   153→    plan: dict,
   154→    stages: dict,
   155→    attestation: str | None,
   156→    triage_input,
   157→    save_plan_fn=None,
   158→    utc_now_fn=None,
   159→) -> bool:
   160→    """Auto-confirm observe inline when the report already exists."""
   161→    observe_stage = stages.get("observe")
   162→    if observe_stage is None:
   163→        return False
   164→    _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   165→    return confirm_stage(
   166→        plan=plan,
   167→        stage_record=observe_stage,
   168→        attestation=attestation,
   169→        request=AutoConfirmStageRequest(
   170→            stage_name="observe",
   171→            stage_label="Observe",
   172→            blocked_heading="Cannot reflect: observe stage not confirmed.",
   173→            confirm_cmd="desloppify plan triage --confirm observe",
   174→            inline_hint="Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm observe inline.",
   175→            dimensions=dim_names,
   176→        ),
   177→        save_plan_fn=save_plan_fn,
   178→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   179→    )
   180→
   181→
   182→def auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize(
   183→    *,
   184→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   185→    plan: dict,
   186→    stages: dict,
   187→    attestation: str | None,
   188→    deps: ReflectAutoConfirmDeps | None = None,
   189→    utc_now_fn=None,
   190→) -> bool:
   191→    """Auto-confirm reflect after validating its issue accounting."""
   192→    reflect_stage = stages.get("reflect")
   193→    if reflect_stage is None:
   194→        return False
   195→
   196→    resolved_deps = deps or ReflectAutoConfirmDeps()
   197→    triage_input = resolved_deps.triage_input
   198→    if triage_input is None:
   199→        runtime_factory = resolved_deps.command_runtime_fn or command_runtime
   200→        runtime = runtime_factory(args)
   201→        triage_input = resolved_deps.collect_triage_input_fn(plan, runtime.state)
   202→
   203→    valid_ids = set(triage_input.open_issues.keys())
   204→    accounting_ok, cited_ids, missing_ids, duplicate_ids = validate_reflect_accounting(
   205→        report=str(reflect_stage.get("report", "")),
   206→        valid_ids=valid_ids,
   207→    )
   208→    if not accounting_ok:
   209→        return False
   210→    reflect_stage["cited_ids"] = sorted(cited_ids)
   211→    reflect_stage["missing_issue_ids"] = missing_ids
   212→    reflect_stage["duplicate_issue_ids"] = duplicate_ids
   213→
   214→    recurring = resolved_deps.detect_recurring_patterns_fn(
   215→        triage_input.open_issues,
   216→        triage_input.resolved_issues,
   217→    )
   218→    _by_dim, observe_dims = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   219→    reflect_dims = sorted(set((list(recurring.keys()) if recurring else []) + observe_dims))
   220→    reflect_clusters = [
   221→        name for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items() if not cluster.get("auto")
   222→    ]
   223→    return confirm_stage(
   224→        plan=plan,
   225→        stage_record=reflect_stage,
   226→        attestation=attestation,
   227→        request=AutoConfirmStageRequest(
   228→            stage_name="reflect",
   229→            stage_label="Reflect",
   230→            blocked_heading="Cannot organize: reflect stage not confirmed.",
   231→            confirm_cmd="desloppify plan triage --confirm reflect",
   232→            inline_hint="Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm reflect inline.",
   233→            dimensions=reflect_dims,
   234→            cluster_names=reflect_clusters,
   235→        ),
   236→        save_plan_fn=resolved_deps.save_plan_fn,
   237→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   238→    )
   239→
   240→
   241→__all__ = [
   242→    "AutoConfirmStageRequest",
   243→    "ReflectAutoConfirmDeps",
   244→    "StagePrerequisite",
   245→    "auto_confirm_observe_if_attested",
   246→    "auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize",
   247→    "confirm_stage",
   248→    "missing_stage_prerequisite",
   249→    "require_prerequisite",
   250→]
   251→

    47→
    48→
    49→def triage_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel, state: dict | None = None) -> str:
    50→    """Return a banner string describing triage status."""
    51→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    52→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    53→    has_triage = any(stage_id in order for stage_id in TRIAGE_IDS)
    54→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    55→    run_hint = (
    56→        f"Run: {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX} "
    57→        f"(or {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE})"
    58→    )
    59→
    60→    if not has_triage:
    61→        undispositioned = meta.get("undispositioned_issue_count", 0)
    62→        if undispositioned:
    63→            return (
    64→                "TRIAGE RECOVERY NEEDED — "
    65→                f"{undispositioned} review issue(s) still need cluster/skip dispositions. "
    66→                f"{run_hint}"
    67→            )
    68→        if meta.get("triage_recommended"):
    69→            return (
    70→                "TRIAGE RECOMMENDED — review issues changed since last triage. "
    71→                f"{run_hint}"
    72→            )
    73→        return ""
    74→
    75→    if state and has_objective_backlog(state, None):
    76→        undispositioned = meta.get("undispositioned_issue_count", 0)
    77→        if undispositioned:
    78→            return (
    79→                "TRIAGE PENDING — "
    80→                f"{undispositioned} review issue(s) still need cluster/skip dispositions after current work. "
    81→                f"{run_hint}"
    82→            )
    83→        return (
    84→            "TRIAGE PENDING — queued and will activate after objective work "
    85→            "is complete."
    86→        )
    87→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    88→    completed = [
    89→        stage
    90→        for stage in ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check")
    91→        if stage in stages
    92→    ]
    93→    if completed:
    94→        return (
    95→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({len(completed)}/5 stages complete) — "
    96→            f"complete all stages to exit. {run_hint}"
    97→        )
    98→    return (
    99→        "TRIAGE MODE — review issues need analysis before fixing. "
   100→        f"{run_hint}"
   101→    )
   102→
   103→__all__ = [
   104→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ADD",
   105→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_CREATE",

1→"""Stage-gate and fold-confirm helpers for triage completion."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE
     7→
     8→from .enrich_checks import _underspecified_steps
     9→from ..review_coverage import manual_clusters_with_issues
    10→from ..stages.helpers import unenriched_clusters
    11→from .stage_policy import (
    12→    AutoConfirmStageRequest,
    13→    confirm_stage,
    14→    missing_stage_prerequisite,
    15→)
    16→
    17→
    18→_COMPLETE_AUTO_CONFIRM_STAGE_CONFIG = {
    19→    "organize": {
    20→        "label": "Organize",
    21→        "blocked_heading": "Cannot complete: organize stage not confirmed.",
    22→        "confirm_cmd": "desloppify plan triage --confirm organize",
    23→        "inline_hint": "Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm organize inline.",
    24→    },
    25→    "enrich": {
    26→        "label": "Enrich",
    27→        "blocked_heading": "Cannot complete: enrich stage not confirmed.",
    28→        "confirm_cmd": "desloppify plan triage --confirm enrich",
    29→        "inline_hint": "Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm enrich inline.",
    30→    },
    31→    "sense-check": {
    32→        "label": "Sense-check",
    33→        "blocked_heading": "Cannot complete: sense-check stage not confirmed.",
    34→        "confirm_cmd": "desloppify plan triage --confirm sense-check",
    35→        "inline_hint": "Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm sense-check inline.",
    36→    },
    37→}
    38→
    39→
    40→def _manual_cluster_names(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
    41→    return [name for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items() if not cluster.get("auto")]
    42→
    43→
    44→def _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete(
    45→    *,
    46→    plan: dict,
    47→    stages: dict,
    48→    stage: str,
    49→    attestation: str | None,
    50→    save_plan_fn=None,
    51→) -> bool:
    52→    stage_record = stages.get(stage)
    53→    if stage_record is None:
    54→        return False
    55→
    56→    config = _COMPLETE_AUTO_CONFIRM_STAGE_CONFIG[stage]
    57→    return confirm_stage(
    58→        plan=plan,
    59→        stage_record=stage_record,
    60→        attestation=attestation,
    61→        request=AutoConfirmStageRequest(
    62→            stage_name=stage,
    63→            stage_label=config["label"],
    64→            blocked_heading=config["blocked_heading"],
    65→            confirm_cmd=config["confirm_cmd"],
    66→            inline_hint=config["inline_hint"],
    67→            cluster_names=_manual_cluster_names(plan),
    68→        ),
    69→        save_plan_fn=save_plan_fn,
    70→    )
    71→
    72→
    73→def _require_enrich_stage_for_complete(
    74→    *,
    75→    plan: dict,
    76→    meta: dict,
    77→    stages: dict,
    78→    underspec: list[tuple[str, int, int]] | None = None,
    79→) -> bool:
    80→    missing = missing_stage_prerequisite(stages, flow="complete:enrich")
    81→    if missing is None:
    82→        return True
    83→    if missing.stage_name != "enrich":
    84→        return _require_organize_stage_for_complete(plan=plan, meta=meta, stages=stages)
    85→
    86→    if underspec is None:
    87→        underspec = _underspecified_steps(plan)
    88→    if underspec:
    89→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: enrich stage not done.", "red"))
    90→        print(colorize(f"  {len(underspec)} cluster(s) have underspecified steps (missing detail or issue_refs):", "yellow"))
    91→        for name, bare, total in underspec[:5]:
    92→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps need enrichment", "yellow"))
    93→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"', "dim"))
    94→        print(colorize('  Then: desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "..."', "dim"))
    95→    else:
    96→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: enrich stage not recorded.", "red"))
    97→        print(colorize("  Steps look enriched. Record the stage:", "dim"))
    98→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "..."', "dim"))
    99→    return False
   100→
   101→
   102→def _auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete(
   103→    *,
   104→    plan: dict,
   105→    stages: dict,
   106→    attestation: str | None,
   107→    save_plan_fn=None,
   108→    underspec: list[tuple[str, int, int]] | None = None,
   109→) -> bool:
   110→    if "enrich" not in stages:
   111→        return False
   112→
   113→    if underspec is None:
   114→        underspec = _underspecified_steps(plan)
   115→    if underspec:
   116→        total_bare = sum(n for _, n, _ in underspec)
   117→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot auto-confirm enrich: {total_bare} step(s) still lack detail or issue_refs.", "red"))
   118→        for name, bare, total in underspec[:5]:
   119→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps", "yellow"))
   120→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"', "dim"))
   121→        return False
   122→
   123→    return _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete(
   124→        plan=plan,
   125→        stages=stages,
   126→        stage="enrich",
   127→        attestation=attestation,
   128→        save_plan_fn=save_plan_fn,
   129→    )
   130→
   131→
   132→def _require_sense_check_stage_for_complete(
   133→    *,
   134→    plan: dict,
   135→    meta: dict,
   136→    stages: dict,
   137→) -> bool:
   138→    missing = missing_stage_prerequisite(stages, flow="complete:sense-check")
   139→    if missing is None:
   140→        return True
   141→    if missing.stage_name != "sense-check":
   142→        return _require_enrich_stage_for_complete(plan=plan, meta=meta, stages=stages)
   143→
   144→    print(colorize("  Cannot complete: sense-check stage not recorded.", "red"))
   145→    print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage sense-check --report "..."', "dim"))
   146→    return False
   147→
   148→
   149→def _require_organize_stage_for_complete(
   150→    *,
   151→    plan: dict,
   152→    meta: dict,
   153→    stages: dict,
   154→) -> bool:
   155→    missing = missing_stage_prerequisite(stages, flow="complete:organize")
   156→    if missing is None:
   157→        return True
   158→    if missing.stage_name == "observe":
   159→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no stages done yet.", "red"))
   160→        print(colorize('  Start with: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   161→        return False
   162→
   163→    print(colorize("  Cannot complete: organize stage not done.", "red"))
   164→    gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
   165→    if gaps:
   166→        print(colorize(f"  {len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment:", "yellow"))
   167→        for name, missing in gaps:
   168→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   169→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "..." --steps "step1" "step2"', "dim"))
   170→        print(colorize(f"  Then: {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   171→    else:
   172→        manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   173→        if manual:
   174→            print(colorize("  Clusters are enriched. Record the organize stage first:", "dim"))
   175→            print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   176→        else:
   177→            print(colorize("  Create enriched clusters first, then record organize:", "dim"))
   178→            print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   179→    if meta.get("strategy_summary"):
   180→        print(colorize('  Or fast-track: --confirm-existing --note "why plan is still valid" --strategy "..."', "dim"))
   181→    return False
   182→
   183→
   184→__all__ = [
   185→    "_auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete",
   186→    "_auto_confirm_stage_for_complete",
   187→    "_require_enrich_stage_for_complete",
   188→    "_require_organize_stage_for_complete",
   189→    "_require_sense_check_stage_for_complete",
   190→]
   191→

1→"""Completion strategy/policy validation helpers for triage workflow."""
     2→

    17→
    18→def _resolve_strategy_input(
    19→    strategy: str | None,
    20→    *,
    21→    meta: dict,
    22→    has_only_additions: bool = False,
    23→) -> str | None:
    24→    if strategy:
    25→        return strategy
    26→    if has_only_additions:
    27→        return "same"
    28→    print(colorize("  --strategy is required.", "red"))
    29→    existing = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
    30→    if existing:
    31→        print(colorize(f"  Current strategy: {existing}", "dim"))
    32→        print(colorize('  Use --strategy "same" to keep it, or provide a new summary.', "dim"))
    33→    else:
    34→        print(
    35→            colorize(
    36→                '  Provide --strategy "execution plan describing priorities, ordering, and verification approach"',
    37→                "dim",
    38→            )
    39→        )
    40→    return None
    41→
    42→
    43→def _strategy_valid_or_error(
    44→    strategy: str,
    45→    *,
    46→    include_guidance: bool,
    47→) -> bool:
    48→    if strategy.strip().lower() == "same":
    49→        return True
    50→    if len(strategy.strip()) >= 200:
    51→        return True
    52→    print(colorize(f"  Strategy too short: {len(strategy.strip())} chars (minimum 200).", "red"))
    53→    if include_guidance:
    54→        print(colorize("  The strategy should describe:", "dim"))
    55→        print(colorize("    - Execution order and priorities", "dim"))
    56→        print(colorize("    - What each cluster accomplishes", "dim"))
    57→        print(colorize("    - How to verify the work is correct", "dim"))
    58→    return False
    59→
    60→
    61→def _completion_clusters_valid(plan: dict, state: dict | None = None) -> bool:
    62→    if state is not None and not open_review_ids_from_state(state):
    63→        return True
    64→
    65→    manual_clusters = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
    66→    if not manual_clusters:
    67→        any_clusters = [
    68→            name for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
    69→            if cluster_issue_ids(cluster)
    70→        ]
    71→        if not any_clusters:
    72→            print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no clusters with issues exist.", "red"))
    73→            print(colorize('  Create clusters: desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
    74→            return False
    75→
    76→    gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
    77→    if gaps:
    78→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot complete: {len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment.", "red"))
    79→        for name, missing in gaps:
    80→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
    81→        print(colorize("  Small clusters (<5 issues) need at least 1 action step per issue.", "dim"))
    82→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "..." --steps "step1" "step2"', "dim"))
    83→        return False
    84→
    85→    unclustered = unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
    86→    if unclustered:
    87→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot complete: {len(unclustered)} review issue(s) have no action plan.", "red"))
    88→        for fid in unclustered[:5]:
    89→            short = fid.rsplit("::", 2)[-2] if "::" in fid else fid
    90→            print(colorize(f"    {short}", "yellow"))
    91→        if len(unclustered) > 5:
    92→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 5} more", "yellow"))
    93→        print(colorize("  Add to a cluster or wontfix each unclustered issue.", "dim"))
    94→        return False
    95→
    96→    return True
    97→
    98→
    99→def _resolve_completion_strategy(strategy: str | None, *, meta: dict) -> str | None:
   100→    return _resolve_strategy_input(strategy, meta=meta)
   101→
   102→
   103→def _completion_strategy_valid(strategy: str) -> bool:
   104→    return _strategy_valid_or_error(strategy, include_guidance=True)
   105→
   106→
   107→def _require_prior_strategy_for_confirm(meta: dict) -> bool:
   108→    if meta.get("strategy_summary", ""):
   109→        return True
   110→    print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: no prior triage has been completed.", "red"))
   111→    print(colorize("  The full OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → COMMIT flow is required the first time.", "dim"))
   112→    print(colorize(f"  Create and enrich clusters, then: {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   113→    return False
   114→
   115→
   116→def _confirm_existing_stages_valid(*, stages: dict, has_only_additions: bool, si) -> bool:
   117→    if has_only_additions:

   119→
   120→        _print_new_issues_since_last(si)

   123→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
   124→        print(colorize("  You must read issues first.", "dim"))
   125→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   126→        return False
   127→    if "reflect" not in stages:
   128→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: reflect stage not complete.", "red"))
   129→        print(colorize("  You must compare against completed work first.", "dim"))
   130→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "..."', "dim"))
   131→        return False
   132→    return True
   133→
   134→
   135→def _confirm_note_valid(note: str | None) -> bool:

   138→        print(colorize('  Explain why the existing plan is still valid (min 100 chars).', "dim"))
   139→        return False
   140→    if len(note) < 100:
   141→        print(colorize(f"  Note too short: {len(note)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   142→        return False
   143→    return True
   144→
   145→
   146→def _resolve_confirm_existing_strategy(
   147→    strategy: str | None,

   157→
   158→
   159→def _confirm_strategy_valid(strategy: str) -> bool:
   160→    return _strategy_valid_or_error(strategy, include_guidance=False)
   161→
   162→
   163→def _confirmed_text_or_error(*, plan: dict, state: dict, confirmed: str | None) -> str | None:
   164→    from ..confirmations.basic import MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN  # noqa: PLC0415
   165→
   166→    if confirmed and len(confirmed.strip()) >= MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   167→        return confirmed.strip()
   168→    print(colorize("  Current plan:", "bold"))
   169→    show_plan_summary(plan, state)
   170→    if confirmed:
   171→        print(colorize(f"\n  --confirmed text too short ({len(confirmed.strip())} chars, min {MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN}).", "red"))
   172→    print(colorize('\n  Add --confirmed "I validate this plan..." to proceed.', "dim"))
   173→    return None
   174→
   175→
   176→def _note_cites_new_issues_or_error(note: str, si) -> bool:
   177→    new_ids = si.new_since_last
   178→    if not new_ids:
   179→        return True
   180→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())
   181→    cited = extract_issue_citations(note, valid_ids)
   182→    new_cited = cited & new_ids
   183→    if new_cited:
   184→        return True
   185→    print(colorize("  Note must cite at least 1 new/changed issue.", "red"))
   186→    print(colorize(f"  {len(new_ids)} new issue(s) since last triage:", "dim"))
   187→    for fid in sorted(new_ids)[:5]:
   188→        print(colorize(f"    {fid}", "dim"))
   189→    if len(new_ids) > 5:
   190→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 5} more", "dim"))
   191→    return False
   192→

1→"""Organize stage command flow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→
     9→from ..display.dashboard import print_organize_result
    10→from ..completion_flow import count_log_activity_since
    11→from ..review_coverage import open_review_ids_from_state
    12→from ..stage_queue import has_triage_in_queue
    13→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    14→from ..validation.stage_policy import (
    15→    ReflectAutoConfirmDeps,
    16→    auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize,
    17→)
    18→from ..validation.organize_policy import (
    19→    _clusters_enriched_or_error,
    20→    _manual_clusters_or_error,
    21→    _organize_report_or_error,
    22→    _unclustered_review_issues_or_error,
    23→    _validate_organize_against_ledger_or_error,
    24→)
    25→from ..validation.core import _require_reflect_stage_for_organize
    26→from .records import record_organize_stage
    27→
    28→
    29→def _enforce_cluster_activity_for_organize(
    30→    *,
    31→    plan: dict,
    32→    stages: dict,
    33→    manual_clusters: list[str],
    34→    open_review_ids: set[str],
    35→    is_reuse: bool,
    36→    attestation: str | None,
    37→) -> bool:
    38→    """Require enough cluster operations after reflect unless explicitly attested."""
    39→    if not open_review_ids:
    40→        return True
    41→    reflect_ts = stages.get("reflect", {}).get("timestamp", "")
    42→    if not reflect_ts or is_reuse:
    43→        return True
    44→    activity = count_log_activity_since(plan, reflect_ts)
    45→    cluster_ops = sum(
    46→        activity.get(key, 0)
    47→        for key in ("cluster_create", "cluster_add", "cluster_update", "cluster_remove")
    48→    )
    49→    min_ops = max(3, len(manual_clusters))
    50→    if cluster_ops >= min_ops:
    51→        return True
    52→    if attestation and len(attestation.strip()) >= 40:
    53→        print(
    54→            colorize(
    55→                f"  Note: only {cluster_ops} cluster op(s) logged (expected {min_ops}+). "
    56→                "Proceeding with attestation override.",
    57→                "yellow",
    58→            )
    59→        )
    60→        return True
    61→    print(
    62→        colorize(
    63→            f"  Cannot organize: only {cluster_ops} cluster operation(s) logged "
    64→            f"since reflect (need {min_ops}+).",
    65→            "red",
    66→        )
    67→    )
    68→    print(
    69→        colorize(
    70→            "  Cluster operations (create/add/update/remove) are logged automatically\n"
    71→            "  when you use the CLI. Did you create clusters, add issues, and enrich them?",
    72→            "dim",
    73→        )
    74→    )
    75→    print(
    76→        colorize(
    77→            '  Override: pass --attestation "reason why fewer ops are expected" (40+ chars).',
    78→            "dim",
    79→        )
    80→    )
    81→    return False
    82→
    83→
    84→def _validate_organize_submission(
    85→    *,
    86→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    87→    plan: dict,
    88→    state: dict,
    89→    stages: dict,
    90→    report: str | None,
    91→    attestation: str | None,
    92→    is_reuse: bool,
    93→    services: TriageServices,
    94→) -> tuple[list[str], str] | None:
    95→    open_review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
    96→    triage_input = services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    97→    if not auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize(
    98→        args=args,
    99→        plan=plan,
   100→        stages=stages,
   101→        attestation=attestation,
   102→        deps=ReflectAutoConfirmDeps(
   103→            triage_input=triage_input,
   104→            detect_recurring_patterns_fn=services.detect_recurring_patterns,
   105→            save_plan_fn=services.save_plan,
   106→        ),
   107→    ):
   108→        return None
   109→
   110→    manual_clusters = _manual_clusters_or_error(plan, open_review_ids=open_review_ids)
   111→    if manual_clusters is None:
   112→        return None
   113→    if not _clusters_enriched_or_error(plan):
   114→        return None
   115→    if not _unclustered_review_issues_or_error(plan, state):
   116→        return None
   117→    if not _validate_organize_against_ledger_or_error(
   118→        plan=plan, stages=stages,
   119→    ):
   120→        return None
   121→    if not _enforce_cluster_activity_for_organize(
   122→        plan=plan,
   123→        stages=stages,
   124→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   125→        open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   126→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   127→        attestation=attestation,
   128→    ):
   129→        return None
   130→
   131→    normalized_report = _organize_report_or_error(report)
   132→    if normalized_report is None:
   133→        return None
   134→
   135→    from .evidence_parsing import (
   136→        format_evidence_failures,
   137→        validate_report_references_clusters,
   138→    )
   139→
   140→    cluster_ref_failures = validate_report_references_clusters(normalized_report, manual_clusters)
   141→    if cluster_ref_failures:
   142→        print(
   143→            colorize(
   144→                format_evidence_failures(cluster_ref_failures, stage_label="organize"),
   145→                "red",
   146→            )
   147→        )
   148→        return None
   149→    return manual_clusters, normalized_report
   150→
   151→
   152→def _persist_organize_stage(
   153→    *,
   154→    plan: dict,
   155→    meta: dict,
   156→    report: str,
   157→    open_review_ids: set[str],
   158→    existing_stage: dict | None,
   159→    is_reuse: bool,
   160→    manual_clusters: list[str],
   161→    services: TriageServices,
   162→) -> tuple[list[str], dict]:
   163→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   164→    cleared = record_organize_stage(
   165→        stages,
   166→        report=report,
   167→        issue_count=len(open_review_ids),
   168→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   169→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   170→    )
   171→    services.save_plan(plan)
   172→    services.append_log_entry(
   173→        plan,
   174→        "triage_organize",
   175→        actor="user",
   176→        detail={"cluster_count": len(manual_clusters), "reuse": is_reuse},
   177→    )
   178→    services.save_plan(plan)
   179→    return cleared, stages
   180→
   181→
   182→def _cmd_stage_organize(
   183→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   184→    *,
   185→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   186→) -> None:
   187→    """Record the ORGANIZE stage: validates cluster enrichment."""
   188→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   189→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   190→
   191→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   192→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   193→
   194→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   195→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to organize.", "yellow"))
   196→        return
   197→
   198→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   199→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   200→    existing_stage = stages.get("organize")
   201→
   202→    is_reuse = False
   203→    if not report and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
   204→        report = existing_stage["report"]
   205→        is_reuse = True
   206→
   207→    if not _require_reflect_stage_for_organize(stages):
   208→        return
   209→
   210→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   211→    state = runtime.state
   212→    open_review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
   213→
   214→    validated = _validate_organize_submission(
   215→        args=args,
   216→        plan=plan,
   217→        state=state,
   218→        stages=stages,
   219→        report=report,
   220→        attestation=attestation,
   221→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   222→        services=resolved_services,
   223→    )
   224→    if validated is None:
   225→        return
   226→    manual_clusters, normalized_report = validated
   227→    cleared, stages = _persist_organize_stage(
   228→        plan=plan,
   229→        meta=meta,
   230→        report=normalized_report,
   231→        open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   232→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   233→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   234→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   235→        services=resolved_services,
   236→    )
   237→
   238→    print_organize_result(
   239→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   240→        plan=plan,
   241→        report=normalized_report,
   242→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   243→        cleared=cleared,
   244→        stages=stages,
   245→    )
   246→
   247→
   248→def cmd_stage_organize(
   249→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   250→    *,
   251→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   252→) -> None:
   253→    """Public entrypoint for organize stage recording."""
   254→    _cmd_stage_organize(args, services=services)
   255→
   256→
   257→__all__ = [
   258→    "_cmd_stage_organize",
   259→    "cmd_stage_organize",
   260→]
   261→

   100→def _require_cluster_update_activity(

   215→def run_stage_enrich(
   216→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   217→    *,
   218→    services: TriageServices | None,
   219→    deps: EnrichStageDeps | None = None,
   220→) -> None:
   221→    """Record the ENRICH stage with validation and optional auto-confirm."""
   222→    resolved_deps = deps or EnrichStageDeps()
   223→    resolved_services, plan, state, stages, report, attestation = _resolve_enrich_stage_context(
   224→        args=args,
   225→        services=services,
   226→        deps=resolved_deps,
   227→    )
   228→
   229→    if not resolved_deps.has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   230→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to enrich.", "yellow"))
   231→        return
   232→
   233→    existing_stage = stages.get("enrich")
   234→    report, is_reuse = resolved_deps.resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
   235→
   236→    if not resolved_deps.require_organize_stage_for_enrich(stages):
   237→        return
   238→
   239→    if not _require_confirmed_organize_stage(
   240→        plan=plan,
   241→        stages=stages,
   242→        attestation=attestation,
   243→        services=resolved_services,
   244→        deps=resolved_deps,
   245→    ):
   246→        return
   247→
   248→    if not is_reuse and not _require_cluster_update_activity(
   249→        plan=plan,
   250→        state=state,
   251→        stages=stages,
   252→        attestation=attestation,
   253→        deps=resolved_deps,
   254→    ):
   255→        return
   256→
   257→    get_project_root = resolved_deps.get_project_root
   258→    if get_project_root is None:
   259→        from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
   260→
   261→    triage_ids = active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state) or None
   262→    quality_report = evaluate_enrich_quality(
   263→        plan,
   264→        get_project_root(),
   265→        phase_label="enrich",
   266→        bad_paths_severity="warning",
   267→        missing_effort_severity="warning",
   268→        include_missing_issue_refs=False,
   269→        include_vague_detail=False,
   270→        stale_issue_refs_severity=None,
   271→        triage_issue_ids=triage_ids,
   272→    )
   273→    underspec = quality_report.failure("underspecified")
   274→    total_bare = underspec.total if underspec else 0
   275→
   276→    if underspec:
   277→        _print_underspecified_step_error(underspec.rows, deps=resolved_deps)
   278→        return
   279→
   280→    print(resolved_deps.colorize("  All steps have detail or issue_refs.", "green"))
   281→    _print_enrich_warnings(report=quality_report, deps=resolved_deps)
   282→
   283→    report = resolved_deps.enrich_report_or_error(report)
   284→    if report is None:
   285→        return
   286→
   287→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
   288→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   289→    cleared = resolved_deps.record_enrich_stage(
   290→        stages,
   291→        report=report,
   292→        shallow_count=total_bare,
   293→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   294→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   295→    )
   296→
   297→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   298→
   299→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   300→        plan,
   301→        "triage_enrich",
   302→        actor="user",
   303→        detail={"shallow_count": total_bare, "reuse": is_reuse},
   304→    )
   305→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   306→
   307→    print(
   308→        resolved_deps.colorize(
   309→            f"  Enrich stage recorded: {total_bare} step(s) still without detail.",
   310→            "green",
   311→        )
   312→    )
   313→    if is_reuse:
   314→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Enrich data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   315→        if cleared:
   316→            resolved_deps.print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   317→    else:
   318→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Now confirm the enrichment.", "yellow"))
   319→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm enrich", "dim"))
   320→
   321→    resolved_deps.print_user_message(
   322→        "Enrich recorded. Before confirming — check the subagent's"
   323→        " work. Could a developer who has never seen this code"
   324→        " execute every step without asking a question? Every step"
   325→        " needs: file path, specific location, specific action."
   326→        " 'Refactor X' fails. 'Extract lines 45-89 into Y' passes."
   327→    )
   328→
   329→
   330→def cmd_stage_enrich(
   331→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   332→    *,
   333→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   334→) -> None:
   335→    """Public entrypoint for enrich stage recording."""
   336→    run_stage_enrich(args, services=services)
   337→
   338→
   339→__all__ = ["ColorizeFn", "EnrichStageDeps", "cmd_stage_enrich", "run_stage_enrich"]
   340→

    43→def _print_completion_coverage_warning(*, organized: int, total: int) -> None:
    44→    """Print warnings for incomplete cluster coverage at completion time."""
    45→    if total <= 0:
    46→        return
    47→    if organized == 0:
    48→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no issues have been organized into clusters.", "red"))
    49→        print(colorize(f"  {total} issues are waiting.", "dim"))
    50→        return
    51→    if organized < total:
    52→        remaining = total - organized
    53→        print(
    54→            colorize(
    55→                f"  Warning: {remaining}/{total} issues are not yet in any cluster.",
    56→                "yellow",
    57→            )
    58→        )
    59→
    60→
    61→def _print_completion_jump_back_guidance() -> None:
    62→    """Print the post-summary guidance for revisiting earlier triage stages."""
    63→    print()
    64→    print(
    65→        colorize(
    66→            "  To revise an earlier stage: desloppify plan triage --stage <observe|reflect|organize|enrich|sense-check>",
    67→            "dim",
    68→        )
    69→    )
    70→    print(colorize("  Pass --report to update, or omit to keep existing analysis.", "dim"))
    71→

    73→def _record_incomplete_recovery(
    74→    *,
    75→    plan: dict,
    76→    state: dict,
    77→    services: TriageServices,
    78→    reason: str,
    79→) -> list[str]:
    80→    """Persist the current missing-disposition set for recovery guidance."""
    81→    missing = sync_undispositioned_triage_meta(plan, state)
    82→    services.save_plan(plan)
    83→    if missing:
    84→        print(
    85→            colorize(
    86→                f"  Triage recovery blocked: {len(missing)} issue(s) still need cluster/skip dispositions ({reason}).",
    87→                "yellow",
    88→            )
    89→        )
    90→        for issue_id in missing[:5]:
    91→            print(colorize(f"    {issue_id}", "dim"))
    92→        if len(missing) > 5:
    93→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(missing) - 5} more", "dim"))
    94→    return missing
    95→

    97→def _resolve_confirm_existing_context(
    98→    *,
    99→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   100→    plan: dict,
   101→    services: TriageServices,
   102→    stages: dict,
   103→) -> tuple[object, bool] | None:
   104→    """Load confirm-existing triage context and validate the stage prerequisites."""
   105→    runtime = services.command_runtime(args)
   106→    state = runtime.state
   107→    si = services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   108→    has_only_additions = bool(si.new_since_last) and not si.resolved_since_last
   109→    if not _confirm_existing_stages_valid(
   110→        stages=stages,
   111→        has_only_additions=has_only_additions,
   112→        si=si,
   113→    ):
   114→        return None
   115→    return si, has_only_additions
   116→
   117→
   118→def _resolve_confirm_existing_inputs(
   119→    *,
   120→    note: str | None,
   121→    strategy: str | None,
   122→    confirmed: str | None,
   123→    plan: dict,
   124→    state: dict,
   125→    meta: dict,
   126→    si,
   127→    has_only_additions: bool,
   128→) -> tuple[str, str, str] | None:
   129→    """Validate and resolve note/strategy/confirmed inputs for confirm-existing."""
   130→    if not _confirm_note_valid(note):
   131→        return None
   132→    resolved_strategy = _resolve_confirm_existing_strategy(
   133→        strategy,
   134→        has_only_additions=has_only_additions,
   135→        meta=meta,
   136→    )
   137→    if resolved_strategy is None:
   138→        return None
   139→    if not _confirm_strategy_valid(resolved_strategy):
   140→        return None
   141→    confirmed_text = _confirmed_text_or_error(
   142→        plan=plan,
   143→        state=state,
   144→        confirmed=confirmed,
   145→    )
   146→    if confirmed_text is None:
   147→        return None
   148→    if not _note_cites_new_issues_or_error(note, si):
   149→        return None
   150→    return note or "", resolved_strategy, confirmed_text
   151→

   153→def _cmd_triage_complete(
   154→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   155→    *,
   156→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   157→) -> None:
   158→    """Complete triage — requires organize stage (or confirm-existing path)."""
   159→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   160→    strategy: str | None = getattr(args, "strategy", None)
   161→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   162→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   163→
   164→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   165→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to complete.", "yellow"))
   166→        return
   167→
   168→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   169→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   170→
   171→    state = resolved_services.command_runtime(args).state
   172→    review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
   173→
   174→    # Organize gate
   175→    if not _require_organize_stage_for_complete(plan=plan, meta=meta, stages=stages):
   176→        return
   177→    if not _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete(
   178→        plan=plan, stages=stages, stage="organize",
   179→        attestation=attestation, save_plan_fn=resolved_services.save_plan,
   180→    ):
   181→        return
   182→
   183→    # Enrich gate — compute underspecified steps once, share across require + confirm
   184→    underspec = _underspecified_steps(plan)
   185→    if not _require_enrich_stage_for_complete(
   186→        plan=plan, meta=meta, stages=stages, underspec=underspec,
   187→    ):
   188→        return
   189→    if not _auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete(
   190→        plan=plan, stages=stages, attestation=attestation,
   191→        save_plan_fn=resolved_services.save_plan, underspec=underspec,
   192→    ):
   193→        return
   194→
   195→    # Sense-check gate
   196→    if not _require_sense_check_stage_for_complete(plan=plan, meta=meta, stages=stages):
   197→        return
   198→    if not _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete(
   199→        plan=plan, stages=stages, stage="sense-check",
   200→        attestation=attestation, save_plan_fn=resolved_services.save_plan,
   201→    ):
   202→        return
   203→
   204→    if not _completion_clusters_valid(plan, state):
   205→        _record_incomplete_recovery(
   206→            plan=plan,
   207→            state=state,
   208→            services=resolved_services,
   209→            reason="completion validation failed",
   210→        )
   211→        return
   212→
   213→    organized, total, _clusters = triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=review_ids)
   214→    if total > 0 and organized == 0:
   215→        _print_completion_coverage_warning(organized=organized, total=total)
   216→        return
   217→    _print_completion_coverage_warning(organized=organized, total=total)
   218→
   219→    strategy = _resolve_completion_strategy(strategy, meta=meta)
   220→    if strategy is None:
   221→        return
   222→    if not _completion_strategy_valid(strategy):
   223→        return
   224→
   225→    # Show summary
   226→    _print_complete_summary(plan, stages)
   227→
   228→    organized, total, _ = triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=review_ids)
   229→
   230→    _print_completion_jump_back_guidance()
   231→
   232→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   233→        plan,
   234→        "triage_complete",
   235→        actor="user",
   236→        detail={
   237→            "strategy_len": len(strategy.strip()),
   238→            "coverage": f"{organized}/{total}",
   239→        },
   240→    )
   241→
   242→    apply_completion(args, plan, strategy, services=resolved_services)
   243→
   244→    print_user_message(
   245→        "Hey — triage is done. Run `desloppify next` and start"
   246→        " executing the plan. Don't stop until you're done —"
   247→        " unless I've told you otherwise, just go."
   248→    )
   249→
   250→
   251→def _cmd_confirm_existing(
   252→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   253→    *,
   254→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   255→) -> None:
   256→    """Fast-track: confirm existing plan structure is still valid."""
   257→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   258→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   259→    strategy: str | None = getattr(args, "strategy", None)
   260→    confirmed: str | None = getattr(args, "confirmed", None)
   261→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   262→
   263→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   264→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to confirm.", "yellow"))
   265→        return
   266→
   267→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   268→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   269→
   270→    # Require a prior completed triage — can't skip the full flow on first run
   271→    if not _require_prior_strategy_for_confirm(meta):
   272→        return
   273→
   274→    context = _resolve_confirm_existing_context(
   275→        args=args,
   276→        plan=plan,
   277→        services=resolved_services,
   278→        stages=stages,
   279→    )
   280→    if context is None:
   281→        return
   282→    si, has_only_additions = context
   283→    state = resolved_services.command_runtime(args).state
   284→
   285→    clusters_with_issues = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   286→    if not clusters_with_issues:
   287→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: no clusters with issues exist.", "red"))
   288→        print(colorize("  Use the full organize flow instead.", "dim"))
   289→        return
   290→
   291→    resolved_inputs = _resolve_confirm_existing_inputs(
   292→        note=note,
   293→        strategy=strategy,
   294→        confirmed=confirmed,
   295→        plan=plan,
   296→        state=state,
   297→        meta=meta,
   298→        si=si,
   299→        has_only_additions=has_only_additions,
   300→    )
   301→    if resolved_inputs is None:
   302→        return
   303→    note_text, strategy, confirmed_text = resolved_inputs
   304→
   305→    # Record organize as confirmed-existing and complete
   306→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   307→    record_confirm_existing_completion(
   308→        stages=stages,
   309→        note=note_text,
   310→        issue_count=len(clusters_with_issues),
   311→        confirmed_text=confirmed_text,
   312→    )
   313→
   314→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   315→        plan,
   316→        "triage_confirm_existing",
   317→        actor="user",
   318→        detail={"confirmed_text": confirmed_text},
   319→    )
   320→
   321→    apply_completion(
   322→        args,
   323→        plan,
   324→        strategy,
   325→        services=resolved_services,
   326→        completion_mode="confirm_existing",
   327→        completion_note=note_text,
   328→    )
   329→    print(
   330→        colorize(
   331→            "  Confirmed existing plan — reused the current enriched cluster plan.",
   332→            "green",
   333→        )
   334→    )
   335→
   336→
   337→def cmd_triage_complete(
   338→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   339→    *,
   340→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   341→) -> None:
   342→    """Public entrypoint for triage completion."""
   343→    _cmd_triage_complete(args, services=services)
   344→
   345→
   346→def cmd_confirm_existing(
   347→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   348→    *,
   349→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   350→) -> None:
   351→    """Public entrypoint for confirm-existing completion path."""
   352→    _cmd_confirm_existing(args, services=services)
   353→
   354→
   355→__all__ = [
   356→    "cmd_confirm_existing",
   357→    "cmd_triage_complete",
   358→    "_cmd_confirm_existing",
   359→    "_cmd_triage_complete",
   360→]
   361→

1→"""Shared triage guardrail helpers for command entrypoints."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.issue_id_display import short_issue_id
     9→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS, CommandError
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.triage import (
    12→    compute_new_issue_ids,
    13→    is_triage_stale,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import load_plan
    16→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import (
    17→    TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE,
    18→    TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX,
    19→    triage_phase_banner,
    20→)
    21→
    22→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass
    26→class TriageGuardrailResult:
    27→    """Structured result from triage staleness detection."""
    28→
    29→    is_stale: bool = False
    30→    new_ids: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    31→    _plan: dict | None = field(default=None, repr=False)
    32→
    33→
    34→def triage_guardrail_status(
    35→    *,
    36→    plan: dict | None = None,
    37→    state: dict | None = None,
    38→) -> TriageGuardrailResult:
    39→    """Pure detection: is triage stale? Returns structured result, no side effects."""
    40→    try:
    41→        resolved_plan = plan if isinstance(plan, dict) else load_plan()
    42→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
    43→        logger.debug("Triage guardrail status skipped: plan could not be loaded.", exc_info=exc)
    44→        return TriageGuardrailResult()
    45→
    46→    resolved_state = state or {}
    47→
    48→    if not is_triage_stale(resolved_plan, resolved_state):
    49→        return TriageGuardrailResult(_plan=resolved_plan)
    50→
    51→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
    52→    if resolved_state:
    53→        new_ids = compute_new_issue_ids(resolved_plan, resolved_state)
    54→
    55→    return TriageGuardrailResult(is_stale=True, new_ids=new_ids, _plan=resolved_plan)
    56→
    57→
    58→def triage_guardrail_messages(
    59→    *,
    60→    plan: dict | None = None,
    61→    state: dict | None = None,
    62→) -> list[str]:
    63→    """Return warning strings without printing."""
    64→    resolved_state = state or {}
    65→    result = triage_guardrail_status(plan=plan, state=state)
    66→    if not result.is_stale:
    67→        return []
    68→
    69→    messages: list[str] = []
    70→    if result.new_ids:
    71→        messages.append(
    72→            f"{len(result.new_ids)} new review issue(s) not yet triaged."
    73→            " Run the staged triage runner to incorporate them "
    74→            f"(`{TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX}` or `{TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE}`)."
    75→        )
    76→
    77→    if result._plan is not None:
    78→        banner = triage_phase_banner(result._plan, resolved_state)
    79→        if banner:
    80→            messages.append(banner)
    81→
    82→    return messages
    83→
    84→
    85→def print_triage_guardrail_info(
    86→    *,
    87→    plan: dict | None = None,
    88→    state: dict | None = None,
    89→) -> bool:
    90→    """Print yellow info banner if triage is stale. Returns True if banner was shown."""
    91→    messages = triage_guardrail_messages(plan=plan, state=state)
    92→    for msg in messages:
    93→        print(colorize(f"  {msg}", "yellow"))
    94→    return bool(messages)
    95→
    96→
    97→def require_triage_current_or_exit(
    98→    *,
    99→    state: dict,
   100→    bypass: bool = False,
   101→    attest: str = "",
   102→) -> None:
   103→    """Gate: exit(1) if triage is stale and not bypassed. Name signals the exit."""
   104→    result = triage_guardrail_status(state=state)
   105→    if not result.is_stale:
   106→        return
   107→
   108→    if bypass and attest and len(attest.strip()) >= 30:
   109→        print(colorize(
   110→            "  Triage guardrail bypassed with attestation.",
   111→            "yellow",
   112→        ))
   113→        return
   114→
   115→    new_ids = result.new_ids
   116→    lines = [
   117→        f"BLOCKED: {len(new_ids) or 'some'} new review issue(s) have not been triaged."
   118→    ]
   119→    if new_ids:
   120→        for fid in sorted(new_ids)[:5]:
   121→            issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(fid, {})
   122→            lines.append(f"    * [{short_issue_id(fid)}] {issue.get('summary', '')}")
   123→        if len(new_ids) > 5:
   124→            lines.append(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 5} more")
   125→    lines.append("")
   126→    lines.append(f"  NEXT STEP (Codex):  {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX}")
   127→    lines.append(f"  NEXT STEP (Claude): {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE}")
   128→    lines.append("  Manual fallback:    desloppify plan triage")
   129→    lines.append("  (Review new issues, then either --confirm-existing or re-plan.)")
   130→    lines.append("")
   131→    lines.append("  View new execution items:  desloppify plan queue --sort recent")
   132→    lines.append("  View broader backlog:      desloppify backlog")
   133→    lines.append('  To bypass: --force-resolve --attest "I understand the plan may be stale..."')
   134→    raise CommandError("\n".join(lines))
   135→
   136→
   137→__all__ = [
   138→    "TriageGuardrailResult",
   139→    "print_triage_guardrail_info",
   140→    "require_triage_current_or_exit",
   141→    "triage_guardrail_messages",
   142→    "triage_guardrail_status",
   143→]
   144→

    82→
    83→def triage_run_stages_command(
    84→    *,
    85→    runner: str = "codex",
    86→    only_stages: str | tuple[str, ...] | list[str] | None = None,
    87→) -> str:
    88→    """Return the canonical staged triage runner command."""
    89→    resolved_runner = str(runner).strip().lower()
    90→    if resolved_runner not in TRIAGE_RUNNERS:
    91→        supported = ", ".join(TRIAGE_RUNNERS)
    92→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported triage runner: {runner!r}. Valid: {supported}")
    93→
    94→    command = f"desloppify plan triage --run-stages --runner {resolved_runner}"
    95→    if only_stages is None:
    96→        return command
    97→
    98→    if isinstance(only_stages, str):
    99→        stages = [only_stages]
   100→    else:
   101→        stages = [str(stage).strip().lower() for stage in only_stages if str(stage).strip()]
   102→
   103→    invalid = [stage for stage in stages if stage not in _RUNNER_STAGE_NAMES]
   104→    if invalid:
   105→        supported = ", ".join(sorted(_RUNNER_STAGE_NAMES))
   106→        bad = ", ".join(sorted(set(invalid)))
   107→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported triage stage(s): {bad}. Valid: {supported}")
   108→
   109→    return f"{command} --only-stages {','.join(stages)}"
   110→
   111→
   112→def triage_runner_commands(
   113→    *,
   114→    only_stages: str | tuple[str, ...] | list[str] | None = None,
   115→) -> tuple[tuple[str, str], tuple[str, str]]:
   116→    """Return the preferred staged-runner commands for Codex and Claude."""
   117→    return (
   118→        ("Codex", triage_run_stages_command(runner="codex", only_stages=only_stages)),
   119→        ("Claude", triage_run_stages_command(runner="claude", only_stages=only_stages)),
   120→    )
   121→
   122→
   123→def triage_manual_stage_command(stage: str) -> str:
   124→    """Return the manual fallback command for a triage stage."""
   125→    resolved_stage = str(stage).strip().lower()
   126→    if resolved_stage not in _MANUAL_STAGE_COMMANDS:
   127→        supported = ", ".join(sorted(_MANUAL_STAGE_COMMANDS))
   128→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported triage stage: {stage!r}. Valid: {supported}")
   129→    return _MANUAL_STAGE_COMMANDS[resolved_stage]

1→"""Enrichment and cluster-level validation helpers for triage stages."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     9→
    10→from ..review_coverage import cluster_issue_ids
    11→from .stage_policy import require_prerequisite
    12→
    13→_PATH_RE = re.compile(r"(?:[\w./-]+/)?(?:src|supabase)/[\w./-]+\.\w+(?::\d+(?:[-:]\d+)?)?")
    14→_LINE_SUFFIX_RE = re.compile(r":\d+(?:[-:]\d+)?$")
    15→_EXT_SWAPS = {".ts": ".tsx", ".tsx": ".ts", ".js": ".jsx", ".jsx": ".js"}
    16→_VALID_EFFORTS = {"trivial", "small", "medium", "large"}
    17→
    18→
    19→def _strip_line_suffix(path_str: str) -> str:
    20→    """Remove trailing :N, :N-M, :N:M line-number suffixes from a path."""
    21→    return _LINE_SUFFIX_RE.sub("", path_str)
    22→
    23→
    24→def _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan: dict):
    25→    """Yield manual clusters that still carry issue IDs."""
    26→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    27→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster_issue_ids(cluster):
    28→            continue
    29→        yield name, cluster
    30→
    31→
    32→def _cluster_steps(cluster: dict) -> list[dict]:
    33→    """Return structured action steps for one cluster."""
    34→    return [
    35→        step
    36→        for step in (cluster.get("action_steps") or [])
    37→        if isinstance(step, dict)
    38→    ]
    39→
    40→
    41→def _detail_paths(detail: str) -> list[str]:
    42→    """Extract file paths from one step detail string."""
    43→    if not detail:
    44→        return []
    45→    return _PATH_RE.findall(detail)
    46→
    47→
    48→def _path_exists_or_alt_exists(repo_root: Path, path_str: str) -> bool:
    49→    """Return whether the referenced path or a ts/js sibling exists."""
    50→    cleaned = _strip_line_suffix(path_str)
    51→    path = repo_root / cleaned
    52→    if path.exists():
    53→        return True
    54→    alt_ext = _EXT_SWAPS.get(path.suffix)
    55→    return bool(alt_ext and path.with_suffix(alt_ext).exists())
    56→
    57→
    58→def _require_organize_stage_for_enrich(stages: dict) -> bool:
    59→    """Gate: organize must be done before enrich."""
    60→    return require_prerequisite(
    61→        stages,
    62→        flow="enrich",
    63→        messages={
    64→            "observe": (
    65→                "  Cannot enrich: observe stage not complete.",
    66→                '  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."',
    67→            ),
    68→            "reflect": (
    69→                "  Cannot enrich: reflect stage not complete.",
    70→                '  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "..."',
    71→            ),
    72→            "organize": (
    73→                "  Cannot enrich: organize stage not complete.",
    74→                '  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage organize --report "..."',
    75→            ),
    76→        },
    77→    )
    78→
    79→
    80→def _underspecified_steps(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, int]]:
    81→    """Return (cluster_name, bare_count, total_count) for steps missing detail or issue_refs."""
    82→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int]] = []
    83→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
    84→        steps = _cluster_steps(cluster)
    85→        if not steps:
    86→            continue
    87→        bare = sum(
    88→            1
    89→            for step in steps
    90→            if isinstance(step, dict) and (not step.get("detail") or not step.get("issue_refs"))
    91→        )
    92→        if bare > 0:
    93→            results.append((name, bare, len(steps)))
    94→    return results
    95→
    96→
    97→def _steps_with_bad_paths(plan: dict, repo_root: Path) -> list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]:
    98→    """Return steps referencing file paths that don't exist on disk."""
    99→    results: list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]] = []
   100→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
   101→        for i, step in enumerate(_cluster_steps(cluster)):
   102→            detail = step.get("detail", "")
   103→            if not detail:
   104→                continue
   105→            bad = [
   106→                path_str
   107→                for path_str in _detail_paths(detail)
   108→                if not _path_exists_or_alt_exists(repo_root, path_str)
   109→            ]
   110→            if bad:
   111→                results.append((name, i + 1, bad))
   112→    return results
   113→
   114→
   115→def _steps_without_effort(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, int]]:
   116→    """Return (cluster_name, missing_count, total) for steps without effort tags."""
   117→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int]] = []
   118→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
   119→        steps = _cluster_steps(cluster)
   120→        if not steps:
   121→            continue
   122→        missing = sum(
   123→            1 for step in steps if isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("effort") not in _VALID_EFFORTS
   124→        )
   125→        if missing:
   126→            results.append((name, missing, len(steps)))
   127→    return results
   128→
   129→
   130→def _cluster_file_overlaps(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, str, list[str]]]:
   131→    """Return pairs of clusters with overlapping file references in step details."""
   132→    cluster_files: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
   133→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
   134→        paths: set[str] = set()
   135→        for step in _cluster_steps(cluster):
   136→            paths.update(_detail_paths(step.get("detail", "")))
   137→        if paths:
   138→            cluster_files[name] = paths
   139→
   140→    overlaps: list[tuple[str, str, list[str]]] = []
   141→    names = sorted(cluster_files.keys())
   142→    for i, left in enumerate(names):
   143→        for right in names[i + 1 :]:
   144→            shared = cluster_files[left] & cluster_files[right]
   145→            if shared:
   146→                overlaps.append((left, right, sorted(shared)))
   147→    return overlaps
   148→
   149→
   150→def _clusters_with_directory_scatter(
   151→    plan: dict,
   152→    *,
   153→    threshold: int = 5,
   154→) -> list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]:
   155→    """Return clusters whose issues span too many unrelated directories."""
   156→    results: list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]] = []
   157→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
   158→        dirs: set[str] = set()
   159→        for step in _cluster_steps(cluster):
   160→            for path_str in _detail_paths(step.get("detail", "")):
   161→                parts = path_str.split("/")
   162→                if len(parts) >= 3:
   163→                    dirs.add("/".join(parts[:3]))
   164→                elif len(parts) >= 2:
   165→                    dirs.add("/".join(parts[:2]))
   166→        if len(dirs) >= threshold:
   167→            results.append((name, len(dirs), sorted(dirs)[:6]))
   168→    return results
   169→
   170→
   171→def _clusters_with_high_step_ratio(
   172→    plan: dict,
   173→    *,
   174→    max_ratio: float = 1.0,
   175→) -> list[tuple[str, int, int, float]]:
   176→    """Return clusters where step count >= issue count (1:1 mapping)."""
   177→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int, float]] = []
   178→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
   179→        steps = len(_cluster_steps(cluster))
   180→        issues = len(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   181→        if issues >= 3 and steps > 0:
   182→            ratio = steps / issues
   183→            if ratio > max_ratio:
   184→                results.append((name, steps, issues, ratio))
   185→    return results
   186→
   187→
   188→def _steps_missing_issue_refs(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, int]]:
   189→    """Return (cluster_name, missing_count, total) for steps without issue_refs."""
   190→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int]] = []
   191→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
   192→        steps = _cluster_steps(cluster)
   193→        if not steps:
   194→            continue
   195→        missing = sum(1 for step in steps if not step.get("issue_refs"))
   196→        if missing:
   197→            results.append((name, missing, len(steps)))
   198→    return results
   199→
   200→
   201→def _steps_with_vague_detail(plan: dict, repo_root: Path) -> list[tuple[str, int, str]]:
   202→    """Return steps with detail too vague to be executor-ready."""
   203→    del repo_root
   204→    results: list[tuple[str, int, str]] = []
   205→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
   206→        for i, step in enumerate(_cluster_steps(cluster)):
   207→            detail = step.get("detail", "")
   208→            if not detail:
   209→                results.append((name, i + 1, step.get("title", "(no title)")))
   210→                continue
   211→            has_path = bool(_detail_paths(detail))
   212→            if len(detail) < 80 and not has_path:
   213→                results.append((name, i + 1, step.get("title", "(no title)")))
   214→    return results
   215→
   216→
   217→def _steps_referencing_skipped_issues(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]:
   218→    """Return steps whose issue_refs include wontfixed/skipped issues."""
   219→    wontfixed = set()
   220→    for fid, issue in plan.get("issues", {}).items():
   221→        if isinstance(issue, dict) and issue.get("status") in ("wontfix", "skipped"):
   222→            wontfixed.add(fid)
   223→    for fid in plan.get("wontfix", {}):
   224→        wontfixed.add(fid)
   225→
   226→    if not wontfixed:
   227→        return []
   228→
   229→    results: list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]] = []
   230→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
   231→        for i, step in enumerate(_cluster_steps(cluster)):
   232→            refs = step.get("issue_refs") or []
   233→            stale = [ref for ref in refs if ref in wontfixed]
   234→            if stale:
   235→                results.append((name, i + 1, stale))
   236→    return results
   237→
   238→
   239→def _enrich_report_or_error(report: str | None) -> str | None:
   240→    if not report:
   241→        print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage enrich.", "red"))
   242→        print(colorize("  Summarize the enrichment work you did:", "dim"))
   243→        print(colorize("  - Which clusters did you add detail/refs to?", "dim"))
   244→        print(colorize("  - Are steps specific enough for an executor with zero context?", "dim"))
   245→        print(colorize("  - Did you link issue_refs so steps auto-complete on resolve?", "dim"))
   246→        return None
   247→    if len(report) < 100:
   248→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   249→        print(colorize("  Explain what enrichment you did and why steps are executor-ready.", "dim"))
   250→        return None
   251→    return report
   252→
   253→
   254→__all__ = [
   255→    "_cluster_file_overlaps",
   256→    "_clusters_with_directory_scatter",
   257→    "_clusters_with_high_step_ratio",
   258→    "_enrich_report_or_error",
   259→    "_require_organize_stage_for_enrich",
   260→    "_steps_missing_issue_refs",
   261→    "_steps_referencing_skipped_issues",
   262→    "_steps_with_bad_paths",
   263→    "_steps_with_vague_detail",
   264→    "_steps_without_effort",
   265→    "_strip_line_suffix",
   266→    "_underspecified_steps",
   267→]
   268→

1→"""Organize-stage validation helpers and reflect-ledger reconciliation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import Literal

    10→from ..review_coverage import cluster_issue_ids, manual_clusters_with_issues
    11→from ..stages.helpers import unclustered_review_issues, unenriched_clusters
    12→from .reflect_accounting import ReflectDisposition
    13→
    14→
    15→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    16→class ActualDisposition:
    17→    """What actually happened to an issue in plan state."""
    18→
    19→    kind: Literal["clustered", "skipped", "unplaced"]
    20→    cluster_name: str = ""
    21→
    22→    def describe(self, intended: ReflectDisposition | None = None) -> str:
    23→        """Human-readable description for error messages."""
    24→        if self.kind == "skipped":
    25→            return "permanently skipped"
    26→        if self.kind == "clustered":
    27→            if intended and intended.decision == "cluster" and self.cluster_name != intended.target:
    28→                return f'in cluster "{self.cluster_name}" (expected "{intended.target}")'
    29→            return f'clustered in "{self.cluster_name}"'
    30→        if self.kind == "unplaced":
    31→            if intended and intended.decision == "cluster":
    32→                return "not in any cluster"
    33→            return "not skipped, not clustered"
    34→        return "unknown state"
    35→
    36→
    37→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    38→class LedgerMismatch:
    39→    """One issue where plan state diverges from the reflect disposition."""
    40→
    41→    intended: ReflectDisposition
    42→    actual: ActualDisposition
    43→
    44→    @property
    45→    def issue_id(self) -> str:
    46→        return self.intended.issue_id
    47→
    48→    @property
    49→    def expected_decision(self) -> str:
    50→        return self.intended.decision
    51→
    52→    @property
    53→    def expected_target(self) -> str:
    54→        return self.intended.target
    55→
    56→    @property
    57→    def actual_state(self) -> str:
    58→        return self.actual.describe(self.intended)
    59→
    60→
    61→def _manual_clusters_or_error(
    62→    plan: dict,
    63→    *,
    64→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    65→) -> list[str] | None:
    66→    manual_clusters = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
    67→    if manual_clusters:
    68→        return manual_clusters
    69→    if open_review_ids is not None and not open_review_ids:
    70→        return []
    71→    any_clusters = [
    72→        name for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
    73→        if cluster_issue_ids(cluster)
    74→    ]
    75→    if any_clusters:
    76→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: only auto-clusters exist.", "red"))
    77→        print(colorize("  Create manual clusters that group issues by root cause:", "dim"))
    78→    else:
    79→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: no clusters with issues exist.", "red"))
    80→    print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
    81→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan cluster add <name> <issue-patterns>", "dim"))
    82→    return None

    85→def _clusters_enriched_or_error(plan: dict) -> bool:

   103→def _unclustered_review_issues_or_error(plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
   104→    """Block if open review issues aren't in any manual cluster. Return True if OK."""
   105→    unclustered = unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
   106→    if not unclustered:
   107→        return True
   108→    print(colorize(f"  Cannot organize: {len(unclustered)} review issue(s) have no cluster.", "red"))
   109→    for fid in unclustered[:10]:
   110→        short = fid.rsplit("::", 2)[-2] if "::" in fid else fid
   111→        print(colorize(f"    {short}", "yellow"))
   112→    if len(unclustered) > 10:
   113→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 10} more", "yellow"))
   114→    print()
   115→    print(colorize("  Every review issue needs an action plan. Either:", "dim"))
   116→    print(colorize("    1. Add to a cluster: desloppify plan cluster add <name> <pattern>", "dim"))
   117→    print(

   119→            '    2. Wontfix it: desloppify plan skip --permanent <pattern> --note "reason" --attest "..."',
