```
   125→
   126→def _organize_report_or_error(report: str | None) -> str | None:
   127→    if not report:
   128→        print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage organize.", "red"))
   129→        print(colorize("  Summarize your prioritized organization:", "dim"))
   130→        print(colorize("  - Did you defer contradictory issues before clustering?", "dim"))
   131→        print(colorize("  - What clusters did you create and why?", "dim"))
   132→        print(colorize("  - Explicit priority ordering: which cluster 1st, 2nd, 3rd and why?", "dim"))
   133→        print(colorize("  - What depends on what? What unblocks the most?", "dim"))
   134→        return None
   135→    if len(report) < 100:
   136→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   137→        print(colorize("  Explain what you organized, your priorities, and focus order.", "dim"))
   138→        return None
   139→    return report
   140→
   141→
   142→def _build_actual_disposition_index(plan: dict) -> dict[str, ActualDisposition]:
   143→    """Build a lookup from issue ID to its actual disposition in plan state."""
   144→    index: dict[str, ActualDisposition] = {}
   145→
   146→    for cluster_name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   147→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   148→            continue
   149→        for fid in cluster_issue_ids(cluster):
   150→            index[fid] = ActualDisposition(kind="clustered", cluster_name=cluster_name)
   151→
   152→    for fid in (plan.get("skipped", {}) or {}):
   153→        if isinstance(fid, str):
   154→            index[fid] = ActualDisposition(kind="skipped")
   155→
   156→    return index
   157→
   158→
   159→def _disposition_matches(intended: ReflectDisposition, actual: ActualDisposition) -> bool:
   160→    """True when the actual plan state satisfies the intended disposition."""
   161→    if intended.decision == "permanent_skip":
   162→        return actual.kind == "skipped"
   163→    if intended.decision == "cluster":
   164→        return actual.kind == "clustered" and actual.cluster_name == intended.target
   165→    return False
   166→
   167→
   168→def validate_organize_against_dispositions(
   169→    *,
   170→    plan: dict,
   171→) -> list[LedgerMismatch]:
   172→    """Check that plan mutations match the unified issue_dispositions map.
   173→
   174→    Preferred over ``validate_organize_against_reflect_ledger`` when the
   175→    disposition map exists.  Only checks entries with a ``decision`` field
   176→    (i.e. entries that have been through observe auto-skip or reflect).
   177→    """
   178→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   179→    dispositions = meta.get("issue_dispositions", {})
   180→    if not dispositions:
   181→        return []
   182→
   183→    # Only validate entries that have a decision
   184→    decided = [
   185→        (issue_id, disp)
   186→        for issue_id, disp in dispositions.items()
   187→        if disp.get("decision")
   188→    ]
   189→    if not decided:
   190→        return []
   191→
   192→    actuals = _build_actual_disposition_index(plan)
   193→    unplaced = ActualDisposition(kind="unplaced")
   194→    mismatches: list[LedgerMismatch] = []
   195→
   196→    for issue_id, disp in decided:
   197→        decision = disp["decision"]
   198→        target = disp.get("target", "")
   199→        # Normalize to ReflectDisposition for the mismatch report
   200→        reflect_decision = "permanent_skip" if decision == "skip" else decision
   201→        intended = ReflectDisposition(
   202→            issue_id=issue_id,
   203→            decision=reflect_decision,
   204→            target=target,
   205→        )
   206→        actual = actuals.get(issue_id, unplaced)
   207→        if not _disposition_matches(intended, actual):
   208→            mismatches.append(LedgerMismatch(intended=intended, actual=actual))
   209→
   210→    return mismatches
   211→
   212→
   213→def validate_organize_against_reflect_ledger(
   214→    *,
   215→    plan: dict,
   216→    stages: dict,
   217→) -> list[LedgerMismatch]:
   218→    """Check that plan mutations match the reflect disposition ledger."""
   219→    raw_ledger = stages.get("reflect", {}).get("disposition_ledger")
   220→    if not raw_ledger:
   221→        return []
   222→
   223→    ledger = [ReflectDisposition.from_dict(d) for d in raw_ledger]
   224→    actuals = _build_actual_disposition_index(plan)
   225→    unplaced = ActualDisposition(kind="unplaced")
   226→
   227→    return [
   228→        LedgerMismatch(intended=d, actual=actuals.get(d.issue_id, unplaced))
   229→        for d in ledger
   230→        if not _disposition_matches(d, actuals.get(d.issue_id, unplaced))
   231→    ]
   232→
   233→
   234→def _validate_organize_against_ledger_or_error(
   235→    *,
   236→    plan: dict,
   237→    stages: dict,
   238→) -> bool:
   239→    """Block organize if plan state diverges from dispositions or the reflect ledger."""
   240→    # Prefer unified disposition map when available
   241→    mismatches = validate_organize_against_dispositions(plan=plan)
   242→    if not mismatches:
   243→        # Fall back to legacy reflect ledger validation
   244→        mismatches = validate_organize_against_reflect_ledger(
   245→            plan=plan,
   246→            stages=stages,
   247→        )
   248→    if not mismatches:
   249→        return True
   250→
   251→    print(
   252→        colorize(
   253→            f"  Cannot organize: {len(mismatches)} issue(s) diverge from the reflect plan.",
   254→            "red",
   255→        )
   256→    )
   257→    for mismatch in mismatches[:10]:
   258→        short_id = mismatch.issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
   259→        print(
   260→            colorize(
   261→                f"    {short_id}: reflect said {mismatch.expected_decision} "
   262→                f'"{mismatch.expected_target}", but {mismatch.actual_state}',
   263→                "yellow",
   264→            )
   265→        )
   266→    if len(mismatches) > 10:
   267→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(mismatches) - 10} more", "yellow"))
   268→    print()
   269→    print(
   270→        colorize(
   271→            "  Fix the plan to match the reflect ledger, or re-run reflect to update dispositions.",
   272→            "dim",
   273→        )
   274→    )
   275→    return False
   276→
   277→
   278→__all__ = [
   279→    "ActualDisposition",
   280→    "LedgerMismatch",
   281→    "_clusters_enriched_or_error",
   282→    "_manual_clusters_or_error",
   283→    "_organize_report_or_error",
   284→    "_unclustered_review_issues_or_error",
   285→    "_validate_organize_against_ledger_or_error",
   286→    "validate_organize_against_dispositions",
   287→    "validate_organize_against_reflect_ledger",
   288→]
```

```
    78→def _auto_confirm_stage(
    79→    *,
    80→    plan: dict,
    81→    stage_record: dict,
    82→    attestation: str | None,
    83→    request: AutoConfirmStageRequest,
    84→    save_plan_fn: Callable[..., None] | None = None,
    85→    utc_now_fn: Callable[[], str] | None = None,
    86→) -> bool:

    90→        attestation=attestation,
    91→        request=request,

    96→
    97→def _auto_confirm_observe_if_attested(
    98→    *,
    99→    plan: dict,
   100→    stages: dict,
   101→    attestation: str | None,
   102→    triage_input,
   103→) -> bool:
   104→    return auto_confirm_observe_if_attested(
   105→        plan=plan,
   106→        stages=stages,
   107→        attestation=attestation,
   108→        triage_input=triage_input,
   109→        save_plan_fn=save_plan,
   110→        utc_now_fn=utc_now,
   111→    )
   112→
   113→
   114→def _auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize(
   115→    *,
   116→    args,
   117→    plan: dict,
   118→    stages: dict,
   119→    attestation: str | None,
   120→    deps: ReflectAutoConfirmDeps | None = None,
   121→) -> bool:
   122→    resolved_deps = deps or ReflectAutoConfirmDeps()
   123→    wrapped_deps = ReflectAutoConfirmDeps(
   124→        triage_input=resolved_deps.triage_input,
   125→        command_runtime_fn=resolved_deps.command_runtime_fn or command_runtime,
   126→        collect_triage_input_fn=resolved_deps.collect_triage_input_fn or collect_triage_input,
   127→        detect_recurring_patterns_fn=(
   128→            resolved_deps.detect_recurring_patterns_fn or detect_recurring_patterns
   129→        ),
   130→        save_plan_fn=resolved_deps.save_plan_fn or save_plan,
   131→    )
   132→    return auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize(
   133→        args=args,
   134→        plan=plan,
   135→        stages=stages,
   136→        attestation=attestation,
   137→        deps=wrapped_deps,
   138→        utc_now_fn=utc_now,
   139→    )
   140→
   141→
   142→def _validate_recurring_dimension_mentions(
   143→    *,
   144→    report: str,
   145→    recurring_dims: list[str],
   146→    recurring: RecurringDimensionBuckets,
   147→) -> bool:
   148→    if not recurring_dims:
   149→        return True
   150→    report_lower = report.lower()
   151→    mentioned = [dim for dim in recurring_dims if dim.lower() in report_lower]
   152→    if mentioned:
   153→        return True
   154→    print(colorize("  Recurring patterns detected but not addressed in report:", "red"))
   155→    for dim in recurring_dims:
   156→        info = recurring[dim]
   157→        print(
   158→            colorize(
   159→                f"    {dim}: {len(info['resolved'])} resolved, "
   160→                f"{len(info['open'])} still open — potential loop",
   161→                "yellow",
   162→            )
   163→        )
   164→    print(colorize("  Your report must mention at least one recurring dimension name.", "dim"))
   165→    return False
   166→
   167→
   168→def _require_reflect_stage_for_organize(stages: dict) -> bool:
   169→    return require_stage_prerequisite(
   170→        stages,
   171→        flow="organize",
   172→        messages={
   173→            "observe": (
   174→                "  Cannot organize: observe stage not complete.",
   175→                '  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."',
   176→            ),
   177→            "reflect": (
   178→                "  Cannot organize: reflect stage not complete.",
   179→                '  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "..."',
   180→            ),
   181→        },
   182→    )
   183→
   184→
   185→__all__ = [
```

```
1→"""Canonical stale/unscored subjective policy helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import hashlib
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import all_subjective_entries
    11→
    12→_REVIEW_DETECTORS = ("review", "concerns")
    13→
    14→
    15→def open_review_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    16→    """Return IDs of open review/concerns issues from state."""
    17→    return {
    18→        fid
    19→        for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    20→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in _REVIEW_DETECTORS
    21→    }
    22→
    23→
    24→def _subjective_entry_id(
    25→    dimension_key: object,
    26→    *,
    27→    subjective_prefix: str,
    28→) -> str:
    29→    return f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(str(dimension_key))}"
    30→
    31→
    32→def _subjective_entries(state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
    33→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    34→    if not dim_scores:
    35→        return []
    36→    return list(all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores))
    37→
    38→
    39→def _collect_subjective_entry_ids(
    40→    entries: list[dict],
    41→    *,
    42→    subjective_prefix: str,
    43→    predicate,
    44→) -> set[str]:
    45→    collected: set[str] = set()
    46→    for entry in entries:
    47→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    48→        if not dim_key or not predicate(entry):
    49→            continue
    50→        collected.add(_subjective_entry_id(dim_key, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix))
    51→    return collected
    52→
    53→
    54→def current_stale_ids(
    55→    state: StateModel,
    56→    *,
    57→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    58→) -> set[str]:
    59→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    60→    return _collect_subjective_entry_ids(
    61→        _subjective_entries(state),
    62→        subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix,
    63→        predicate=lambda entry: bool(entry.get("stale")),
    64→    )
    65→
    66→
    67→def _unscored_ids_from_assessments(
    68→    assessments: dict,
    69→    *,
    70→    subjective_prefix: str,
    71→) -> set[str]:
    72→    unscored: set[str] = set()
    73→    for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    74→        if not isinstance(payload, dict) or not payload.get("placeholder") or not dim_key:
    75→            continue
    76→        unscored.add(_subjective_entry_id(dim_key, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix))
    77→    return unscored
    78→
    79→
    80→def _unscored_ids_from_dimension_scores(
    81→    entries: list[dict],
    82→    *,
    83→    subjective_prefix: str,
    84→) -> set[str]:
    85→    return _collect_subjective_entry_ids(
    86→        entries,
    87→        subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix,
    88→        predicate=lambda entry: bool(entry.get("placeholder")),
    89→    )
    90→
    91→
    92→def current_unscored_ids(
    93→    state: StateModel,
    94→    *,
    95→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    96→) -> set[str]:
    97→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored."""
    98→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    99→    if isinstance(assessments, dict) and assessments:
   100→        return _unscored_ids_from_assessments(
   101→            assessments,
   102→            subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix,
   103→        )
   104→    return _unscored_ids_from_dimension_scores(
   105→        _subjective_entries(state),
   106→        subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix,
   107→    )
   108→
   109→
   110→def current_under_target_ids(
   111→    state: StateModel,
   112→    *,
   113→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   114→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
   115→) -> set[str]:
   116→    """Return under-target subjective IDs that are neither stale nor unscored."""
   117→    entries = _subjective_entries(state)
   118→    if not entries:
   119→        return set()
   120→
   121→    stale_ids = current_stale_ids(state, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix)
   122→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix)
   123→
   124→    return {
   125→        item_id
   126→        for item_id in _collect_subjective_entry_ids(
   127→            entries,
   128→            subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix,
   129→            predicate=lambda entry: (
   130→                not entry.get("placeholder")
   131→                and not entry.get("stale")
   132→                and float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))) < target_strict
   133→            ),
   134→        )
   135→        if item_id not in stale_ids and item_id not in unscored_ids
   136→    }
   137→
   138→
   139→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   140→    """Hash open review/concerns issue IDs to detect triage-relevant changes."""
   141→    review_ids = sorted(open_review_ids(state))
   142→    if not review_ids:
   143→        return ""
   144→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   145→
   146→
   147→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   148→    """Return open review/concerns IDs that appeared since the last triage."""
   149→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   150→    triaged = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   151→    return open_review_ids(state) - triaged if triaged else set()
   152→
   153→
   154→def is_triage_stale(
   155→    plan: dict,
   156→    state: StateModel,
   157→) -> bool:
   158→    """Return True when genuinely new review issues appeared since last triage.
   159→
   160→    In-progress triage (confirmed stages + stage IDs in queue) is NOT
   161→    considered stale — the lifecycle filter in the work queue already
   162→    forces triage stages to the front.
   163→    """
   164→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   165→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   166→    return bool(open_review_ids(state) - triaged_ids)
   167→
   168→
   169→__all__ = [
   170→    "compute_new_issue_ids",
   171→    "current_stale_ids",
   172→    "current_under_target_ids",
   173→    "current_unscored_ids",
   174→    "is_triage_stale",
   175→    "open_review_ids",
   176→    "review_issue_snapshot_hash",
   177→]
   178→
```

```
1→"""Shared constants and helpers for plan internals."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"
     9→
    10→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    11→TRIAGE_ID = "triage::pending"  # deprecated, kept for migration
    12→
    13→TRIAGE_PREFIX = "triage::"
    14→TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS = (
    15→    "triage::observe",
    16→    "triage::reflect",
    17→    "triage::organize",
    18→    "triage::enrich",
    19→    "triage::sense-check",
    20→    "triage::commit",
    21→)
    22→TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS = tuple(
    23→    (stage_id.removeprefix("triage::"), stage_id) for stage_id in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS
    24→)
    25→TRIAGE_STAGE_ORDER = {
    26→    stage_name: index for index, (stage_name, _stage_id) in enumerate(TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS)
    27→}
    28→TRIAGE_IDS = set(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
    29→_TRIAGE_STAGE_NAMES = {
    30→    stage_id.removeprefix("triage::") for stage_id in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS
    31→}
    32→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    33→WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
    34→WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
    35→WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"
    36→WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID = "workflow::deferred-disposition"
    37→WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID = "workflow::run-scan"
    38→WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
    39→WORKFLOW_IDS = {
    40→    WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID,
    41→    WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
    42→    WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
    43→    WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
    44→    WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
    45→    WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID,
    46→}
    47→WORKFLOW_PRIORITY_ORDER = (
    48→    WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
    49→    WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID,
    50→    WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID,
    51→    WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
    52→    WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
    53→    WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
    54→)
    55→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
    56→
    57→
    58→@dataclass
    59→class QueueSyncResult:
    60→    """Unified result for all queue sync operations."""
    61→
    62→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    63→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    64→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    65→    deferred: bool = False
    66→
    67→    @property
    68→    def changes(self) -> int:
    69→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned) + len(self.resurfaced)
    70→
    71→
    72→def _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    73→    """Extract the triage stages dict from meta or a raw stages dict."""
    74→    if not isinstance(meta_or_stages, dict):
    75→        return {}
    76→    if "triage_stages" in meta_or_stages:
    77→        raw = meta_or_stages.get("triage_stages")
    78→        return raw if isinstance(raw, dict) else {}
    79→    candidate_names = {str(name) for name in meta_or_stages.keys()}
    80→    if candidate_names and candidate_names.issubset(_TRIAGE_STAGE_NAMES):
    81→        return meta_or_stages
    82→    return {}
    83→
    84→
    85→def confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
    86→    """Return triage stage names with an explicit ``confirmed_at`` marker."""
    87→    return {
    88→        str(name)
    89→        for name, payload in _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages).items()
    90→        if isinstance(payload, dict) and payload.get("confirmed_at")
    91→    }
    92→
    93→
    94→def recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
    95→    """Return recorded triage stage names that still need confirmation."""
    96→    return {
    97→        str(name)
    98→        for name, payload in _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages).items()
    99→        if isinstance(payload, dict) and payload and not payload.get("confirmed_at")
   100→    }
   101→
   102→
   103→def normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(queue_order: list[str]) -> None:
   104→    """Keep workflow items ahead of triage, then preserve the rest as-is."""
   105→    seen: set[str] = set()
   106→    normalized: list[str] = []
   107→
   108→    for issue_id in WORKFLOW_PRIORITY_ORDER:
   109→        if issue_id in queue_order and issue_id not in seen:
   110→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   111→            seen.add(issue_id)
   112→
   113→    for issue_id in queue_order:
   114→        if issue_id.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX) and issue_id not in seen:
   115→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   116→            seen.add(issue_id)
   117→
   118→    for issue_id in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   119→        if issue_id in queue_order and issue_id not in seen:
   120→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   121→            seen.add(issue_id)
   122→
   123→    for issue_id in queue_order:
   124→        if issue_id in seen:
   125→            continue
   126→        normalized.append(issue_id)
   127→        seen.add(issue_id)
   128→
   129→    queue_order[:] = normalized
   130→
   131→
   132→__all__ = [
   133→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
   134→    "QueueSyncResult",
   135→    "normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix",
   136→    "confirmed_triage_stage_names",
   137→    "recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names",
   138→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   139→    "SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES",
   140→    "TRIAGE_IDS",
```

```
1→"""Triage workflow orchestration for command routing and stage dispatch."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from collections.abc import Callable
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE
    12→
    13→from .confirmations.router import cmd_confirm_stage
    14→from .display.dashboard import cmd_triage_dashboard
    15→from .lifecycle import ensure_triage_started
    16→from .review_coverage import ensure_active_triage_issue_ids
    17→from .runner.orchestrator_claude import run_claude_orchestrator
    18→from .runner.orchestrator_codex_pipeline import run_codex_pipeline
    19→from .runner.orchestrator_common import parse_only_stages
    20→from .runner.stage_prompts import cmd_stage_prompt
    21→from .services import TriageServices
    22→from .stage_queue import has_triage_in_queue, inject_triage_stages
    23→from .stages.completion import cmd_confirm_existing, cmd_triage_complete
    24→from .stages.commands import run_stage_command
    25→
    26→
    27→def _cmd_triage_start(
    28→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    29→    *,
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    services: TriageServices,
    32→) -> None:
    33→    """Manually inject triage stage IDs into the queue and clear prior stages."""
    34→    plan = services.load_plan()
    35→
    36→    if has_triage_in_queue(plan):
    37→        print(colorize("  Planning mode stages are already in the queue.", "yellow"))
    38→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    39→        stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    40→        if stages:
    41→            print(
    42→                colorize(
    43→                    f"  {len(stages)} stage(s) in progress - clearing to restart.",
    44→                    "yellow",
    45→                )
    46→            )
    47→            meta["triage_stages"] = {}
    48→            ensure_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
    49→            inject_triage_stages(plan)
    50→            services.save_plan(plan)
    51→            services.append_log_entry(
    52→                plan,
    53→                "triage_start",
    54→                actor="user",
    55→                detail={"action": "restart", "cleared_stages": list(stages.keys())},
    56→            )
    57→            services.save_plan(plan)
    58→            print(colorize("  Stages cleared. Begin with observe:", "green"))
    59→        else:
    60→            ensure_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
    61→            inject_triage_stages(plan)
    62→            services.save_plan(plan)
    63→            print(colorize("  Begin with observe:", "green"))
    64→        print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}", "dim"))
    65→        return
    66→
    67→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
    68→    start_outcome = ensure_triage_started(
    69→        plan,
    70→        services=services,
    71→        state=state,
    72→        attestation=attestation,
    73→        log_action="triage_start",
    74→        log_actor="user",
    75→        log_detail={"action": "start"},
    76→        start_message="  Planning mode started (6 stages queued).",
    77→        start_message_style="green",
    78→    )
    79→    if start_outcome.status == "blocked":
    80→        return
    81→
    82→    si = services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    83→    print(f"  Open review issues: {len(si.open_issues)}")
    84→    print(colorize("  Begin with observe:", "dim"))
    85→    print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}", "dim"))
    86→
    87→
    88→def _run_staged_runner(
    89→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    90→    *,
    91→    services: TriageServices,
    92→) -> None:
    93→    runner = str(getattr(args, "runner", "codex")).strip().lower()
    94→    try:
    95→        stages_to_run = parse_only_stages(getattr(args, "only_stages", None))
    96→    except ValueError as exc:
    97→        raise CommandError(str(exc), exit_code=1) from exc
    98→    if runner == "claude":
    99→        run_claude_orchestrator(args, services=services)
   100→        return
   101→    if runner == "codex":
   102→        run_codex_pipeline(
   103→            args,
   104→            stages_to_run=stages_to_run,
   105→            services=services,
   106→        )
   107→        return
   108→    raise CommandError(
   109→        f"Unknown runner: {runner}. Use 'codex' or 'claude'.",
   110→        exit_code=1,
   111→    )
   112→
   113→
   114→def _run_dry_run(
   115→    *,
   116→    services: TriageServices,
   117→    state: dict,
   118→) -> None:
   119→    plan = services.load_plan()
   120→    si = services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   121→    prompt = services.build_triage_prompt(si)
   122→    existing_clusters = si.existing_clusters
   123→    print(colorize("  Cluster triage - dry run", "bold"))
   124→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   125→    print(f"  Open review issues: {len(si.open_issues)}")
   126→    print(f"  Existing clusters: {len(existing_clusters)}")
   127→    print(f"  New since last: {len(si.new_since_last)}")
   128→    print(f"  Resolved since last: {len(si.resolved_since_last)}")
   129→    print(colorize("\n  Prompt that would be sent to LLM:", "dim"))
   130→    print()
   131→    print(prompt)
   132→
   133→
   134→def _read_report_file(report_file: str) -> str:
   135→    """Read report text from a file path or raise ``CommandError``."""
   136→    path = Path(report_file)
   137→    if not path.is_file():
   138→        raise CommandError(f"--report-file not found: {report_file}", exit_code=1)
   139→    try:
   140→        return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
   141→    except OSError as exc:
   142→        raise CommandError(f"Cannot read --report-file: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   143→
   144→
   145→def run_triage_workflow(
   146→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   147→    *,
   148→    services: TriageServices,
   149→    require_issue_inventory_fn: Callable[[dict], bool],
   150→) -> None:
   151→    """Route `plan triage` args through one orchestration seam."""
   152→    # Resolve --report-file to --report (--report takes precedence)
   153→    if not getattr(args, "report", None):
   154→        report_file = getattr(args, "report_file", None)
   155→        if report_file:
   156→            args.report = _read_report_file(report_file)
   157→
   158→    runtime = services.command_runtime(args)
   159→    state = runtime.state
   160→    if not require_issue_inventory_fn(state):
   161→        return
   162→
   163→    if getattr(args, "stage_prompt", None):
   164→        cmd_stage_prompt(args, services=services)
   165→        return
   166→    if getattr(args, "run_stages", False):
   167→        _run_staged_runner(args, services=services)
   168→        return
   169→    if getattr(args, "start", False):
   170→        _cmd_triage_start(args, state=state, services=services)
   171→        return
   172→    if getattr(args, "confirm", None):
   173→        cmd_confirm_stage(args, services=services)
   174→        return
   175→    if getattr(args, "complete", False):
   176→        cmd_triage_complete(args, services=services)
   177→        return
   178→    if getattr(args, "confirm_existing", False):
   179→        cmd_confirm_existing(args, services=services)
   180→        return
   181→
   182→    stage = getattr(args, "stage", None)
   183→    if run_stage_command(stage, args, services=services):
   184→        return
   185→
   186→    if getattr(args, "dry_run", False):
   187→        _run_dry_run(services=services, state=state)
   188→        return
   189→
   190→    cmd_triage_dashboard(args, services=services)
   191→
   192→
   193→__all__ = ["run_triage_workflow"]
   194→
```

```
1→"""Triage sync — inject/prune triage stage IDs based on review issue changes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy import stale as stale_policy_mod
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (
     7→    TRIAGE_IDS,
     8→    TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
     9→    QueueSyncResult,
    10→    confirmed_triage_stage_names,
    11→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix,
    12→    recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import SubjectiveVisibility
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    17→
    18→from .defer_policy import (
    19→    DeferEscalationOptions,
    20→    DeferUpdateOptions,
    21→    should_escalate_defer_state,
    22→    update_defer_state,
    23→)
    24→from .triage_start_policy import decide_triage_start
    25→
    26→[REDACTED]
    27→_TRIAGE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD = "deferred_review_ids"
    28→[REDACTED]
    29→
    30→
    31→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→# Helpers
    33→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→
    35→def _new_review_ids_since_triage(
    36→    state: StateModel,
    37→    meta: dict,
    38→) -> set[str]:
    39→    """Return review issue IDs that are new since the last triage."""
    40→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
    41→    return stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state) - triaged_ids
    42→
    43→
    44→def _prune_all_triage_stages(order: list[str]) -> None:
    45→    """Remove all ``triage::*`` stage IDs from *order*."""
    46→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    47→        while sid in order:
    48→            order.remove(sid)
    49→
    50→
    51→def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
    52→    order: list[str],
    53→    confirmed: set[str],
    54→    *,
    55→    skipped: dict[str, object] | None = None,
    56→) -> list[str]:
    57→    """Inject triage stages for pending (unconfirmed) items.
    58→
    59→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
    60→    Returns list of injected stage IDs.
    61→    """
    62→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "commit")
    63→    existing = set(order)
    64→    injected: list[str] = []
    65→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
    66→        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
    67→            if skipped is not None:
    68→                skipped.pop(sid, None)
    69→            order.append(sid)
    70→            injected.append(sid)
    71→            existing.add(sid)
    72→    return injected
    73→
    74→
    75→def _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta: dict) -> None:
    76→    """Clear defer metadata once triage is no longer deferred."""
    77→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY, None)
    78→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY, None)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _store_triage_meta(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
    82→    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
    83→
    84→
    85→def _inject_triage_result(
    86→    order: list[str],
    87→    confirmed: set[str],
    88→    *,
    89→    skipped: dict[str, object] | None = None,
    90→    normalize: bool = False,
    91→) -> QueueSyncResult:
    92→    injected = _inject_pending_triage_stages(order, confirmed, skipped=skipped)
    93→    if not injected:
    94→        return QueueSyncResult()
    95→    if normalize:
    96→        normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(order)
    97→    return QueueSyncResult(injected=injected)
    98→
    99→
   100→def _mark_triage_deferred(
   101→    plan: PlanModel,
   102→    meta: dict,
   103→    *,
   104→    defer_state: dict,
   105→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   106→    meta[_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   107→    meta["triage_recommended"] = True
   108→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY, None)
   109→    _store_triage_meta(plan, meta)
   110→    return QueueSyncResult(deferred=True)
   111→
   112→
   113→def _mark_triage_escalated(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
   114→    meta[_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY] = True
   115→    meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
   116→    _store_triage_meta(plan, meta)
   117→
   118→
   119→def _mark_triage_ready(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
   120→    _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta)
   121→    meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
   122→    _store_triage_meta(plan, meta)
   123→
   124→
   125→def _prune_stale_present_stages(
   126→    *,
   127→    plan: PlanModel,
   128→    state: StateModel,
   129→    order: list[str],
   130→    meta: dict,
   131→    last_hash: str,
   132→    confirmed: set[str],
   133→    recorded_unconfirmed: set[str],
   134→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   135→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   136→    if not last_hash or confirmed or recorded_unconfirmed:
   137→        return result
   138→    new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   139→    if new_since_triage:
   140→        return result
   141→    _prune_all_triage_stages(order)
   142→    _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta)
   143→    current_hash = stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   144→    if current_hash:
   145→        meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   146→        plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   147→    result.pruned = list(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
   148→    return result
   149→
   150→
   151→def _backfill_partial_triage_snapshot(
   152→    *,
   153→    plan: PlanModel,
   154→    state: StateModel,
   155→    meta: dict,
   156→    last_hash: str,
   157→) -> None:
   158→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   159→    has_completed_stage = any(
   160→        isinstance(v, dict) and v.get("confirmed_at")
   161→        for v in stages.values()
   162→    )
   163→    if not has_completed_stage or meta.get("triaged_ids") or last_hash:
   164→        return
   165→    current_review = sorted(stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state))
   166→    if current_review:
   167→        meta["triaged_ids"] = current_review
   168→        meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   169→        plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   170→
   171→
   172→def _defer_or_inject_triage(
   173→    *,
   174→    plan: PlanModel,
   175→    state: StateModel,
   176→    order: list[str],
   177→    meta: dict,
   178→    confirmed: set[str],
   179→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   180→    new_since_triage: set[str],
   181→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   182→    decision = decide_triage_start(
   183→        plan,
   184→        state,
   185→        policy=policy,
   186→        explicit_start=False,
   187→        attested_override=False,
   188→    )
   189→    if decision.action == "defer":
   190→        defer_state = update_defer_state(
   191→            meta.get(_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY),
   192→            state=state,
   193→            deferred_ids=new_since_triage,
   194→            options=DeferUpdateOptions(
   195→                deferred_ids_field=_TRIAGE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD,
   196→            ),
   197→        )
   198→        meta[_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   199→        escalated = should_escalate_defer_state(
   200→            defer_state,
   201→            state=state,
   202→            options=DeferEscalationOptions(
   203→                deferred_ids_field=_TRIAGE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD,
   204→            ),
   205→        )
   206→        if not escalated:
   207→            return _mark_triage_deferred(plan, meta, defer_state=defer_state)
   208→        _mark_triage_escalated(plan, meta)
   209→        return _inject_triage_result(
   210→            order,
   211→            confirmed,
   212→            skipped=plan.get("skipped", {}),
   213→            normalize=True,
   214→        )
   215→
   216→    _mark_triage_ready(plan, meta)
   217→    return _inject_triage_result(
   218→        order,
   219→        confirmed,
   220→        skipped=plan.get("skipped", {}),
   221→    )
   222→
   223→
   224→def _sync_hash_change(
   225→    *,
   226→    plan: PlanModel,
   227→    state: StateModel,
   228→    order: list[str],
   229→    meta: dict,
   230→    confirmed: set[str],
   231→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   232→    current_hash: str,
   233→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   234→    new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   235→    if new_since_triage:
   236→        return _defer_or_inject_triage(
   237→            plan=plan,
   238→            state=state,
   239→            order=order,
   240→            meta=meta,
   241→            confirmed=confirmed,
   242→            policy=policy,
   243→            new_since_triage=new_since_triage,
   244→        )
   245→
   246→    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   247→    meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
   248→    _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta)
   249→    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   250→    return QueueSyncResult()
   251→
   252→
   253→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   254→# Public API
   255→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   256→
   257→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   258→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   259→
   260→    Returns True when genuinely *new* review issues appeared since the
   261→    last triage.  Triage stage IDs being in the queue alone is not
   262→    sufficient — the new issues that triggered injection may have been
   263→    resolved since then.
   264→
   265→    When issues are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   266→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   267→    through the plan.
   268→    """
   269→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   270→    return stale_policy_mod.is_triage_stale(plan, state)
   271→
   272→
   273→def compute_open_issue_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   274→    """Return the set of currently-open review/concerns issue IDs."""
   275→    return stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state)
   276→
   277→
   278→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   279→    """Return the set of open review/concerns issue IDs added since last triage.
   280→
   281→    Returns an empty set when no prior triage has recorded ``triaged_ids``.
   282→    """
   283→    return stale_policy_mod.compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   284→
   285→
   286→def sync_triage_needed(
   287→    plan: PlanModel,
   288→    state: StateModel,
   289→    *,
   290→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   291→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   292→    """Append triage stage IDs to back of queue when review issues change.
   293→
   294→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   295→
   296→    **Mid-cycle guard**: when the objective backlog still has work, triage
   297→    stages are NOT injected.  Instead, ``epic_triage_meta["triage_recommended"]``
   298→    is set so the UI can show a non-blocking banner.  Stages are injected
   299→    once the objective backlog drains (or on manual ``plan triage``). If
   300→    deferral repeats for multiple scans/days, triage escalates and stages are
   301→    injected in workflow/triage priority order with a forced-visibility marker.
   302→
   303→    When stages are already present but all new issues have been resolved
   304→    since injection, auto-prunes the stale stages and updates the hash.
   305→
   306→    When issues are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   307→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   308→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   309→    """
   310→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   311→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   312→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   313→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   314→    confirmed = confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   315→    recorded_unconfirmed = recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   316→
   317→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   318→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   319→
   320→    current_hash = stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   321→    last_hash = meta.get("issue_snapshot_hash", "")
   322→
   323→    if already_present:
   324→        return _prune_stale_present_stages(
   325→            plan=plan,
   326→            state=state,
   327→            order=order,
   328→            meta=meta,
   329→            last_hash=last_hash,
   330→            confirmed=confirmed,
   331→            recorded_unconfirmed=recorded_unconfirmed,
   332→        )
   333→
   334→    _backfill_partial_triage_snapshot(
   335→        plan=plan,
   336→        state=state,
   337→        meta=meta,
   338→        last_hash=last_hash,
   339→    )
   340→
   341→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   342→        return _sync_hash_change(
   343→            plan=plan,
   344→            state=state,
   345→            order=order,
   346→            meta=meta,
   347→            confirmed=confirmed,
   348→            policy=policy,
   349→            current_hash=current_hash,
   350→        )
   351→
   352→    return result
   353→
   354→
   355→__all__ = [
   356→    "compute_open_issue_ids",
   357→    "compute_new_issue_ids",
   358→    "is_triage_stale",
   359→    "sync_triage_needed",
   360→]
   361→
```

```
1→"""Shared triage lifecycle helpers used by command and runner entrypoints."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from typing import Literal
     8→
     9→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import PlanModel
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import (
    11→    TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
    12→    TriageStartDecision,
    13→    decide_triage_start,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    16→from desloppify.state_io import StateModel
    17→
    18→from .review_coverage import ensure_active_triage_issue_ids
    19→from .services import TriageServices
    20→from .stage_queue import has_triage_in_queue, inject_triage_stages
    21→
    22→TriageStartStatus = Literal["started", "blocked", "already_active"]
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class TriageStartOutcome:
    27→    """Lifecycle result for triage-start attempts."""
    28→
    29→    status: TriageStartStatus
    30→    reason: str
    31→
    32→
    33→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    34→class TriageLifecycleDeps:
    35→    """Dependency seams for triage lifecycle starts (for tests/callers)."""
    36→
    37→    has_triage_in_queue: Callable[[PlanModel], bool] = has_triage_in_queue
    38→    inject_triage_stages: Callable[[PlanModel], None] = inject_triage_stages
    39→    decide_triage_start: Callable[..., TriageStartDecision] = decide_triage_start
    40→    colorize: Callable[[str, str], str] = colorize
    41→
    42→
    43→def _print_triage_start_block(reason: str, *, deps: TriageLifecycleDeps) -> None:
    44→    """Render reason-specific guard guidance for triage auto-start attempts."""
    45→    if reason == "unfinished_triage_stage_records":
    46→        print(
    47→            deps.colorize(
    48→                "  Cannot start triage while previous stage reports are still unconfirmed.",
    49→                "red",
    50→            )
    51→        )
    52→        print(
    53→            deps.colorize(
    54→                "  Confirm or complete the current triage stages first, or pass --attestation "
    55→                "(30+ chars) to override explicitly.",
    56→                "dim",
    57→            )
    58→        )
    59→        return
    60→
    61→    print(deps.colorize("  Cannot start triage while objective backlog is still open.", "red"))
    62→    print(
    63→        deps.colorize(
    64→            "  Finish current objective work first, or pass --attestation "
    65→            "(30+ chars) to override this guard explicitly.",
    66→            "dim",
    67→        )
    68→    )
    69→
    70→
    71→def ensure_triage_started(
    72→    plan: PlanModel,
    73→    *,
    74→    services: TriageServices,
    75→    state: StateModel | None = None,
    76→    attestation: str | None = None,
    77→    log_action: str | None = None,
    78→    log_actor: str = "system",
    79→    log_detail: Mapping[str, object] | None = None,
    80→    start_message: str | None = None,
    81→    start_message_style: str = "cyan",
    82→    deps: TriageLifecycleDeps | None = None,
    83→) -> TriageStartOutcome:
    84→    """Ensure triage stages are injected or return a blocked/already-active outcome."""
    85→    resolved_deps = deps or TriageLifecycleDeps()
    86→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    87→
    88→    if resolved_deps.has_triage_in_queue(plan):
    89→        if state is not None:
    90→            ensure_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
    91→            services.save_plan(plan)
    92→        meta.pop("triage_start_blocked", None)
    93→        return TriageStartOutcome(status="already_active", reason="already_active")
    94→
    95→    decision = resolved_deps.decide_triage_start(
    96→        plan,
    97→        state,
    98→        explicit_start=True,
    99→        attested_override=bool(attestation and len(attestation.strip()) >= 30),
   100→    )
   101→    if decision.action == "defer":
   102→        meta["triage_start_blocked"] = decision.reason
   103→        services.save_plan(plan)
   104→        _print_triage_start_block(decision.reason, deps=resolved_deps)
   105→        return TriageStartOutcome(status="blocked", reason=decision.reason)
   106→
   107→    resolved_deps.inject_triage_stages(plan)
   108→    meta.pop("triage_start_blocked", None)
   109→    meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   110→    if state is not None:
   111→        ensure_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   112→
   113→    if log_action:
   114→        detail = dict(log_detail or {})
   115→        detail.setdefault("injected_stage_ids", list(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS))
   116→        services.append_log_entry(
   117→            plan,
   118→            log_action,
   119→            actor=log_actor,
   120→            detail=detail,
   121→        )
   122→
   123→    services.save_plan(plan)
   124→    if start_message:
   125→        print(
   126→            resolved_deps.colorize(
   127→                start_message,
   128→                start_message_style,
   129→            )
   130→        )
   131→    return TriageStartOutcome(status="started", reason=decision.reason)
   132→
   133→
   134→__all__ = [
   135→    "TriageLifecycleDeps",
   136→    "TriageStartOutcome",
   137→    "ensure_triage_started",
   138→]
   139→
```

```
1→"""Stage-gate and coverage helpers for triage command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import TRIAGE_IDS
     7→
     8→from ..review_coverage import cluster_issue_ids, live_active_triage_issue_ids
     9→
    10→
    11→def _require_triage_pending(plan: dict, *, action: str) -> bool:
    12→    """Require at least one triage stage ID to be present in queue for an action."""
    13→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    14→    if order & TRIAGE_IDS:
    15→        return True
    16→    print(colorize(f"  No triage stage in the queue — nothing to {action}.", "yellow"))
    17→    return False
    18→
    19→
    20→def _validate_stage_report(
    21→    report: str | None,
    22→    *,
    23→    stage: str,
    24→    min_chars: int,
    25→    missing_guidance: list[str] | None = None,
    26→    short_guidance: list[str] | None = None,
    27→) -> str | None:
    28→    """Validate staged report presence/length and print consistent guidance."""
    29→    if not report:
    30→        print(colorize(f"  --report is required for --stage {stage}.", "red"))
    31→        for line in missing_guidance or []:
    32→            print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
    33→        return None
    34→    cleaned = report.strip()
    35→    if len(cleaned) < min_chars:
    36→        print(
    37→            colorize(
    38→                f"  Report too short: {len(cleaned)} chars (minimum {min_chars}).",
    39→                "red",
    40→            )
    41→        )
    42→        for line in short_guidance or []:
    43→            print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
    44→        return None
    45→    return cleaned
    46→
    47→
    48→def unenriched_clusters(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, list[str]]]:
    49→    """Return clusters with issues that are missing required enrichment.
    50→
    51→    Requirements:
    52→    - Every cluster needs a description and at least one action_step.
    53→    - Small clusters (< 5 issues) need at least 1 action step per issue,
    54→      so each item has a concrete plan. Large clusters (>= 5) just need
    55→      steps overall (cluster-level plan is sufficient).
    56→    """
    57→    gaps: list[tuple[str, list[str]]] = []
    58→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    59→        issue_ids = cluster_issue_ids(cluster)
    60→        if not issue_ids:
    61→            continue
    62→        if cluster.get("auto"):
    63→            continue
    64→        missing: list[str] = []
    65→        if not cluster.get("description"):
    66→            missing.append("description")
    67→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
    68→        issue_count = len(issue_ids)
    69→        if not steps:
    70→            missing.append("action_steps")
    71→        elif issue_count < 5 and len(steps) < issue_count:
    72→            missing.append(
    73→                f"action_steps (have {len(steps)}, need >= {issue_count} for small cluster)"
    74→            )
    75→        if missing:
    76→            gaps.append((name, missing))
    77→    return gaps
    78→
    79→
    80→def unclustered_review_issues(plan: dict, state: dict | None = None) -> list[str]:
    81→    """Return review issue IDs that aren't in any manual cluster.
    82→
    83→    When *state* is provided, uses open review/concerns issues from state
    84→    (the canonical source). Falls back to scanning queue_order for backwards
    85→    compatibility.
    86→    """
    87→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    88→    clustered_ids: set[str] = set()
    89→    for cluster in clusters.values():
    90→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
    91→            clustered_ids.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
    92→    skipped_ids = {
    93→        fid for fid in (plan.get("skipped", {}) or {}).keys() if isinstance(fid, str)
    94→    }
    95→
    96→    if state is not None:
    97→        review_ids = [
    98→            fid for fid, finding in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    99→            if finding.get("status") == "open"
   100→            and finding.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   101→        ]
   102→        frozen_ids = (plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}) or {}).get("active_triage_issue_ids")
   103→        if isinstance(frozen_ids, list) and frozen_ids:
   104→            frozen_id_set = live_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   105→            review_ids = [fid for fid in review_ids if fid in frozen_id_set]
   106→    else:
   107→        review_ids = [
   108→            fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   109→            if not fid.startswith("triage::") and not fid.startswith("workflow::")
   110→            and (fid.startswith("review::") or fid.startswith("concerns::"))
   111→        ]
   112→
   113→    return [
   114→        fid for fid in review_ids
   115→        if fid not in clustered_ids and fid not in skipped_ids
   116→    ]
   117→
```

```
    27→def _cmd_triage_start(
    28→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    29→    *,
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    services: TriageServices,
    32→) -> None:
    33→    """Manually inject triage stage IDs into the queue and clear prior stages."""
    34→    plan = services.load_plan()
    35→
    36→    if has_triage_in_queue(plan):
    37→        print(colorize("  Planning mode stages are already in the queue.", "yellow"))
    38→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    39→        stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    40→        if stages:
    41→            print(
    42→                colorize(
    43→                    f"  {len(stages)} stage(s) in progress - clearing to restart.",
    44→                    "yellow",
    45→                )
    46→            )
    47→            meta["triage_stages"] = {}
    48→            ensure_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
    49→            inject_triage_stages(plan)
    50→            services.save_plan(plan)
    51→            services.append_log_entry(
    52→                plan,
    53→                "triage_start",
    54→                actor="user",
    55→                detail={"action": "restart", "cleared_stages": list(stages.keys())},
    56→            )
    57→            services.save_plan(plan)
    58→            print(colorize("  Stages cleared. Begin with observe:", "green"))
    59→        else:
    60→            ensure_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
    61→            inject_triage_stages(plan)
    62→            services.save_plan(plan)
    63→            print(colorize("  Begin with observe:", "green"))
    64→        print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}", "dim"))
    65→        return
    66→

    68→    start_outcome = ensure_triage_started(
    69→        plan,
    70→        services=services,
    71→        state=state,
    72→        attestation=attestation,
    73→        log_action="triage_start",
    74→        log_actor="user",
    75→        log_detail={"action": "start"},
    76→        start_message="  Planning mode started (6 stages queued).",
    77→        start_message_style="green",
    78→    )
    79→    if start_outcome.status == "blocked":
    80→        return
    81→
    82→    si = services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    83→    print(f"  Open review issues: {len(si.open_issues)}")
    84→    print(colorize("  Begin with observe:", "dim"))
    85→    print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}", "dim"))
    86→
    87→
    88→def _run_staged_runner(
    89→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    90→    *,
    91→    services: TriageServices,
    92→) -> None:
    93→    runner = str(getattr(args, "runner", "codex")).strip().lower()
    94→    try:
    95→        stages_to_run = parse_only_stages(getattr(args, "only_stages", None))
    96→    except ValueError as exc:
    97→        raise CommandError(str(exc), exit_code=1) from exc
    98→    if runner == "claude":
    99→        run_claude_orchestrator(args, services=services)
   100→        return
   101→    if runner == "codex":
   102→        run_codex_pipeline(
   103→            args,
   104→            stages_to_run=stages_to_run,
   105→            services=services,
   106→        )
   107→        return
   108→    raise CommandError(
   109→        f"Unknown runner: {runner}. Use 'codex' or 'claude'.",
   110→        exit_code=1,
   111→    )
   112→
   113→

   129→    print(colorize("\n  Prompt that would be sent to LLM:", "dim"))
   130→    print()

   136→    path = Path(report_file)
```

```
1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import enum
     6→import importlib
     7→import logging
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
    10→
    11→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    12→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.refresh_lifecycle import (
    14→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE,
    15→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
    16→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN,
    17→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
    18→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import load_plan
    21→from desloppify.state_scoring import score_snapshot
    22→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import QueueBuildOptions
    23→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import queue_context
    24→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import is_subjective_queue_item
    25→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
    26→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.snapshot import coarse_phase_name
    27→from desloppify.engine.planning.queue_policy import build_execution_queue
    28→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress_render import (
    29→    format_plan_delta,
    30→    format_queue_block,
    31→    format_queue_headline,
    32→)
    33→
    34→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    35→
    36→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    37→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import QueueContext
    38→
    39→
    40→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    41→# QueueBreakdown — single source of truth for queue numbers
    42→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    43→
    44→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    45→class QueueBreakdown:
    46→    """All numbers needed to render the standardized queue display."""
    47→
    48→    queue_total: int = 0
    49→    plan_ordered: int = 0
    50→    stale_plan_ordered: int = 0
    51→    skipped: int = 0
    52→    subjective: int = 0
    53→    workflow: int = 0
    54→    lifecycle_phase: str | None = None
    55→    focus_cluster: str | None = None
    56→    focus_cluster_count: int = 0
    57→    focus_cluster_total: int = 0
    58→
    59→    @property
    60→    def objective_actionable(self) -> int:
    61→        """Objective items only — excludes subjective and workflow.
    62→
    63→        This is the count used by :func:`score_display_mode` to decide
    64→        frozen vs live display.  Do not use it directly for gating —
    65→        use ``score_display_mode()`` instead.
    66→        """
    67→        if self.lifecycle_phase in {
    68→            LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN,
    69→            LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
    70→            LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
    71→            LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
    72→        }:
    73→            return 0
    74→        return max(0, self.queue_total - self.subjective - self.workflow)
    75→
    76→
    77→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    78→# ScoreDisplayMode — single decision point for frozen vs live score display
    79→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    80→
    81→class ScoreDisplayMode(enum.Enum):
    82→    """How to display the score given the current plan cycle and queue state.
    83→
    84→    - ``FROZEN``: objective work remains → show frozen plan-start score.
    85→    - ``PHASE_TRANSITION``: objective drained, subjective/workflow remain
    86→      → show live scores + transition banner.
    87→    - ``LIVE``: no active plan cycle or queue fully clear → show live scores.
    88→    """
    89→    FROZEN = "frozen"
    90→    PHASE_TRANSITION = "phase_transition"
    91→    LIVE = "live"
    92→
    93→
    94→def score_display_mode(
    95→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None,
    96→    plan_start_strict: float | None,
    97→) -> ScoreDisplayMode:
    98→    """Determine the score display mode from breakdown + plan-start score.
    99→
   100→    This is the **single source of truth** for the frozen-vs-live decision.
   101→    Every call site (status, scan reveal, next nudges, plan reconcile, plan
   102→    nudge) must use this instead of ad-hoc count checks.
   103→    """
   104→    if plan_start_strict is None:
   105→        return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
   106→    if breakdown is None:
   107→        return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
   108→    if breakdown.lifecycle_phase == LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN:
   109→        return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
   110→    if breakdown.lifecycle_phase == LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE:
   111→        return ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   112→    if breakdown.lifecycle_phase in {
   113→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
   114→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
   115→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
   116→    }:
   117→        return ScoreDisplayMode.PHASE_TRANSITION
   118→    if breakdown.objective_actionable > 0:
   119→        return ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   120→    if breakdown.queue_total > 0:
   121→        return ScoreDisplayMode.PHASE_TRANSITION
   122→    return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
   123→
   124→
   125→def plan_aware_queue_breakdown(
   126→    state: dict,
   127→    plan: dict | None = None,
   128→    *,
   129→    context: QueueContext | None = None,
   130→) -> QueueBreakdown:
   131→    """Build a full :class:`QueueBreakdown` from a single ``build_work_queue`` call.
   132→
   133→    When *context* is provided, its ``plan`` and ``policy`` are forwarded to
   134→    ``build_work_queue`` so the counts agree with the caller's resolution.
   135→    """
   136→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else plan
   137→
   138→    result = build_execution_queue(
   139→        state,
   140→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   141→            status="open",
   142→            count=None,
   143→            plan=effective_plan if context is None else None,
   144→            include_skipped=False,
   145→            context=context,
   146→        ),
   147→    )
   148→
   149→    # Collapse clusters for display-level counting
   150→    items = result.get("items", [])
   151→    snapshot = (
   152→        context.snapshot
   153→        if context is not None
   154→        else queue_context(state, plan=effective_plan).snapshot
   155→    )
   156→    lifecycle_phase = coarse_phase_name(snapshot.phase)
   157→    if effective_plan and not effective_plan.get("active_cluster"):
   158→        items = collapse_clusters(items, effective_plan)
   159→
   160→    queue_total = len(items)
   161→
   162→    # Count subjective and workflow items in the queue.
   163→    # Collapsed clusters whose members are all subjective count as subjective.
   164→    subjective = sum(
   165→        1 for item in items
   166→        if is_subjective_queue_item(item)
   167→    )
   168→    workflow = sum(
   169→        1 for item in items
   170→        if item.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
   171→    )
   172→
   173→    # Plan-derived counts
   174→    plan_ordered = 0
   175→    stale_plan_ordered = 0
   176→    skipped = 0
   177→    if effective_plan:
   178→        skipped = len(effective_plan.get("skipped", {}))
   179→        # plan_ordered = live queue_order items that still exist in the current
   180→        # snapshot; stale_plan_ordered captures dead queue_order entries so the
   181→        # summary does not imply they are still blocking the live queue.
   182→        queue_order = effective_plan.get("queue_order", [])
   183→        skipped_ids = set(effective_plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   184→        current_item_ids = _snapshot_item_ids(snapshot)
   185→        if current_item_ids is None:
   186→            plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
   187→        else:
   188→            plan_ordered = sum(
   189→                1 for fid in queue_order
   190→                if fid not in skipped_ids and fid in current_item_ids
   191→            )
   192→            stale_plan_ordered = sum(
   193→                1 for fid in queue_order
   194→                if fid not in skipped_ids and fid not in current_item_ids
   195→            )
   196→
   197→    # Focus cluster info
   198→    focus_cluster = None
   199→    focus_cluster_count = 0
   200→    focus_cluster_total = 0
   201→    if effective_plan:
   202→        active = effective_plan.get("active_cluster")
   203→        if active:
   204→            focus_cluster = active
   205→            cluster_data = effective_plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
   206→            focus_cluster_total = len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   207→            # Count how many cluster members are still in the queue
   208→            cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   209→            open_issues = {
   210→                fid
   211→                for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
   212→                if f.get("status") == "open"
   213→            }
   214→            focus_cluster_count = len(cluster_member_ids & open_issues)
   215→
   216→    return QueueBreakdown(
   217→        queue_total=queue_total,
   218→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
   219→        stale_plan_ordered=stale_plan_ordered,
   220→        skipped=skipped,
   221→        subjective=subjective,
   222→        workflow=workflow,
   223→        lifecycle_phase=lifecycle_phase,
   224→        focus_cluster=focus_cluster,
   225→        focus_cluster_count=focus_cluster_count,
   226→        focus_cluster_total=focus_cluster_total,
   227→    )
   228→
   229→
   230→def _snapshot_item_ids(snapshot: object) -> set[str] | None:
   231→    """Return all live queue item IDs represented by a snapshot.
   232→
   233→    When tests stub ``snapshot`` with only a ``phase`` attribute, return
   234→    ``None`` so callers can fall back to legacy plan_ordered counting.
   235→    """
   236→    partition_names = (
   237→        "all_objective_items",
   238→        "all_initial_review_items",
   239→        "all_postflight_review_items",
   240→        "all_scan_items",
   241→        "all_postflight_workflow_items",
   242→        "all_postflight_triage_items",
   243→    )
   244→    item_ids: set[str] = set()
   245→    saw_partition = False
   246→    for name in partition_names:
   247→        items = getattr(snapshot, name, None)
   248→        if items is None:
   249→            continue
   250→        saw_partition = True
   251→        for item in items:
   252→            if isinstance(item, dict):
   253→                item_id = item.get("id")
   254→                if isinstance(item_id, str) and item_id:
   255→                    item_ids.add(item_id)
   256→    if not saw_partition:
   257→        return None
   258→    return item_ids
   259→
   260→
   261→def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
   262→    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
   263→    if not plan:
   264→        return None
   265→    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   266→
   267→
   268→def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   269→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   270→    *,
   271→    frozen_strict: float,
   272→    live_score: float | None = None,
   273→) -> None:
   274→    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress.
   275→
   276→    Only call from a ``ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN`` path — assumes objective
   277→    work remains in the queue.
   278→    """
   279→    block = format_queue_block(breakdown, frozen_score=frozen_strict, live_score=live_score)
   280→    print()
   281→    for text, style in block:
   282→        print(colorize(text, style))
   283→    print(colorize(
   284→        "  Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   285→        "dim",
   286→    ))
   287→
   288→
   289→def print_objective_drained_banner(
   290→    frozen_strict: float,
   291→    remaining: int,
   292→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   293→) -> None:
   294→    """Show a phase-transition banner when objective work is drained."""
   295→    kind_labels: list[str] = []
   296→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   297→        kind_labels.append("subjective")
   298→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   299→        kind_labels.append("workflow")
   300→    kind_desc = " + ".join(kind_labels) if kind_labels else "non-objective"
   301→    print(colorize(
   302→        f"\n  Objective queue complete (plan-start was {frozen_strict:.1f})."
   303→        f" {remaining} {kind_desc} item{'s' if remaining != 1 else ''} remain.",
   304→        "cyan",
   305→    ))
   306→    print(colorize(
   307→        "  Run `desloppify next` for remaining work,"
   308→        " then `desloppify scan` to finalize.",
   309→        "dim",
   310→    ))
   311→
   312→
   313→def print_execution_or_reveal(
   314→    state: dict,
   315→    prev,
   316→    plan: dict | None,
   317→) -> None:
   318→    """Context-aware score display using :func:`score_display_mode`."""
   319→    frozen_strict = get_plan_start_strict(plan)
   320→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None
   321→
   322→    if frozen_strict is not None:
   323→        try:
   324→            breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   325→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   326→            _logger.debug("queue breakdown computation skipped", exc_info=True)
   327→
   328→    mode = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict)
   329→
   330→    if mode is ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN:
   331→        print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   332→            breakdown,
   333→            frozen_strict=frozen_strict,
   334→            live_score=score_snapshot(state).strict,
   335→        )
   336→        return
   337→
   338→    # LIVE or PHASE_TRANSITION: show current scores
   339→    score_update_mod = importlib.import_module(
   340→        "desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update"
   341→    )
   342→    score_update_mod.print_score_update(state, prev)
   343→
   344→    if mode is ScoreDisplayMode.PHASE_TRANSITION:
   345→        print_objective_drained_banner(frozen_strict, breakdown.queue_total, breakdown)
   346→
   347→
   348→def show_score_with_plan_context(state: dict, prev) -> None:
   349→    """Load plan (best-effort) and show frozen or live score context.
   350→
   351→    Encapsulates the common load_plan + PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS + reveal
   352→    choreography so command modules don't each repeat it.
   353→    """
   354→    try:
   355→        plan = load_plan()
   356→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   357→        _logger.debug("score display skipped plan-aware context", exc_info=exc)
   358→        plan = None
   359→    print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, plan)
   360→
   361→
   362→__all__ = [
   363→    "QueueBreakdown",
   364→    "ScoreDisplayMode",
   365→    "format_plan_delta",
   366→    "format_queue_block",
   367→    "format_queue_headline",
   368→    "get_plan_start_strict",
   369→    "plan_aware_queue_breakdown",
   370→    "print_execution_or_reveal",
   371→    "print_objective_drained_banner",
   372→    "print_frozen_score_with_queue_context",
   373→    "score_display_mode",
   374→    "show_score_with_plan_context",
   375→]
   376→
```

```
1→"""Queue-policy helpers shared by planning render/select modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import replace
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import queue_context
    10→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import (
    11→    QueueBuildOptions,
    12→    QueueVisibility,
    13→    WorkQueueResult,
    14→    _build_work_queue_with_visibility,
    15→    build_work_queue,
    16→)
    17→
    18→
    19→def _subjective_threshold(state: StateModel, *, default: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE) -> float:
    20→    config = state.get("config", {})
    21→    raw_target = default
    22→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    23→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", default)
    24→    try:
    25→        value = float(raw_target)
    26→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    27→        value = default
    28→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, value))
    29→
    30→
    31→def _queue_plan_from_options(options: QueueBuildOptions) -> dict | None:
    32→    if options.context is not None:
    33→        return options.context.plan
    34→    return options.plan
    35→
    36→
    37→def build_open_plan_queue(
    38→    state: StateModel,
    39→    *,
    40→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    41→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    42→    """Build one open-status queue with consistent planning policy defaults."""
    43→    opts = replace(options) if options is not None else QueueBuildOptions(count=None)
    44→    if options is None:
    45→        opts = replace(opts, status="open", include_subjective=True)
    46→    elif opts.status == QueueBuildOptions().status:
    47→        opts = replace(opts, status="open")
    48→    if opts.subjective_threshold == QueueBuildOptions().subjective_threshold:
    49→        opts = replace(opts, subjective_threshold=_subjective_threshold(state))
    50→    return build_work_queue(
    51→        state,
    52→        options=opts,
    53→    )
    54→
    55→
    56→def build_execution_queue(
    57→    state: StateModel,
    58→    *,
    59→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    60→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    61→    """Build the execution queue that follows the living plan when present."""
    62→    options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    63→    ctx = options.context or queue_context(
    64→        state,
    65→        plan=_queue_plan_from_options(options),
    66→        target_strict=_subjective_threshold(state),
    67→    )
    68→    return _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    69→        state,
    70→        options=replace(options, context=ctx),
    71→        visibility=QueueVisibility.EXECUTION,
    72→    )
    73→
    74→
    75→def build_backlog_queue(
    76→    state: StateModel,
    77→    *,
    78→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    79→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    80→    """Build the broader backlog, excluding work already tracked in the plan."""
    81→    options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    82→    ctx = options.context or queue_context(
    83→        state,
    84→        plan=_queue_plan_from_options(options),
    85→        target_strict=_subjective_threshold(state),
    86→    )
    87→    return _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    88→        state,
    89→        options=replace(options, context=ctx),
    90→        visibility=QueueVisibility.BACKLOG,
    91→    )
    92→
    93→
    94→__all__ = [
    95→    "build_backlog_queue",
    96→    "build_execution_queue",
    97→    "build_open_plan_queue",
    98→]
    99→
```

```
    26→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    27→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""

    40→

    45→        state,
    46→        config,

    48→        deps=QueueRenderDeps(
    49→            resolve_lang_fn=resolve_lang,

    53→        ),
    54→    )
```
