   135→    plan_data: dict | None,
   136→    guardrail_warnings: list[str],
   137→    write_query_fn,
   138→    command_name: str,
   139→) -> dict:
   140→    """Build and persist the payload for the current queue view."""
   141→    payload = _build_next_payload(
   142→        queue=queue,
   143→        items=items,
   144→        state=state,
   145→        narrative=narrative,
   146→        plan_data=plan_data,
   147→        command_name=command_name,
   148→    )
   149→    if guardrail_warnings:
   150→        payload["warnings"] = guardrail_warnings
   151→    write_query_fn(payload)
   152→    return payload
   153→
   154→
   155→def _render_empty_queue_view(
   156→    *,
   157→    queue: dict,
   158→    items: list[dict],
   159→    state: dict,
   160→    plan_for_queue: dict,
   161→    plan_data: dict | None,
   162→    ctx,
   163→    target_strict: float,
   164→    opts: NextOptions,
   165→    guardrail_warnings: list[str],
   166→    write_query_fn,
   167→    command_name: str,
   168→    show_plan_context: bool,
   169→) -> None:
   170→    """Render and persist the empty queue state."""
   171→    strict_score = score_snapshot(state).strict
   172→    plan_start_strict = None
   173→    if show_plan_context and plan_for_queue:
   174→        plan_start_strict, _ = _plan_queue_context(
   175→            state=state,
   176→            plan_data=plan_for_queue,
   177→            context=ctx,
   178→        )
   179→    _render_queue_header(queue, opts.explain)
   180→    _show_empty_queue(
   181→        queue,
   182→        strict_score,
   183→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   184→        target_strict=target_strict,
   185→    )
   186→    _write_next_payload(
   187→        queue=queue,
   188→        items=items,
   189→        state=state,
   190→        narrative={},
   191→        plan_data=plan_data,
   192→        guardrail_warnings=guardrail_warnings,
   193→        write_query_fn=write_query_fn,
   194→        command_name=command_name,
   195→    )
   196→
   197→
   198→def _render_terminal_queue_view(
   199→    *,
   200→    queue: dict,
   201→    items: list[dict],
   202→    state: dict,
   203→    opts: NextOptions,
   204→    plan_for_queue: dict,
   205→    plan_data: dict | None,
   206→    effective_cluster: str | None,
   207→    target_strict: float,
   208→    ctx,
   209→    show_plan_context: bool,
   210→    show_execution_prompt: bool,
   211→) -> None:
   212→    """Render terminal output for a non-empty queue."""
   213→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   214→    issues_scoped = path_scoped_issues(
   215→        state.get("issues", {}),
   216→        state.get("scan_path"),
   217→    )
   218→    plan_start_strict = None
   219→    breakdown = None
   220→    if show_plan_context:
   221→        plan_start_strict, breakdown = _plan_queue_context(
   222→            state=state,
   223→            plan_data=plan_for_queue,
   224→            context=ctx,
   225→        )
   226→    queue_total = breakdown.queue_total if breakdown else 0
   227→
   228→    _render_queue_header(queue, opts.explain)
   229→    strict_score = score_snapshot(state).strict
   230→    if _show_empty_queue(
   231→        queue,
   232→        strict_score,
   233→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   234→        target_strict=target_strict,
   235→    ):
   236→        return
   237→
   238→    potentials = _merge_potentials_safe(state.get("potentials", {}))
   239→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   240→        items,
   241→        dim_scores,
   242→        issues_scoped,
   243→        group=opts.group,
   244→        explain=opts.explain,
   245→        potentials=potentials,
   246→        plan=plan_data,
   247→        cluster_filter=effective_cluster,
   248→    )
   249→    next_nudges_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   250→    if show_plan_context:
   251→        next_nudges_mod.render_uncommitted_reminder(plan_data)
   252→        next_nudges_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   253→            state,
   254→            dim_scores,
   255→            issues_scoped,
   256→            strict_score=strict_score,
   257→            target_strict_score=target_strict,
   258→            queue_total=queue_total,
   259→            plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   260→            breakdown=breakdown,
   261→        )
   262→    print()
   263→
   264→    if items and plan_data and show_execution_prompt:
   265→        print_user_message(
   266→            "Start working on the task above. When done:"
   267→            " `desloppify plan resolve`. Full queue:"
   268→            " `desloppify plan show`."
   269→        )
   270→
   271→
   272→def _plan_data_for_view(plan: dict, *, show_plan_context: bool) -> dict | None:
   273→    if not show_plan_context:
   274→        return None
   275→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
   276→        return plan
   277→    return None
   278→
   279→
   280→def _resolve_queue_items(
   281→    *,
   282→    state: dict,
   283→    config: dict,
   284→    opts: NextOptions,
   285→    plan_for_queue: dict,
   286→    target_strict: float,
   287→    build_work_queue_fn,
   288→) -> tuple[object, dict, list[dict], str | None]:
   289→    ctx = queue_context(
   290→        state,
   291→        target_strict=target_strict,
   292→        config=config,
   293→        plan=plan_for_queue,
   294→    )
   295→    effective_cluster = _resolve_cluster_focus(
   296→        plan_for_queue,
   297→        cluster_arg=opts.cluster,
   298→        scope=opts.scope,
   299→    )
   300→    queue = build_work_queue_fn(
   301→        state,
   302→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   303→            count=None,
   304→            scope=opts.scope,
   305→            status=opts.status,
   306→            include_subjective=True,
   307→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   308→            explain=opts.explain,
   309→            include_skipped=opts.include_skipped,
   310→            context=ctx,
   311→        ),
   312→    )
   313→    return ctx, queue, queue.get("items", []), effective_cluster
   314→
   315→
   316→def _apply_queue_view_transforms(
   317→    *,
   318→    items: list[dict],
   319→    queue: dict,
   320→    opts: NextOptions,
   321→    plan_for_queue: dict,
   322→    effective_cluster: str | None,
   323→    collapse_plan_clusters: bool,
   324→) -> list[dict]:
   325→    visible = items
   326→    if collapse_plan_clusters and effective_cluster and plan_for_queue:
   327→        visible = filter_cluster_focus(visible, plan_for_queue, effective_cluster)
   328→    elif (
   329→        collapse_plan_clusters
   330→        and plan_for_queue
   331→        and not effective_cluster
   332→        and not plan_for_queue.get("active_cluster")
   333→    ):
   334→        visible = collapse_clusters(visible, plan_for_queue)
   335→
   336→    if opts.count:
   337→        visible = visible[: opts.count]
   338→        queue["items"] = visible
   339→        queue["total"] = len(visible)
   340→    return visible
   341→
   342→
   343→def _render_non_empty_queue(
   344→    *,
   345→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   346→    state: dict,
   347→    items: list[dict],
   348→    queue: dict,
   349→    plan_data: dict | None,
   350→    guardrail_warnings: list[str],
   351→    write_query_fn,
   352→    view: QueueViewConfig,
   353→    opts: NextOptions,
   354→    plan_for_queue: dict,
   355→    effective_cluster: str | None,
   356→    target_strict: float,
   357→    ctx,
   358→    resolve_lang_fn,
   359→) -> None:
   360→    lang = resolve_lang_fn(args)
   361→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   362→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   363→        state,
   364→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command=view.command_name, plan=plan_data),
   365→    )
   366→    payload = _write_next_payload(
   367→        queue=queue,
   368→        items=items,
   369→        state=state,
   370→        narrative=narrative,
   371→        plan_data=plan_data,
   372→        guardrail_warnings=guardrail_warnings,
   373→        write_query_fn=write_query_fn,
   374→        command_name=view.command_name,
   375→    )
   376→
   377→    if _emit_requested_output(opts, payload, items, command_name=view.command_name):
   378→        return
   379→
   380→    _render_terminal_queue_view(
   381→        queue=queue,
   382→        items=items,
   383→        state=state,
   384→        opts=opts,
   385→        plan_for_queue=plan_for_queue,
   386→        plan_data=plan_data,
   387→        effective_cluster=effective_cluster,
   388→        target_strict=target_strict,
   389→        ctx=ctx,
   390→        show_plan_context=view.show_plan_context,
   391→        show_execution_prompt=view.show_execution_prompt,
   392→    )
   393→
   394→
   395→def _build_and_render_queue_view(
   396→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   397→    state: dict,
   398→    config: dict,
   399→    *,
   400→    deps: QueueRenderDeps,
   401→    view: QueueViewConfig,
   402→) -> None:
   403→    """Build queue payload and render output for a queue surface."""
   404→    opts = NextOptions.from_args(args)
   405→    guardrail_warnings = triage_guardrail_messages(state=state)
   406→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   407→
   408→    plan = deps.load_plan_fn()
   409→    plan_for_queue = plan
   410→    plan_data = _plan_data_for_view(plan, show_plan_context=view.show_plan_context)
   411→    ctx, queue, items, effective_cluster = _resolve_queue_items(
   412→        state=state,
   413→        config=config,
   414→        opts=opts,
   415→        plan_for_queue=plan_for_queue,
   416→        target_strict=target_strict,
   417→        build_work_queue_fn=deps.build_work_queue_fn,
   418→    )
   419→    items = _apply_queue_view_transforms(
   420→        items=items,
   421→        queue=queue,
   422→        opts=opts,
   423→        plan_for_queue=plan_for_queue,
   424→        effective_cluster=effective_cluster,
   425→        collapse_plan_clusters=view.collapse_plan_clusters,
   426→    )
   427→
   428→    if not items:
   429→        _render_empty_queue_view(
   430→            queue=queue,
   431→            items=items,
   432→            state=state,
   433→            plan_for_queue=plan_for_queue,
   434→            plan_data=plan_data,
   435→            ctx=ctx,
   436→            target_strict=target_strict,
   437→            opts=opts,
   438→            guardrail_warnings=guardrail_warnings,
   439→            write_query_fn=deps.write_query_fn,
   440→            command_name=view.command_name,
   441→            show_plan_context=view.show_plan_context,
   442→        )
   443→        return
   444→
   445→    _render_non_empty_queue(
   446→        args=args,
   447→        state=state,
   448→        items=items,
   449→        queue=queue,
   450→        plan_data=plan_data,
   451→        guardrail_warnings=guardrail_warnings,
   452→        write_query_fn=deps.write_query_fn,
   453→        view=view,
   454→        opts=opts,
   455→        plan_for_queue=plan_for_queue,
   456→        effective_cluster=effective_cluster,
   457→        target_strict=target_strict,
   458→        ctx=ctx,
   459→        resolve_lang_fn=deps.resolve_lang_fn,
   460→    )
   461→
   462→
   463→def build_and_render_execution_queue(
   464→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   465→    state: dict,
   466→    config: dict,
   467→    *,
   468→    deps: QueueRenderDeps | None = None,
   469→) -> None:
   470→    """Build queue payload and render output for `desloppify next`."""
   471→    build_and_render_queue(
   472→        args,
   473→        state,
   474→        config,
   475→        view=EXECUTION_QUEUE_VIEW,
   476→        deps=deps
   477→        or QueueRenderDeps(
   478→            build_work_queue_fn=build_execution_queue,
   479→        ),
   480→    )
   481→
   482→
   483→def build_and_render_backlog_queue(
   484→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   485→    state: dict,
   486→    config: dict,
   487→    *,
   488→    deps: QueueRenderDeps | None = None,
   489→) -> None:
   490→    """Build queue payload and render output for `desloppify backlog`."""
   491→    build_and_render_queue(
   492→        args,
   493→        state,
   494→        config,
   495→        view=BACKLOG_QUEUE_VIEW,
   496→        deps=deps
   497→        or QueueRenderDeps(
   498→            build_work_queue_fn=build_backlog_queue,
   499→        ),
   500→    )
   501→
   502→
   503→def build_and_render_queue(
   504→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   505→    state: dict,
   506→    config: dict,
   507→    *,
   508→    view: QueueViewConfig = EXECUTION_QUEUE_VIEW,
   509→    deps: QueueRenderDeps | None = None,
   510→) -> None:
   511→    """Build queue payload and render output using one stable view contract."""
   512→    _build_and_render_queue_view(
   513→        args,
   514→        state,
   515→        config,
   516→        deps=deps or QueueRenderDeps(build_work_queue_fn=build_execution_queue),
   517→        view=view,
   518→    )
   519→
   520→
   521→__all__ = [
   522→    "BACKLOG_QUEUE_VIEW",
   523→    "EXECUTION_QUEUE_VIEW",
   524→    "QueueRenderDeps",
   525→    "QueueViewConfig",
   526→    "build_and_render_backlog_queue",
   527→    "build_and_render_execution_queue",
   528→    "build_and_render_queue",
   529→]
   530→

1→"""Queue/stage mutation helpers for triage workflow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix
     9→from desloppify.engine.plan_ops import purge_ids
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import PlanModel
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import TRIAGE_IDS, TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS
    12→
    13→from .plan_state_access import ensure_queue_order, ensure_skipped_map
    14→
    15→STAGE_ORDER = ["observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check"]
    16→
    17→
    18→def has_triage_in_queue(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    19→    """Return True when any triage stage IDs are currently queued."""
    20→    order = set(ensure_queue_order(plan))
    21→    return bool(order & TRIAGE_IDS)
    22→
    23→
    24→def clear_triage_stage_skips(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
    25→    """Remove skipped markers for triage stages before reinjection."""
    26→    skipped = ensure_skipped_map(plan)
    27→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    28→        skipped.pop(sid, None)
    29→
    30→
    31→def inject_triage_stages(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
    32→    """Inject the canonical triage stage IDs at the queue front."""
    33→    order = ensure_queue_order(plan)
    34→    clear_triage_stage_skips(plan)
    35→    remaining = [issue_id for issue_id in order if issue_id not in TRIAGE_IDS]
    36→    order[:] = [*remaining, *TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS]
    37→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(order)
    38→
    39→
    40→def purge_triage_stage(plan: PlanModel, stage_name: str) -> None:
    41→    """Remove one triage stage workflow item from the plan."""
    42→    purge_ids(plan, [f"triage::{stage_name}"])
    43→
    44→
    45→def cascade_clear_dispositions(meta: dict[str, Any], from_stage: str) -> None:
    46→    """Reset issue_dispositions when an earlier stage reruns.
    47→
    48→    - observe rerun: wipe the entire disposition map (verdicts change)
    49→    - reflect rerun: clear decision/target/decision_source from all entries
    50→      (observe verdicts remain, but reflect decisions are outdated)
    51→    """
    52→    dispositions = meta.get("issue_dispositions")
    53→    if not dispositions:
    54→        return
    55→    if from_stage == "observe":
    56→        meta["issue_dispositions"] = {}
    57→    elif from_stage == "reflect":
    58→        for entry in dispositions.values():
    59→            entry.pop("decision", None)
    60→            entry.pop("target", None)
    61→            entry.pop("decision_source", None)
    62→
    63→
    64→def cascade_clear_later_confirmations(
    65→    stages: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    66→    from_stage: str,
    67→) -> list[str]:
    68→    """Clear later-stage confirmations after mutating an earlier stage."""
    69→    try:
    70→        idx = STAGE_ORDER.index(from_stage)
    71→    except ValueError:
    72→        return []
    73→    cleared: list[str] = []
    74→    for later in STAGE_ORDER[idx + 1:]:
    75→        if later in stages and stages[later].get("confirmed_at"):
    76→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    77→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    78→            cleared.append(later)
    79→    return cleared
    80→
    81→
    82→def print_cascade_clear_feedback(
    83→    cleared: list[str],
    84→    stages: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    85→) -> None:
    86→    """Render confirmation-clearing feedback after stage rewrites."""
    87→    if not cleared:
    88→        return
    89→    print(colorize(f"  Cleared confirmations on: {', '.join(cleared)}", "yellow"))
    90→    next_unconfirmed = next(
    91→        (
    92→            stage
    93→            for stage in STAGE_ORDER
    94→            if stage in stages and not stages[stage].get("confirmed_at")
    95→        ),
    96→        None,
    97→    )
    98→    if next_unconfirmed:
    99→        print(
   100→            colorize(
   101→                f"  Re-confirm with: desloppify plan triage --confirm {next_unconfirmed}",
   102→                "dim",
   103→            )
   104→        )
   105→
   106→
   107→__all__ = [
   108→    "STAGE_ORDER",
   109→    "cascade_clear_dispositions",
   110→    "cascade_clear_later_confirmations",
   111→    "has_triage_in_queue",
   112→    "inject_triage_stages",
   113→    "print_cascade_clear_feedback",
   114→    "purge_triage_stage",
   115→]
   116→

    74→def _build_next_payload(
    75→    *,
    76→    queue: dict,
    77→    items: list[dict],
    78→    state: dict,
    79→    narrative: dict,
    80→    plan_data: dict | None,

    82→) -> dict:

    85→    )
    86→    scores = score_snapshot(state)
    87→    payload["overall_score"] = getattr(scores, "overall", state.get("overall_score"))
    88→    payload["objective_score"] = getattr(
    89→        scores, "objective", state.get("objective_score")
    90→    )
    91→    payload["strict_score"] = getattr(scores, "strict", state.get("strict_score"))
    92→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
    93→        state,
    94→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
    95→    )
    96→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
    97→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
    98→    ]
    99→    return payload
   100→
   101→
   102→def _emit_requested_output(
   103→    opts: NextOptions,
   104→    payload: dict,
   105→    items: list[dict],
   106→    *,
   107→    command_name: str = "next",
   108→) -> bool:
   109→    if opts.output_file:
   110→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   111→            opts.output_file,
   112→            payload,
   113→            len(items),
   114→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   115→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   116→            label=f"{command_name} output",
   117→        ):
   118→            return True
   119→        raise CommandError("Failed to write output file")
   120→
   121→    return next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(
   122→        opts.output_format,
   123→        payload,
   124→        items,
   125→        command=command_name,
   126→    )
   127→
   128→
   129→def _write_next_payload(
   130→    *,
   131→    queue: dict,
   132→    items: list[dict],
   133→    state: dict,
   134→    narrative: dict,
   135→    plan_data: dict | None,
   136→    guardrail_warnings: list[str],
   137→    write_query_fn,
   138→    command_name: str,
   139→) -> dict:
   140→    """Build and persist the payload for the current queue view."""
   141→    payload = _build_next_payload(
   142→        queue=queue,
   143→        items=items,
   144→        state=state,
   145→        narrative=narrative,
   146→        plan_data=plan_data,
   147→        command_name=command_name,
   148→    )
   149→    if guardrail_warnings:
   150→        payload["warnings"] = guardrail_warnings
   151→    write_query_fn(payload)
   152→    return payload
   153→
   154→
   155→def _render_empty_queue_view(
   156→    *,
   157→    queue: dict,
   158→    items: list[dict],
   159→    state: dict,
   160→    plan_for_queue: dict,
   161→    plan_data: dict | None,
   162→    ctx,
   163→    target_strict: float,
   164→    opts: NextOptions,
   165→    guardrail_warnings: list[str],
   166→    write_query_fn,
   167→    command_name: str,
   168→    show_plan_context: bool,
   169→) -> None:
   170→    """Render and persist the empty queue state."""
   171→    strict_score = score_snapshot(state).strict
   172→    plan_start_strict = None
   173→    if show_plan_context and plan_for_queue:
   174→        plan_start_strict, _ = _plan_queue_context(
   175→            state=state,
   176→            plan_data=plan_for_queue,
   177→            context=ctx,
   178→        )
   179→    _render_queue_header(queue, opts.explain)
   180→    _show_empty_queue(
   181→        queue,
   182→        strict_score,
   183→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   184→        target_strict=target_strict,
   185→    )
   186→    _write_next_payload(
   187→        queue=queue,
   188→        items=items,
   189→        state=state,
   190→        narrative={},
   191→        plan_data=plan_data,
   192→        guardrail_warnings=guardrail_warnings,
   193→        write_query_fn=write_query_fn,
   194→        command_name=command_name,
   195→    )
   196→
   197→

   199→    *,
   200→    queue: dict,

    48→
    49→def triage_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel, state: dict | None = None) -> str:
    50→    """Return a banner string describing triage status."""
    51→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    52→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    53→    has_triage = any(stage_id in order for stage_id in TRIAGE_IDS)
    54→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    55→    run_hint = (
    56→        f"Run: {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX} "
    57→        f"(or {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE})"
    58→    )
    59→
    60→    if not has_triage:
    61→        undispositioned = meta.get("undispositioned_issue_count", 0)
    62→        if undispositioned:
    63→            return (
    64→                "TRIAGE RECOVERY NEEDED — "
    65→                f"{undispositioned} review issue(s) still need cluster/skip dispositions. "
    66→                f"{run_hint}"
    67→            )
    68→        if meta.get("triage_recommended"):
    69→            return (
    70→                "TRIAGE RECOMMENDED — review issues changed since last triage. "
    71→                f"{run_hint}"
    72→            )
    73→        return ""
    74→
    75→    if state and has_objective_backlog(state, None):
    76→        undispositioned = meta.get("undispositioned_issue_count", 0)
    77→        if undispositioned:
    78→            return (
    79→                "TRIAGE PENDING — "
    80→                f"{undispositioned} review issue(s) still need cluster/skip dispositions after current work. "
    81→                f"{run_hint}"
    82→            )
    83→        return (
    84→            "TRIAGE PENDING — queued and will activate after objective work "
    85→            "is complete."
    86→        )
    87→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    88→    completed = [
    89→        stage
    90→        for stage in ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check")
    91→        if stage in stages
    92→    ]
    93→    if completed:
    94→        return (
    95→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({len(completed)}/5 stages complete) — "
    96→            f"complete all stages to exit. {run_hint}"
    97→        )
    98→    return (
    99→        "TRIAGE MODE — review issues need analysis before fixing. "
   100→        f"{run_hint}"
   101→    )
   102→
   103→__all__ = [
   104→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ADD",
   105→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_CREATE",
   106→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH",
   107→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT",

1→"""Queue-policy helpers shared by planning render/select modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import replace
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import queue_context
    10→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import (
    11→    QueueBuildOptions,
    12→    QueueVisibility,
    13→    WorkQueueResult,
    14→    _build_work_queue_with_visibility,
    15→    build_work_queue,
    16→)
    17→
    18→
    19→def _subjective_threshold(state: StateModel, *, default: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE) -> float:
    20→    config = state.get("config", {})
    21→    raw_target = default
    22→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    23→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", default)
    24→    try:
    25→        value = float(raw_target)
    26→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    27→        value = default
    28→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, value))
    29→
    30→
    31→def _queue_plan_from_options(options: QueueBuildOptions) -> dict | None:
    32→    if options.context is not None:
    33→        return options.context.plan
    34→    return options.plan
    35→
    36→
    37→def build_open_plan_queue(
    38→    state: StateModel,
    39→    *,
    40→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    41→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    42→    """Build one open-status queue with consistent planning policy defaults."""
    43→    opts = replace(options) if options is not None else QueueBuildOptions(count=None)
    44→    if options is None:
    45→        opts = replace(opts, status="open", include_subjective=True)
    46→    elif opts.status == QueueBuildOptions().status:
    47→        opts = replace(opts, status="open")
    48→    if opts.subjective_threshold == QueueBuildOptions().subjective_threshold:
    49→        opts = replace(opts, subjective_threshold=_subjective_threshold(state))
    50→    return build_work_queue(
    51→        state,
    52→        options=opts,
    53→    )
    54→
    55→
    56→def build_execution_queue(
    57→    state: StateModel,
    58→    *,
    59→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    60→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    61→    """Build the execution queue that follows the living plan when present."""
    62→    options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    63→    ctx = options.context or queue_context(
    64→        state,
    65→        plan=_queue_plan_from_options(options),
    66→        target_strict=_subjective_threshold(state),
    67→    )
    68→    return _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    69→        state,
    70→        options=replace(options, context=ctx),
    71→        visibility=QueueVisibility.EXECUTION,
    72→    )
    73→
    74→
    75→def build_backlog_queue(
    76→    state: StateModel,
    77→    *,
    78→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    79→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    80→    """Build the broader backlog, excluding work already tracked in the plan."""
    81→    options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    82→    ctx = options.context or queue_context(
    83→        state,
    84→        plan=_queue_plan_from_options(options),
    85→        target_strict=_subjective_threshold(state),
    86→    )
    87→    return _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    88→        state,
    89→        options=replace(options, context=ctx),
    90→        visibility=QueueVisibility.BACKLOG,
    91→    )
    92→
    93→
    94→__all__ = [
    95→    "build_backlog_queue",
    96→    "build_execution_queue",
    97→    "build_open_plan_queue",
    98→]
    99→

    36→def _print_runner_paths(
    37→    *,
    38→    only_stages: str | None = None,
    39→    manual_fallback: str | None = None,
    40→    intro: str = "  Preferred runners:",
    41→) -> None:
    42→    print(colorize(intro, "yellow"))
    43→    for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages=only_stages):
    44→        print(f"    {label}:  {command}")
    45→    if manual_fallback:
    46→        print(colorize(f"  Manual fallback: {manual_fallback}", "dim"))
    47→
    48→
    49→def print_dashboard_header(si: object, stages: dict, meta: dict, plan: dict) -> None:
    50→    """Print the header section: title, open issues count, stage progress, overall status."""
    51→    print(colorize("  Cluster triage", "bold"))
    52→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
    53→    print(f"  Open review issues: {len(si.open_issues)}")
    54→    print(colorize("  Goal: identify contradictions, resolve them, then group the coherent", "cyan"))
    55→    print(colorize("  remainder into clusters by root cause with action steps and priorities.", "cyan"))
    56→    print(colorize("  Preferred: staged runner workflow (Codex or Claude).", "cyan"))
    57→    print(colorize(f"    Codex:  {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX}", "dim"))
    58→    print(colorize(f"    Claude: {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE}", "dim"))
    59→    print(colorize("  Manual stage commands below are fallback/debug paths.", "dim"))
    60→    existing_clusters = si.existing_clusters
    61→    if existing_clusters:
    62→        print(f"  Existing clusters: {len(existing_clusters)}")
    63→    if si.new_since_last:
    64→        print(colorize(f"  New since last triage: {len(si.new_since_last)}", "yellow"))
    65→        for fid in sorted(si.new_since_last):
    66→            issue = si.open_issues.get(fid, {})
    67→            dim = ""
    68→            detail = issue.get("detail")
    69→            if isinstance(detail, dict):
    70→                dim = detail.get("dimension", "")
    71→            dim_tag = f" ({dim})" if dim else ""
    72→            print(colorize(f"    * [{short_issue_id(fid)}] {issue.get('summary', '')}{dim_tag}", "yellow"))
    73→    if si.resolved_since_last:
    74→        print(f"  Resolved since last triage: {len(si.resolved_since_last)}")
    75→
    76→    print()
    77→    print_stage_progress(stages, plan)
    78→    if meta.get("stage_refresh_required"):
    79→        print(
    80→            colorize(
    81→                "  Note: review issues changed since stage progress started; "
    82→                "refresh stage reports before completion.",
    83→                "yellow",
    84→            )
    85→        )
    86→
    87→
    88→def _print_completed_guidance(si: object) -> None:
    89→    """Triage completed, no new issues — point to execution."""
    90→    print(colorize("  Triage complete. Executing current plan.", "green"))
    91→    print(colorize("    desloppify next", "dim"))
    92→    if si.resolved_since_last:
    93→        print(colorize(f"  {len(si.resolved_since_last)} issue(s) resolved since last triage.", "dim"))
    94→
    95→
    96→def _print_retriage_guidance(si: object, meta: dict) -> None:
    97→    """Triage completed but new issues arrived — offer re-triage paths."""
    98→    has_only_additions = bool(si.new_since_last) and not si.resolved_since_last
    99→    if has_only_additions and meta.get("strategy_summary"):
   100→        print(colorize("  Two paths available:", "yellow"))
   101→        print()
   102→        print(colorize("  To reuse the existing enriched cluster plan (without rewriting clusters):", "cyan"))
   103→        print('    desloppify plan triage --confirm-existing --note "..." --strategy "same" --confirmed "I have reviewed..."')
   104→        print()
   105→        print(colorize("  To re-prioritize and restructure:", "cyan"))
   106→        print(f"    Codex:  {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX}")
   107→        print(f"    Claude: {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE}")
   108→        print(colorize(f"    Manual fallback: {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}", "dim"))
   109→    else:
   110→        _print_runner_paths(
   111→            only_stages="observe",
   112→            manual_fallback=TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE,
   113→            intro="  Next step:",
   114→        )
   115→        print(colorize("    (themes, root causes, contradictions between issues — NOT a list of IDs)", "dim"))
   116→
   117→
   118→def _print_in_progress_guidance(stages: dict, meta: dict, plan: dict) -> None:
   119→    """Triage stages are active — guide through the stage chain."""
   120→    if "reflect" not in stages:
   121→        _print_runner_paths(
   122→            only_stages="reflect",
   123→            manual_fallback=TRIAGE_CMD_REFLECT,
   124→            intro="  Next step: use the completed work and patterns below to write your reflect report.",
   125→        )
   126→        print(colorize("    (Contradictions, recurring patterns, which direction to take, what to defer)", "dim"))
   127→    elif "organize" not in stages:
   128→        gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
   129→        manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   130→
   131→        if not manual:
   132→            _print_runner_paths(
   133→                only_stages="organize",
   134→                manual_fallback=TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE,
   135→                intro="  Next steps:",
   136→            )
   137→            print("    0. Defer contradictory issues: `desloppify plan skip <hash>`")
   138→            print(f"    1. Create clusters:  {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_CREATE}")
   139→            print(f"    2. Add issues:     {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ADD}")
   140→            print(f"    3. Enrich clusters:  {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_STEPS}")
   141→            print(f"    4. Record stage:     {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}")
   142→        elif gaps:
   143→            print(colorize("  Enrich these clusters before recording organize:", "yellow"))
   144→            for name, missing in gaps:
   145→                print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   146→            print(colorize(f"    Fix: {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT}", "dim"))
   147→            print(colorize(f"    Then: {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   148→        else:
   149→            print(colorize("  All clusters enriched! Record the organize stage:", "green"))
   150→            _print_runner_paths(
   151→                only_stages="organize",
   152→                manual_fallback=TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE,
   153→                intro="  Preferred runner paths:",
   154→            )
   155→
   156→        if meta.get("strategy_summary"):
   157→            print()
   158→            print(colorize("  Or fast-track by reusing the current enriched cluster plan:", "dim"))
   159→            print(colorize("    (This confirms the existing manual clusters; it does not materialize a new reflect blueprint.)", "dim"))
   160→            print(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_CONFIRM_EXISTING}")
   161→    elif "enrich" not in stages:
   162→        shallow = unenriched_clusters(plan)
   163→        if shallow:
   164→            print(colorize("  Next step: enrich steps with detail and issue_refs.", "yellow"))
   165→            print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"', "dim"))
   166→        else:
   167→            print(colorize("  Steps look enriched. Record the enrich stage:", "green"))
   168→        _print_runner_paths(
   169→            only_stages="enrich",
   170→            manual_fallback=TRIAGE_CMD_ENRICH,
   171→            intro="  Preferred runner paths:",
   172→        )
   173→        print(colorize("  You can still reorganize: add/remove clusters, reorder items.", "dim"))
   174→    else:
   175→        print(colorize("  Ready to complete:", "green"))
   176→        _print_runner_paths(
   177→            manual_fallback=TRIAGE_CMD_COMPLETE_VERBOSE,
   178→            intro="  Preferred runner paths:",
   179→        )
   180→        print(colorize('    (use --strategy "same" to keep existing strategy)', "dim"))
   181→
   182→
   183→def print_action_guidance(stages: dict, meta: dict, si: object, plan: dict) -> None:
   184→    """Print the 'What to do' action guidance section based on current stage.
   185→
   186→    Three states, matching the engine's canonical triage lifecycle:
   187→      1. Completed & current — triaged_ids present, no new issues
   188→      2. Needs (re-)triage — observe not done yet (fresh start or new issues)
   189→      3. In progress — observe done, working through remaining stages
   190→    """
   191→    print()
   192→    triage_has_run = bool(meta.get("triaged_ids"))
   193→    has_new_issues = bool(si.new_since_last)
   194→
   195→    if "observe" not in stages:
   196→        if triage_has_run and not has_new_issues:
   197→            _print_completed_guidance(si)
   198→        else:
   199→            _print_retriage_guidance(si, meta)
   200→    else:
   201→        _print_in_progress_guidance(stages, meta, plan)
   202→
   203→
   204→def print_prior_stage_reports(stages: dict) -> None:
   205→    """Print prior stage reports (observe/reflect) as context for current action."""
   206→    if "observe" in stages:
   207→        obs_report = stages["observe"].get("report", "")
   208→        if obs_report:
   209→            print(colorize("\n  Your observe analysis:", "dim"))
   210→            for line in obs_report.strip().splitlines()[:8]:
   211→                print(colorize(f"    {line}", "dim"))
   212→            if len(obs_report.strip().splitlines()) > 8:
   213→                print(colorize("    ...", "dim"))
   214→    if "reflect" in stages:
   215→        ref_report = stages["reflect"].get("report", "")
   216→        if ref_report:
   217→            print(colorize("\n  Your reflect strategy:", "dim"))
   218→            for line in ref_report.strip().splitlines()[:8]:
   219→                print(colorize(f"    {line}", "dim"))
   220→            if len(ref_report.strip().splitlines()) > 8:
   221→                print(colorize("    ...", "dim"))
   222→
   223→
   224→def print_issues_by_dimension(open_issues: dict) -> None:
   225→    """Print issues grouped by dimension with suggestions to surface contradictions."""
   226→    by_dim: dict[str, list[tuple[str, dict]]] = defaultdict(list)
   227→    for fid, issue in open_issues.items():
   228→        detail = issue.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(issue.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   229→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
   230→        by_dim[dim].append((fid, issue))
   231→
   232→    print(colorize("\n  Review issues by dimension:", "cyan"))
   233→    print(colorize("  (Look for contradictions: issues in the same dimension that", "dim"))
   234→    print(colorize("  recommend opposite changes. These must be resolved before clustering.)", "dim"))
   235→    max_per_dim = 5
   236→    for dim in sorted(by_dim, key=lambda d: (-len(by_dim[d]), d)):
   237→        items = by_dim[dim]
   238→        print(colorize(f"\n    {dim} ({len(items)}):", "bold"))
   239→        for fid, issue in items[:max_per_dim]:
   240→            summary = issue.get("summary", "")
   241→            short = short_issue_id(fid)
   242→            detail = issue.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(issue.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   243→            suggestion = (detail.get("suggestion") or "")[:120]
   244→            print(f"      [{short}] {summary}")
   245→            if suggestion:
   246→                print(colorize(f"        → {suggestion}", "dim"))
   247→        if len(items) > max_per_dim:
   248→            print(colorize(f"      ... and {len(items) - max_per_dim} more", "dim"))
   249→    print(colorize("\n  Use hash in commands: desloppify plan skip <hash>  |  desloppify show <hash>", "dim"))
   250→

1→"""Whole-plan triage API.
     2→
     3→Primary runtime triage orchestration is staged and lives in
     4→``app.commands.plan.triage``. The single-shot ``triage_epics`` runtime
     5→lives here; callers import from ``engine.plan_triage``.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import json
    11→import logging
    12→from dataclasses import dataclass
    13→from typing import Any
    14→
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    17→
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.triage.apply import (
    19→    TriageMutationResult,
    20→    apply_triage_to_plan,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._plan.triage.parsing import (
    23→    ISSUE_ID_RE,
    24→    extract_issue_citations,
    25→    parse_triage_result,
    26→)
    27→from desloppify.engine._plan.triage.prompt import (
    28→    _TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
    29→    ContradictionNote,
    30→    DismissedIssue,
    31→    TriageInput,
    32→    TriageResult,
    33→    build_triage_prompt,
    34→    collect_triage_input,
    35→)
    36→
    37→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    38→
    39→
    40→def last_real_review_timestamp(state: dict) -> str | None:
    41→    """ISO timestamp of most recent genuine review import (not manual override/scan reset)."""
    42→    real_modes = {"holistic", "per_file", "trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
    43→    audit = state.get("assessment_import_audit", [])
    44→    if isinstance(audit, list):
    45→        for entry in reversed(audit):
    46→            if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get("mode") in real_modes:
    47→                ts = entry.get("timestamp")
    48→                if ts:
    49→                    return str(ts)
    50→    holistic = (state.get("review_cache") or {}).get("holistic")
    51→    if isinstance(holistic, dict):
    52→        return holistic.get("reviewed_at")
    53→    return None
    54→
    55→
    56→def detect_recurring_patterns(
    57→    open_issues: dict[str, dict],
    58→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict],
    59→) -> dict[str, dict]:
    60→    """Detect dimensions with both resolved AND current open issues.
    61→
    62→    Returns ``{dimension: {"open": [ids], "resolved": [ids]}}``.
    63→    A dimension with both resolved and open issues signals a potential
    64→    loop — similar issues recur after previous fixes.
    65→    """
    66→
    67→    def _dimension(finding: dict) -> str:
    68→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
    69→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
    70→            return detail.get("dimension", "")
    71→        return ""
    72→
    73→    open_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    74→    for fid, finding in open_issues.items():
    75→        dim = _dimension(finding)
    76→        if dim:
    77→            open_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
    78→
    79→    resolved_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    80→    for fid, finding in resolved_issues.items():
    81→        dim = _dimension(finding)
    82→        if dim:
    83→            resolved_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
    84→
    85→    recurring: dict[str, dict] = {}
    86→    for dim in set(open_by_dim) & set(resolved_by_dim):
    87→        recurring[dim] = {
    88→            "open": open_by_dim[dim],
    89→            "resolved": resolved_by_dim[dim],
    90→        }
    91→    return recurring
    92→
    93→
    94→@dataclass
    95→class TriageDeps:
    96→    """Injectable dependencies for the triage engine."""
    97→
    98→    llm_call: Any = None  # Callable[[str, str], str] — (system, user) -> response
    99→
   100→
   101→def triage_epics(
   102→    plan: PlanModel,
   103→    state: StateModel,
   104→    *,
   105→    deps: TriageDeps | None = None,
   106→    dry_run: bool = False,
   107→    trigger: str = "manual",
   108→) -> TriageMutationResult:
   109→    """Run legacy whole-plan triage: collect input, call LLM, apply results.
   110→
   111→    If ``dry_run`` is True, collects input and builds prompt but does not
   112→    call the LLM or mutate the plan.
   113→
   114→    If ``deps.llm_call`` is None, returns a dry-run result with the prompt.
   115→    """
   116→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   117→    si = collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   118→
   119→    prompt = build_triage_prompt(si)
   120→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())
   121→
   122→    if dry_run or deps is None or deps.llm_call is None:
   123→        result = TriageMutationResult(dry_run=True)
   124→        result.strategy_summary = f"[dry-run] Prompt built with {len(si.open_issues)} issues"
   125→        return result
   126→
   127→    try:
   128→        raw_response = deps.llm_call(_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT, prompt)
   129→        raw_json = json.loads(raw_response)
   130→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   131→        logger.error("Cluster triage LLM response parse error: %s", exc)
   132→        result = TriageMutationResult()
   133→        result.strategy_summary = f"Triage failed: {exc}"
   134→        return result
   135→
   136→    triage = parse_triage_result(raw_json, valid_ids)
   137→    return apply_triage_to_plan(plan, state, triage, trigger=trigger)
   138→
   139→
   140→__all__ = [
   141→    "ContradictionNote",
   142→    "DismissedIssue",
   143→    "ISSUE_ID_RE",
   144→    "TriageDeps",
   145→    "TriageInput",
   146→    "TriageMutationResult",
   147→    "TriageResult",
   148→    "apply_triage_to_plan",
   149→    "build_triage_prompt",
   150→    "collect_triage_input",
   151→    "detect_recurring_patterns",
   152→    "extract_issue_citations",
   153→    "last_real_review_timestamp",
   154→    "parse_triage_result",
   155→    "triage_epics",
   156→]
   157→
```

1→"""Prompt/data contracts for whole-plan triage orchestration."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     9→    Cluster,
    10→    PlanModel,
    11→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    12→    triage_clusters,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass
    18→class TriageInput:
    19→    """All data needed to produce/update triage clusters."""
    20→
    21→    open_issues: dict[str, dict]       # id -> issue (review + concerns)
    22→    mechanical_issues: dict[str, dict]  # id -> issue (non-review, for context)
    23→    existing_clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
    24→    dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]      # for context
    25→    new_since_last: set[str]             # issue IDs new since last triage
    26→    resolved_since_last: set[str]        # issue IDs resolved since last
    27→    previously_dismissed: list[str]      # IDs dismissed in prior triage
    28→    triage_version: int                  # next version number
    29→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict]   # full issue objects for resolved IDs
    30→    completed_clusters: list[dict]       # clusters completed since last triage
    31→
    32→@dataclass
    33→class DismissedIssue:
    34→    """A issue the LLM says doesn't make sense."""
    35→
    36→    issue_id: str
    37→    reason: str
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class ContradictionNote:
    41→    """Record of a resolved contradiction."""
    42→
    43→    kept: str
    44→    dismissed: str
    45→    reason: str
    46→
    47→@dataclass
    48→class TriageResult:
    49→    """Parsed and validated LLM triage output."""
    50→
    51→    strategy_summary: str
    52→    epics: list[dict]
    53→    dismissed_issues: list[DismissedIssue] = field(default_factory=list)
    54→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = field(default_factory=list)
    55→    priority_rationale: str = ""
    56→
    57→    @property
    58→    def clusters(self) -> list[dict]:
    59→        """Canonical cluster view for triage results."""
    60→        return self.epics
    61→
    62→
    63→def _issue_dimension(issue: dict) -> str:
    64→    detail = issue.get("detail", {})
    65→    if isinstance(detail, dict):
    66→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    67→        if isinstance(dimension, str):
    68→            return dimension
    69→    return ""
    70→
    71→
    72→def _recurring_dimensions(
    73→    open_issues: dict[str, dict],
    74→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict],
    75→) -> dict[str, dict[str, list[str]]]:
    76→    open_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    77→    for issue_id, issue in open_issues.items():
    78→        dimension = _issue_dimension(issue)
    79→        if dimension:
    80→            open_by_dim.setdefault(dimension, []).append(issue_id)
    81→
    82→    resolved_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    83→    for issue_id, issue in resolved_issues.items():
    84→        dimension = _issue_dimension(issue)
    85→        if dimension:
    86→            resolved_by_dim.setdefault(dimension, []).append(issue_id)
    87→
    88→    recurring: dict[str, dict[str, list[str]]] = {}
    89→    for dimension in sorted(set(open_by_dim) & set(resolved_by_dim)):
    90→        recurring[dimension] = {
    91→            "open": sorted(open_by_dim[dimension]),
    92→            "resolved": sorted(resolved_by_dim[dimension]),
    93→        }
    94→    return recurring
    95→
    96→
    97→def _split_open_issue_buckets(
    98→    issues: dict[str, dict],
    99→) -> tuple[dict[str, dict], dict[str, dict]]:
   100→    open_review: dict[str, dict] = {}
   101→    open_mechanical: dict[str, dict] = {}
   102→    for issue_id, issue in issues.items():
   103→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
   104→            continue
   105→        if issue.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns"):
   106→            open_review[issue_id] = issue
   107→            continue
   108→        open_mechanical[issue_id] = issue
   109→    return open_review, open_mechanical
   110→
   111→
   112→def _recent_completed_clusters(meta: dict, plan: PlanModel) -> list[dict]:
   113→    last_completed = meta.get("last_completed_at", "")
   114→    all_completed: list[dict] = plan.get("completed_clusters", [])
   115→    if not last_completed:
   116→        return list(all_completed)
   117→    return [
   118→        cluster for cluster in all_completed
   119→        if cluster.get("completed_at", "") > last_completed
   120→    ]
   121→
   122→
   123→def collect_triage_input(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> TriageInput:
   124→    """Gather all data needed for the triage LLM prompt."""
   125→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   126→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   127→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   128→    epics = triage_clusters(plan)
   129→    open_review, open_mechanical = _split_open_issue_buckets(issues)
   130→
   131→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   132→    current_review_ids = set(open_review.keys())
   133→    new_since = current_review_ids - triaged_ids
   134→    resolved_since = triaged_ids - current_review_ids
   135→    previously_dismissed = list(meta.get("dismissed_ids", []))
   136→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
   137→
   138→    # Resolved issue objects (for REFLECT stage)
   139→    resolved_issue_objs = {
   140→        fid: issues[fid] for fid in resolved_since if fid in issues
   141→    }
   142→
   143→    return TriageInput(
   144→        open_issues=open_review,
   145→        mechanical_issues=open_mechanical,
   146→        existing_clusters=dict(epics),
   147→        dimension_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   148→        new_since_last=new_since,
   149→        resolved_since_last=resolved_since,
   150→        previously_dismissed=previously_dismissed,
   151→        triage_version=version,
   152→        resolved_issues=resolved_issue_objs,
   153→        completed_clusters=_recent_completed_clusters(meta, plan),
   154→    )
   155→
   156→_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
   157→You are maintaining the meta-plan for this codebase. Produce a coherent
   158→prioritized strategy for all open review issues.
   159→
   160→Your plan should:
   161→- Cluster issues by ROOT CAUSE, not by dimension or detector
   162→- Give each cluster a clear thesis: one imperative sentence
   163→- Order clusters by dependency: what must be done first for later work to make sense
   164→- Dismiss issues that don't make sense, are contradictory, or are false positives
   165→- Mark which clusters are agent-safe (can be executed mechanically) vs need human judgment
   166→- Avoid creating work that contradicts other work in the plan
   167→- Be ambitious but realistic — aim to resolve all issues coherently
   168→
   169→Available directions for clusters: delete, merge, flatten, enforce, simplify, decompose, extract, inline.
   170→
   171→Available plan tools (the agent executing your plan has access to these):
   172→- `desloppify plan queue` — view the execution queue in priority order
   173→- `desloppify backlog` — inspect broader open work outside the execution queue
   174→- `desloppify plan focus <name>` — focus the queue on one cluster
   175→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --permanent --note "why" --attest "..."` — permanently dismiss
   176→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --note "revisit later"` — temporarily defer
   177→- `desloppify plan resolve <id> --note "what I did" --attest "..."` — mark resolved
   178→- `desloppify plan reorder <id> top|bottom|before|after <target>` — reorder
   179→- `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` — inspect a cluster
   180→- `desloppify scan` — re-scan after making changes to verify progress
   181→- `desloppify show review --status open` — see all open review issues
   182→
   183→Your output defines the ENTIRE work plan. Issues not assigned to any cluster
   184→will remain in the queue as individual items. Dismissed issues will be
   185→removed from the queue with your stated reason.
   186→
   187→Respond with a single JSON object matching this schema:
   188→{
   189→  "strategy_summary": "2-4 sentence narrative: what the meta-plan says, top priorities, current state",
   190→  "clusters": [
   191→    {
   192→      "name": "slug-name",
   193→      "thesis": "imperative one-liner",
   194→      "direction": "delete|merge|flatten|enforce|simplify|decompose|extract|inline",
   195→      "root_cause": "why this cluster exists",
   196→      "issue_ids": ["id1", "id2"],
   197→      "dismissed": ["id3"],
   198→      "agent_safe": true,
   199→      "dependency_order": 1,
   200→      "action_steps": [
   201→        {
   202→          "title": "Short imperative step title",
   203→          "detail": "Concrete implementation detail with file paths/locations",
   204→          "issue_refs": ["id1"]
   205→        }
   206→      ],
   207→      "status": "pending"
   208→    }
   209→  ],
   210→  "dismissed_issues": [
   211→    {"issue_id": "id", "reason": "why this issue doesn't make sense"}
   212→  ],
   213→  "contradiction_notes": [
   214→    {"kept": "issue_id", "dismissed": "issue_id", "reason": "why"}
   215→  ],
   216→  "priority_rationale": "why the dependency_order is what it is"
   217→}
   218→"""
   219→
   220→def _append_existing_clusters_section(parts: list[str], clusters: dict[str, Cluster]) -> None:
   221→    if not clusters:
   222→        return
   223→    parts.append("## Existing clusters")
   224→    for name, cluster in sorted(clusters.items()):
   225→        status = cluster.get("status", "pending")
   226→        thesis = cluster.get("thesis", "")
   227→        direction = cluster.get("direction", "")
   228→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   229→        parts.append(
   230→            f"- {name} [{status}] ({direction}): {thesis}"
   231→            f"\n  Issues: {', '.join(issue_ids[:10])}"
   232→            f"{'...' if len(issue_ids) > 10 else ''}"
   233→        )
   234→    parts.append("")
   235→
   236→
   237→def _append_changed_issue_section(
   238→    parts: list[str],
   239→    *,
   240→    title: str,
   241→    issue_ids: set[str],
   242→    issues: dict[str, dict] | None = None,
   243→) -> None:
   244→    if not issue_ids:
   245→        return
   246→    parts.append(f"## {title} ({len(issue_ids)})")
   247→    for issue_id in sorted(issue_ids):
   248→        if issues is None:
   249→            parts.append(f"- {issue_id}")
   250→            continue
   251→        issue = issues.get(issue_id, {})
   252→        parts.append(f"- {issue_id}: {issue.get('summary', '(no summary)')}")
   253→    parts.append("")
   254→
   255→
   256→def _append_resolved_issue_context(parts: list[str], resolved_issues: dict[str, dict]) -> None:
   257→    if not resolved_issues:
   258→        return
   259→    parts.append(
   260→        "## Resolved review issues available for recurrence context "
   261→        f"({len(resolved_issues)})"
   262→    )
   263→    resolved_ids = sorted(resolved_issues)
   264→    for issue_id in resolved_ids[:30]:
   265→        issue = resolved_issues.get(issue_id, {})
   266→        summary = str(issue.get("summary", "(no summary)"))
   267→        dimension = _issue_dimension(issue)
   268→        dim_suffix = f" [{dimension}]" if dimension else ""
   269→        parts.append(f"- {issue_id}{dim_suffix}: {summary}")
   270→    if len(resolved_ids) > 30:
   271→        parts.append(f"- ... and {len(resolved_ids) - 30} more resolved issues")
   272→    parts.append("")
   273→
   274→
   275→def _append_completed_clusters_section(parts: list[str], completed_clusters: list[dict]) -> None:
   276→    if not completed_clusters:
   277→        return
   278→    parts.append(
   279→        "## Completed clusters since last triage "
   280→        f"({len(completed_clusters)})"
   281→    )
   282→    completed = sorted(
   283→        completed_clusters,
   284→        key=lambda cluster: str(cluster.get("completed_at", "")),
   285→        reverse=True,
   286→    )
   287→    for cluster in completed[:10]:
   288→        name = str(cluster.get("name", "(unnamed cluster)"))
   289→        thesis = str(cluster.get("thesis") or cluster.get("description") or "")
   290→        completed_at = str(cluster.get("completed_at", ""))
   291→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   292→        issue_count = len(issue_ids) if isinstance(issue_ids, list) else 0
   293→        parts.append(
   294→            f"- {name}: {thesis} (issues: {issue_count}, completed_at: {completed_at})"
   295→        )
   296→    if len(completed) > 10:
   297→        parts.append(f"- ... and {len(completed) - 10} more completed clusters")
   298→    parts.append("")
   299→
   300→

    51→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
    52→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
    53→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()

    55→

    59→    return slugify(cleaned)

    64→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()

    69→        slugify(cleaned),
    70→        canonical.lower(),

   110→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   111→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   112→
   113→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
   114→    """
   115→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   116→    order_set = set(order)
   117→    present_ids = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   118→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   119→    confirmed = confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   120→    recorded_unconfirmed = recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   121→    present_names = {
   122→        name
   123→        for name, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS
   124→        if sid in present_ids
   125→    }
   126→    present_names.update(recorded_unconfirmed)
   127→    if not present_names:
   128→        return []
   129→
   130→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   131→    open_review_count = sum(
   132→        1 for f in issues.values()
   133→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   134→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   135→    )
   136→
   137→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   138→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   139→    for name, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
   140→        if name not in present_names:
   141→            continue
   142→        if name in confirmed:
   143→            continue
   144→
   145→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   146→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   147→        blocked_by = sorted(
   148→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps if dep in present_names and dep not in confirmed
   149→        )
   150→
   151→        only_stages = None if name == "commit" else name
   152→        cmd = triage_run_stages_command(only_stages=only_stages)
   153→
   154→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   155→            "id": sid,
   156→            "tier": 1,
   157→            "confidence": "high",
   158→            "detector": "triage",
   159→            "file": ".",
   160→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   161→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   162→            "detail": {
   163→                "total_review_issues": open_review_count,
   164→                "stage": name,
   165→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   166→                "runner_commands": [
   167→                    {"label": label, "command": command}
   168→                    for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages=only_stages)
   169→                ],
   170→                "manual_fallback": triage_manual_stage_command(name),
   171→            },
   172→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   173→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   174→        }
   175→        item["primary_command"] = cmd
   176→        items.append(item)
   177→    return items
   178→
   179→
   180→def build_subjective_items(
   181→    state: dict, issues: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   182→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   183→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   184→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   185→    if not dim_scores:
   186→        return []
   187→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   188→
   189→    subjective_entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   190→    if not subjective_entries:
   191→        return []
   192→    unassessed_dims = {
   193→        str(name).strip()
   194→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   195→            dim_scores
   196→        )
   197→    }
   198→
   199→    # Review issues are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   200→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   201→    for issue in issues.values():
   202→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
   203→            continue
   204→        if issue.get("detector") == "review":
   205→            dim_key = str(detail_dict(issue).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   206→            if dim_key:
   207→                review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   208→
   209→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   210→    def _prepare_command(
   211→        cli_keys: list[str],
   212→        *,
   213→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   214→    ) -> str:
   215→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   216→        if cli_keys:
   217→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   218→        if force_review_rerun:
   219→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   220→        return command
   221→
   222→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   223→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   224→        if not name:
   225→            continue
   226→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   227→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   228→            continue
   229→
   230→        [REDACTED](name)
   231→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   232→        cli_keys = [
   233→            str(key).strip().lower()
   234→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   235→            if str(key).strip()
   236→        ]
   237→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   238→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   239→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   240→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   241→            name in unassessed_dims
   242→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("failing", 0)) == 0)
   243→        )
   244→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   245→        # If review issues already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   246→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   247→        if open_review > 0:
   248→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   249→        else:
   250→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   251→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   252→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   253→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   254→            "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   255→            "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   256→            "file": ".",
   257→            "confidence": "medium",
   258→            "summary": summary,
   259→            "detail": {
   260→                "dimension_name": name,
   261→                "dimension": dim_key,
   262→                "failing": int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
   263→                "strict_score": strict_val,
   264→                "open_review_issues": open_review,
   265→                "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   266→            },
   267→            "status": "open",
   268→        }
   269→        item["kind"] = "subjective_dimension"
   270→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command
   271→        item["initial_review"] = is_unassessed
   272→        items.append(item)
   273→    return items
   274→

1→"""Shared render helpers for ``desloppify next`` terminal output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import Counter
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import format_plan_delta
     9→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    10→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import group_queue_items
    11→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    12→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import subjective_review_open_breakdown
    15→
    16→_ACTION_TYPE_LABELS = {
    17→    "auto_fix": "Auto-fixable batch",
    18→    "reorganize": "Reorganize batch",
    19→    "refactor": "Refactor batch",
    20→    "manual_fix": "Grouped task",
    21→}
    22→_CLUSTER_NAME_LABELS = {
    23→    "auto/initial-review": "Initial subjective review",
    24→    "auto/stale-review": "Stale subjective review",
    25→    "auto/under-target-review": "Below-target re-review",
    26→}
    27→
    28→
    29→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    30→class ClusterTypeLabels:
    31→    action_types: dict[str, str]
    32→    cluster_names: dict[str, str]
    33→
    34→
    35→CLUSTER_TYPE_LABELS = ClusterTypeLabels(
    36→    action_types=_ACTION_TYPE_LABELS,
    37→    cluster_names=_CLUSTER_NAME_LABELS,
    38→)
    39→
    40→
    41→def scorecard_subjective(
    42→    state: dict,
    43→    dim_scores: dict,
    44→) -> list[dict]:
    45→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    46→    if not dim_scores:
    47→        return []
    48→    return scorecard_subjective_entries(
    49→        state,
    50→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
    51→    )
    52→
    53→
    54→def subjective_coverage_breakdown(
    55→    issues_scoped: dict,
    56→) -> tuple[int, dict[str, int], dict[str, int]]:
    57→    """Return open subjective-review count plus reason and holistic-reason breakdowns."""
    58→    return subjective_review_open_breakdown(issues_scoped)
    59→
    60→
    61→def is_auto_fix_command(command: str | None) -> bool:
    62→    cmd = (command or "").strip()
    63→    return cmd.startswith("desloppify autofix ") and "--dry-run" in cmd
    64→
    65→
    66→def effort_tag(item: dict) -> str:
    67→    """Return a short effort/type tag for a queue item."""
    68→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
    69→        return "[review]"
    70→    if is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
    71→        return "[auto]"
    72→    return ""
    73→
    74→
    75→def render_grouped(items: list[dict], group: str) -> None:
    76→    grouped = group_queue_items(items, group)
    77→    for key, grouped_items in grouped.items():
    78→        print(colorize(f"\n  {key} ({len(grouped_items)})", "cyan"))
    79→        for item in grouped_items:
    80→            confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
    81→            tag = effort_tag(item)
    82→            tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
    83→            print(
    84→                f"    [{confidence}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}"
    85→            )
    86→
    87→
    88→def _cluster_type_label(cluster_name: str, action_type: str) -> str:
    89→    if cluster_name in CLUSTER_TYPE_LABELS.cluster_names:
    90→        return CLUSTER_TYPE_LABELS.cluster_names[cluster_name]
    91→    return CLUSTER_TYPE_LABELS.action_types.get(action_type, "Grouped task")
    92→
    93→
    94→def _render_cluster_files(members: list[dict]) -> None:
    95→    file_counts = Counter(m.get("file", "?") for m in members)
    96→    if len(file_counts) <= 5:
    97→        print(colorize("\n  Files:", "dim"))
    98→        for filename, count in file_counts.most_common():
    99→            print(f"    {filename} ({count})")
   100→        return
   101→
   102→    print(colorize(f"\n  Spread across {len(file_counts)} files:", "dim"))
   103→    for filename, count in file_counts.most_common(3):
   104→        print(f"    {filename} ({count})")
   105→    remaining = len(file_counts) - 3
   106→    print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more files", "dim"))
   107→
   108→
   109→def _render_cluster_sample(members: list[dict]) -> None:
   110→    print(colorize("\n  Sample:", "dim"))
   111→    for member in members[:3]:
   112→        summary = member.get("summary") or member.get("id", "")
   113→        print(f"    - {summary}")
   114→    if len(members) > 3:
   115→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(members) - 3} more", "dim"))
   116→
   117→
   118→def cluster_action_commands(cluster_name: str) -> dict[str, str]:
   119→    """Return semantic cluster action commands independent of terminal labels."""
   120→    return {
   121→        "resolve_all": f'desloppify plan resolve "{cluster_name}" --note "<what>" --confirm',
   122→        "drill_in": f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10",
   123→        "skip": f"desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}",
   124→    }
   125→
   126→
   127→def _render_optional_cluster_commands(cluster_name: str) -> None:
   128→    commands = cluster_action_commands(cluster_name)
   129→    print(colorize(f"\n  Skip:          {commands['skip']}", "dim"))
   130→    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      {commands['drill_in']}", "dim"))
   131→    print(
   132→        colorize(
   133→            f"  Resolve all:   {commands['resolve_all']}",
   134→            "dim",
   135→        )
   136→    )
   137→
   138→
   139→def _render_required_cluster_commands(cluster_name: str) -> None:
   140→    commands = cluster_action_commands(cluster_name)
   141→    print(
   142→        colorize(
   143→            f"\n  Resolve all:   {commands['resolve_all']}",
   144→            "dim",
   145→        )
   146→    )
   147→    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      {commands['drill_in']}", "dim"))
   148→    print(colorize(f"  Skip cluster:  {commands['skip']}", "dim"))
   149→
   150→

1→"""Plan mutation helpers for cluster triage."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.stale import review_issue_snapshot_hash
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     9→    EPIC_PREFIX,
    10→    Cluster,
    11→    PlanModel,
    12→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.skip_policy import skip_kind_state_status
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now

    17→from .dismiss import dismiss_triage_issues
    18→from .prompt import TriageResult
    19→
    20→
    21→@dataclass
    22→class TriageMutationResult:
    23→    """What changed when triage was applied to the plan."""
    24→
    25→    epics_created: int = 0
    26→    epics_updated: int = 0
    27→    epics_completed: int = 0
    28→    issues_dismissed: int = 0
    29→    issues_reassigned: int = 0
    30→    strategy_summary: str = ""
    31→    triage_version: int = 0
    32→    dry_run: bool = False
    33→
    34→    @property
    35→    def clusters_created(self) -> int:
    36→        return self.epics_created
    37→
    38→    @property
    39→    def clusters_updated(self) -> int:
    40→        return self.epics_updated
    41→
    42→    @property
    43→    def clusters_completed(self) -> int:
    44→        return self.epics_completed
    45→
    46→
    47→def _epic_sort_key(epic_data: dict) -> int:
    48→    return int(epic_data.get("dependency_order", 999))
    49→
    50→
    51→def _normalized_epic_name(raw_name: str) -> str:
    52→    return raw_name if raw_name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX) else f"{EPIC_PREFIX}{raw_name}"
    53→
    54→
    55→def _update_existing_epic_cluster(
    56→    existing: Cluster,
    57→    epic_data: dict,
    58→    *,
    59→    now: str,
    60→    version: int,
    61→) -> None:
    62→    existing["thesis"] = epic_data["thesis"]
    63→    existing["direction"] = epic_data["direction"]
    64→    existing["root_cause"] = epic_data.get("root_cause", "")
    65→    existing["issue_ids"] = epic_data["issue_ids"]
    66→    existing["dismissed"] = epic_data.get("dismissed", [])
    67→    existing["agent_safe"] = epic_data.get("agent_safe", False)
    68→    existing["dependency_order"] = epic_data["dependency_order"]
    69→    existing["action_steps"] = epic_data.get("action_steps", [])
    70→    existing["updated_at"] = now
    71→    existing["triage_version"] = version
    72→    existing["description"] = epic_data["thesis"]
    73→    # Don't overwrite in_progress status from agent
    74→    if existing.get("status") != "in_progress":
    75→        existing["status"] = epic_data.get("status", "pending")
    76→
    77→
    78→def _create_epic_cluster(
    79→    *,
    80→    epic_name: str,
    81→    epic_data: dict,
    82→    now: str,
    83→    version: int,
    84→) -> Cluster:
    85→    return {
    86→        "name": epic_name,
    87→        "description": epic_data["thesis"],
    88→        "issue_ids": epic_data["issue_ids"],
    89→        "auto": True,
    90→        "cluster_key": f"epic::{epic_name}",
    91→        "action": f"desloppify plan focus {epic_name}",
    92→        "user_modified": False,
    93→        "created_at": now,
    94→        "updated_at": now,
    95→        # Epic fields
    96→        "thesis": epic_data["thesis"],
    97→        "direction": epic_data["direction"],
    98→        "root_cause": epic_data.get("root_cause", ""),
    99→        "supersedes": [],
   100→        "dismissed": epic_data.get("dismissed", []),
   101→        "agent_safe": epic_data.get("agent_safe", False),
   102→        "dependency_order": epic_data["dependency_order"],
   103→        "action_steps": epic_data.get("action_steps", []),
   104→        "source_clusters": [],
   105→        "status": epic_data.get("status", "pending"),
   106→        "triage_version": version,
   107→    }
   108→
   109→
   110→def _upsert_triage_clusters(
   111→    *,
   112→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster],
   113→    triage: TriageResult,
   114→    now: str,
   115→    version: int,
   116→) -> tuple[int, int]:
   117→    created = 0
   118→    updated = 0
   119→    for epic_data in sorted(triage.clusters, key=_epic_sort_key):
   120→        raw_name = epic_data["name"]
   121→        epic_name = _normalized_epic_name(raw_name)
   122→        existing = clusters.get(epic_name)
   123→        if existing and existing.get("thesis"):
   124→            _update_existing_epic_cluster(existing, epic_data, now=now, version=version)
   125→            updated += 1
   126→            continue
   127→        clusters[epic_name] = _create_epic_cluster(
   128→            epic_name=epic_name,
   129→            epic_data=epic_data,
   130→            now=now,
   131→            version=version,
   132→        )
   133→        created += 1
   134→    return created, updated
   135→
   136→
   137→def _reorder_queue_by_dependency(
   138→    *,
   139→    order: list[str],
   140→    triage: TriageResult,
   141→    dismissed_ids: list[str],
   142→) -> None:
   143→    epic_issue_ids: set[str] = set()
   144→    epic_ordered_ids: list[str] = []
   145→    dismissed_set = set(dismissed_ids)
   146→    for epic_data in sorted(triage.clusters, key=_epic_sort_key):
   147→        for fid in epic_data["issue_ids"]:
   148→            if fid in epic_issue_ids or fid in dismissed_set:
   149→                continue
   150→            epic_issue_ids.add(fid)
```
