1→"""Shared triage display primitives used by multiple rendering modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import (
     7→    TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT,
     8→    TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
     9→    TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS,
    10→    triage_manual_stage_command,
    11→    triage_runner_commands,
    12→)
    13→
    14→from ..review_coverage import manual_clusters_with_issues
    15→from ..stages.helpers import unenriched_clusters
    16→
    17→
    18→def print_stage_progress(stages: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> None:
    19→    """Print the triage-stage progress indicator."""
    20→    print(colorize("  Stages:", "dim"))
    21→    for stage_name, label in TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS:
    22→        if stage_name in stages:
    23→            if stages[stage_name].get("confirmed_at"):
    24→                print(colorize(f"    ✓ {label} (confirmed)", "green"))
    25→            else:
    26→                print(colorize(f"    ✓ {label} (needs confirm)", "yellow"))
    27→        elif TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES[stage_name].issubset(stages):
    28→            print(colorize(f"    → {label} (current)", "yellow"))
    29→        else:
    30→            print(colorize(f"    ○ {label}", "dim"))
    31→
    32→    if plan and "reflect" in stages and "organize" not in stages:
    33→        gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
    34→        manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
    35→        if not manual:
    36→            print(colorize("\n    No manual clusters yet. Preferred next step:", "yellow"))
    37→            for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages="organize"):
    38→                print(colorize(f"      {label}: {command}", "dim"))
    39→            print(
    40→                colorize(
    41→                    f"      Manual fallback: {triage_manual_stage_command('organize')}",
    42→                    "dim",
    43→                )
    44→            )
    45→        elif gaps:
    46→            print(colorize(f"\n    {len(gaps)} cluster(s) need enrichment:", "yellow"))
    47→            for name, missing in gaps:
    48→                print(colorize(f"      {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
    49→            print(colorize(f"      Fix: {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT}", "dim"))
    50→        else:
    51→            print(colorize(f"\n    All {len(manual)} manual cluster(s) enriched.", "green"))
    52→
    53→
    54→__all__ = ["print_stage_progress"]
    55→

     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.stale import open_review_ids

    22→
    23→def open_review_ids_from_state(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    24→    """Return open review IDs from the current state snapshot."""
    25→    return open_review_ids(state)
    26→
    27→
    28→def has_open_review_issues(state: StateModel | dict | None) -> bool:
    29→    """Return True when any open review issues exist."""
    30→    return bool(open_review_ids_from_state(state or {}))
    31→
    32→
    33→def plan_review_ids(plan: PlanModel) -> list[str]:
    34→    """Return review/concerns IDs currently represented in queue_order."""
    35→    return [
    36→        fid
    37→        for fid in ensure_queue_order(plan)
    38→        if not fid.startswith("triage::")
    39→        and not fid.startswith("workflow::")
    40→        and (fid.startswith("review::") or fid.startswith("concerns::"))
    41→    ]
    42→
    43→
    44→def coverage_open_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    45→    """Return the frozen or live open review IDs covered by this triage run."""
    46→    active_ids = normalized_issue_id_list(
    47→        ensure_triage_meta(plan).get(_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY)
    48→    )
    49→    if active_ids:
    50→        return set(active_ids)
    51→    has_completed_scan = bool(state.get("last_scan"))
    52→    review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
    53→    if not has_completed_scan and not review_ids:
    54→        return set(plan_review_ids(plan))
    55→    return review_ids
    56→
    57→
    58→def active_triage_issue_ids(
    59→    plan: PlanModel,
    60→    state: StateModel | None = None,
    61→) -> set[str]:
    62→    """Return the frozen review issue set for the current triage run."""
    63→    meta = ensure_triage_meta(plan)
    64→    active_ids = normalized_issue_id_list(meta.get(_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY))
    65→    if active_ids:
    66→        return set(active_ids)
    67→    if state is None:
    68→        return set()
    69→    return coverage_open_ids(plan, state)
    70→
    71→
    72→def live_active_triage_issue_ids(
    73→    plan: PlanModel,
    74→    state: StateModel | None = None,
    75→) -> set[str]:
    76→    """Return frozen triage IDs that are still open review issues in state."""
    77→    frozen_ids = active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
    78→    if state is None or not frozen_ids:
    79→        return frozen_ids
    80→    return frozen_ids & open_review_ids(state)
    81→
    82→
    83→def ensure_active_triage_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> list[str]:
    84→    """Freeze the current triage issue set for validation across stage reruns."""
    85→    meta = ensure_triage_meta(plan)
    86→    active_ids = sorted(coverage_open_ids(plan, state))
    87→    meta[_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY] = active_ids
    88→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY, None)
    89→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY, None)
    90→    return active_ids
    91→
    92→
    93→def clear_active_triage_issue_tracking(meta: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    94→    """Clear frozen triage coverage metadata after successful completion."""
    95→    meta.pop(_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY, None)
    96→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY, None)
    97→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY, None)
    98→
    99→
   100→def undispositioned_triage_issue_ids(
   101→    plan: PlanModel,
   102→    state: StateModel | None = None,
   103→) -> list[str]:
   104→    """Return frozen triage issues still lacking cluster/skip/dismiss coverage."""
   105→    target_ids = live_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   106→    if not target_ids:
   107→        return []
   108→    covered_ids: set[str] = set()
   109→    for cluster in ensure_cluster_map(plan).values():
   110→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   111→            continue
   112→        covered_ids.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   113→    covered_ids.update(
   114→        issue_id for issue_id in ensure_skipped_map(plan) if isinstance(issue_id, str)
   115→    )
   116→    covered_ids.update(
   117→        normalized_issue_id_list(ensure_triage_meta(plan).get("dismissed_ids"))
   118→    )
   119→    # Also check disposition map for auto-skipped entries that have matching
   120→    # skip entries in plan state (belt-and-suspenders with the skipped map check)
   121→    dispositions = ensure_triage_meta(plan).get("issue_dispositions", {})
   122→    for issue_id, disp in dispositions.items():
   123→        if disp.get("decision_source") == "observe_auto" and issue_id in ensure_skipped_map(plan):
   124→            covered_ids.add(issue_id)
   125→    return sorted(issue_id for issue_id in target_ids if issue_id not in covered_ids)
   126→
   127→
   128→def sync_undispositioned_triage_meta(
   129→    plan: PlanModel,
   130→    state: StateModel | None = None,
   131→) -> list[str]:
   132→    """Persist the current undispositioned triage issue set for recovery UX."""
   133→    meta = ensure_triage_meta(plan)
   134→    missing = undispositioned_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   135→    if missing:
   136→        meta[_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY] = missing
   137→        meta[_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY] = len(missing)
   138→    else:
   139→        meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY, None)
   140→        meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY, None)
   141→    return missing
   142→
   143→
   144→def triage_coverage(
   145→    plan: PlanModel,
   146→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   147→) -> tuple[int, int, dict[str, Cluster]]:
   148→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for review issues in triage."""
   149→    clusters = ensure_cluster_map(plan)
   150→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()

    44→
    45→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    46→class PlanImportSyncRequest:
    47→    """Bundle optional inputs for post-import plan synchronization."""
    48→
    49→    state_file: str | Path | None = None
    50→    config: dict | None = None
    51→    import_file: str | None = None
    52→    import_payload: NormalizedReviewImportPayload | None = None
    53→
    54→
    55→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    56→class _ImportSyncInputs:
    57→    has_review_issue_delta: bool
    58→    assessment_keys: frozenset[str]
    59→    covered_ids: tuple[str, ...]
    60→
    61→
    62→@dataclass
    63→class _PlanImportMutations:
    64→    dirty: bool = False
    65→    workflow_injected_ids: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    66→    import_result: ReviewImportSyncResult | None = None
    67→    stale_sync_result: QueueSyncResult | None = None
    68→    auto_cluster_changes: int = 0
    69→
    70→
    71→def _has_postflight_review_work(state: dict, *, policy) -> bool:
    72→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
    73→    if any(
    74→        isinstance(issue, dict)
    75→        and issue.get("status") == "open"
    76→        and issue.get("detector") in {"review", "concerns", "subjective_review"}
    77→        for issue in issues.values()
    78→    ):
    79→        return True
    80→    return bool(policy.stale_ids or policy.under_target_ids)
    81→
    82→
    83→def _has_deferred_disposition_work(plan: dict) -> bool:
    84→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    85→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
    86→        return False
    87→    return any(
    88→        isinstance(entry, dict) and str(entry.get("kind", "temporary")) == "temporary"
    89→        for entry in skipped.values()
    90→    )
    91→
    92→
    93→def _sync_lifecycle_phase_after_import(plan: dict, state: dict, *, policy) -> bool:
    94→    return sync_lifecycle_phase(
    95→        plan,
    96→        has_initial_reviews=bool(policy.unscored_ids),
    97→        has_objective_backlog=bool(policy.has_objective_backlog),
    98→        has_postflight_review=_has_postflight_review_work(state, policy=policy),
    99→        has_postflight_workflow=any(
   100→            item_id in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   101→            for item_id in (
   102→                "workflow::import-scores",
   103→                "workflow::communicate-score",
   104→                "workflow::score-checkpoint",
   105→                "workflow::create-plan",
   106→            )
   107→        ),
   108→        has_triage=any(
   109→            isinstance(item_id, str) and item_id.startswith("triage::")
   110→            for item_id in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   111→        ),
   112→        has_deferred=_has_deferred_disposition_work(plan),
   113→    )[1]
   114→
   115→
   116→def _print_review_import_sync(
   117→    state: dict,
   118→    result: ReviewImportSyncResult,
   119→    *,
   120→    workflow_injected: bool,
   121→) -> None:
   122→    """Print summary of plan changes after review import sync."""
   123→    new_ids = result.new_ids
   124→    stale_pruned = result.stale_pruned_from_queue
   125→    print()
   126→    _print_new_review_items(state, new_ids)
   127→    _print_stale_review_prunes(stale_pruned)
   128→    _print_review_import_footer(result, workflow_injected=workflow_injected)
   129→
   130→
   131→def _print_new_review_items(state: dict, new_ids: list[str]) -> None:
   132→    if not new_ids:
   133→        return
   134→    print(colorize(
   135→        f"  Plan updated: {len(new_ids)} new review issue(s) added to queue.",
   136→        "bold",
   137→    ))
   138→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   139→    for finding_id in sorted(new_ids)[:10]:
   140→        finding = issues.get(finding_id, {})
   141→        print(f"    * [{short_issue_id(finding_id)}] {finding.get('summary', '')}")
   142→    if len(new_ids) > 10:
   143→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 10} more", "dim"))
   144→
   145→
   146→def _print_stale_review_prunes(stale_pruned: list[str]) -> None:
   147→    if not stale_pruned:
   148→        return
   149→    print(colorize(
   150→        f"  Plan updated: {len(stale_pruned)} stale review issue(s) removed from queue.",
   151→        "bold",
   152→    ))
   153→
   154→
   155→def _print_review_import_footer(
   156→    result: ReviewImportSyncResult,
   157→    *,
   158→    workflow_injected: bool,
   159→) -> None:
   160→    print()

   167→    print(colorize("  View newest first:     desloppify plan queue --sort recent", "dim"))
   168→    print(colorize("  View broader backlog:  desloppify backlog", "dim"))
   169→    print()
   170→    print(colorize("  NEXT STEP:", "yellow"))
   171→    print(colorize("    Run:    desloppify next", "yellow"))
   172→    if result.triage_injected and not workflow_injected:

   174→        print(colorize(f"    Claude: {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE}", "dim"))
   175→        print(colorize("    Manual dashboard: desloppify plan triage", "dim"))
   176→    print(colorize(
   177→        "  (Follow the queue in order; score communication and planning come before triage.)",
   178→        "dim",
   179→    ))
   180→
   181→
   182→def _review_delta_present(diff: dict) -> bool:
   183→    return any(
   184→        int(diff.get(key, 0) or 0) > 0
   185→        for key in ("new", "reopened", "auto_resolved")
   186→    )
   187→
   188→
   189→def _print_workflow_injected_message(workflow_injected_ids: list[str]) -> None:
   190→    if not workflow_injected_ids:
   191→        return
   192→    injected_parts = [f"`{workflow_id}`" for workflow_id in workflow_injected_ids]
   193→    print(colorize(
   194→        f"  Plan: {' and '.join(injected_parts)} queued. Run `desloppify next`.",
   195→        "cyan",
   196→    ))

    70→
    71→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    72→    if output:
    73→        try:
    74→            from pathlib import Path
    75→            output_path = Path(output)
    76→            if output_path.exists():
    77→                print(colorize(
    78→                    f"  {output} already exists — refusing to overwrite.",
    79→                    "red",
    80→                ))

1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import enum
     6→import importlib
     7→import logging
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
    10→
    11→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    12→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.refresh_lifecycle import (
    14→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE,
    15→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
    16→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN,
    17→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
    18→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import load_plan
    21→from desloppify.state_scoring import score_snapshot
    22→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import QueueBuildOptions

    24→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import is_subjective_queue_item
    25→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
    26→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.snapshot import coarse_phase_name
    27→from desloppify.engine.planning.queue_policy import build_execution_queue
    28→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress_render import (
    29→    format_plan_delta,
    30→    format_queue_block,
    31→    format_queue_headline,
    32→)
    33→
    34→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    35→
    36→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    37→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import QueueContext
    38→
    39→
    40→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    41→# QueueBreakdown — single source of truth for queue numbers
    42→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    43→
    44→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    45→class QueueBreakdown:
    46→    """All numbers needed to render the standardized queue display."""
    47→
    48→    queue_total: int = 0
    49→    plan_ordered: int = 0
    50→    stale_plan_ordered: int = 0
    51→    skipped: int = 0
    52→    subjective: int = 0
    53→    workflow: int = 0
    54→    lifecycle_phase: str | None = None
    55→    focus_cluster: str | None = None
    56→    focus_cluster_count: int = 0
    57→    focus_cluster_total: int = 0
    58→
    59→    @property
    60→    def objective_actionable(self) -> int:
    61→        """Objective items only — excludes subjective and workflow.
    62→
    63→        This is the count used by :func:`score_display_mode` to decide
    64→        frozen vs live display.  Do not use it directly for gating —
    65→        use ``score_display_mode()`` instead.
    66→        """
    67→        if self.lifecycle_phase in {
    68→            LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN,
    69→            LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
    70→            LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
    71→            LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
    72→        }:
    73→            return 0
    74→        return max(0, self.queue_total - self.subjective - self.workflow)
    75→
    76→
    77→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    78→# ScoreDisplayMode — single decision point for frozen vs live score display
    79→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    80→
    81→class ScoreDisplayMode(enum.Enum):
    82→    """How to display the score given the current plan cycle and queue state.
    83→
    84→    - ``FROZEN``: objective work remains → show frozen plan-start score.
    85→    - ``PHASE_TRANSITION``: objective drained, subjective/workflow remain
    86→      → show live scores + transition banner.
    87→    - ``LIVE``: no active plan cycle or queue fully clear → show live scores.
    88→    """
    89→    FROZEN = "frozen"
    90→    PHASE_TRANSITION = "phase_transition"
    91→    LIVE = "live"
    92→
    93→
    94→def score_display_mode(
    95→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None,
    96→    plan_start_strict: float | None,
    97→) -> ScoreDisplayMode:
    98→    """Determine the score display mode from breakdown + plan-start score.
    99→
   100→    This is the **single source of truth** for the frozen-vs-live decision.
   101→    Every call site (status, scan reveal, next nudges, plan reconcile, plan
   102→    nudge) must use this instead of ad-hoc count checks.
   103→    """
   104→    if plan_start_strict is None:
   105→        return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
   106→    if breakdown is None:
   107→        return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
   108→    if breakdown.lifecycle_phase == LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN:
   109→        return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
   110→    if breakdown.lifecycle_phase == LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE:
   111→        return ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   112→    if breakdown.lifecycle_phase in {
   113→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
   114→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
   115→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
   116→    }:
   117→        return ScoreDisplayMode.PHASE_TRANSITION
   118→    if breakdown.objective_actionable > 0:
   119→        return ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   120→    if breakdown.queue_total > 0:
   121→        return ScoreDisplayMode.PHASE_TRANSITION
   122→    return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
   123→
   124→
   125→def plan_aware_queue_breakdown(
   126→    state: dict,
   127→    plan: dict | None = None,
   128→    *,
   129→    context: QueueContext | None = None,
   130→) -> QueueBreakdown:
   131→    """Build a full :class:`QueueBreakdown` from a single ``build_work_queue`` call.
   132→
   133→    When *context* is provided, its ``plan`` and ``policy`` are forwarded to
   134→    ``build_work_queue`` so the counts agree with the caller's resolution.
   135→    """
   136→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else plan
   137→
   138→    result = build_execution_queue(
   139→        state,
   140→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   141→            status="open",
   142→            count=None,
   143→            plan=effective_plan if context is None else None,
   144→            include_skipped=False,
   145→            context=context,
   146→        ),
   147→    )
   148→
   149→    # Collapse clusters for display-level counting
   150→    items = result.get("items", [])
   151→    snapshot = (
   152→        context.snapshot
   153→        if context is not None
   154→        else queue_context(state, plan=effective_plan).snapshot
   155→    )
   156→    lifecycle_phase = coarse_phase_name(snapshot.phase)
   157→    if effective_plan and not effective_plan.get("active_cluster"):
   158→        items = collapse_clusters(items, effective_plan)
   159→
   160→    queue_total = len(items)
   161→
   162→    # Count subjective and workflow items in the queue.
   163→    # Collapsed clusters whose members are all subjective count as subjective.
   164→    subjective = sum(
   165→        1 for item in items
   166→        if is_subjective_queue_item(item)
   167→    )
   168→    workflow = sum(
   169→        1 for item in items
   170→        if item.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
   171→    )
   172→
   173→    # Plan-derived counts
   174→    plan_ordered = 0
   175→    stale_plan_ordered = 0
   176→    skipped = 0
   177→    if effective_plan:
   178→        skipped = len(effective_plan.get("skipped", {}))
   179→        # plan_ordered = live queue_order items that still exist in the current
   180→        # snapshot; stale_plan_ordered captures dead queue_order entries so the
   181→        # summary does not imply they are still blocking the live queue.
   182→        queue_order = effective_plan.get("queue_order", [])
   183→        skipped_ids = set(effective_plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   184→        current_item_ids = _snapshot_item_ids(snapshot)
   185→        if current_item_ids is None:
   186→            plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
   187→        else:
   188→            plan_ordered = sum(
   189→                1 for fid in queue_order
   190→                if fid not in skipped_ids and fid in current_item_ids
   191→            )
   192→            stale_plan_ordered = sum(
   193→                1 for fid in queue_order
   194→                if fid not in skipped_ids and fid not in current_item_ids
   195→            )
   196→
   197→    # Focus cluster info
   198→    focus_cluster = None
   199→    focus_cluster_count = 0
   200→    focus_cluster_total = 0
   201→    if effective_plan:
   202→        active = effective_plan.get("active_cluster")
   203→        if active:
   204→            focus_cluster = active
   205→            cluster_data = effective_plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
   206→            focus_cluster_total = len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   207→            # Count how many cluster members are still in the queue
   208→            cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   209→            open_issues = {
   210→                fid
   211→                for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
   212→                if f.get("status") == "open"
   213→            }
   214→            focus_cluster_count = len(cluster_member_ids & open_issues)
   215→
   216→    return QueueBreakdown(
   217→        queue_total=queue_total,
   218→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
   219→        stale_plan_ordered=stale_plan_ordered,
   220→        skipped=skipped,
   221→        subjective=subjective,
   222→        workflow=workflow,
   223→        lifecycle_phase=lifecycle_phase,
   224→        focus_cluster=focus_cluster,
   225→        focus_cluster_count=focus_cluster_count,
   226→        focus_cluster_total=focus_cluster_total,
   227→    )
   228→
   229→
   230→def _snapshot_item_ids(snapshot: object) -> set[str] | None:
   231→    """Return all live queue item IDs represented by a snapshot.
   232→
   233→    When tests stub ``snapshot`` with only a ``phase`` attribute, return
   234→    ``None`` so callers can fall back to legacy plan_ordered counting.
   235→    """
   236→    partition_names = (
   237→        "all_objective_items",
   238→        "all_initial_review_items",
   239→        "all_postflight_review_items",
   240→        "all_scan_items",
   241→        "all_postflight_workflow_items",
   242→        "all_postflight_triage_items",
   243→    )
   244→    item_ids: set[str] = set()
   245→    saw_partition = False
   246→    for name in partition_names:
   247→        items = getattr(snapshot, name, None)
   248→        if items is None:
   249→            continue
   250→        saw_partition = True
   251→        for item in items:
   252→            if isinstance(item, dict):
   253→                item_id = item.get("id")
   254→                if isinstance(item_id, str) and item_id:
   255→                    item_ids.add(item_id)
   256→    if not saw_partition:
   257→        return None
   258→    return item_ids
   259→
   260→
   261→def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
   262→    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
   263→    if not plan:
   264→        return None
   265→    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   266→
   267→
   268→def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   269→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   270→    *,
   271→    frozen_strict: float,
   272→    live_score: float | None = None,
   273→) -> None:
   274→    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress.
   275→
   276→    Only call from a ``ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN`` path — assumes objective
   277→    work remains in the queue.
   278→    """
   279→    block = format_queue_block(breakdown, frozen_score=frozen_strict, live_score=live_score)
   280→    print()
   281→    for text, style in block:
   282→        print(colorize(text, style))
   283→    print(colorize(
   284→        "  Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   285→        "dim",
   286→    ))
   287→
   288→
   289→def print_objective_drained_banner(
   290→    frozen_strict: float,
   291→    remaining: int,
   292→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   293→) -> None:
   294→    """Show a phase-transition banner when objective work is drained."""
   295→    kind_labels: list[str] = []
   296→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   297→        kind_labels.append("subjective")
   298→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   299→        kind_labels.append("workflow")
   300→    kind_desc = " + ".join(kind_labels) if kind_labels else "non-objective"
   301→    print(colorize(
   302→        f"\n  Objective queue complete (plan-start was {frozen_strict:.1f})."
   303→        f" {remaining} {kind_desc} item{'s' if remaining != 1 else ''} remain.",
   304→        "cyan",
   305→    ))
   306→    print(colorize(
   307→        "  Run `desloppify next` for remaining work,"
   308→        " then `desloppify scan` to finalize.",
   309→        "dim",
   310→    ))
   311→
   312→
   313→def print_execution_or_reveal(
   314→    state: dict,
   315→    prev,
   316→    plan: dict | None,
   317→) -> None:
   318→    """Context-aware score display using :func:`score_display_mode`."""
   319→    frozen_strict = get_plan_start_strict(plan)
   320→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None
   321→
   322→    if frozen_strict is not None:
   323→        try:
   324→            breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   325→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   326→            _logger.debug("queue breakdown computation skipped", exc_info=True)
   327→
   328→    mode = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict)
   329→
   330→    if mode is ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN:
   331→        print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   332→            breakdown,
   333→            frozen_strict=frozen_strict,
   334→            live_score=score_snapshot(state).strict,
   335→        )
   336→        return
   337→
   338→    # LIVE or PHASE_TRANSITION: show current scores
   339→    score_update_mod = importlib.import_module(
   340→        "desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update"
   341→    )
   342→    score_update_mod.print_score_update(state, prev)
   343→
   344→    if mode is ScoreDisplayMode.PHASE_TRANSITION:
   345→        print_objective_drained_banner(frozen_strict, breakdown.queue_total, breakdown)
   346→
   347→
   348→def show_score_with_plan_context(state: dict, prev) -> None:
   349→    """Load plan (best-effort) and show frozen or live score context.
   350→
   351→    Encapsulates the common load_plan + PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS + reveal
   352→    choreography so command modules don't each repeat it.
   353→    """
   354→    try:
   355→        plan = load_plan()
   356→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   357→        _logger.debug("score display skipped plan-aware context", exc_info=exc)
   358→        plan = None
   359→    print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, plan)
   360→
   361→
   362→__all__ = [
   363→    "QueueBreakdown",
   364→    "ScoreDisplayMode",
   365→    "format_plan_delta",
   366→    "format_queue_block",
   367→    "format_queue_headline",
   368→    "get_plan_start_strict",
   369→    "plan_aware_queue_breakdown",
   370→    "print_execution_or_reveal",
   371→    "print_objective_drained_banner",
   372→    "print_frozen_score_with_queue_context",
   373→    "score_display_mode",
   374→    "show_score_with_plan_context",
   375→]
   376→

    49→def triage_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel, state: dict | None = None) -> str:
    50→    """Return a banner string describing triage status."""
    51→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    52→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    53→    has_triage = any(stage_id in order for stage_id in TRIAGE_IDS)
    54→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    55→    run_hint = (
    56→        f"Run: {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX} "
    57→        f"(or {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE})"
    58→    )
    59→
    60→    if not has_triage:
    61→        undispositioned = meta.get("undispositioned_issue_count", 0)
    62→        if undispositioned:
    63→            return (
    64→                "TRIAGE RECOVERY NEEDED — "
    65→                f"{undispositioned} review issue(s) still need cluster/skip dispositions. "
    66→                f"{run_hint}"
    67→            )
    68→        if meta.get("triage_recommended"):
    69→            return (
    70→                "TRIAGE RECOMMENDED — review issues changed since last triage. "
    71→                f"{run_hint}"
    72→            )
    73→        return ""
    74→
    75→    if state and has_objective_backlog(state, None):
    76→        undispositioned = meta.get("undispositioned_issue_count", 0)
    77→        if undispositioned:
    78→            return (
    79→                "TRIAGE PENDING — "
    80→                f"{undispositioned} review issue(s) still need cluster/skip dispositions after current work. "
    81→                f"{run_hint}"
    82→            )
    83→        return (
    84→            "TRIAGE PENDING — queued and will activate after objective work "
    85→            "is complete."
    86→        )
    87→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    88→    completed = [
    89→        stage
    90→        for stage in ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check")
    91→        if stage in stages
    92→    ]
    93→    if completed:
    94→        return (
    95→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({len(completed)}/5 stages complete) — "
    96→            f"complete all stages to exit. {run_hint}"
    97→        )
    98→    return (
    99→        "TRIAGE MODE — review issues need analysis before fixing. "
   100→        f"{run_hint}"

     3→from __future__ import annotations

    19→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import read_code_snippet
    20→from desloppify.base.enums import canonical_issue_status
    21→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import print_write_error

    28→def write_show_output_file(output_file: str, payload: dict, surfaced_count: int) -> bool:
    29→    """Write serialized show payload to file."""
    30→    try:
    31→        safe_write_text(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
    32→        print(colorize(f"Wrote {surfaced_count} issues to {output_file}", "green"))
    33→    except OSError as exc:
    34→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
    35→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="show output")
    36→        return False
    37→    return True
    38→
    39→
    40→def group_matches_by_file(matches: list[dict]) -> list[tuple[str, list]]:
    41→    """Group issues by file and sort by descending count."""
    42→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
    43→    for issue in matches:
    44→        by_file[issue["file"]].append(issue)
    45→    return sorted(by_file.items(), key=lambda item: -len(item[1]))
    46→
    47→
    48→def _print_single_issue(issue: dict, *, show_code: bool) -> None:
    49→    """Render a single issue to terminal."""
    50→    normalized_status = canonical_issue_status(issue.get("status"))
    51→    status_icon = {
    52→        "open": "○",
    53→        "fixed": "✓",
    54→        "wontfix": "—",
    55→        "false_positive": "✗",
    56→        "auto_resolved": "◌",
    57→        "deferred": "⏸",
    58→        "triaged_out": "△",
    59→    }.get(normalized_status, "?")
    60→    zone = issue.get("zone", "production")

    64→    )

    67→    if detail_parts:
    68→        print(colorize(f"      {' · '.join(detail_parts)}", "dim"))
    69→    if show_code:
    70→        detail_raw = issue.get("detail", {})
    71→        detail = detail_raw if isinstance(detail_raw, dict) else {}
    72→        target_line = (
    73→            detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
    74→        )
    75→        if target_line and issue["file"] not in (".", ""):
    76→            snippet = read_code_snippet(issue["file"], target_line)
    77→            if snippet:
    78→                print(snippet)
    79→    if issue.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2:
    80→        print(
    81→            colorize(
    82→                f"      ⟳ reopened {issue['reopen_count']} times — fix properly or wontfix",
    83→                "red",
    84→            )
    85→        )
    86→    if issue.get("note"):
    87→        print(colorize(f"      note: {issue['note']}", "dim"))
    88→    print(colorize(f"      {issue['id']}", "dim"))
    89→
    90→
    91→def render_issues(
    92→    matches: list[dict],
    93→    *,
    94→    pattern: str,
    95→    status_filter: str,
    96→    show_code: bool,
    97→    top: int,
    98→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int],
    99→    hidden_total: int,
   100→    noise_budget: int,
   101→    global_noise_budget: int,
   102→    budget_warning: str | None,
   103→) -> None:
   104→    """Render grouped issues and rollup summary to terminal."""
   105→    sorted_files = group_matches_by_file(matches)
   106→    print(
   107→        colorize(
   108→            f"\n  {len(matches)} {status_filter} issues matching '{pattern}'\n",
   109→            "bold",
   110→        )
   111→    )
   112→    if budget_warning:
   113→        print(colorize(f"  {budget_warning}\n", "yellow"))
   114→    if hidden_total:
   115→        global_label = (
   116→            f", {global_noise_budget} global" if global_noise_budget > 0 else ""
   117→        )
   118→        hidden_parts = ", ".join(
   119→            f"{det}: +{count}" for det, count in hidden_by_detector.items()
   120→        )

   100→    )
   101→    if decision.action == "defer":

   104→        _print_triage_start_block(decision.reason, deps=resolved_deps)

   115→        detail.setdefault("injected_stage_ids", list(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS))
   116→        services.append_log_entry(

1→"""Queue/stage mutation helpers for triage workflow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix
     9→from desloppify.engine.plan_ops import purge_ids
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import PlanModel
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import TRIAGE_IDS, TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS
    12→
    13→from .plan_state_access import ensure_queue_order, ensure_skipped_map
    14→
    15→STAGE_ORDER = ["observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check"]
    16→
    17→
    18→def has_triage_in_queue(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    19→    """Return True when any triage stage IDs are currently queued."""
    20→    order = set(ensure_queue_order(plan))
    21→    return bool(order & TRIAGE_IDS)
    22→
    23→
    24→def clear_triage_stage_skips(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
    25→    """Remove skipped markers for triage stages before reinjection."""
    26→    skipped = ensure_skipped_map(plan)
    27→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    28→        skipped.pop(sid, None)
    29→
    30→
    31→def inject_triage_stages(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
    32→    """Inject the canonical triage stage IDs at the queue front."""
    33→    order = ensure_queue_order(plan)
    34→    clear_triage_stage_skips(plan)
    35→    remaining = [issue_id for issue_id in order if issue_id not in TRIAGE_IDS]
    36→    order[:] = [*remaining, *TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS]
    37→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(order)
    38→
    39→
    40→def purge_triage_stage(plan: PlanModel, stage_name: str) -> None:
    41→    """Remove one triage stage workflow item from the plan."""
    42→    purge_ids(plan, [f"triage::{stage_name}"])
    43→
    44→
    45→def cascade_clear_dispositions(meta: dict[str, Any], from_stage: str) -> None:
    46→    """Reset issue_dispositions when an earlier stage reruns.
    47→
    48→    - observe rerun: wipe the entire disposition map (verdicts change)
    49→    - reflect rerun: clear decision/target/decision_source from all entries
    50→      (observe verdicts remain, but reflect decisions are outdated)
    51→    """
    52→    dispositions = meta.get("issue_dispositions")
    53→    if not dispositions:
    54→        return
    55→    if from_stage == "observe":
    56→        meta["issue_dispositions"] = {}
    57→    elif from_stage == "reflect":
    58→        for entry in dispositions.values():
    59→            entry.pop("decision", None)
    60→            entry.pop("target", None)
    61→            entry.pop("decision_source", None)
    62→
    63→
    64→def cascade_clear_later_confirmations(
    65→    stages: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    66→    from_stage: str,
    67→) -> list[str]:
    68→    """Clear later-stage confirmations after mutating an earlier stage."""
    69→    try:
    70→        idx = STAGE_ORDER.index(from_stage)
    71→    except ValueError:
    72→        return []
    73→    cleared: list[str] = []
    74→    for later in STAGE_ORDER[idx + 1:]:
    75→        if later in stages and stages[later].get("confirmed_at"):
    76→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    77→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    78→            cleared.append(later)
    79→    return cleared
    80→
    81→
    82→def print_cascade_clear_feedback(
    83→    cleared: list[str],
    84→    stages: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    85→) -> None:
    86→    """Render confirmation-clearing feedback after stage rewrites."""
    87→    if not cleared:
    88→        return
    89→    print(colorize(f"  Cleared confirmations on: {', '.join(cleared)}", "yellow"))
    90→    next_unconfirmed = next(
    91→        (
    92→            stage
    93→            for stage in STAGE_ORDER
    94→            if stage in stages and not stages[stage].get("confirmed_at")
    95→        ),
    96→        None,
    97→    )
    98→    if next_unconfirmed:
    99→        print(
   100→            colorize(
   101→                f"  Re-confirm with: desloppify plan triage --confirm {next_unconfirmed}",
   102→                "dim",
   103→            )
   104→        )
   105→
   106→
   107→__all__ = [
   108→    "STAGE_ORDER",
   109→    "cascade_clear_dispositions",
   110→    "cascade_clear_later_confirmations",
   111→    "has_triage_in_queue",
   112→    "inject_triage_stages",
   113→    "print_cascade_clear_feedback",
   114→    "purge_triage_stage",
   115→]
   116→

## Summary of Triage Completion and Validation Systems

I've thoroughly explored the triage stage completion and validation systems in the desloppify project. Here are the critical findings:

### 1. **Stage Prerequisites Definition** (`stage_policy.py`, lines 51-79)

The canonical prerequisites are defined in `_STAGE_PREREQUISITES`:

```python
_STAGE_PREREQUISITES = {
    "organize": (
        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
        StagePrerequisite("reflect"),
    ),
    "enrich": (
        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
        StagePrerequisite("reflect"),
        StagePrerequisite("organize"),
    ),
    "sense-check": (
        StagePrerequisite("enrich", require_confirmation=True),
    ),
    "complete:organize": (
        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
        StagePrerequisite("organize"),
    ),
    "complete:enrich": (
        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
        StagePrerequisite("organize"),
        StagePrerequisite("enrich"),
    ),
    "complete:sense-check": (
        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
        StagePrerequisite("organize"),
        StagePrerequisite("enrich"),
        StagePrerequisite("sense-check"),
    ),
}
```

**Critical Issue #1:** The prerequisites for **enrich stage** require `organize` to be completed, BUT the display/validation logic in `display/primitives.py` line 28 and `completion_stages.py` uses `TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES` from `playbook.py`, which defines:

```python
("enrich", {"organize"}),  # enrich depends on organize
```

However, **there's a mismatch in what's checked**: `_STAGE_PREREQUISITES["enrich"]` requires confirmation (implicit), but the `enrich.py` stage (line 70-97) has a function `_require_confirmed_organize_stage()` that explicitly checks for `confirmed_at` on the organize stage. This is **stricter** than what `_STAGE_PREREQUISITES` enforces.

### 2. **How "Current" Stage is Determined** (`display/primitives.py`, lines 18-30)

The display logic determines which stage is "current":

```python
def print_stage_progress(stages: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> None:
    for stage_name, label in TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS:
        if stage_name in stages:
            if stages[stage_name].get("confirmed_at"):
                print(colorize(f"    ✓ {label} (confirmed)", "green"))
            else:
                print(colorize(f"    ✓ {label} (needs confirm)", "yellow"))
        elif TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES[stage_name].issubset(stages):
            print(colorize(f"    → {label} (current)", "yellow"))  # LINE 28
        else:
            print(colorize(f"    ○ {label}", "dim"))
```

**Critical Issue #2:** A stage is shown as "current" when:
- All its prerequisites exist in `stages` dict (from `TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES`)
- But the prerequisites might not be **confirmed**

This creates a **mismatch situation**: organize can be shown as "current" when observe+reflect exist (regardless of confirmation), but the enrich stage command (lines 239-246 in `enrich.py`) explicitly gates on **confirmed** organize:

```python
if not _require_confirmed_organize_stage(
    plan=plan,
    stages=stages,
    attestation=attestation,
    services=resolved_services,
    deps=resolved_deps,
):
    return
```

### 3. **Organize Stage Gate** (`organize.py`, lines 207-208)

Organize stage uses `_require_reflect_stage_for_organize()` from `validation/core.py` (lines 168-182), which checks:

```python
def _require_reflect_stage_for_organize(stages: dict) -> bool:
    return require_stage_prerequisite(
        stages,
        flow="organize",
        messages={
            "observe": (...),
            "reflect": (...),
        },
    )
```

**This only checks presence, not confirmation** — it uses `missing_stage_prerequisite()` which at line 92 of `stage_policy.py` checks:

```python
if prerequisite.require_confirmation and not stage_record.get("confirmed_at"):
    return prerequisite
```

But the "organize" prerequisites don't have `require_confirmation=True` in the definition. **They should, but they don't.**

### 4. **Enrich Stage Double-Gate** (`enrich.py`, lines 70-97)

```python
def _require_confirmed_organize_stage(
    *,
    plan: dict,
    stages: dict,
    attestation: str | None,
    services: TriageServices,
    deps: EnrichStageDeps,
) -> bool:
    if stages.get("organize", {}).get("confirmed_at"):
        return True
    if not attestation:
        print(deps.colorize("  Cannot enrich: organize stage not confirmed.", "red"))
        ...
        return False
    # ... auto-confirm via attestation
```

**This is stricter than the display logic suggests.** The display shows organize as "current" when prerequisites exist, but enrich won't let you proceed unless organize is explicitly **confirmed**.

### 5. **Completion Flow Redundancy** (`completion.py` lines 153-202)

The completion flow gates on stages in this order:
1. Line 175: `_require_organize_stage_for_complete()` (calls `completion_stages.py` lines 149-181)
2. Line 177-181: Auto-confirm organize
3. Line 185-187: Require enrich (with underspec calculation)
4. Line 189-193: Auto-confirm enrich
5. Line 196-202: Require sense-check and auto-confirm

**Each "require" and "auto-confirm" pair is independent**, but there's calculation duplication:
- `_underspecified_steps(plan)` is computed once at line 184 and shared with both the require (line 186) and confirm (line 191) checks

This is good, but lines in `completion_stages.py` use the same logic independently:
- `_require_enrich_stage_for_complete()` (line 80) calls `missing_stage_prerequisite(stages, flow="complete:enrich")`
- `_auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete()` (lines 102-129) also checks underspecified steps

### 6. **Mismatch: Display vs. Stage Command Consistency**

**File paths involved:**
- Display logic: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/display/primitives.py` (line 28)
- Stage policy: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/stage_policy.py` (lines 51-79)
- Playbook definitions: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/triage/playbook.py` (lines 14-23)
- Enrich gate: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stages/enrich.py` (lines 70-97)

**The Problem:** 
- `TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES` says organize depends on reflect (not on confirmation)
- Display shows enrich as "current" when organize exists + prerequisites (line 28)
- But enrich command requires organize to be **confirmed** (enrich.py line 78)
- This creates a UX inconsistency: organize appears as "current" but enrich won't run

### 7. **Files with Duplicated or Inconsistent Logic**

1. **`validation/stage_policy.py`** - Canonical prerequisites, but `_STAGE_PREREQUISITES["organize"]` lacks `require_confirmation=True`
2. **`validation/enrich_checks.py`** (line 58-77) - `_require_organize_stage_for_enrich()` only checks presence via `require_prerequisite()`
3. **`stages/enrich.py`** (lines 70-97) - `_require_confirmed_organize_stage()` explicitly checks confirmation
4. **`display/primitives.py`** (line 28) - Uses `TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES` to show "current", which doesn't account for confirmation
5. **`validation/completion_stages.py`** - `_require_enrich_stage_for_complete()` and `_auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete()` both check underspecified steps independently

### **Root Cause**

The inconsistency stems from two sources of truth for stage progression:

1. **`TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES` in playbook.py** - Simple set-based dependencies, used for "current" display
2. **`_STAGE_PREREQUISITES` in stage_policy.py** - More detailed, with `require_confirmation` flags, but not used by display logic

And a **third location** that diverges:
- **`enrich.py` line 78** - Explicitly requires confirmed organize, bypassing the prerequisite framework

This means the display tells users "organize is current" but the enrich command says "organize must be confirmed first" — a disconnect that confuses the workflow.

## Comprehensive Analysis: Triage Coverage and Queue State Modeling in Desloppify

I've completed a thorough exploration of how triage coverage and queue state are modeled across the desloppify project. Here are my findings:

---

### 1. **ACTIVE TRIAGE ISSUE IDS: How Coverage Gets Frozen**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/review_coverage.py`

The active triage issue set is **frozen and persisted** to enable consistent validation across stage reruns:

- **`active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)`** (lines 58-69): Returns the frozen review issue set by checking `epic_triage_meta["active_triage_issue_ids"]`. If not present, computes from `coverage_open_ids()`.

- **`ensure_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)`** (lines 83-90): **Freezes** the current open review IDs at triage start:
  ```python
  active_ids = sorted(coverage_open_ids(plan, state))
  meta[_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY] = active_ids
  ```
  This ensures all stages operate on the same set, preventing inconsistency if new issues appear mid-triage.

- **`live_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)`** (lines 72-80): Returns frozen IDs that **still exist** as open review issues in the current state. This allows pruning of resolved issues while keeping the triage boundary fixed.

- **`undispositioned_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)`** (lines 100-125): Identifies frozen issues lacking cluster/skip/dismiss coverage by comparing against:
  - Manual clusters (excluding `"auto"` clusters)
  - Skipped map entries
  - Dismissed IDs
  - Auto-skipped entries in disposition map

---

### 2. **QUEUE ORDER, SKIPPED MAP, AND TRIAGE META: State Access Layer**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/plan_state_access.py`

This is the **single abstraction layer** for plan state mutation:

- **`ensure_queue_order(plan)`** (lines 10-17): Lazily creates `plan["queue_order"]` list if missing. This list stores all prioritized issue/workflow/triage IDs in execution order.

- **`ensure_skipped_map(plan)`** (lines 20-27): Lazily creates `plan["skipped"]` dict. Keys are issue IDs; values contain `kind` ("temporary"/"permanent"/"false_positive"), notes, attestations, and `skipped_at_scan` metadata.

- **`ensure_cluster_map(plan)`** (lines 30-37): Lazily creates `plan["clusters"]` dict. Keys are cluster names; values are `Cluster` TypedDicts with issue_ids, auto flag, status, thesis, direction, etc.

- **`ensure_triage_meta(plan)`** (lines 40-47): Lazily creates `plan["epic_triage_meta"]` dict. This holds:
  - `active_triage_issue_ids`: frozen issue set
  - `triaged_ids`: historically triaged IDs (for stale detection)
  - `undispositioned_issue_ids`: issues lacking coverage
  - `issue_dispositions`: per-issue verdict/decision/reasoning
  - `triage_stages`: stage completion checkpoints
  - And more (see TypedDict in schema)

- **`normalized_issue_id_list(raw_ids)`** (lines 63-67): Type-guard normalizing stored IDs to strings only.

### 3. **DETECTING NEW REVIEW ISSUES: The "N New Issues Not Yet Triaged" Message**

**Primary Source:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/policy/stale.py`

**Display Source:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/guardrails.py` (lines 58-82)

**How new issues are detected:**

```python
# stale.py, lines 147-151
def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    triaged = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
    return open_review_ids(state) - triaged if triaged else set()
```

- **`triaged_ids`** is the **canonical snapshot** set when triage completes. It records all review/concerns IDs that existed at that moment.
- **`open_review_ids(state)`** returns the **current** set of open review/concerns issues from state.
- **Difference** = genuinely new issues that appeared after the last triage.

**Staleness vs. Resolution:** 
- **Triage is stale** if `open_review_ids(state) - triaged_ids` is non-empty (lines 154-166).
- **Resolution is NOT stale**: if current IDs are a strict subset of triaged IDs, the user is just working through the plan—no re-triage needed.

**Message Generation** in `guardrails.py`:

```python
# lines 70-75
if result.new_ids:
    messages.append(
        f"{len(result.new_ids)} new review issue(s) not yet triaged."
        " Run the staged triage runner to incorporate them "
        f"(`{TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX}` or `{TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE}`)."
    )
```

This message is printed via:
- **`print_triage_guardrail_info(state=state, plan=plan)`**: Shows banner when entering `next` or `scan` commands (line 42 in `next/cmd.py`)
- **`require_triage_current_or_exit(...)`**: Hard gate that blocks command execution if triage is stale (lines 97-134)

---

### 4. **HOW TRIAGE COVERAGE IS COMPUTED**

**Key Function:** `undispositioned_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)` (lines 100-125)

A triage issue is **covered** if it appears in ANY of these:

1. **Manual clusters** (non-auto): `cluster_issue_ids(cluster)` yields membership
2. **Skipped map**: any key in `ensure_skipped_map(plan)`
3. **Dismissed IDs**: `ensure_triage_meta(plan).get("dismissed_ids")`
4. **Auto-skipped with disposition**: `issue_dispositions[id]["decision_source"] == "observe_auto"` AND in skipped map (belt-and-suspenders)

**Uncovered = still need cluster/skip/dismiss decision.**

**Persisting for UX Recovery:** `sync_undispositioned_triage_meta(plan, state)` (lines 128-141) writes the current list to:
- `undispositioned_issue_ids`: full list of IDs
- `undispositioned_issue_count`: count only

This allows the UI to show "N more issues still need dispositions" in banners.

---

### 5. **HOW `desloppify plan` AND `desloppify next` RENDER TRIAGE STATE INDEPENDENTLY**

Both commands **derive their own views** of triage state by reading the plan and calling display functions independently:

#### **`desloppify plan` Rendering** (`plan/cmd.py`, lines 166-194)

- **`_cmd_plan_show()`**: Shows living plan status with:
  - Queue line via `_plan_queue_line(state, plan)` → `plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)` (lines 109-117)
  - Skipped counts via `_skip_kind_counts(plan)` (lines 120-133)
  - Cluster counts and focus via `_print_cluster_summary(plan, active=active)` (lines 136-147)
  - Commit tracking via `_print_commit_tracking(plan)` (lines 150-163)

- **`plan_aware_queue_breakdown()`** (`queue_progress.py`, lines 125-227): Builds a **QueueBreakdown** dataclass with:
  - `queue_total`: items from `build_execution_queue()`
  - `plan_ordered`: count of queue_order IDs still live in snapshot
  - `stale_plan_ordered`: count of dead queue_order IDs
  - `skipped`: len(plan["skipped"])
  - `subjective`, `workflow`: queue item counts by kind
  - `lifecycle_phase`: current phase (EXECUTE, TRIAGE, REVIEW, etc.)
  - `focus_cluster`, `focus_cluster_count`, `focus_cluster_total`

#### **`desloppify next` Rendering** (`next/cmd.py`, lines 26-54)

- **`cmd_next()`** orchestrates the full queue display:
  1. Calls `print_triage_guardrail_info(state=state)` to show stale-triage banner (line 42)
  2. Calls `build_and_render_queue()` with `EXECUTION_QUEUE_VIEW` config (lines 43-54)
  3. Uses `build_execution_queue()` from queue_policy.py (not plan queue)

- **`build_and_render_queue()`** (`queue_flow.py`, lines 74-196):
  1. **Loads plan** via `load_plan_fn()` (seam for testing)
  2. **Builds work queue** via `build_work_queue_fn()` = `build_execution_queue(state, options=...)`
  3. **Extracts guardrail messages** via `triage_guardrail_messages(state=state)` to inject into payload
  4. **Renders terminal** with life narrative via `compute_narrative()`
  5. **Writes query payload** with all context for JSON/CLI display

- **Key Difference**: `next` uses `build_execution_queue()` which respects plan **queue_order** priority, while `plan` uses `build_work_queue()` directly for broader counts.

### 6. **HOW TRIAGE STAGES USE COVERAGE AND QUEUE STATE**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/lifecycle.py`

- **`ensure_triage_started(plan, state, services, ...)`** (lines 71-131):
  1. Checks if triage already in queue via `has_triage_in_queue(plan)`
  2. Calls `ensure_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)` to freeze the issue set
  3. Calls `services.save_plan(plan)` to persist frozen state
  4. Returns `TriageStartOutcome` with status: "started", "blocked", or "already_active"

- **`inject_triage_stages(plan)`** (`stage_queue.py`, lines 31-37): **Appends** stage IDs to back of queue (never reorders existing):
  ```python
  remaining = [issue_id for issue_id in order if issue_id not in TRIAGE_IDS]
  order[:] = [*remaining, *TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS]
  ```

- **Cascade clearing** (`stage_queue.py`, lines 45-79): If an earlier stage reruns, clears later confirmations and dispositions to stay consistent.

---

### 7. **HOW NEW REVIEW ISSUES TRIGGER TRIAGE INJECTION**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/triage.py`

- **`sync_triage_needed(plan, state, policy=None)`** (lines 286-352): **Main orchestrator** that handles stage injection based on review issue changes:

  1. **Hash snapshot** of open review IDs: `review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)` (SHA256 of sorted IDs, truncated to 16 chars)
  2. **Compare hashes**: if `current_hash != last_hash` in meta, review issues changed
  3. **If changed**, compute new IDs: `new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)`
  4. **If new IDs found**, decide whether to **defer or inject**:
     - **Defer** if objective backlog still open (mid-cycle guard via `decide_triage_start()`)
     - **Inject** stages if backlog clear or escalation threshold hit
  5. **If no new IDs but hash changed**, just update the hash (user resolved issues without re-triage needed)

- **Defer/Inject Logic** (`_defer_or_inject_triage()`, lines 172-221):
  - Calls `decide_triage_start()` with `explicit_start=False` (auto-detection mode)
  - Returns "defer" → sets `meta["triage_recommended"] = True` (UI shows non-blocking banner)
  - Returns "accept" → injects stages immediately via `_inject_triage_result()`

- **Escalation** (`defer_policy.py`): If deferral repeats over multiple scans/days, triage escalates and stages are force-injected.

### 8. **TRIAGE INPUT COLLECTION AND WHAT DATA FLOWS TO STAGES**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/triage/prompt.py` (lines 123-154)

```python
def collect_triage_input(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> TriageInput:
    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
    current_review_ids = set(open_review.keys())
    new_since = current_review_ids - triaged_ids
    resolved_since = triaged_ids - current_review_ids
    
    return TriageInput(
        open_issues=open_review,           # current review/concerns
        mechanical_issues=open_mechanical, # context only
        existing_clusters=dict(epics),     # existing manual clusters
        new_since_last=new_since,          # IDs new since last triage
        resolved_since_last=resolved_since,# IDs resolved since last
        previously_dismissed=dismissed_ids,# from prior triage
        ...
    )
```

**Data passed to LLM prompt:**
- Existing clusters (for context/updates)
- New issues since last triage (for incorporation)
- Resolved issues (for recurrence detection)
- Completed clusters (for context)
- Recurring dimension patterns

### 9. **TRIAGE PHASE BANNER (What Shows in `next` Output)**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/plan_triage.py` (lines 49-101)

```python
def triage_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel, state: dict | None = None) -> str:
    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    
    if not has_triage:
        undispositioned = meta.get("undispositioned_issue_count", 0)
        if undispositioned:
            return f"TRIAGE RECOVERY NEEDED — {undispositioned} review issue(s)..."
        if meta.get("triage_recommended"):
            return "TRIAGE RECOMMENDED — review issues changed since last triage..."
    
    if state and has_objective_backlog(state, None):
        if undispositioned:
            return f"TRIAGE PENDING — {undispositioned} review issue(s) still need..."
        return "TRIAGE PENDING — queued and will activate after objective work..."
    
    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    completed = [s for s in ("observe",...) if s in stages]
    if completed:
        return f"TRIAGE MODE ({len(completed)}/5 stages complete)..."
```

This banner appears in `next` output and is built from **meta-derived state**, not live queue state.

### 10. **WHERE THE QUEUE ORDER ACTUALLY COMES FROM**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/planning/queue_policy.py` (lines 56-72)

```python
def build_execution_queue(state: StateModel, *, options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None) -> WorkQueueResult:
    ctx = options.context or queue_context(state, plan=_queue_plan_from_options(options), ...)
    return _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
        state,
        options=replace(options, context=ctx),
        visibility=QueueVisibility.EXECUTION,
    )
```

- **`queue_context(state, plan=...)`** creates a **QueueContext** that holds:
  - The plan's `queue_order` list
  - Snapshot of current state issues
  - Visibility policy (EXECUTION vs BACKLOG)

- **`_build_work_queue_with_visibility(...)`** builds the actual queue respecting the plan's ordering preference.

- **Different from `plan show`**: which calls `plan_aware_queue_breakdown()` that ALSO honors the plan but provides different counts.

### 11. **SCHEMA DEFINITION: Where Triage State Lives**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/schema/__init__.py` (lines 177-197)

```python
class EpicTriageMeta(TypedDict, total=False):
    triaged_ids: list[str]              # snapshot when triage completed
    active_triage_issue_ids: list[str]  # FROZEN set for current triage cycle
    dismissed_ids: list[str]
    undispositioned_issue_ids: list[str]
    undispositioned_issue_count: int
    issue_snapshot_hash: str            # SHA256 of sorted open review IDs
    triage_stages: dict[str, TriageStagePayload]
    issue_dispositions: dict[str, IssueDisposition]
    triage_force_visible: bool
    triage_defer_state: dict[str, Any]
    triage_recommended: bool            # UI banner for auto-deferred triage
```

## Summary of Architecture

| **Concept** | **Where** | **Key Function** |
|-------------|-----------|------------------|
| **Frozen issue set** | `epic_triage_meta["active_triage_issue_ids"]` | `ensure_active_triage_issue_ids()`, `active_triage_issue_ids()` |
| **Covered detection** | `undispositioned_triage_issue_ids()` | Checks clusters, skipped, dismissed, dispositions |
| **New issue detection** | `open_review_ids(state) - triaged_ids` | `compute_new_issue_ids()` via hash diff |
| **Message generation** | `triage_guardrail_messages()` | Formats "{N} new issue(s) not yet triaged" |
| **Queue state** | `plan["queue_order"]`, `plan["skipped"]`, `plan["clusters"]` | Accessed via `ensure_*` functions in plan_state_access.py |
| **Plan rendering** | `plan show` → `_plan_queue_line()` | `plan_aware_queue_breakdown()` for counts |
| **Next rendering** | `next` → `build_and_render_queue()` | `build_execution_queue()` respects plan order |
| **Triage lifecycle** | `lifecycle.py`, `stage_queue.py` | `ensure_triage_started()`, `inject_triage_stages()` |
| **Sync after scan** | `sync_triage_needed()` | Auto-injects or defers based on review delta |
| **Triage phase UI** | `triage_phase_banner()` | Renders recovery/recommended/pending/mode status |

---

### Key Insights

1. **Dual Views**: `plan` and `next` **independently** compute their queue breakdowns—they don't share a single view object. Both call their own builders.

2. **Frozen Triage Boundary**: `active_triage_issue_ids` is explicitly **frozen at triage start** to ensure all 6 stages see the same issue set, preventing mid-triage inconsistency.

3. **Snapshot Hash**: Review issue staleness is detected via **SHA256 hash** of sorted open review ID lists, allowing deterministic change detection across commands.

4. **No Backward Compat for APIs**: Plan state is mutated via type-guarded `ensure_*()` functions that create missing keys on demand; there's no versioning shim—data migrations handle format upgrades.

5. **Cascading Clarity**: When triage stages rerun, earlier stage changes cascade to clear later confirmations, keeping the disposition ledger coherent.

6. **Deferred Injection**: New review issues don't immediately inject triage stages if objective work remains—they're deferred with `triage_recommended` flag until backlog drains or escalation threshold hits.

## Comprehensive Exploration Report: Triage Dashboard & Stage Progression Logic

Based on my thorough exploration of the desloppify codebase, I've identified the key architectural components, stage progression logic, and potential duplication points. Here's what I found:

---

### 1. **How "Current Stage" is Determined in Display Rendering**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/display/primitives.py` (lines 18-51)

The `print_stage_progress()` function determines and renders the current stage using a **dependency-checking algorithm**:

```python
for stage_name, label in TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS:
    if stage_name in stages:
        if stages[stage_name].get("confirmed_at"):
            print(f"    ✓ {label} (confirmed)", "green")
        else:
            print(f"    ✓ {label} (needs confirm)", "yellow")
    elif TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES[stage_name].issubset(stages):
        print(f"    → {label} (current)", "yellow")  # KEY LINE: current stage
    else:
        print(f"    ○ {label}", "dim")
```

**The algorithm:**
- If a stage exists in `stages` dict: show as completed/confirmed
- **Else if** all of its upstream dependencies are complete: mark as **"(current)"** — this is the next stage to work on
- Else: show as not yet available

**Stage Dependencies** (from `playbook.py`):
- `observe` → no deps (always first)
- `reflect` → depends on `observe`
- `organize` → depends on `reflect`
- `enrich` → depends on `organize`
- `sense-check` → depends on `enrich`
- `commit` → depends on `sense-check`

---

### 2. **Stage Readiness/Gating Logic — Spread Across Multiple Files**

**Stage prerequisites validation** (NOT centralized):

| File | Purpose | Key Function |
|------|---------|--------------|
| `/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/stage_policy.py` | General prerequisite checking | `missing_stage_prerequisite()`, `require_prerequisite()` |
| `/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/completion_stages.py` | Completion workflow gates | `_require_organize_stage_for_complete()`, `_require_enrich_stage_for_complete()`, `_require_sense_check_stage_for_complete()` |
| `/app/commands/plan/triage/display/layout.py` | Guidance rendering based on stage | `print_action_guidance()` — routes to `_print_completed_guidance()`, `_print_retriage_guidance()`, or `_print_in_progress_guidance()` |
| `/app/commands/plan/triage/stage_queue.py` | Queue mutation & confirmation cascading | `inject_triage_stages()`, `cascade_clear_later_confirmations()` |

**Key discovery:** Stage gating is **replicated across multiple locations**:
1. **`stage_policy.py`**: Defines `_STAGE_PREREQUISITES` dict (lines 51-79) with explicit upstream requirements
2. **`completion_stages.py`**: Reimplements prerequisite checking for `--complete` flow
3. **`playbook.py`**: Defines `TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES` dict separately (line 23)

This creates **two sources of truth** for stage sequencing:
- `_STAGE_PREREQUISITES` (stage_policy.py) — used by `missing_stage_prerequisite()`
- `TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES` (playbook.py) — used by display/synthetic queue items

---

### 3. **How `desloppify next` Determines Triage Blockers vs Regular Queue Items**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/synthetic.py` (lines 110-177)

The `build_triage_stage_items()` function builds synthetic queue items for triage stages and **explicitly computes blockers**:

```python
def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    # ... extract confirmed and unconfirmed stages ...
    for name, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
        if name not in present_names:
            continue
        if name in confirmed:
            continue
        
        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
        blocked_by = sorted(
            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps 
            if dep in present_names and dep not in confirmed
        )
        
        item: WorkQueueItem = {
            "id": sid,
            "blocked_by": blocked_by,          # <-- propagated to queue
            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
            # ...
        }
```

**Queue integration flow:**
1. `cmd_next()` calls `build_work_queue` → `build_execution_queue()` (queue_policy.py)
2. `build_execution_queue()` uses `QueueVisibility.EXECUTION` + stage context
3. Synthetic items are merged into the queue via `build_triage_stage_items()`
4. Items with `blocked_by` list are rendered with blocker information in terminal output

---

### 4. **Queue Count Rendering — Different Sources Across Commands**

**Three separate counting mechanisms:**

#### A. **`plan_aware_queue_breakdown()` in `queue_progress.py`** (lines 125-227)
Used by: `desloppify plan`, `desloppify next` (indirectly via guardrails)

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class QueueBreakdown:
    queue_total: int = 0
    plan_ordered: int = 0          # Live queue_order items still in snapshot
    stale_plan_ordered: int = 0    # Dead queue_order entries
    skipped: int = 0
    subjective: int = 0
    workflow: int = 0
```

**Counting logic:**
- Builds execution queue, counts items
- Separates plan-ordered from subjective/workflow
- Distinguishes "stale" (dead) plan entries from live ones
- Used by `score_display_mode()` to decide frozen vs live score

#### B. **`triage_coverage()` in `review_coverage.py`** (lines 144-165+)
Used by: Triage dashboard, stage progression tracking

```python
def triage_coverage(
    plan: PlanModel,
    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
) -> tuple[int, int, dict[str, Cluster]]:
    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for review issues in triage."""
```

Returns tuple of `(organized_count, total_count, clusters)` based on:
- Issues in manual (non-auto) clusters
- Issues in skipped map
- Issues in dismissed_ids

#### C. **`undispositioned_triage_issue_ids()` in `review_coverage.py`** (lines 100-125)
Used by: Recovery workflows, triage meta sync

```python
def undispositioned_triage_issue_ids(
    plan: PlanModel,
    state: StateModel | None = None,
) -> list[str]:
    """Return frozen triage issues still lacking cluster/skip/dismiss coverage."""
```

Checks three disposition sources:
1. Manual cluster membership
2. Skipped map
3. Dismissed IDs

**Problem:** These three mechanisms compute overlapping metrics with **different inclusion rules**, creating potential for divergence.

---

### 5. **Key Duplication & Inconsistency Points Found**

#### **Issue 1: Dual Stage-Dependency Definitions**
- **`playbook.py` line 23**: `TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES` (used by display/synthetic items)
- **`stage_policy.py` line 51-79**: `_STAGE_PREREQUISITES` (used by confirmation flow)
- Both define the same relationships but are separate structures

**Risk:** If one is updated without the other, prerequisites won't match dependencies.

#### **Issue 2: Multiple Readiness/Gate Checks**
- **`stage_policy.py`**: Generic `missing_stage_prerequisite()` for any flow
- **`completion_stages.py`**: Duplicate checks for `complete` workflow with slightly different messages
- **`layout.py`**: Ad-hoc checks via `_print_in_progress_guidance()` (lines 118-201)

**Risk:** Different code paths may reach different conclusions about what's "blocked."

#### **Issue 3: Coverage Counting Fragmentation**
Three independent functions compute overlapping metrics:
- `plan_aware_queue_breakdown()` → counts queue items, plan_ordered, stale
- `triage_coverage()` → counts organized vs total for review issues
- `undispositioned_triage_issue_ids()` → counts unassigned triage issues

These don't cross-validate. A bug in one won't be caught by the others.

#### **Issue 4: Stage Confirmation Tracking**
- **`stage_policy.py`**: Checks `confirmed_at` field to determine confirmation
- **`synthetic.py`**: Uses `confirmed_triage_stage_names()` helper to identify confirmed stages
- **`primitives.py`**: Checks `stages[name].get("confirmed_at")` directly

All three are compatible but represent the same logic in three places.

#### **Issue 5: Action Guidance Routing Mismatch**
- **`layout.py` lines 183-201**: `print_action_guidance()` determines what to show based on presence of stages in `stages` dict
- But the actual "current stage" logic is in `primitives.py` with the dependency check
- If display logic and actual progression logic diverge, users see mismatched guidance

---

### 6. **Data Source Divergence**

When determining if triage is "blocked" or "in progress":

| Source | How It Checks | What It Sees |
|--------|---------------|-------------|
| **Dashboard rendering** | Looks at `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["triage_stages"]` | Only what's been explicitly recorded |
| **Queue blockers** | Uses `TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES` + presence in `present_names` | Synthetic view of what *should* be queued |
| **Coverage tracking** | Calls `coverage_open_ids()` which may use frozen `active_triage_issue_ids` | Potentially stale frozen set |
| **Guardrails** | Calls `plan_aware_queue_breakdown()` | Live queue snapshot |

These sources can give different answers if:
- Stages are recorded but not confirmed (should they block?)
- New review issues arrive mid-triage (do they count toward coverage?)
- Queue is modified outside triage workflow

---

**Core Architecture:**

- `/engine/_plan/constants.py` — Queue IDs & stage ordering

- `/app/commands/plan/triage/display/primitives.py` — Current stage determination (line 27-28)
