All paths traced and documented. The duplication is minimal but strategically important — the stage dependency graph is defined twice, and completion-workflow gates duplicate prerequisite logic. Queue counts are fragmented across three independent functions that don't cross-validate.

> DEVELOPER

Design an implementation plan for holistic triage reconciliation in the desloppify project.

## Problem Statement

The triage workflow has multiple independent state representations that drift apart, causing contradictory UX:

1. **Stage progression**: Two sources of truth — `TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES` (playbook.py, used by display) and `_STAGE_PREREQUISITES` (stage_policy.py, used by commands). Display shows organize as "current" based on presence-only, but enrich command requires confirmed organize.

2. **Coverage counting**: Three independent functions compute overlapping metrics — `plan_aware_queue_breakdown()`, `triage_coverage()`, `undispositioned_triage_issue_ids()` — each from different inputs.

3. **New-issue vs active-cycle confusion**: "N new review issues not yet triaged" can appear for issues already in the active triage cycle.

4. **Queue messaging divergence**: `plan`, `next`, and `show review` independently compute their views of triage state.

## Current Architecture (from exploration)

### Stage Dependencies (two definitions)
- `playbook.py` line 23: `TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES` — simple set-based, used by display's `print_stage_progress()`
- `stage_policy.py` lines 51-79: `_STAGE_PREREQUISITES` — richer (with `require_confirmation` flag), used by command gating

### Display "current stage" logic (primitives.py line 28)
```python
elif TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES[stage_name].issubset(stages):
    print(f"    → {label} (current)", "yellow")
```
This checks presence only, not confirmation. But enrich.py explicitly requires confirmed organize (line 78).

### Coverage computation
- `undispositioned_triage_issue_ids()` in review_coverage.py — checks clusters, skipped, dismissed, dispositions
- `triage_coverage()` in review_coverage.py — counts organized/total for display
- `plan_aware_queue_breakdown()` in queue_progress.py — counts queue items, plan_ordered, stale
- `sync_undispositioned_triage_meta()` — persists for recovery UX

### New-issue detection
- `compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)` in stale.py: `open_review_ids(state) - triaged_ids`
- `triaged_ids` is set when triage COMPLETES, not when it starts
- Mid-cycle, `active_triage_issue_ids` holds the frozen set, but guardrails check against `triaged_ids`

### Queue rendering
- `next/cmd.py`: calls `build_and_render_queue()` → `build_execution_queue()`
- `plan/cmd.py`: calls `plan_aware_queue_breakdown()` independently
- `triage_phase_banner()` in engine/plan_triage.py: yet another independent derivation

### Key files
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/triage/playbook.py` — stage labels & dependency graph
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/stage_policy.py` — prerequisite validation
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/display/primitives.py` — current stage display
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/display/layout.py` — action guidance routing
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/review_coverage.py` — coverage computation
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/queue_progress.py` — QueueBreakdown
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/guardrails.py` — triage guardrail messages
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/policy/stale.py` — new-issue detection
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/triage.py` — auto-injection
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/synthetic.py` — triage blocker computation
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/completion_stages.py` — completion gates
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stages/enrich.py` — enrich stage (has own confirmed-organize check)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/lifecycle.py` — triage start/freeze
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/plan_triage.py` — public facade + triage_phase_banner

### Recent work already done
The `issue_dispositions` unified map was just implemented (Steps 1-5 in a prior plan). This gives us:
- `IssueDisposition` TypedDict in schema
- Observe writes verdicts → disposition map
- Observe confirmation auto-skips false-positive/exaggerated
- Reflect writes decisions → disposition map
- `validate_organize_against_dispositions()` exists
- `cascade_clear_dispositions()` exists

## Design Constraints

1. **No backward compat for import paths/APIs** — internal tool, not library
2. **Keep data migration code** for `.desloppify/` state/plan JSON formats
3. Prefer fixing ownership and reconciliation over redesigning the workflow
4. Keep the existing 5-stage triage flow (observe → reflect → organize → enrich → sense-check)
5. `plan["clusters"]` and `plan["skipped"]` remain authoritative for "what is actually true now"
6. PLAN_VERSION stays 8 (additive fields)

## What I need from you

Design a concrete implementation plan that:

1. **Introduces a `TriageSnapshot` model** — a single derived data class built once per command that answers all triage-status questions. Define what fields it needs and where it's built.

2. **Unifies stage progression** — one function that both display and commands use to determine current stage, blocked reason, and next action. Eliminate the dual dependency definitions.

3. **Separates triage-cycle coverage from live review backlog** — make the frozen set authoritative during a cycle, stop "new issues not yet triaged" for active-cycle issues.

4. **Makes display/commands/guardrails all consume the same snapshot** — identify exactly which call sites need to change.

Break this into implementable steps (3-5 steps), identify the files to modify, and note what tests need to change. Keep it concrete — specify function signatures, not just concepts.

    83→def triage_run_stages_command(
    84→    *,
    85→    runner: str = "codex",
    86→    only_stages: str | tuple[str, ...] | list[str] | None = None,
    87→) -> str:
    88→    """Return the canonical staged triage runner command."""
    89→    resolved_runner = str(runner).strip().lower()
    90→    if resolved_runner not in TRIAGE_RUNNERS:
    91→        supported = ", ".join(TRIAGE_RUNNERS)
    92→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported triage runner: {runner!r}. Valid: {supported}")
    93→
    94→    command = f"desloppify plan triage --run-stages --runner {resolved_runner}"
    95→    if only_stages is None:
    96→        return command
    97→
    98→    if isinstance(only_stages, str):
    99→        stages = [only_stages]
   100→    else:
   101→        stages = [str(stage).strip().lower() for stage in only_stages if str(stage).strip()]
   102→
   103→    invalid = [stage for stage in stages if stage not in _RUNNER_STAGE_NAMES]
   104→    if invalid:
   105→        supported = ", ".join(sorted(_RUNNER_STAGE_NAMES))
   106→        bad = ", ".join(sorted(set(invalid)))
   107→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported triage stage(s): {bad}. Valid: {supported}")
   108→
   109→    return f"{command} --only-stages {','.join(stages)}"
   110→
   111→
   112→def triage_runner_commands(
   113→    *,
   114→    only_stages: str | tuple[str, ...] | list[str] | None = None,
   115→) -> tuple[tuple[str, str], tuple[str, str]]:
   116→    """Return the preferred staged-runner commands for Codex and Claude."""
   117→    return (
   118→        ("Codex", triage_run_stages_command(runner="codex", only_stages=only_stages)),
   119→        ("Claude", triage_run_stages_command(runner="claude", only_stages=only_stages)),
   120→    )
   121→
   122→
   123→def triage_manual_stage_command(stage: str) -> str:
   124→    """Return the manual fallback command for a triage stage."""
   125→    resolved_stage = str(stage).strip().lower()
   126→    if resolved_stage not in _MANUAL_STAGE_COMMANDS:
   127→        supported = ", ".join(sorted(_MANUAL_STAGE_COMMANDS))
   128→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported triage stage: {stage!r}. Valid: {supported}")

    82→def missing_stage_prerequisite(
    83→    stages: dict,
    84→    *,
    85→    flow: str,
    86→) -> StagePrerequisite | None:
    87→    """Return the first missing upstream stage required by a triage flow."""
    88→    for prerequisite in _STAGE_PREREQUISITES.get(flow, ()):
    89→        stage_record = stages.get(prerequisite.stage_name)
    90→        if stage_record is None:
    91→            return prerequisite
    92→        if prerequisite.require_confirmation and not stage_record.get("confirmed_at"):
    93→            return prerequisite
    94→    return None

    96→
    97→def require_prerequisite(
    98→    stages: dict,
    99→    *,
   100→    flow: str,
   101→    messages: dict[str, tuple[str, str]],
   102→) -> bool:
   103→    """Print consistent prerequisite guidance for one triage flow."""
   104→    missing = missing_stage_prerequisite(stages, flow=flow)
   105→    if missing is None:
   106→        return True

   108→    print(colorize(blocked_heading, "red"))
   109→    print(colorize(command_hint, "dim"))

   113→def confirm_stage(
   114→    *,
   115→    plan: dict,
   116→    stage_record: dict,
   117→    attestation: str | None,
   118→    request: AutoConfirmStageRequest,
   119→    save_plan_fn=None,
   120→    utc_now_fn=None,
   121→) -> bool:
   122→    """Auto-confirm one recorded stage with a validated attestation."""
   123→    resolved_save_plan = save_plan_fn or save_plan
   124→    resolved_utc_now = utc_now_fn or utc_now
   125→    if stage_record.get("confirmed_at"):
   126→        return True
   127→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   128→        print(colorize(f"  {request.blocked_heading}", "red"))
   129→        print(colorize(f"  Run: {request.confirm_cmd}", "dim"))
   130→        print(colorize(f"  {request.inline_hint}", "dim"))
   131→        return False
   132→
   133→    confirmed_text = attestation.strip()
   134→    validation_err = validate_attestation(
   135→        confirmed_text,

   137→        dimensions=request.dimensions,
   138→        cluster_names=request.cluster_names,
   139→    )
   140→    if validation_err:
   141→        print(colorize(f"  {validation_err}", "red"))
   142→        return False
   143→
   144→    stage_record["confirmed_at"] = resolved_utc_now()
   145→    stage_record["confirmed_text"] = confirmed_text
   146→    resolved_save_plan(plan)
   147→    print(colorize(f"  ✓ {request.stage_label} auto-confirmed via --attestation.", "green"))
   148→    return True

   182→def auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize(
   183→    *,
   184→    args: argparse.Namespace,

   212→    reflect_stage["duplicate_issue_ids"] = duplicate_ids
   213→
   214→    recurring = resolved_deps.detect_recurring_patterns_fn(
   215→        triage_input.open_issues,
   216→        triage_input.resolved_issues,
   217→    )
   218→    _by_dim, observe_dims = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   219→    reflect_dims = sorted(set((list(recurring.keys()) if recurring else []) + observe_dims))
   220→    reflect_clusters = [
   221→        name for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items() if not cluster.get("auto")
   222→    ]
   223→    return confirm_stage(
   224→        plan=plan,
   225→        stage_record=reflect_stage,
   226→        attestation=attestation,
   227→        request=AutoConfirmStageRequest(
   228→            stage_name="reflect",
   229→            stage_label="Reflect",
   230→            blocked_heading="Cannot organize: reflect stage not confirmed.",
   231→            confirm_cmd="desloppify plan triage --confirm reflect",
   232→            inline_hint="Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm reflect inline.",
   233→            dimensions=reflect_dims,
   234→            cluster_names=reflect_clusters,
   235→        ),
   236→        save_plan_fn=resolved_deps.save_plan_fn,
   237→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   238→    )
   239→
   240→
   241→__all__ = [
   242→    "AutoConfirmStageRequest",
   243→    "ReflectAutoConfirmDeps",
   244→    "StagePrerequisite",
   245→    "auto_confirm_observe_if_attested",
   246→    "auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize",
   247→    "confirm_stage",
   248→    "missing_stage_prerequisite",
   249→    "require_prerequisite",
   250→]
   251→

1→"""Shared triage display primitives used by multiple rendering modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import (
     7→    TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT,
     8→    TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
     9→    TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS,
    10→    triage_manual_stage_command,
    11→    triage_runner_commands,
    12→)
    13→
    14→from ..review_coverage import manual_clusters_with_issues
    15→from ..stages.helpers import unenriched_clusters
    16→
    17→
    18→def print_stage_progress(stages: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> None:
    19→    """Print the triage-stage progress indicator."""
    20→    print(colorize("  Stages:", "dim"))
    21→    for stage_name, label in TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS:
    22→        if stage_name in stages:
    23→            if stages[stage_name].get("confirmed_at"):
    24→                print(colorize(f"    ✓ {label} (confirmed)", "green"))
    25→            else:
    26→                print(colorize(f"    ✓ {label} (needs confirm)", "yellow"))
    27→        elif TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES[stage_name].issubset(stages):
    28→            print(colorize(f"    → {label} (current)", "yellow"))
    29→        else:
    30→            print(colorize(f"    ○ {label}", "dim"))
    31→
    32→    if plan and "reflect" in stages and "organize" not in stages:
    33→        gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
    34→        manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
    35→        if not manual:
    36→            print(colorize("\n    No manual clusters yet. Preferred next step:", "yellow"))
    37→            for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages="organize"):
    38→                print(colorize(f"      {label}: {command}", "dim"))
    39→            print(
    40→                colorize(
    41→                    f"      Manual fallback: {triage_manual_stage_command('organize')}",
    42→                    "dim",
    43→                )
    44→            )
    45→        elif gaps:
    46→            print(colorize(f"\n    {len(gaps)} cluster(s) need enrichment:", "yellow"))
    47→            for name, missing in gaps:
    48→                print(colorize(f"      {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
    49→            print(colorize(f"      Fix: {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT}", "dim"))
    50→        else:
    51→            print(colorize(f"\n    All {len(manual)} manual cluster(s) enriched.", "green"))
    52→
    53→
    54→__all__ = ["print_stage_progress"]
    55→

1→"""Review-coverage helpers for triage planning."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.shared.cluster_membership import cluster_issue_ids
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.stale import open_review_ids
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     8→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import Cluster, PlanModel
     9→
    10→from .plan_state_access import (
    11→    ensure_cluster_map,
    12→    ensure_queue_order,
    13→    ensure_skipped_map,
    14→    ensure_triage_meta,
    15→    normalized_issue_id_list,
    16→)
    17→
    18→[REDACTED]
    19→[REDACTED]
    20→[REDACTED]
    21→
    22→
    23→def open_review_ids_from_state(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    24→    """Return open review IDs from the current state snapshot."""
    25→    return open_review_ids(state)
    26→
    27→
    28→def has_open_review_issues(state: StateModel | dict | None) -> bool:
    29→    """Return True when any open review issues exist."""
    30→    return bool(open_review_ids_from_state(state or {}))
    31→
    32→
    33→def plan_review_ids(plan: PlanModel) -> list[str]:
    34→    """Return review/concerns IDs currently represented in queue_order."""
    35→    return [
    36→        fid
    37→        for fid in ensure_queue_order(plan)
    38→        if not fid.startswith("triage::")
    39→        and not fid.startswith("workflow::")
    40→        and (fid.startswith("review::") or fid.startswith("concerns::"))
    41→    ]
    42→
    43→
    44→def coverage_open_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    45→    """Return the frozen or live open review IDs covered by this triage run."""
    46→    active_ids = normalized_issue_id_list(
    47→        ensure_triage_meta(plan).get(_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY)
    48→    )
    49→    if active_ids:
    50→        return set(active_ids)
    51→    has_completed_scan = bool(state.get("last_scan"))
    52→    review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
    53→    if not has_completed_scan and not review_ids:
    54→        return set(plan_review_ids(plan))
    55→    return review_ids
    56→
    57→
    58→def active_triage_issue_ids(
    59→    plan: PlanModel,
    60→    state: StateModel | None = None,
    61→) -> set[str]:
    62→    """Return the frozen review issue set for the current triage run."""
    63→    meta = ensure_triage_meta(plan)
    64→    active_ids = normalized_issue_id_list(meta.get(_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY))
    65→    if active_ids:
    66→        return set(active_ids)
    67→    if state is None:
    68→        return set()
    69→    return coverage_open_ids(plan, state)
    70→
    71→
    72→def live_active_triage_issue_ids(
    73→    plan: PlanModel,
    74→    state: StateModel | None = None,
    75→) -> set[str]:
    76→    """Return frozen triage IDs that are still open review issues in state."""
    77→    frozen_ids = active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
    78→    if state is None or not frozen_ids:
    79→        return frozen_ids
    80→    return frozen_ids & open_review_ids(state)
    81→
    82→
    83→def ensure_active_triage_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> list[str]:
    84→    """Freeze the current triage issue set for validation across stage reruns."""
    85→    meta = ensure_triage_meta(plan)
    86→    active_ids = sorted(coverage_open_ids(plan, state))
    87→    meta[_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY] = active_ids
    88→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY, None)
    89→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY, None)
    90→    return active_ids
    91→
    92→
    93→def clear_active_triage_issue_tracking(meta: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    94→    """Clear frozen triage coverage metadata after successful completion."""
    95→    meta.pop(_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY, None)
    96→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY, None)
    97→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY, None)
    98→
    99→
   100→def undispositioned_triage_issue_ids(
   101→    plan: PlanModel,
   102→    state: StateModel | None = None,
   103→) -> list[str]:
   104→    """Return frozen triage issues still lacking cluster/skip/dismiss coverage."""
   105→    target_ids = live_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   106→    if not target_ids:
   107→        return []
   108→    covered_ids: set[str] = set()
   109→    for cluster in ensure_cluster_map(plan).values():
   110→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   111→            continue
   112→        covered_ids.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   113→    covered_ids.update(
   114→        issue_id for issue_id in ensure_skipped_map(plan) if isinstance(issue_id, str)
   115→    )
   116→    covered_ids.update(
   117→        normalized_issue_id_list(ensure_triage_meta(plan).get("dismissed_ids"))
   118→    )
   119→    # Also check disposition map for auto-skipped entries that have matching
   120→    # skip entries in plan state (belt-and-suspenders with the skipped map check)
   121→    dispositions = ensure_triage_meta(plan).get("issue_dispositions", {})
   122→    for issue_id, disp in dispositions.items():
   123→        if disp.get("decision_source") == "observe_auto" and issue_id in ensure_skipped_map(plan):
   124→            covered_ids.add(issue_id)
   125→    return sorted(issue_id for issue_id in target_ids if issue_id not in covered_ids)
   126→
   127→
   128→def sync_undispositioned_triage_meta(
   129→    plan: PlanModel,
   130→    state: StateModel | None = None,
   131→) -> list[str]:
   132→    """Persist the current undispositioned triage issue set for recovery UX."""
   133→    meta = ensure_triage_meta(plan)
   134→    missing = undispositioned_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   135→    if missing:
   136→        meta[_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY] = missing
   137→        meta[_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY] = len(missing)
   138→    else:
   139→        meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY, None)
   140→        meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY, None)
   141→    return missing
   142→
   143→
   144→def triage_coverage(
   145→    plan: PlanModel,
   146→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   147→) -> tuple[int, int, dict[str, Cluster]]:
   148→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for review issues in triage."""
   149→    clusters = ensure_cluster_map(plan)
   150→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
   151→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   152→        all_cluster_ids.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   153→    review_ids = list(open_review_ids) if open_review_ids is not None else plan_review_ids(plan)
   154→    organized = sum(1 for fid in review_ids if fid in all_cluster_ids)
   155→    return organized, len(review_ids), clusters
   156→
   157→
   158→def manual_clusters_with_issues(plan: PlanModel) -> list[str]:
   159→    """Return manual clusters that currently own at least one issue."""
   160→    return [
   161→        name
   162→        for name, cluster in ensure_cluster_map(plan).items()
   163→        if cluster_issue_ids(cluster) and not cluster.get("auto")
   164→    ]
   165→
   166→
   167→def find_cluster_for(fid: str, clusters: dict[str, Cluster]) -> str | None:
   168→    """Return the owning cluster name for an issue ID, if any."""
   169→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   170→        if fid in cluster_issue_ids(cluster):
   171→            return name
   172→    return None
   173→
   174→
   175→__all__ = [
   176→    "active_triage_issue_ids",
   177→    "clear_active_triage_issue_tracking",
   178→    "cluster_issue_ids",
   179→    "coverage_open_ids",
   180→    "ensure_active_triage_issue_ids",
   181→    "find_cluster_for",
   182→    "has_open_review_issues",
   183→    "live_active_triage_issue_ids",
   184→    "manual_clusters_with_issues",
   185→    "open_review_ids_from_state",
   186→    "plan_review_ids",
   187→    "sync_undispositioned_triage_meta",
   188→    "triage_coverage",
   189→    "undispositioned_triage_issue_ids",
   190→]
   191→

1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import enum
     6→import importlib
     7→import logging
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
    10→
    11→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    12→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.refresh_lifecycle import (
    14→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE,
    15→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
    16→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN,
    17→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
    18→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import load_plan
    21→from desloppify.state_scoring import score_snapshot
    22→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import QueueBuildOptions
    23→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import queue_context
    24→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import is_subjective_queue_item
    25→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
    26→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.snapshot import coarse_phase_name
    27→from desloppify.engine.planning.queue_policy import build_execution_queue
    28→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress_render import (
    29→    format_plan_delta,
    30→    format_queue_block,
    31→    format_queue_headline,
    32→)
    33→
    34→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    35→
    36→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    37→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import QueueContext
    38→
    39→
    40→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    41→# QueueBreakdown — single source of truth for queue numbers
    42→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    43→
    44→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    45→class QueueBreakdown:
    46→    """All numbers needed to render the standardized queue display."""
    47→
    48→    queue_total: int = 0
    49→    plan_ordered: int = 0
    50→    stale_plan_ordered: int = 0
    51→    skipped: int = 0
    52→    subjective: int = 0
    53→    workflow: int = 0
    54→    lifecycle_phase: str | None = None
    55→    focus_cluster: str | None = None
    56→    focus_cluster_count: int = 0
    57→    focus_cluster_total: int = 0
    58→
    59→    @property
    60→    def objective_actionable(self) -> int:
    61→        """Objective items only — excludes subjective and workflow.
    62→
    63→        This is the count used by :func:`score_display_mode` to decide
    64→        frozen vs live display.  Do not use it directly for gating —
    65→        use ``score_display_mode()`` instead.
    66→        """
    67→        if self.lifecycle_phase in {
    68→            LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN,
    69→            LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
    70→            LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
    71→            LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
    72→        }:
    73→            return 0
    74→        return max(0, self.queue_total - self.subjective - self.workflow)
    75→
    76→
    77→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    78→# ScoreDisplayMode — single decision point for frozen vs live score display
    79→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    80→
    81→class ScoreDisplayMode(enum.Enum):
    82→    """How to display the score given the current plan cycle and queue state.
    83→
    84→    - ``FROZEN``: objective work remains → show frozen plan-start score.
    85→    - ``PHASE_TRANSITION``: objective drained, subjective/workflow remain
    86→      → show live scores + transition banner.
    87→    - ``LIVE``: no active plan cycle or queue fully clear → show live scores.
    88→    """
    89→    FROZEN = "frozen"
    90→    PHASE_TRANSITION = "phase_transition"
    91→    LIVE = "live"
    92→
    93→
    94→def score_display_mode(
    95→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None,
    96→    plan_start_strict: float | None,
    97→) -> ScoreDisplayMode:
    98→    """Determine the score display mode from breakdown + plan-start score.
    99→
   100→    This is the **single source of truth** for the frozen-vs-live decision.
   101→    Every call site (status, scan reveal, next nudges, plan reconcile, plan
   102→    nudge) must use this instead of ad-hoc count checks.
   103→    """
   104→    if plan_start_strict is None:
   105→        return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
   106→    if breakdown is None:
   107→        return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
   108→    if breakdown.lifecycle_phase == LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN:
   109→        return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
   110→    if breakdown.lifecycle_phase == LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE:
   111→        return ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   112→    if breakdown.lifecycle_phase in {
   113→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
   114→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
   115→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
   116→    }:
   117→        return ScoreDisplayMode.PHASE_TRANSITION
   118→    if breakdown.objective_actionable > 0:
   119→        return ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   120→    if breakdown.queue_total > 0:
   121→        return ScoreDisplayMode.PHASE_TRANSITION
   122→    return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
   123→
   124→
   125→def plan_aware_queue_breakdown(
   126→    state: dict,
   127→    plan: dict | None = None,
   128→    *,
   129→    context: QueueContext | None = None,
   130→) -> QueueBreakdown:
   131→    """Build a full :class:`QueueBreakdown` from a single ``build_work_queue`` call.
   132→
   133→    When *context* is provided, its ``plan`` and ``policy`` are forwarded to
   134→    ``build_work_queue`` so the counts agree with the caller's resolution.
   135→    """
   136→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else plan
   137→
   138→    result = build_execution_queue(
   139→        state,
   140→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   141→            status="open",
   142→            count=None,
   143→            plan=effective_plan if context is None else None,
   144→            include_skipped=False,
   145→            context=context,
   146→        ),
   147→    )
   148→
   149→    # Collapse clusters for display-level counting
   150→    items = result.get("items", [])
   151→    snapshot = (
   152→        context.snapshot
   153→        if context is not None
   154→        else queue_context(state, plan=effective_plan).snapshot
   155→    )
   156→    lifecycle_phase = coarse_phase_name(snapshot.phase)
   157→    if effective_plan and not effective_plan.get("active_cluster"):
   158→        items = collapse_clusters(items, effective_plan)
   159→
   160→    queue_total = len(items)
   161→
   162→    # Count subjective and workflow items in the queue.
   163→    # Collapsed clusters whose members are all subjective count as subjective.
   164→    subjective = sum(
   165→        1 for item in items
   166→        if is_subjective_queue_item(item)
   167→    )
   168→    workflow = sum(
   169→        1 for item in items
   170→        if item.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
   171→    )
   172→
   173→    # Plan-derived counts
   174→    plan_ordered = 0
   175→    stale_plan_ordered = 0
   176→    skipped = 0
   177→    if effective_plan:
   178→        skipped = len(effective_plan.get("skipped", {}))
   179→        # plan_ordered = live queue_order items that still exist in the current
   180→        # snapshot; stale_plan_ordered captures dead queue_order entries so the
   181→        # summary does not imply they are still blocking the live queue.
   182→        queue_order = effective_plan.get("queue_order", [])
   183→        skipped_ids = set(effective_plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   184→        current_item_ids = _snapshot_item_ids(snapshot)
   185→        if current_item_ids is None:
   186→            plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
   187→        else:
   188→            plan_ordered = sum(
   189→                1 for fid in queue_order
   190→                if fid not in skipped_ids and fid in current_item_ids
   191→            )
   192→            stale_plan_ordered = sum(
   193→                1 for fid in queue_order
   194→                if fid not in skipped_ids and fid not in current_item_ids
   195→            )
   196→
   197→    # Focus cluster info
   198→    focus_cluster = None
   199→    focus_cluster_count = 0
   200→    focus_cluster_total = 0
   201→    if effective_plan:
   202→        active = effective_plan.get("active_cluster")
   203→        if active:
   204→            focus_cluster = active
   205→            cluster_data = effective_plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
   206→            focus_cluster_total = len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   207→            # Count how many cluster members are still in the queue
   208→            cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   209→            open_issues = {
   210→                fid
   211→                for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
   212→                if f.get("status") == "open"
   213→            }
   214→            focus_cluster_count = len(cluster_member_ids & open_issues)
   215→
   216→    return QueueBreakdown(
   217→        queue_total=queue_total,
   218→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
   219→        stale_plan_ordered=stale_plan_ordered,
   220→        skipped=skipped,
   221→        subjective=subjective,
   222→        workflow=workflow,
   223→        lifecycle_phase=lifecycle_phase,
   224→        focus_cluster=focus_cluster,
   225→        focus_cluster_count=focus_cluster_count,
   226→        focus_cluster_total=focus_cluster_total,
   227→    )
   228→
   229→
   230→def _snapshot_item_ids(snapshot: object) -> set[str] | None:
   231→    """Return all live queue item IDs represented by a snapshot.
   232→
   233→    When tests stub ``snapshot`` with only a ``phase`` attribute, return
   234→    ``None`` so callers can fall back to legacy plan_ordered counting.
   235→    """
   236→    partition_names = (
   237→        "all_objective_items",
   238→        "all_initial_review_items",
   239→        "all_postflight_review_items",
   240→        "all_scan_items",
   241→        "all_postflight_workflow_items",
   242→        "all_postflight_triage_items",
   243→    )
   244→    item_ids: set[str] = set()
   245→    saw_partition = False
   246→    for name in partition_names:
   247→        items = getattr(snapshot, name, None)
   248→        if items is None:
   249→            continue
   250→        saw_partition = True
   251→        for item in items:
   252→            if isinstance(item, dict):
   253→                item_id = item.get("id")
   254→                if isinstance(item_id, str) and item_id:
   255→                    item_ids.add(item_id)
   256→    if not saw_partition:
   257→        return None
   258→    return item_ids
   259→
   260→
   261→def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
   262→    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
   263→    if not plan:
   264→        return None
   265→    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   266→
   267→
   268→def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   269→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   270→    *,
   271→    frozen_strict: float,
   272→    live_score: float | None = None,
   273→) -> None:
   274→    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress.
   275→
   276→    Only call from a ``ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN`` path — assumes objective
   277→    work remains in the queue.
   278→    """
   279→    block = format_queue_block(breakdown, frozen_score=frozen_strict, live_score=live_score)
   280→    print()
   281→    for text, style in block:
   282→        print(colorize(text, style))
   283→    print(colorize(
   284→        "  Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   285→        "dim",
   286→    ))
   287→
   288→
   289→def print_objective_drained_banner(
   290→    frozen_strict: float,
   291→    remaining: int,
   292→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   293→) -> None:
   294→    """Show a phase-transition banner when objective work is drained."""
   295→    kind_labels: list[str] = []
   296→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   297→        kind_labels.append("subjective")
   298→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   299→        kind_labels.append("workflow")
   300→    kind_desc = " + ".join(kind_labels) if kind_labels else "non-objective"
   301→    print(colorize(
   302→        f"\n  Objective queue complete (plan-start was {frozen_strict:.1f})."
   303→        f" {remaining} {kind_desc} item{'s' if remaining != 1 else ''} remain.",
   304→        "cyan",
   305→    ))
   306→    print(colorize(
   307→        "  Run `desloppify next` for remaining work,"
   308→        " then `desloppify scan` to finalize.",
   309→        "dim",
   310→    ))
   311→
   312→
   313→def print_execution_or_reveal(
   314→    state: dict,
   315→    prev,
   316→    plan: dict | None,
   317→) -> None:
   318→    """Context-aware score display using :func:`score_display_mode`."""
   319→    frozen_strict = get_plan_start_strict(plan)
   320→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None
   321→
   322→    if frozen_strict is not None:
   323→        try:
   324→            breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   325→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   326→            _logger.debug("queue breakdown computation skipped", exc_info=True)
   327→
   328→    mode = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict)
   329→
   330→    if mode is ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN:
   331→        print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   332→            breakdown,
   333→            frozen_strict=frozen_strict,
   334→            live_score=score_snapshot(state).strict,
   335→        )
   336→        return
   337→
   338→    # LIVE or PHASE_TRANSITION: show current scores
   339→    score_update_mod = importlib.import_module(
   340→        "desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update"
   341→    )
   342→    score_update_mod.print_score_update(state, prev)
   343→
   344→    if mode is ScoreDisplayMode.PHASE_TRANSITION:
   345→        print_objective_drained_banner(frozen_strict, breakdown.queue_total, breakdown)
   346→
   347→
   348→def show_score_with_plan_context(state: dict, prev) -> None:
   349→    """Load plan (best-effort) and show frozen or live score context.
   350→
   351→    Encapsulates the common load_plan + PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS + reveal
   352→    choreography so command modules don't each repeat it.
   353→    """
   354→    try:
   355→        plan = load_plan()
   356→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   357→        _logger.debug("score display skipped plan-aware context", exc_info=exc)
   358→        plan = None
   359→    print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, plan)
   360→
   361→
   362→__all__ = [
   363→    "QueueBreakdown",
   364→    "ScoreDisplayMode",
   365→    "format_plan_delta",
   366→    "format_queue_block",
   367→    "format_queue_headline",
   368→    "get_plan_start_strict",
   369→    "plan_aware_queue_breakdown",
   370→    "print_execution_or_reveal",
   371→    "print_objective_drained_banner",
   372→    "print_frozen_score_with_queue_context",
   373→    "score_display_mode",
   374→    "show_score_with_plan_context",
   375→]
   376→

1→"""Shared triage guardrail helpers for command entrypoints."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.issue_id_display import short_issue_id
     9→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS, CommandError
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.triage import (
    12→    compute_new_issue_ids,
    13→    is_triage_stale,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import load_plan
    16→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import (
    17→    TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE,
    18→    TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX,
    19→    triage_phase_banner,
    20→)
    21→
    22→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass
    26→class TriageGuardrailResult:
    27→    """Structured result from triage staleness detection."""
    28→
    29→    is_stale: bool = False
    30→    new_ids: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    31→    _plan: dict | None = field(default=None, repr=False)
    32→
    33→
    34→def triage_guardrail_status(
    35→    *,
    36→    plan: dict | None = None,
    37→    state: dict | None = None,
    38→) -> TriageGuardrailResult:
    39→    """Pure detection: is triage stale? Returns structured result, no side effects."""
    40→    try:
    41→        resolved_plan = plan if isinstance(plan, dict) else load_plan()
    42→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
    43→        logger.debug("Triage guardrail status skipped: plan could not be loaded.", exc_info=exc)
    44→        return TriageGuardrailResult()
    45→
    46→    resolved_state = state or {}
    47→
    48→    if not is_triage_stale(resolved_plan, resolved_state):
    49→        return TriageGuardrailResult(_plan=resolved_plan)
    50→
    51→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
    52→    if resolved_state:
    53→        new_ids = compute_new_issue_ids(resolved_plan, resolved_state)
    54→
    55→    return TriageGuardrailResult(is_stale=True, new_ids=new_ids, _plan=resolved_plan)
    56→
    57→
    58→def triage_guardrail_messages(
    59→    *,
    60→    plan: dict | None = None,
    61→    state: dict | None = None,
    62→) -> list[str]:
    63→    """Return warning strings without printing."""
    64→    resolved_state = state or {}
    65→    result = triage_guardrail_status(plan=plan, state=state)
    66→    if not result.is_stale:
    67→        return []
    68→
    69→    messages: list[str] = []
    70→    if result.new_ids:
    71→        messages.append(
    72→            f"{len(result.new_ids)} new review issue(s) not yet triaged."
    73→            " Run the staged triage runner to incorporate them "
    74→            f"(`{TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX}` or `{TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE}`)."
    75→        )
    76→
    77→    if result._plan is not None:
    78→        banner = triage_phase_banner(result._plan, resolved_state)
    79→        if banner:
    80→            messages.append(banner)
    81→
    82→    return messages
    83→
    84→
    85→def print_triage_guardrail_info(
    86→    *,
    87→    plan: dict | None = None,
    88→    state: dict | None = None,
    89→) -> bool:
    90→    """Print yellow info banner if triage is stale. Returns True if banner was shown."""
    91→    messages = triage_guardrail_messages(plan=plan, state=state)
    92→    for msg in messages:
    93→        print(colorize(f"  {msg}", "yellow"))
    94→    return bool(messages)
    95→
    96→
    97→def require_triage_current_or_exit(
    98→    *,
    99→    state: dict,
   100→    bypass: bool = False,
   101→    attest: str = "",
   102→) -> None:
   103→    """Gate: exit(1) if triage is stale and not bypassed. Name signals the exit."""
   104→    result = triage_guardrail_status(state=state)
   105→    if not result.is_stale:
   106→        return
   107→
   108→    if bypass and attest and len(attest.strip()) >= 30:
   109→        print(colorize(
   110→            "  Triage guardrail bypassed with attestation.",
   111→            "yellow",
   112→        ))
   113→        return
   114→
   115→    new_ids = result.new_ids
   116→    lines = [
   117→        f"BLOCKED: {len(new_ids) or 'some'} new review issue(s) have not been triaged."
   118→    ]
   119→    if new_ids:
   120→        for fid in sorted(new_ids)[:5]:
   121→            issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(fid, {})
   122→            lines.append(f"    * [{short_issue_id(fid)}] {issue.get('summary', '')}")
   123→        if len(new_ids) > 5:
   124→            lines.append(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 5} more")
   125→    lines.append("")
   126→    lines.append(f"  NEXT STEP (Codex):  {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX}")
   127→    lines.append(f"  NEXT STEP (Claude): {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE}")
   128→    lines.append("  Manual fallback:    desloppify plan triage")
   129→    lines.append("  (Review new issues, then either --confirm-existing or re-plan.)")
   130→    lines.append("")
   131→    lines.append("  View new execution items:  desloppify plan queue --sort recent")
   132→    lines.append("  View broader backlog:      desloppify backlog")
   133→    lines.append('  To bypass: --force-resolve --attest "I understand the plan may be stale..."')
   134→    raise CommandError("\n".join(lines))
   135→
   136→
   137→__all__ = [
   138→    "TriageGuardrailResult",
   139→    "print_triage_guardrail_info",
   140→    "require_triage_current_or_exit",
   141→    "triage_guardrail_messages",
   142→    "triage_guardrail_status",
   143→]
   144→

1→"""Canonical stale/unscored subjective policy helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import hashlib
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import all_subjective_entries
    11→
    12→_REVIEW_DETECTORS = ("review", "concerns")
    13→
    14→
    15→def open_review_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    16→    """Return IDs of open review/concerns issues from state."""
    17→    return {
    18→        fid
    19→        for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    20→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in _REVIEW_DETECTORS
    21→    }
    22→
    23→
    24→def _subjective_entry_id(
    25→    dimension_key: object,
    26→    *,
    27→    subjective_prefix: str,
    28→) -> str:
    29→    return f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(str(dimension_key))}"
    30→
    31→
    32→def _subjective_entries(state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
    33→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    34→    if not dim_scores:
    35→        return []
    36→    return list(all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores))
    37→
    38→
    39→def _collect_subjective_entry_ids(
    40→    entries: list[dict],
    41→    *,
    42→    subjective_prefix: str,
    43→    predicate,
    44→) -> set[str]:
    45→    collected: set[str] = set()
    46→    for entry in entries:
    47→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    48→        if not dim_key or not predicate(entry):
    49→            continue
    50→        collected.add(_subjective_entry_id(dim_key, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix))
    51→    return collected
    52→
    53→
    54→def current_stale_ids(
    55→    state: StateModel,
    56→    *,
    57→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    58→) -> set[str]:
    59→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    60→    return _collect_subjective_entry_ids(
    61→        _subjective_entries(state),
    62→        subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix,
    63→        predicate=lambda entry: bool(entry.get("stale")),
    64→    )
    65→
    66→
    67→def _unscored_ids_from_assessments(
    68→    assessments: dict,
    69→    *,
    70→    subjective_prefix: str,
    71→) -> set[str]:
    72→    unscored: set[str] = set()
    73→    for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    74→        if not isinstance(payload, dict) or not payload.get("placeholder") or not dim_key:
    75→            continue
    76→        unscored.add(_subjective_entry_id(dim_key, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix))
    77→    return unscored
    78→
    79→
    80→def _unscored_ids_from_dimension_scores(
    81→    entries: list[dict],
    82→    *,
    83→    subjective_prefix: str,
    84→) -> set[str]:
    85→    return _collect_subjective_entry_ids(
    86→        entries,
    87→        subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix,
    88→        predicate=lambda entry: bool(entry.get("placeholder")),
    89→    )
    90→
    91→
    92→def current_unscored_ids(
    93→    state: StateModel,
    94→    *,
    95→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    96→) -> set[str]:
    97→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored."""
    98→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    99→    if isinstance(assessments, dict) and assessments:
   100→        return _unscored_ids_from_assessments(
   101→            assessments,
   102→            subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix,
   103→        )
   104→    return _unscored_ids_from_dimension_scores(
   105→        _subjective_entries(state),
   106→        subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix,
   107→    )
   108→
   109→
   110→def current_under_target_ids(
   111→    state: StateModel,
   112→    *,
   113→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   114→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
   115→) -> set[str]:
   116→    """Return under-target subjective IDs that are neither stale nor unscored."""
   117→    entries = _subjective_entries(state)
   118→    if not entries:
   119→        return set()
   120→
   121→    stale_ids = current_stale_ids(state, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix)
   122→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix)
   123→
   124→    return {
   125→        item_id
   126→        for item_id in _collect_subjective_entry_ids(
   127→            entries,
   128→            subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix,
   129→            predicate=lambda entry: (
   130→                not entry.get("placeholder")
   131→                and not entry.get("stale")
   132→                and float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))) < target_strict
   133→            ),
   134→        )
   135→        if item_id not in stale_ids and item_id not in unscored_ids
   136→    }
   137→
   138→
   139→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   140→    """Hash open review/concerns issue IDs to detect triage-relevant changes."""
   141→    review_ids = sorted(open_review_ids(state))
   142→    if not review_ids:
   143→        return ""
   144→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   145→
   146→
   147→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   148→    """Return open review/concerns IDs that appeared since the last triage."""
   149→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   150→    triaged = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   151→    return open_review_ids(state) - triaged if triaged else set()
   152→
   153→
   154→def is_triage_stale(
   155→    plan: dict,
   156→    state: StateModel,
   157→) -> bool:
   158→    """Return True when genuinely new review issues appeared since last triage.
   159→
   160→    In-progress triage (confirmed stages + stage IDs in queue) is NOT
   161→    considered stale — the lifecycle filter in the work queue already
   162→    forces triage stages to the front.
   163→    """
   164→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   165→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   166→    return bool(open_review_ids(state) - triaged_ids)
   167→
   168→
   169→__all__ = [
   170→    "compute_new_issue_ids",
   171→    "current_stale_ids",
   172→    "current_under_target_ids",
   173→    "current_unscored_ids",
   174→    "is_triage_stale",
   175→    "open_review_ids",
   176→    "review_issue_snapshot_hash",
   177→]
   178→

1→"""Focused public plan API for triage orchestration surfaces."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (
     6→    TRIAGE_IDS,
     7→    TRIAGE_PREFIX,
     8→    TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
     9→    TRIAGE_STAGE_ORDER,
    10→    TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.triage.core import (
    13→    TriageInput,
    14→    build_triage_prompt,
    15→    collect_triage_input,
    16→    detect_recurring_patterns,
    17→    extract_issue_citations,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.triage.playbook import (
    20→    TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ADD,
    21→    TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_CREATE,
    22→    TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH,
    23→    TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT,
    24→    TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_STEPS,
    25→    TRIAGE_CMD_COMPLETE,
    26→    TRIAGE_CMD_COMPLETE_VERBOSE,
    27→    TRIAGE_CMD_CONFIRM_EXISTING,
    28→    TRIAGE_CMD_ENRICH,
    29→    TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE,
    30→    TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE,
    31→    TRIAGE_CMD_REFLECT,
    32→    TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE,
    33→    TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX,
    34→    TRIAGE_CMD_SENSE_CHECK,
    35→    TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
    36→    TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS,
    37→    triage_manual_stage_command,
    38→    triage_run_stages_command,
    39→    triage_runner_commands,
    40→)
    41→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.triage_start_policy import (
    42→    TriageStartDecision,
    43→    decide_triage_start,
    44→)
    45→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.context import has_objective_backlog
    46→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    47→
    48→
    49→def triage_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel, state: dict | None = None) -> str:
    50→    """Return a banner string describing triage status."""
    51→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    52→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    53→    has_triage = any(stage_id in order for stage_id in TRIAGE_IDS)
    54→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    55→    run_hint = (
    56→        f"Run: {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX} "
    57→        f"(or {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE})"
    58→    )
    59→
    60→    if not has_triage:
    61→        undispositioned = meta.get("undispositioned_issue_count", 0)
    62→        if undispositioned:
    63→            return (
    64→                "TRIAGE RECOVERY NEEDED — "
    65→                f"{undispositioned} review issue(s) still need cluster/skip dispositions. "
    66→                f"{run_hint}"
    67→            )
    68→        if meta.get("triage_recommended"):
    69→            return (
    70→                "TRIAGE RECOMMENDED — review issues changed since last triage. "
    71→                f"{run_hint}"
    72→            )
    73→        return ""
    74→
    75→    if state and has_objective_backlog(state, None):
    76→        undispositioned = meta.get("undispositioned_issue_count", 0)
    77→        if undispositioned:
    78→            return (
    79→                "TRIAGE PENDING — "
    80→                f"{undispositioned} review issue(s) still need cluster/skip dispositions after current work. "
    81→                f"{run_hint}"
    82→            )
    83→        return (
    84→            "TRIAGE PENDING — queued and will activate after objective work "
    85→            "is complete."
    86→        )
    87→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    88→    completed = [
    89→        stage
    90→        for stage in ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check")
    91→        if stage in stages
    92→    ]
    93→    if completed:
    94→        return (
    95→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({len(completed)}/5 stages complete) — "
    96→            f"complete all stages to exit. {run_hint}"
    97→        )
    98→    return (
    99→        "TRIAGE MODE — review issues need analysis before fixing. "
   100→        f"{run_hint}"
   101→    )
   102→
   103→__all__ = [
   104→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ADD",
   105→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_CREATE",
   106→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH",
   107→    "TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT",

1→"""Triage sync — inject/prune triage stage IDs based on review issue changes."""

     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy import stale as stale_policy_mod
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (

     9→    QueueSyncResult,
    10→    confirmed_triage_stage_names,

    34→
    35→def _new_review_ids_since_triage(
    36→    state: StateModel,
    37→    meta: dict,
    38→) -> set[str]:
    39→    """Return review issue IDs that are new since the last triage."""
    40→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
    41→    return stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state) - triaged_ids
    42→
    43→
    44→def _prune_all_triage_stages(order: list[str]) -> None:
    45→    """Remove all ``triage::*`` stage IDs from *order*."""
    46→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    47→        while sid in order:
    48→            order.remove(sid)
    49→
    50→
    51→def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
    52→    order: list[str],
    53→    confirmed: set[str],
    54→    *,
    55→    skipped: dict[str, object] | None = None,
    56→) -> list[str]:
    57→    """Inject triage stages for pending (unconfirmed) items.
    58→
    59→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
    60→    Returns list of injected stage IDs.
    61→    """
    62→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "commit")
    63→    existing = set(order)
    64→    injected: list[str] = []
    65→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
    66→        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
    67→            if skipped is not None:
    68→                skipped.pop(sid, None)
    69→            order.append(sid)
    70→            injected.append(sid)
    71→            existing.add(sid)
    72→    return injected
    73→
    74→
    75→def _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta: dict) -> None:
    76→    """Clear defer metadata once triage is no longer deferred."""
    77→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY, None)
    78→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY, None)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _store_triage_meta(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
    82→    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
    83→
    84→
    85→def _inject_triage_result(
    86→    order: list[str],
    87→    confirmed: set[str],
    88→    *,
    89→    skipped: dict[str, object] | None = None,
    90→    normalize: bool = False,
    91→) -> QueueSyncResult:
    92→    injected = _inject_pending_triage_stages(order, confirmed, skipped=skipped)
    93→    if not injected:
    94→        return QueueSyncResult()
    95→    if normalize:
    96→        normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(order)
    97→    return QueueSyncResult(injected=injected)
    98→
    99→
   100→def _mark_triage_deferred(
   101→    plan: PlanModel,
   102→    meta: dict,
   103→    *,
   104→    defer_state: dict,
   105→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   106→    meta[_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   107→    meta["triage_recommended"] = True
   108→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY, None)
   109→    _store_triage_meta(plan, meta)
   110→    return QueueSyncResult(deferred=True)
   111→
   112→
   113→def _mark_triage_escalated(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
   114→    meta[_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY] = True
   115→    meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
   116→    _store_triage_meta(plan, meta)
   117→
   118→
   119→def _mark_triage_ready(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
   120→    _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta)
   121→    meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
   122→    _store_triage_meta(plan, meta)
   123→
   124→
   125→def _prune_stale_present_stages(
   126→    *,
   127→    plan: PlanModel,
   128→    state: StateModel,
   129→    order: list[str],
   130→    meta: dict,
   131→    last_hash: str,
   132→    confirmed: set[str],
   133→    recorded_unconfirmed: set[str],
   134→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   135→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   136→    if not last_hash or confirmed or recorded_unconfirmed:
   137→        return result
   138→    new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   139→    if new_since_triage:
   140→        return result
   141→    _prune_all_triage_stages(order)
   142→    _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta)
   143→    current_hash = stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   144→    if current_hash:
   145→        meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   146→        plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   147→    result.pruned = list(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
   148→    return result
   149→
   150→
   151→def _backfill_partial_triage_snapshot(
   152→    *,
   153→    plan: PlanModel,
   154→    state: StateModel,
   155→    meta: dict,
   156→    last_hash: str,
   157→) -> None:
   158→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   159→    has_completed_stage = any(
   160→        isinstance(v, dict) and v.get("confirmed_at")
   161→        for v in stages.values()
   162→    )
   163→    if not has_completed_stage or meta.get("triaged_ids") or last_hash:
   164→        return
   165→    current_review = sorted(stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state))
   166→    if current_review:
   167→        meta["triaged_ids"] = current_review
   168→        meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   169→        plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   170→
   171→
   172→def _defer_or_inject_triage(
   173→    *,
   174→    plan: PlanModel,
   175→    state: StateModel,
   176→    order: list[str],
   177→    meta: dict,
   178→    confirmed: set[str],
   179→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   180→    new_since_triage: set[str],
   181→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   182→    decision = decide_triage_start(
   183→        plan,
   184→        state,
   185→        policy=policy,
   186→        explicit_start=False,
   187→        attested_override=False,
   188→    )
   189→    if decision.action == "defer":
   190→        defer_state = update_defer_state(
   191→            meta.get(_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY),
   192→            state=state,
   193→            deferred_ids=new_since_triage,
   194→            options=DeferUpdateOptions(
   195→                deferred_ids_field=_TRIAGE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD,
   196→            ),
   197→        )
   198→        meta[_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   199→        escalated = should_escalate_defer_state(
   200→            defer_state,
   201→            state=state,
   202→            options=DeferEscalationOptions(
   203→                deferred_ids_field=_TRIAGE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD,
   204→            ),
   205→        )
   206→        if not escalated:
   207→            return _mark_triage_deferred(plan, meta, defer_state=defer_state)
   208→        _mark_triage_escalated(plan, meta)
   209→        return _inject_triage_result(
   210→            order,
   211→            confirmed,
   212→            skipped=plan.get("skipped", {}),
   213→            normalize=True,
   214→        )
   215→
   216→    _mark_triage_ready(plan, meta)
   217→    return _inject_triage_result(
   218→        order,
   219→        confirmed,
   220→        skipped=plan.get("skipped", {}),
   221→    )
   222→
   223→
   224→def _sync_hash_change(
   225→    *,
   226→    plan: PlanModel,
   227→    state: StateModel,
   228→    order: list[str],
   229→    meta: dict,
   230→    confirmed: set[str],
   231→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   232→    current_hash: str,
   233→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   234→    new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   235→    if new_since_triage:
   236→        return _defer_or_inject_triage(
   237→            plan=plan,
   238→            state=state,
   239→            order=order,
   240→            meta=meta,
   241→            confirmed=confirmed,
   242→            policy=policy,
   243→            new_since_triage=new_since_triage,
   244→        )
   245→
   246→    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   247→    meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
   248→    _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta)
   249→    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   250→    return QueueSyncResult()
   251→
   252→
   253→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   254→# Public API
   255→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   256→
   257→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   258→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   259→
   260→    Returns True when genuinely *new* review issues appeared since the
   261→    last triage.  Triage stage IDs being in the queue alone is not
   262→    sufficient — the new issues that triggered injection may have been
   263→    resolved since then.
   264→
   265→    When issues are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   266→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   267→    through the plan.
   268→    """
   269→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   270→    return stale_policy_mod.is_triage_stale(plan, state)
   271→
   272→
   273→def compute_open_issue_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   274→    """Return the set of currently-open review/concerns issue IDs."""
   275→    return stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state)
   276→
   277→
   278→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   279→    """Return the set of open review/concerns issue IDs added since last triage.
   280→
   281→    Returns an empty set when no prior triage has recorded ``triaged_ids``.
   282→    """
   283→    return stale_policy_mod.compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   284→
   285→
   286→def sync_triage_needed(
   287→    plan: PlanModel,
   288→    state: StateModel,
   289→    *,
   290→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   291→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   292→    """Append triage stage IDs to back of queue when review issues change.
   293→
   294→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   295→
   296→    **Mid-cycle guard**: when the objective backlog still has work, triage
   297→    stages are NOT injected.  Instead, ``epic_triage_meta["triage_recommended"]``
   298→    is set so the UI can show a non-blocking banner.  Stages are injected
   299→    once the objective backlog drains (or on manual ``plan triage``). If
   300→    deferral repeats for multiple scans/days, triage escalates and stages are
   301→    injected in workflow/triage priority order with a forced-visibility marker.
   302→
   303→    When stages are already present but all new issues have been resolved
   304→    since injection, auto-prunes the stale stages and updates the hash.
   305→
   306→    When issues are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   307→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   308→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   309→    """
   310→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   311→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   312→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   313→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   314→    confirmed = confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   315→    recorded_unconfirmed = recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   316→
   317→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   318→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   319→
   320→    current_hash = stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   321→    last_hash = meta.get("issue_snapshot_hash", "")
   322→
   323→    if already_present:
   324→        return _prune_stale_present_stages(
   325→            plan=plan,
   326→            state=state,
   327→            order=order,
   328→            meta=meta,
   329→            last_hash=last_hash,
   330→            confirmed=confirmed,
   331→            recorded_unconfirmed=recorded_unconfirmed,
   332→        )
   333→
   334→    _backfill_partial_triage_snapshot(
   335→        plan=plan,
   336→        state=state,
   337→        meta=meta,
   338→        last_hash=last_hash,
   339→    )
   340→
   341→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   342→        return _sync_hash_change(
   343→            plan=plan,
   344→            state=state,
   345→            order=order,
   346→            meta=meta,
   347→            confirmed=confirmed,
   348→            policy=policy,
   349→            current_hash=current_hash,
   350→        )
   351→
   352→    return result
   353→
   354→
   355→__all__ = [
   356→    "compute_open_issue_ids",
   357→    "compute_new_issue_ids",
   358→    "is_triage_stale",
   359→    "sync_triage_needed",
   360→]
   361→

1→"""Synthetic work-queue item builders and dimension scoring.

    47→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    48→# Dimension key normalization
    49→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    50→
    51→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
    52→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
    53→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    54→    target = cleaned.lower()
    55→
    56→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    57→        if str(label).lower() == target:
    58→            return str(dim_key)
    59→    return slugify(cleaned)
    60→
    61→
    62→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
    63→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with issue dimension keys."""
    64→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    65→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
    66→    return {
    67→        cleaned.lower(),
    68→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
    69→        slugify(cleaned),
    70→        canonical.lower(),
    71→        slugify(canonical),
    72→    }
    73→
    74→

    76→# Subjective strict scores
    77→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    78→
    79→def subjective_strict_scores(state: StateModel | dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, float]:
    80→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    81→    if not dim_scores:
    82→        return {}
    83→
    84→    entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    85→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
    86→    for entry in entries:
    87→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    88→        if not name:
    89→            continue
    90→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    91→        [REDACTED](name)
    92→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
    93→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
    94→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
    95→            if not key:
    96→                continue
    97→            aliases.add(key)
    98→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
    99→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   100→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   101→        for alias in aliases:
   102→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   103→    return scores
   104→

   106→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   107→# Synthetic item builders

   109→
   110→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   111→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   112→
   113→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
   114→    """
   115→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   116→    order_set = set(order)
   117→    present_ids = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   118→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   119→    confirmed = confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   120→    recorded_unconfirmed = recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   121→    present_names = {
   122→        name
   123→        for name, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS
   124→        if sid in present_ids
   125→    }
   126→    present_names.update(recorded_unconfirmed)
   127→    if not present_names:
   128→        return []
   129→
   130→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   131→    open_review_count = sum(
   132→        1 for f in issues.values()
   133→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   134→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   135→    )
   136→
   137→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   138→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   139→    for name, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
   140→        if name not in present_names:
   141→            continue
   142→        if name in confirmed:
   143→            continue
   144→
   145→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   146→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   147→        blocked_by = sorted(
   148→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps if dep in present_names and dep not in confirmed
   149→        )
   150→
   151→        only_stages = None if name == "commit" else name
   152→        cmd = triage_run_stages_command(only_stages=only_stages)
   153→
   154→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   155→            "id": sid,
   156→            "tier": 1,
   157→            "confidence": "high",
   158→            "detector": "triage",
   159→            "file": ".",
   160→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   161→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   162→            "detail": {
   163→                "total_review_issues": open_review_count,
   164→                "stage": name,
   165→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   166→                "runner_commands": [
   167→                    {"label": label, "command": command}
   168→                    for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages=only_stages)
   169→                ],
   170→                "manual_fallback": triage_manual_stage_command(name),
   171→            },
   172→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   173→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   174→        }
   175→        item["primary_command"] = cmd
   176→        items.append(item)
   177→    return items
   178→
   179→
   180→def build_subjective_items(
   181→    state: dict, issues: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   182→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   183→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   184→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   185→    if not dim_scores:
   186→        return []
   187→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   188→
   189→    subjective_entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   190→    if not subjective_entries:
   191→        return []
   192→    unassessed_dims = {
   193→        str(name).strip()
   194→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   195→            dim_scores
   196→        )
   197→    }
   198→
   199→    # Review issues are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   200→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   201→    for issue in issues.values():
   202→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
   203→            continue
   204→        if issue.get("detector") == "review":
   205→            dim_key = str(detail_dict(issue).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   206→            if dim_key:
   207→                review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   208→
   209→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   210→    def _prepare_command(
   211→        cli_keys: list[str],
   212→        *,
   213→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   214→    ) -> str:
   215→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   216→        if cli_keys:
   217→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   218→        if force_review_rerun:
   219→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   220→        return command
   221→
   222→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   223→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   224→        if not name:
   225→            continue
   226→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   227→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   228→            continue
   229→
   230→        [REDACTED](name)
   231→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   232→        cli_keys = [
   233→            str(key).strip().lower()
   234→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   235→            if str(key).strip()
   236→        ]
   237→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   238→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   239→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   240→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   241→            name in unassessed_dims
   242→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("failing", 0)) == 0)
   243→        )
   244→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   245→        # If review issues already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   246→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   247→        if open_review > 0:
   248→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   249→        else:
   250→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   251→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   252→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   253→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   254→            "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   255→            "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   256→            "file": ".",
   257→            "confidence": "medium",
   258→            "summary": summary,
   259→            "detail": {
   260→                "dimension_name": name,
   261→                "dimension": dim_key,
   262→                "failing": int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
   263→                "strict_score": strict_val,
   264→                "open_review_issues": open_review,
   265→                "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   266→            },
   267→            "status": "open",
   268→        }
   269→        item["kind"] = "subjective_dimension"
   270→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command
   271→        item["initial_review"] = is_unassessed
   272→        items.append(item)
   273→    return items
   274→
   275→
   276→__all__ = [
   277→    "build_communicate_score_item",
   278→    "build_create_plan_item",
   279→    "build_deferred_disposition_item",
   280→    "build_import_scores_item",
   281→    "build_run_scan_item",
   282→    "build_score_checkpoint_item",
   283→    "build_subjective_items",
   284→    "build_triage_stage_items",
   285→    "subjective_strict_scores",
   286→]
   287→
