```
1→"""Shared triage lifecycle helpers used by command and runner entrypoints."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from typing import Literal
     8→
     9→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import PlanModel
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import (
    11→    TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
    12→    TriageStartDecision,
    13→    decide_triage_start,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    16→from desloppify.state_io import StateModel
    17→
    18→from .review_coverage import ensure_active_triage_issue_ids
    19→from .services import TriageServices
    20→from .stage_queue import has_triage_in_queue, inject_triage_stages
    21→
    22→TriageStartStatus = Literal["started", "blocked", "already_active"]
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class TriageStartOutcome:
    27→    """Lifecycle result for triage-start attempts."""
    28→
    29→    status: TriageStartStatus
    30→    reason: str
    31→
    32→
    33→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    34→class TriageLifecycleDeps:
    35→    """Dependency seams for triage lifecycle starts (for tests/callers)."""
    36→
    37→    has_triage_in_queue: Callable[[PlanModel], bool] = has_triage_in_queue
    38→    inject_triage_stages: Callable[[PlanModel], None] = inject_triage_stages
    39→    decide_triage_start: Callable[..., TriageStartDecision] = decide_triage_start
    40→    colorize: Callable[[str, str], str] = colorize
    41→
    42→
    43→def _print_triage_start_block(reason: str, *, deps: TriageLifecycleDeps) -> None:
    44→    """Render reason-specific guard guidance for triage auto-start attempts."""
    45→    if reason == "unfinished_triage_stage_records":
    46→        print(
    47→            deps.colorize(
    48→                "  Cannot start triage while previous stage reports are still unconfirmed.",
    49→                "red",
    50→            )
    51→        )
    52→        print(
    53→            deps.colorize(
    54→                "  Confirm or complete the current triage stages first, or pass --attestation "
    55→                "(30+ chars) to override explicitly.",
    56→                "dim",
    57→            )
    58→        )
    59→        return
    60→
    61→    print(deps.colorize("  Cannot start triage while objective backlog is still open.", "red"))
    62→    print(
    63→        deps.colorize(
    64→            "  Finish current objective work first, or pass --attestation "
    65→            "(30+ chars) to override this guard explicitly.",
    66→            "dim",
    67→        )
    68→    )
    69→
    70→
    71→def ensure_triage_started(
    72→    plan: PlanModel,
    73→    *,
    74→    services: TriageServices,
    75→    state: StateModel | None = None,
    76→    attestation: str | None = None,
    77→    log_action: str | None = None,
    78→    log_actor: str = "system",
    79→    log_detail: Mapping[str, object] | None = None,
    80→    start_message: str | None = None,
    81→    start_message_style: str = "cyan",
    82→    deps: TriageLifecycleDeps | None = None,
    83→) -> TriageStartOutcome:
    84→    """Ensure triage stages are injected or return a blocked/already-active outcome."""
    85→    resolved_deps = deps or TriageLifecycleDeps()
    86→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    87→
    88→    if resolved_deps.has_triage_in_queue(plan):
    89→        if state is not None:
    90→            ensure_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
    91→            services.save_plan(plan)
    92→        meta.pop("triage_start_blocked", None)
    93→        return TriageStartOutcome(status="already_active", reason="already_active")
    94→
    95→    decision = resolved_deps.decide_triage_start(
    96→        plan,
    97→        state,
    98→        explicit_start=True,
    99→        attested_override=bool(attestation and len(attestation.strip()) >= 30),
   100→    )
   101→    if decision.action == "defer":
   102→        meta["triage_start_blocked"] = decision.reason
   103→        services.save_plan(plan)
   104→        _print_triage_start_block(decision.reason, deps=resolved_deps)
   105→        return TriageStartOutcome(status="blocked", reason=decision.reason)
   106→
   107→    resolved_deps.inject_triage_stages(plan)
   108→    meta.pop("triage_start_blocked", None)
   109→    meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   110→    if state is not None:
   111→        ensure_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   112→
   113→    if log_action:
   114→        detail = dict(log_detail or {})
   115→        detail.setdefault("injected_stage_ids", list(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS))
   116→        services.append_log_entry(
   117→            plan,
   118→            log_action,
   119→            actor=log_actor,
   120→            detail=detail,
   121→        )
   122→
   123→    services.save_plan(plan)
   124→    if start_message:
   125→        print(
   126→            resolved_deps.colorize(
   127→                start_message,
   128→                start_message_style,
   129→            )
   130→        )
   131→    return TriageStartOutcome(status="started", reason=decision.reason)
   132→
   133→
   134→__all__ = [
   135→    "TriageLifecycleDeps",
   136→    "TriageStartOutcome",
   137→    "ensure_triage_started",
   138→]
   139→
```

```
    49→def print_dashboard_header(si: object, stages: dict, meta: dict, plan: dict) -> None:

    63→    if si.new_since_last:

    88→def _print_completed_guidance(si: object) -> None:
    89→    """Triage completed, no new issues — point to execution."""

    96→def _print_retriage_guidance(si: object, meta: dict) -> None:
    97→    """Triage completed but new issues arrived — offer re-triage paths."""
    98→    has_only_additions = bool(si.new_since_last) and not si.resolved_since_last
    99→    if has_only_additions and meta.get("strategy_summary"):
   100→        print(colorize("  Two paths available:", "yellow"))
   101→        print()
   102→        print(colorize("  To reuse the existing enriched cluster plan (without rewriting clusters):", "cyan"))
   103→        print('    desloppify plan triage --confirm-existing --note "..." --strategy "same" --confirmed "I have reviewed..."')
   104→        print()
   105→        print(colorize("  To re-prioritize and restructure:", "cyan"))
   106→        print(f"    Codex:  {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX}")

   108→        print(colorize(f"    Manual fallback: {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}", "dim"))
   109→    else:
   110→        _print_runner_paths(

   112→            manual_fallback=TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE,
   113→            intro="  Next step:",
   114→        )
   115→        print(colorize("    (themes, root causes, contradictions between issues — NOT a list of IDs)", "dim"))
   116→

   118→def _print_in_progress_guidance(stages: dict, meta: dict, plan: dict) -> None:
   119→    """Triage stages are active — guide through the stage chain."""

   181→
   182→
   183→def print_action_guidance(stages: dict, meta: dict, si: object, plan: dict) -> None:
   184→    """Print the 'What to do' action guidance section based on current stage.
   185→
   186→    Three states, matching the engine's canonical triage lifecycle:
   187→      1. Completed & current — triaged_ids present, no new issues
   188→      2. Needs (re-)triage — observe not done yet (fresh start or new issues)
   189→      3. In progress — observe done, working through remaining stages
   190→    """
   191→    print()
   192→    triage_has_run = bool(meta.get("triaged_ids"))
   193→    has_new_issues = bool(si.new_since_last)
   194→
   195→    if "observe" not in stages:
   196→        if triage_has_run and not has_new_issues:
   197→            _print_completed_guidance(si)
   198→        else:
   199→            _print_retriage_guidance(si, meta)
   200→    else:
   201→        _print_in_progress_guidance(stages, meta, plan)
   202→
   203→
   204→def print_prior_stage_reports(stages: dict) -> None:
   205→    """Print prior stage reports (observe/reflect) as context for current action."""
   206→    if "observe" in stages:
   207→        obs_report = stages["observe"].get("report", "")
   208→        if obs_report:
   209→            print(colorize("\n  Your observe analysis:", "dim"))
   210→            for line in obs_report.strip().splitlines()[:8]:
   211→                print(colorize(f"    {line}", "dim"))
   212→            if len(obs_report.strip().splitlines()) > 8:
   213→                print(colorize("    ...", "dim"))
   214→    if "reflect" in stages:
   215→        ref_report = stages["reflect"].get("report", "")
   216→        if ref_report:
   217→            print(colorize("\n  Your reflect strategy:", "dim"))
   218→            for line in ref_report.strip().splitlines()[:8]:
   219→                print(colorize(f"    {line}", "dim"))
   220→            if len(ref_report.strip().splitlines()) > 8:
   221→                print(colorize("    ...", "dim"))
   222→
   223→
   224→def print_issues_by_dimension(open_issues: dict) -> None:
   225→    """Print issues grouped by dimension with suggestions to surface contradictions."""
   226→    by_dim: dict[str, list[tuple[str, dict]]] = defaultdict(list)
   227→    for fid, issue in open_issues.items():
   228→        detail = issue.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(issue.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   229→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
   230→        by_dim[dim].append((fid, issue))
   231→
   232→    print(colorize("\n  Review issues by dimension:", "cyan"))
   233→    print(colorize("  (Look for contradictions: issues in the same dimension that", "dim"))
   234→    print(colorize("  recommend opposite changes. These must be resolved before clustering.)", "dim"))
   235→    max_per_dim = 5
   236→    for dim in sorted(by_dim, key=lambda d: (-len(by_dim[d]), d)):
   237→        items = by_dim[dim]
   238→        print(colorize(f"\n    {dim} ({len(items)}):", "bold"))
   239→        for fid, issue in items[:max_per_dim]:
   240→            summary = issue.get("summary", "")
   241→            short = short_issue_id(fid)
   242→            detail = issue.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(issue.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   243→            suggestion = (detail.get("suggestion") or "")[:120]
   244→            print(f"      [{short}] {summary}")
   245→            if suggestion:
   246→                print(colorize(f"        → {suggestion}", "dim"))
   247→        if len(items) > max_per_dim:
   248→            print(colorize(f"      ... and {len(items) - max_per_dim} more", "dim"))
   249→    print(colorize("\n  Use hash in commands: desloppify plan skip <hash>  |  desloppify show <hash>", "dim"))
   250→
   251→
   252→def show_plan_summary(plan: dict, state: dict) -> None:
   253→    """Print a compact plan rendering: clusters + queue order + coverage."""
   254→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   255→    active = {
   256→        name: cluster
   257→        for name, cluster in clusters.items()
   258→        if cluster_issue_ids(cluster) and not cluster.get("auto")
   259→    }
   260→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   261→
   262→    if active:
   263→        print(colorize(f"\n  Clusters ({len(active)}):", "bold"))
   264→        for name, cluster in active.items():
   265→            count = len(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   266→            steps = len(cluster.get("action_steps", []))
   267→            desc = (cluster.get("description") or "")[:60]
   268→            print(f"    {name}: {count} items, {steps} steps — {desc}")
   269→
   270→    queue_order = [
   271→        fid
   272→        for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   273→        if not fid.startswith("triage::") and not fid.startswith("workflow::")
   274→    ]
   275→    if queue_order:
   276→        show = min(15, len(queue_order))
   277→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue order (first {show} of {len(queue_order)}):", "bold"))
   278→        for i, fid in enumerate(queue_order[:show]):
   279→            issue = issues.get(fid, {})
   280→            summary = (issue.get("summary") or fid)[:60]
   281→            detector = issue.get("detector", "?")
   282→            cluster_name = find_cluster_for(fid, active)
   283→            print(f"    {i + 1}. [{detector}] {summary}{f' ({cluster_name})' if cluster_name else ''}")
   284→
   285→    organized, total, _ = triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=open_review_ids_from_state(state))
   286→    pct = int(organized / total * 100) if total else 0
   287→    print(colorize(f"\n  Coverage: {organized}/{total} in clusters ({pct}%)", "bold"))
   288→
   289→
   290→__all__ = [
   291→    "print_action_guidance",
   292→    "print_dashboard_header",
   293→    "print_issues_by_dimension",
   294→    "print_prior_stage_reports",
   295→    "show_plan_summary",
   296→]
   297→
```

```
1→"""Stage-gate and fold-confirm helpers for triage completion."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE

    17→
    18→_COMPLETE_AUTO_CONFIRM_STAGE_CONFIG = {
    19→    "organize": {
    20→        "label": "Organize",
    21→        "blocked_heading": "Cannot complete: organize stage not confirmed.",
    22→        "confirm_cmd": "desloppify plan triage --confirm organize",
    23→        "inline_hint": "Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm organize inline.",
    24→    },
    25→    "enrich": {
    26→        "label": "Enrich",
    27→        "blocked_heading": "Cannot complete: enrich stage not confirmed.",
    28→        "confirm_cmd": "desloppify plan triage --confirm enrich",
    29→        "inline_hint": "Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm enrich inline.",
    30→    },
    31→    "sense-check": {
    32→        "label": "Sense-check",
    33→        "blocked_heading": "Cannot complete: sense-check stage not confirmed.",
    34→        "confirm_cmd": "desloppify plan triage --confirm sense-check",
    35→        "inline_hint": "Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm sense-check inline.",
    36→    },
    37→}
    38→
    39→
    40→def _manual_cluster_names(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
    41→    return [name for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items() if not cluster.get("auto")]
    42→
    43→
    44→def _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete(
    45→    *,
    46→    plan: dict,
    47→    stages: dict,
    48→    stage: str,
    49→    attestation: str | None,
    50→    save_plan_fn=None,
    51→) -> bool:
    52→    stage_record = stages.get(stage)
    53→    if stage_record is None:
    54→        return False
    55→
    56→    config = _COMPLETE_AUTO_CONFIRM_STAGE_CONFIG[stage]
    57→    return confirm_stage(
    58→        plan=plan,
    59→        stage_record=stage_record,
    60→        attestation=attestation,
    61→        request=AutoConfirmStageRequest(
    62→            stage_name=stage,
    63→            stage_label=config["label"],
    64→            blocked_heading=config["blocked_heading"],
    65→            confirm_cmd=config["confirm_cmd"],
    66→            inline_hint=config["inline_hint"],
    67→            cluster_names=_manual_cluster_names(plan),
    68→        ),
    69→        save_plan_fn=save_plan_fn,
    70→    )
    71→
    72→
    73→def _require_enrich_stage_for_complete(
    74→    *,
    75→    plan: dict,
    76→    meta: dict,
    77→    stages: dict,
    78→    underspec: list[tuple[str, int, int]] | None = None,
    79→) -> bool:
    80→    missing = missing_stage_prerequisite(stages, flow="complete:enrich")
    81→    if missing is None:
    82→        return True
    83→    if missing.stage_name != "enrich":
    84→        return _require_organize_stage_for_complete(plan=plan, meta=meta, stages=stages)
    85→
    86→    if underspec is None:
    87→        underspec = _underspecified_steps(plan)
    88→    if underspec:
    89→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: enrich stage not done.", "red"))
    90→        print(colorize(f"  {len(underspec)} cluster(s) have underspecified steps (missing detail or issue_refs):", "yellow"))
    91→        for name, bare, total in underspec[:5]:
    92→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps need enrichment", "yellow"))
    93→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"', "dim"))
    94→        print(colorize('  Then: desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "..."', "dim"))
    95→    else:
    96→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: enrich stage not recorded.", "red"))
    97→        print(colorize("  Steps look enriched. Record the stage:", "dim"))
    98→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "..."', "dim"))
    99→    return False
   100→
   101→
   102→def _auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete(
   103→    *,
   104→    plan: dict,
   105→    stages: dict,
   106→    attestation: str | None,
   107→    save_plan_fn=None,
   108→    underspec: list[tuple[str, int, int]] | None = None,
   109→) -> bool:
   110→    if "enrich" not in stages:
   111→        return False
   112→
   113→    if underspec is None:
   114→        underspec = _underspecified_steps(plan)
   115→    if underspec:
   116→        total_bare = sum(n for _, n, _ in underspec)
   117→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot auto-confirm enrich: {total_bare} step(s) still lack detail or issue_refs.", "red"))
   118→        for name, bare, total in underspec[:5]:
   119→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps", "yellow"))
   120→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"', "dim"))
   121→        return False
   122→
   123→    return _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete(
   124→        plan=plan,
   125→        stages=stages,
   126→        stage="enrich",
   127→        attestation=attestation,
   128→        save_plan_fn=save_plan_fn,
   129→    )
   130→
   131→
   132→def _require_sense_check_stage_for_complete(
   133→    *,
   134→    plan: dict,
   135→    meta: dict,
   136→    stages: dict,
   137→) -> bool:
   138→    missing = missing_stage_prerequisite(stages, flow="complete:sense-check")
   139→    if missing is None:
   140→        return True
   141→    if missing.stage_name != "sense-check":
   142→        return _require_enrich_stage_for_complete(plan=plan, meta=meta, stages=stages)
   143→
   144→    print(colorize("  Cannot complete: sense-check stage not recorded.", "red"))
   145→    print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage sense-check --report "..."', "dim"))
   146→    return False
   147→
   148→
   149→def _require_organize_stage_for_complete(
   150→    *,
   151→    plan: dict,
   152→    meta: dict,
   153→    stages: dict,
   154→) -> bool:
   155→    missing = missing_stage_prerequisite(stages, flow="complete:organize")
   156→    if missing is None:
   157→        return True
   158→    if missing.stage_name == "observe":
   159→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no stages done yet.", "red"))
   160→        print(colorize('  Start with: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   161→        return False
   162→
   163→    print(colorize("  Cannot complete: organize stage not done.", "red"))
   164→    gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
   165→    if gaps:
   166→        print(colorize(f"  {len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment:", "yellow"))
   167→        for name, missing in gaps:
   168→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   169→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "..." --steps "step1" "step2"', "dim"))
   170→        print(colorize(f"  Then: {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   171→    else:
   172→        manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   173→        if manual:
   174→            print(colorize("  Clusters are enriched. Record the organize stage first:", "dim"))
   175→            print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   176→        else:
   177→            print(colorize("  Create enriched clusters first, then record organize:", "dim"))
   178→            print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   179→    if meta.get("strategy_summary"):
   180→        print(colorize('  Or fast-track: --confirm-existing --note "why plan is still valid" --strategy "..."', "dim"))
   181→    return False
   182→
   183→
   184→__all__ = [

   187→    "_require_enrich_stage_for_complete",
   188→    "_require_organize_stage_for_complete",
   189→    "_require_sense_check_stage_for_complete",
   190→]
```

```
1→"""Enrich stage command flow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from collections.abc import Callable
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→from desloppify.base.output.user_message import print_user_message
    12→
    13→from .records import record_enrich_stage, resolve_reusable_report
    14→from ..validation.enrich_quality import evaluate_enrich_quality
    15→from ..validation.enrich_checks import (
    16→    _enrich_report_or_error,
    17→    _require_organize_stage_for_enrich,
    18→    _steps_with_bad_paths,
    19→    _steps_without_effort,
    20→    _underspecified_steps,
    21→)
    22→from ..completion_flow import count_log_activity_since
    23→from ..review_coverage import (
    24→    active_triage_issue_ids,
    25→    open_review_ids_from_state,
    26→)
    27→from ..stage_queue import has_triage_in_queue, print_cascade_clear_feedback
    28→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    29→
    30→ColorizeFn = Callable[[str, str], str]
    31→
    32→
    33→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    34→class EnrichStageDeps:
    35→    has_triage_in_queue: Callable[[dict], bool] = has_triage_in_queue
    36→    require_organize_stage_for_enrich: Callable[[dict], bool] = _require_organize_stage_for_enrich
    37→    underspecified_steps: Callable[[dict], list[tuple[str, int, int]]] = _underspecified_steps
    38→    steps_with_bad_paths: Callable[[dict, Path], list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]] = _steps_with_bad_paths
    39→    steps_without_effort: Callable[[dict], list[tuple[str, int, int]]] = _steps_without_effort
    40→    enrich_report_or_error: Callable[[str | None], str | None] = _enrich_report_or_error
    41→    resolve_reusable_report: Callable[[str | None, dict | None], tuple[str | None, bool]] = (
    42→        resolve_reusable_report
    43→    )
    44→    record_enrich_stage: Callable[..., list[str]] = record_enrich_stage
    45→    count_log_activity_since: Callable[[dict, str], dict[str, int]] = count_log_activity_since
    46→    colorize: ColorizeFn = colorize
    47→    print_user_message: Callable[[str], None] = print_user_message
    48→    print_cascade_clear_feedback: Callable[[list[str], dict], None] = print_cascade_clear_feedback
    49→    default_triage_services: Callable[[], TriageServices] = default_triage_services
    50→    get_project_root: Callable[[], Path] | None = None
    51→    auto_confirm_organize_for_complete: Callable[..., bool] | None = None
    52→
    53→
    54→def _resolve_enrich_stage_context(
    55→    *,
    56→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    57→    services: TriageServices | None,
    58→    deps: EnrichStageDeps,
    59→) -> tuple[TriageServices, dict, dict, dict, str | None, str | None]:
    60→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
    61→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
    62→    resolved_services = services or deps.default_triage_services()
    63→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
    64→    state = resolved_services.command_runtime(args).state
    65→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    66→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    67→    return resolved_services, plan, state, stages, report, attestation
    68→
    69→
    70→def _require_confirmed_organize_stage(
    71→    *,
    72→    plan: dict,
    73→    stages: dict,
    74→    attestation: str | None,
    75→    services: TriageServices,
    76→    deps: EnrichStageDeps,
    77→) -> bool:
    78→    if stages.get("organize", {}).get("confirmed_at"):
    79→        return True
    80→    if not attestation:
    81→        print(deps.colorize("  Cannot enrich: organize stage not confirmed.", "red"))
    82→        print(deps.colorize("  Run: desloppify plan triage --confirm organize", "dim"))
    83→        print(deps.colorize("  Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm organize inline.", "dim"))
    84→        return False
    85→
    86→    auto_confirm_organize_for_complete = deps.auto_confirm_organize_for_complete
    87→    if auto_confirm_organize_for_complete is None:
    88→        from ..validation.completion_stages import _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete
    89→
    90→        auto_confirm_organize_for_complete = _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete
    91→    return auto_confirm_organize_for_complete(
    92→        plan=plan,
    93→        stages=stages,
    94→        stage="organize",
    95→        attestation=attestation,
    96→        save_plan_fn=services.save_plan,
    97→    )
    98→
    99→
   100→def _require_cluster_update_activity(
   101→    *,
   102→    plan: dict,
   103→    state: dict,
   104→    stages: dict,
   105→    attestation: str | None,
   106→    deps: EnrichStageDeps,
   107→) -> bool:
   108→    organize_ts = stages.get("organize", {}).get("timestamp", "")
   109→    if not organize_ts:
   110→        return True
   111→    activity = deps.count_log_activity_since(plan, organize_ts)
   112→    update_ops = activity.get("cluster_update", 0)
   113→    if update_ops != 0 or not open_review_ids_from_state(state):
   114→        return True
   115→    if attestation and len(attestation.strip()) >= 40:
   116→        print(
   117→            deps.colorize(
   118→                "  Note: 0 cluster_update ops logged since organize. "
   119→                "Proceeding with attestation override.",
   120→                "yellow",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→        return True
   124→    print(
   125→        deps.colorize(
   126→            "  Cannot enrich: no cluster_update operations logged since organize.",
   127→            "red",
   128→        )
   129→    )
   130→    print(
   131→        deps.colorize(
   132→            "  Enriching steps requires running cluster update commands.\n"
   133→            '  e.g. desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "..."',
   134→            "dim",
   135→        )
   136→    )
   137→    print(
   138→        deps.colorize(
   139→            '  Override: pass --attestation "reason why no update ops" (40+ chars).',
   140→            "dim",
   141→        )
   142→    )
   143→    return False
   144→
   145→
   146→def _print_underspecified_step_error(
   147→    underspec: list[tuple[str, int, int]],
   148→    *,
   149→    deps: EnrichStageDeps,
   150→) -> None:
   151→    total_bare = sum(n for _, n, _ in underspec)
   152→    print(
   153→        deps.colorize(
   154→            f"  Cannot enrich: {total_bare} step(s) across {len(underspec)} cluster(s) lack detail or issue_refs:",
   155→            "red",
   156→        )
   157→    )
   158→    for name, bare, total in underspec:
   159→        print(deps.colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps need enrichment", "yellow"))
   160→    print()
   161→    print(
   162→        deps.colorize(
   163→            "  Every step needs --detail (sub-points) or --issue-refs (for auto-completion).",
   164→            "dim",
   165→        )
   166→    )
   167→    print(deps.colorize("  Fix:", "dim"))
   168→    print(
   169→        deps.colorize(
   170→            '    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"',
   171→            "dim",
   172→        )
   173→    )
   174→    print(
   175→        deps.colorize(
   176→            "  You can also still reorganize: add/remove clusters, reorder, etc.",
   177→            "dim",
   178→        )
   179→    )
   180→
   181→
   182→def _print_enrich_warnings(
   183→    *,
   184→    report,
   185→    deps: EnrichStageDeps,
   186→) -> None:
   187→    bad_paths = report.warning("bad_paths")
   188→    if bad_paths:
   189→        print(
   190→            deps.colorize(
   191→                f"  Warning: {bad_paths.total} file path(s) in step details don't exist on disk:",
   192→                "yellow",
   193→            )
   194→        )
   195→        for name, step_num, paths in bad_paths.rows[:5]:
   196→            print(deps.colorize(f"    {name} step {step_num}: {', '.join(paths[:3])}", "yellow"))
   197→        print(
   198→            deps.colorize(
   199→                "  Fix paths before confirming enrich (confirmation will block on bad paths).",
   200→                "dim",
   201→            )
   202→        )
   203→
   204→    untagged = report.warning("missing_effort")
   205→    if untagged:
   206→        print(deps.colorize(f"  Note: {untagged.total} step(s) have no effort tag.", "yellow"))
   207→        print(
   208→            deps.colorize(
   209→                "  Consider: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --effort small",
   210→                "dim",
   211→            )
   212→        )
   213→
   214→
   215→def run_stage_enrich(
   216→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   217→    *,
   218→    services: TriageServices | None,
   219→    deps: EnrichStageDeps | None = None,
   220→) -> None:
   221→    """Record the ENRICH stage with validation and optional auto-confirm."""
   222→    resolved_deps = deps or EnrichStageDeps()
   223→    resolved_services, plan, state, stages, report, attestation = _resolve_enrich_stage_context(
   224→        args=args,
   225→        services=services,
   226→        deps=resolved_deps,
   227→    )
   228→
   229→    if not resolved_deps.has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   230→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to enrich.", "yellow"))
   231→        return
   232→
   233→    existing_stage = stages.get("enrich")
   234→    report, is_reuse = resolved_deps.resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
   235→
   236→    if not resolved_deps.require_organize_stage_for_enrich(stages):
   237→        return
   238→
   239→    if not _require_confirmed_organize_stage(
   240→        plan=plan,
   241→        stages=stages,
   242→        attestation=attestation,
   243→        services=resolved_services,
   244→        deps=resolved_deps,
   245→    ):
   246→        return
   247→
   248→    if not is_reuse and not _require_cluster_update_activity(
   249→        plan=plan,
   250→        state=state,
   251→        stages=stages,
   252→        attestation=attestation,
   253→        deps=resolved_deps,
   254→    ):
   255→        return
   256→
   257→    get_project_root = resolved_deps.get_project_root
   258→    if get_project_root is None:
   259→        from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
   260→
   261→    triage_ids = active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state) or None
   262→    quality_report = evaluate_enrich_quality(
   263→        plan,
   264→        get_project_root(),
   265→        phase_label="enrich",
   266→        bad_paths_severity="warning",
   267→        missing_effort_severity="warning",
   268→        include_missing_issue_refs=False,
   269→        include_vague_detail=False,
   270→        stale_issue_refs_severity=None,
   271→        triage_issue_ids=triage_ids,
   272→    )
   273→    underspec = quality_report.failure("underspecified")
   274→    total_bare = underspec.total if underspec else 0
   275→
   276→    if underspec:
   277→        _print_underspecified_step_error(underspec.rows, deps=resolved_deps)
   278→        return
   279→
   280→    print(resolved_deps.colorize("  All steps have detail or issue_refs.", "green"))
   281→    _print_enrich_warnings(report=quality_report, deps=resolved_deps)
   282→
   283→    report = resolved_deps.enrich_report_or_error(report)
   284→    if report is None:
   285→        return
   286→
   287→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
   288→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   289→    cleared = resolved_deps.record_enrich_stage(
   290→        stages,
   291→        report=report,
   292→        shallow_count=total_bare,
   293→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   294→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   295→    )
   296→
   297→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   298→
   299→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   300→        plan,
   301→        "triage_enrich",
   302→        actor="user",
   303→        detail={"shallow_count": total_bare, "reuse": is_reuse},
   304→    )
   305→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   306→
   307→    print(
   308→        resolved_deps.colorize(
   309→            f"  Enrich stage recorded: {total_bare} step(s) still without detail.",
   310→            "green",
   311→        )
   312→    )
   313→    if is_reuse:
   314→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Enrich data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   315→        if cleared:
   316→            resolved_deps.print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   317→    else:
   318→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Now confirm the enrichment.", "yellow"))
   319→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm enrich", "dim"))
   320→
   321→    resolved_deps.print_user_message(
   322→        "Enrich recorded. Before confirming — check the subagent's"
   323→        " work. Could a developer who has never seen this code"
   324→        " execute every step without asking a question? Every step"
   325→        " needs: file path, specific location, specific action."
   326→        " 'Refactor X' fails. 'Extract lines 45-89 into Y' passes."
   327→    )
   328→
   329→
   330→def cmd_stage_enrich(
   331→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   332→    *,
   333→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   334→) -> None:
   335→    """Public entrypoint for enrich stage recording."""
   336→    run_stage_enrich(args, services=services)
   337→
   338→
   339→__all__ = ["ColorizeFn", "EnrichStageDeps", "cmd_stage_enrich", "run_stage_enrich"]
   340→
```

```
1→"""Progress rendering helpers for triage command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.issue_id_display import short_issue_id
     6→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage.review_coverage import (
     7→    cluster_issue_ids,
     8→    triage_coverage,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→
    12→
    13→def _print_progress(plan: dict, open_issues: dict) -> None:
    14→    """Show cluster state and unclustered issues."""
    15→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    16→    _print_active_clusters(clusters)
    17→    unclustered = _collect_unclustered_issues(clusters, open_issues)
    18→    _print_unclustered_issues(plan, open_issues, unclustered)
    19→
    20→
    21→def _print_active_clusters(clusters: dict[str, dict]) -> None:
    22→    """Print current clusters that contain issues."""
    23→    active_clusters = {
    24→        name: cluster for name, cluster in clusters.items() if cluster_issue_ids(cluster)
    25→    }
    26→    if not active_clusters:
    27→        return
    28→    print(colorize("\n  Current clusters:", "cyan"))
    29→    for name, cluster in active_clusters.items():
    30→        count = len(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
    31→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    32→        tag_str = _cluster_tag_summary(cluster)
    33→        desc_str = f" - {desc}" if desc else ""
    34→        print(f"    {name}: {count} items{tag_str}{desc_str}")
    35→
    36→
    37→def _cluster_tag_summary(cluster: dict) -> str:
    38→    """Build compact tag summary for one cluster row."""
    39→    steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
    40→    auto = cluster.get("auto", False)
    41→    tags: list[str] = []
    42→    tags.append("auto" if auto else "manual")
    43→    tags.append("desc" if cluster.get("description") else "no desc")
    44→    if steps:
    45→        tags.append(f"{len(steps)} steps")
    46→    elif not auto:
    47→        tags.append("no steps")
    48→    return f" [{', '.join(tags)}]"
    49→
    50→
    51→def _collect_unclustered_issues(clusters: dict[str, dict], open_issues: dict) -> list[str]:
    52→    """Return issue IDs that are not attached to any cluster."""
    53→    all_clustered: set[str] = set()
    54→    for cluster in clusters.values():
    55→        all_clustered.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
    56→    return [issue_id for issue_id in open_issues if issue_id not in all_clustered]
    57→
    58→
    59→def _print_unclustered_issues(
    60→    plan: dict,
    61→    open_issues: dict,
    62→    unclustered: list[str],
    63→) -> None:
    64→    """Print unclustered issues summary or all-clustered confirmation."""
    65→    if unclustered:
    66→        print(colorize(f"\n  {len(unclustered)} issues not yet in a cluster:", "yellow"))
    67→        for issue_id in unclustered[:10]:
    68→            issue = open_issues[issue_id]
    69→            dim = (
    70→                (issue.get("detail", {}) or {}).get("dimension", "")
    71→                if isinstance(issue.get("detail"), dict)
    72→                else ""
    73→            )
    74→            short = short_issue_id(issue_id)
    75→            print(f"    [{short}] [{dim}] {issue.get('summary', '')}")
    76→        if len(unclustered) > 10:
    77→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 10} more", "dim"))
    78→        return
    79→    if open_issues:
    80→        organized, total, _ = triage_coverage(plan)
    81→        print(colorize(f"\n  All {organized}/{total} issues are in clusters.", "green"))
    82→
```

```
1→"""Plan-state accessors for triage workflow helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, cast
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import Cluster, PlanModel
     8→
     9→
    10→def ensure_queue_order(plan: PlanModel) -> list[str]:
    11→    """Return queue order, creating the stored list when missing."""
    12→    order = plan.get("queue_order")
    13→    if isinstance(order, list):
    14→        return order
    15→    normalized: list[str] = []
    16→    plan["queue_order"] = normalized
    17→    return normalized
    18→
    19→
    20→def ensure_skipped_map(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, Any]:
    21→    """Return skipped metadata, creating the stored map when missing."""
    22→    skipped = plan.get("skipped")
    23→    if isinstance(skipped, dict):
    24→        return cast(dict[str, Any], skipped)
    25→    normalized: dict[str, Any] = {}
    26→    plan["skipped"] = normalized
    27→    return normalized
    28→
    29→
    30→def ensure_cluster_map(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
    31→    """Return cluster map, creating the stored map when missing."""
    32→    clusters = plan.get("clusters")
    33→    if isinstance(clusters, dict):
    34→        return cast(dict[str, Cluster], clusters)
    35→    normalized: dict[str, Cluster] = {}
    36→    plan["clusters"] = normalized
    37→    return normalized
    38→
    39→
    40→def ensure_triage_meta(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, Any]:
    41→    """Return triage metadata, creating the stored map when missing."""
    42→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta")
    43→    if isinstance(meta, dict):
    44→        return cast(dict[str, Any], meta)
    45→    normalized: dict[str, Any] = {}
    46→    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = normalized
    47→    return normalized
    48→
    49→
    50→def ensure_execution_log(plan: PlanModel) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    51→    """Return execution log, creating the stored list when missing."""
    52→    log = plan.get("execution_log")
    53→    if isinstance(log, list):
    54→        normalized = [entry for entry in log if isinstance(entry, dict)]
    55→        if normalized is not log:
    56→            plan["execution_log"] = normalized
    57→        return normalized
    58→    normalized: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    59→    plan["execution_log"] = normalized
    60→    return normalized
    61→
    62→
    63→def normalized_issue_id_list(raw_ids: object) -> list[str]:
    64→    """Normalize a raw stored ID collection to strings only."""
    65→    if not isinstance(raw_ids, list):
    66→        return []
    67→    return [issue_id for issue_id in raw_ids if isinstance(issue_id, str)]
    68→
    69→
    70→__all__ = [
    71→    "ensure_cluster_map",
    72→    "ensure_execution_log",
    73→    "ensure_queue_order",
    74→    "ensure_skipped_map",
    75→    "ensure_triage_meta",
    76→    "normalized_issue_id_list",
    77→]
    78→
```

```
    27→def _cluster_tags(cluster: dict) -> str:
    28→    """Build display tags for cluster metadata."""
    29→    desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    30→    steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
    31→    auto = cluster.get("auto", False)
    32→    tags: list[str] = []
    33→    if auto:
    34→        tags.append("auto")

    41→    elif not auto:
    42→        tags.append("no steps")
    43→    return f" [{', '.join(tags)}]"

    45→
    46→def _print_active_clusters(active_clusters: dict[str, dict]) -> None:
    47→    """Render currently active clusters with tags and descriptions."""
    48→    if not active_clusters:

    50→    print(colorize("\n  Current clusters:", "cyan"))
    51→    for name, cluster in active_clusters.items():
    52→        count = len(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
    53→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    54→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    55→        print(f"    {name}: {count} items{_cluster_tags(cluster)}{desc_str}")

    58→def _unclustered_issue_ids(clusters: dict, open_issues: dict) -> list[str]:

    62→        all_clustered.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
    63→    return [fid for fid in open_issues if fid not in all_clustered]

    65→

    94→
    95→def print_reflect_result(
    96→    *,
    97→    issue_count: int,
    98→    recurring_dims: list[str],
    99→    recurring: dict,
   100→    report: str,
   101→    is_reuse: bool,
   102→    cleared: list,
   103→    stages: dict,
   104→) -> None:
   105→    """Print reflect stage output including briefing box and next steps."""
   106→    print(colorize(f"  Reflect stage recorded: {issue_count} issues, {len(recurring_dims)} recurring dimension(s).", "green"))
   107→    if is_reuse:
   108→        print(colorize("  Reflect data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   109→        if cleared:
   110→            print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   111→    else:
   112→        print(colorize("  Now confirm your strategy.", "yellow"))
   113→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm reflect", "dim"))
   114→    if recurring_dims:
   115→        for dim in recurring_dims:
   116→            info = recurring[dim]
   117→            print(colorize(f"    {dim}: {len(info['resolved'])} resolved, {len(info['open'])} still open", "dim"))
   118→
   119→    print()
   120→    print(colorize("  ┌─ Strategic briefing (share with user before organizing) ─┐", "cyan"))
   121→    for line in report.strip().splitlines():
   122→        print(colorize(f"  │ {line}", "cyan"))
   123→    print(colorize("  └" + "─" * 57 + "┘", "cyan"))
   124→    print_user_message(
   125→        "Reflect recorded. Before confirming — check the"
   126→        " subagent's report. Is it a strategy or just observe"
   127→        " restated? It should include a concrete cluster"
   128→        " blueprint: which clusters, which issues, what to skip"
   129→        " (with per-issue reasons). Confirm when the blueprint"
   130→        " is specific enough for organize to execute mechanically."
   131→    )
   132→
   133→
   134→def print_organize_result(
   135→    *,
   136→    manual_clusters: list[str],
   137→    plan: dict,
   138→    report: str,
   139→    is_reuse: bool,
   140→    cleared: list,
   141→    stages: dict,
   142→) -> None:
   143→    """Print organize stage output including cluster summary and next steps."""
   144→    print(colorize(f"  Organize stage recorded: {len(manual_clusters)} enriched cluster(s).", "green"))
   145→    if is_reuse:
   146→        print(colorize("  Organize data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   147→        if cleared:
   148→            print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   149→    else:
   150→        print(colorize("  Now confirm the plan.", "yellow"))
   151→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm organize", "dim"))
   152→    for name in manual_clusters:
   153→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(name, {})
   154→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
   155→        desc = cluster.get("description", "")
   156→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   157→        print(colorize(f"    {name}: {len(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))} issues, {len(steps)} steps{desc_str}", "dim"))
   158→
   159→    print()
   160→    print(colorize("  ┌─ Prioritized organization (share with user) ────────────┐", "cyan"))
   161→    for line in report.strip().splitlines():
   162→        print(colorize(f"  │ {line}", "cyan"))
   163→    print(colorize("  └" + "─" * 57 + "┘", "cyan"))
   164→    print_user_message(
   165→        "Organize recorded. Before confirming — does the"
   166→        " organize output match the reflect blueprint? Clusters"
   167→        " by file area (same PR), not by theme? Step count <"
   168→        " issue count (consolidated)? Cluster names describe"
   169→        " locations, not problem types? This should read like"
   170→        " a set of PR plans."
   171→    )
   172→
   173→
   174→def print_reflect_dashboard(
   175→    si: object,
   176→    plan: dict,
   177→    *,
   178→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   179→) -> None:
   180→    """Show completed clusters, resolved issues, and recurring patterns."""
   181→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   182→    completed = getattr(si, "completed_clusters", [])
   183→    resolved = getattr(si, "resolved_issues", {})
   184→    open_issues = getattr(si, "open_issues", {})
   185→
   186→    _print_completed_clusters(completed)
   187→    _print_resolved_issue_deltas(resolved)
   188→    _print_recurring_or_first_triage(
   189→        recurring=resolved_services.detect_recurring_patterns(open_issues, resolved),
   190→        completed=completed,
   191→        resolved=resolved,
   192→    )
   193→
   194→
   195→def _print_completed_clusters(completed: list[dict]) -> None:
   196→    """Render previously completed cluster snapshots."""
   197→    if not completed:
   198→        return
   199→    print(colorize("\n  Previously completed clusters:", "cyan"))
   200→    for cluster in completed[:10]:
   201→        name = cluster.get("name", "?")
   202→        count = len(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   203→        thesis = cluster.get("thesis", "")
   204→        print(f"    {name}: {count} issues")
   205→        if thesis:
   206→            print(colorize(f"      {thesis}", "dim"))
   207→        for step in cluster.get("action_steps", [])[:3]:
   208→            print(colorize(f"      - {step}", "dim"))
   209→    if len(completed) > 10:
   210→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(completed) - 10} more", "dim"))
   211→
   212→
   213→def _print_resolved_issue_deltas(resolved: dict[str, dict]) -> None:
   214→    """Render issues resolved since the previous triage cycle."""
   215→    if not resolved:
   216→        return
   217→    print(colorize(f"\n  Resolved issues since last triage: {len(resolved)}", "cyan"))
   218→    for fid, issue in sorted(resolved.items())[:10]:
   219→        status = issue.get("status", "")
   220→        summary = issue.get("summary", "")
   221→        detail = issue.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(issue.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   222→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "")
   223→        print(f"    [{status}] [{dim}] {summary}")
   224→        print(colorize(f"      {fid}", "dim"))
   225→    if len(resolved) > 10:
   226→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(resolved) - 10} more", "dim"))
   227→
   228→
   229→def _print_recurring_or_first_triage(
   230→    *,
   231→    recurring: dict,
   232→    completed: list[dict],
   233→    resolved: dict,
   234→) -> None:
   235→    """Render recurring-pattern warnings or first-triage guidance."""
   236→    if recurring:
   237→        print(colorize("\n  Recurring patterns detected:", "yellow"))
   238→        for dim, info in sorted(recurring.items()):
   239→            resolved_count = len(info["resolved"])
   240→            open_count = len(info["open"])
   241→            label = "potential loop" if open_count >= resolved_count else "root cause unaddressed"
   242→            print(colorize(f"    {dim}: {resolved_count} resolved, {open_count} still open — {label}", "yellow"))
   243→        return
   244→    if completed or resolved:
   245→        return
   246→    print(colorize("\n  First triage — no prior work to compare against.", "dim"))
   247→    print(colorize("  Focus your reflect report on your strategy:", "yellow"))
   248→    print(colorize("  - How will you resolve contradictions you identified in observe?", "dim"))
   249→    print(colorize("  - Which issues will you cluster together vs defer?", "dim"))
   250→    print(colorize("  - What's the overall arc of work and why?", "dim"))
   251→
   252→
   253→def cmd_triage_dashboard(
   254→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   255→    *,
   256→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   257→) -> None:
   258→    """Default view: show issues, stage progress, and next command."""
   259→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   260→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   261→    state = runtime.state
   262→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   263→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   264→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   265→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   266→
   267→    print_dashboard_header(si, stages, meta, plan)
   268→    print_action_guidance(stages, meta, si, plan)
   269→    print_prior_stage_reports(stages)
   270→    print_issues_by_dimension(si.open_issues)
   271→
   272→    if "observe" in stages and "reflect" not in stages:
   273→        print_reflect_dashboard(si, plan, services=resolved_services)
   274→
   275→    print_progress(plan, si.open_issues)
   276→
   277→
   278→__all__ = [
   279→    "cmd_triage_dashboard",
   280→    "print_organize_result",
   281→    "print_progress",
   282→    "print_reflect_dashboard",
   283→    "print_reflect_result",
   284→    "print_stage_progress",
   285→    "show_plan_summary",
   286→]
   287→
```

```
    28→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:

    40→) -> None:

    42→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    43→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    44→    plan_skipped_total = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    45→
    46→    # Count subjective items in the visible list
    47→    subjective = sum(
    48→        1 for it in items if it.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    49→    )
    50→
    51→    # Count plan-ordered items (minus skipped)
    52→    queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    53→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    54→    plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    55→
    56→    breakdown = QueueBreakdown(
    57→        queue_total=non_skipped,
    58→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    59→        skipped=plan_skipped_total,
    60→        subjective=subjective,
```

```
    10→
    11→def _require_triage_pending(plan: dict, *, action: str) -> bool:
    12→    """Require at least one triage stage ID to be present in queue for an action."""
    13→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))

    18→
    19→
    20→def _validate_stage_report(
    21→    report: str | None,
    22→    *,
    23→    stage: str,
    24→    min_chars: int,
    25→    missing_guidance: list[str] | None = None,
    26→    short_guidance: list[str] | None = None,
    27→) -> str | None:
    28→    """Validate staged report presence/length and print consistent guidance."""
    29→    if not report:
    30→        print(colorize(f"  --report is required for --stage {stage}.", "red"))
    31→        for line in missing_guidance or []:
    32→            print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
    33→        return None
    34→    cleaned = report.strip()
    35→    if len(cleaned) < min_chars:
    36→        print(
    37→            colorize(
    38→                f"  Report too short: {len(cleaned)} chars (minimum {min_chars}).",
    39→                "red",
    40→            )
    41→        )
    42→        for line in short_guidance or []:
    43→            print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
    44→        return None
    45→    return cleaned
    46→
    47→
    48→def unenriched_clusters(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, list[str]]]:
    49→    """Return clusters with issues that are missing required enrichment.
    50→
    51→    Requirements:
    52→    - Every cluster needs a description and at least one action_step.
    53→    - Small clusters (< 5 issues) need at least 1 action step per issue,
    54→      so each item has a concrete plan. Large clusters (>= 5) just need
    55→      steps overall (cluster-level plan is sufficient).
    56→    """
    57→    gaps: list[tuple[str, list[str]]] = []
    58→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    59→        issue_ids = cluster_issue_ids(cluster)
    60→        if not issue_ids:
    61→            continue
    62→        if cluster.get("auto"):
    63→            continue
    64→        missing: list[str] = []
    65→        if not cluster.get("description"):
    66→            missing.append("description")
    67→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
    68→        issue_count = len(issue_ids)
    69→        if not steps:
    70→            missing.append("action_steps")
    71→        elif issue_count < 5 and len(steps) < issue_count:
    72→            missing.append(
    73→                f"action_steps (have {len(steps)}, need >= {issue_count} for small cluster)"
    74→            )
    75→        if missing:
    76→            gaps.append((name, missing))
    77→    return gaps
    78→
    79→
    80→def unclustered_review_issues(plan: dict, state: dict | None = None) -> list[str]:
    81→    """Return review issue IDs that aren't in any manual cluster.
    82→
    83→    When *state* is provided, uses open review/concerns issues from state
    84→    (the canonical source). Falls back to scanning queue_order for backwards
    85→    compatibility.
    86→    """
    87→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    88→    clustered_ids: set[str] = set()
    89→    for cluster in clusters.values():
    90→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
    91→            clustered_ids.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
    92→    skipped_ids = {
    93→        fid for fid in (plan.get("skipped", {}) or {}).keys() if isinstance(fid, str)
    94→    }
    95→
    96→    if state is not None:
    97→        review_ids = [
    98→            fid for fid, finding in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    99→            if finding.get("status") == "open"
   100→            and finding.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   101→        ]
   102→        frozen_ids = (plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}) or {}).get("active_triage_issue_ids")
   103→        if isinstance(frozen_ids, list) and frozen_ids:
   104→            frozen_id_set = live_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   105→            review_ids = [fid for fid in review_ids if fid in frozen_id_set]
   106→    else:
   107→        review_ids = [
   108→            fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   109→            if not fid.startswith("triage::") and not fid.startswith("workflow::")
   110→            and (fid.startswith("review::") or fid.startswith("concerns::"))
   111→        ]
   112→
   113→    return [
   114→        fid for fid in review_ids
   115→        if fid not in clustered_ids and fid not in skipped_ids
   116→    ]
   117→
```

Now I have a thorough understanding of the codebase. Let me design the implementation plan.

---

## Implementation Plan: Holistic Triage Reconciliation

### Overview of the Problem

There are four independent derivations of "triage status" across the codebase:

1. **`print_stage_progress()`** in `primitives.py` -- uses `TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES` (presence-only check) to determine "current" stage
2. **`missing_stage_prerequisite()`** in `stage_policy.py` -- uses `_STAGE_PREREQUISITES` (with `require_confirmation` flags) to gate commands
3. **`triage_phase_banner()`** in `engine/plan_triage.py` -- derives banner text by inspecting `queue_order` and `epic_triage_meta` directly
4. **`triage_guardrail_messages()`** in `guardrails.py` -- combines `is_triage_stale()` + `compute_new_issue_ids()` + `triage_phase_banner()`

Coverage is similarly fragmented: `triage_coverage()`, `undispositioned_triage_issue_ids()`, and `plan_aware_queue_breakdown()` each independently walk plan state.

The "new issues" confusion is that `compute_new_issue_ids()` compares against `triaged_ids` (set at triage completion), not `active_triage_issue_ids` (set at triage start). So mid-cycle, issues frozen into the active set still appear as "new."

---

### Step 1: Introduce `TriageSnapshot` dataclass in `engine/_plan/triage/snapshot.py`

**New file**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/triage/snapshot.py`

This is a pure-data, frozen dataclass built once per command invocation:

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class StageStatus:
    """Status of one triage stage."""
    name: str
    recorded: bool
    confirmed: bool
    
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TriageSnapshot:
    """Single derived view of all triage state for one command invocation."""
    
    # Stage progression
    stages: tuple[StageStatus, ...]  # all 6 stages in order
    current_stage: str | None         # first stage that is "actionable"
    blocked_reason: str | None        # why current stage can't proceed (or None)
    next_action: str | None           # suggested command for next step
    completed_count: int              # how many stages are recorded
    confirmed_count: int              # how many stages are confirmed
    
    # Coverage (frozen-set authoritative during cycle)
    frozen_issue_ids: frozenset[str]  # active_triage_issue_ids (empty if no cycle)
    live_open_ids: frozenset[str]     # current open review/concerns from state
    new_since_triage_ids: frozenset[str]  # live_open - (triaged | frozen)
    undispositioned_ids: frozenset[str]   # frozen issues lacking cluster/skip/dismiss
    
    # Cluster coverage
    organized_count: int              # issues in manual clusters
    total_in_scope: int               # total issues in triage scope
    
    # Lifecycle flags
    has_triage_in_queue: bool
    is_triage_stale: bool             # new issues exist outside frozen+triaged
    triage_has_completed: bool        # triaged_ids is non-empty
    cycle_active: bool                # frozen_issue_ids is non-empty OR stages in queue
```

**Builder function** `build_triage_snapshot(plan, state) -> TriageSnapshot`:
- Reads `epic_triage_meta` once
- Computes all stage statuses from `triage_stages` dict using the unified dependency definition (see Step 2)
- Determines `current_stage` as the first unrecorded stage whose prerequisites (with confirmation where required) are met
- Computes `new_since_triage_ids` as `live_open_ids - triaged_ids - frozen_issue_ids` (this fixes the mid-cycle confusion)
- Calls existing helpers for undispositioned/coverage counts but caches results

**Key design decision**: `TriageSnapshot` lives in `engine/_plan/triage/` (not `app/`) because it is a pure derivation from plan+state. No imports from `app/`.

**Files to create**:
- `desloppify/engine/_plan/triage/snapshot.py` -- `TriageSnapshot`, `StageStatus`, `build_triage_snapshot()`

**Files to modify**:
- `desloppify/engine/plan_triage.py` -- re-export `TriageSnapshot`, `build_triage_snapshot`

---

### Step 2: Unify stage progression into a single canonical definition

**Problem**: `TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES` in `playbook.py` and `_STAGE_PREREQUISITES` in `stage_policy.py` are two independent representations of the same DAG. The former is presence-only; the latter supports `require_confirmation`.

**Solution**: Merge into a single `TRIAGE_STAGE_PREREQUISITES` definition in `playbook.py` that includes the confirmation requirement. Delete `_STAGE_PREREQUISITES` from `stage_policy.py`.

In `playbook.py`, replace `_TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCY_ITEMS` with:

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class StagePrerequisite:
    stage_name: str
    require_confirmation: bool = False

TRIAGE_STAGE_PREREQUISITES: dict[str, tuple[StagePrerequisite, ...]] = {
    "observe": (),
    "reflect": (StagePrerequisite("observe"),),
    "organize": (StagePrerequisite("observe"), StagePrerequisite("reflect")),
    "enrich": (
        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
        StagePrerequisite("reflect"),
        StagePrerequisite("organize", require_confirmation=True),
    ),
    "sense-check": (StagePrerequisite("enrich", require_confirmation=True),),
    "commit": (StagePrerequisite("sense-check"),),
}

# Derive the simple set-based form for backward compat with synthetic.py
TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES: dict[str, set[str]] = {
    stage: {p.stage_name for p in prereqs}
    for stage, prereqs in TRIAGE_STAGE_PREREQUISITES.items()
}
```

Then add a single function that both display and commands use:

```python
def compute_stage_status(
    stages: dict,
    prerequisites: dict[str, tuple[StagePrerequisite, ...]] = TRIAGE_STAGE_PREREQUISITES,
) -> tuple[list[StageStatus], str | None, str | None]:
    """Return (stage_statuses, current_stage_name, blocked_reason).
    
    current_stage is the first stage whose prerequisites are met
    but is not yet recorded. blocked_reason explains if the current
    stage's upstream is recorded but not confirmed.
    """
```

This function replaces the inline logic in `print_stage_progress()` (line 27 presence check) and the `missing_stage_prerequisite()` function in `stage_policy.py`.

**Files to modify**:
- `desloppify/engine/_plan/triage/playbook.py` -- add `StagePrerequisite` (move from stage_policy.py), `TRIAGE_STAGE_PREREQUISITES`, `compute_stage_status()`
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/stage_policy.py` -- delete `StagePrerequisite`, `_STAGE_PREREQUISITES`, `missing_stage_prerequisite()`; import from playbook. Keep `require_prerequisite()` but have it call `compute_stage_status()`.
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/display/primitives.py` -- rewrite `print_stage_progress()` to consume `TriageSnapshot.stages` or call `compute_stage_status()` directly
- `desloppify/engine/_work_queue/synthetic.py` -- keep using `TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES` (derived), no change needed

The completion-gate functions in `completion_stages.py` currently call `missing_stage_prerequisite()` with flow strings like `"complete:organize"`. These collapse into calling `compute_stage_status()` directly, since the prerequisites for completion are just "all stages up to X must be recorded." The `complete:*` pseudo-flows in `_STAGE_PREREQUISITES` can be replaced by checking the snapshot's stage list.

### Step 3: Fix new-issue detection to exclude active-cycle issues

**Problem**: `compute_new_issue_ids()` in `stale.py` does `open_review_ids(state) - triaged_ids`. Mid-cycle, `triaged_ids` hasn't been updated, so frozen active issues appear "new."

**Fix**: Change `compute_new_issue_ids()` to also subtract `active_triage_issue_ids`:

```python
def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    triaged = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
    active = set(meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids", []))
    known = triaged | active
    return open_review_ids(state) - known if known else set()
```

Similarly update `is_triage_stale()`:

```python
def is_triage_stale(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> bool:
    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
    active_ids = set(meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids", []))
    known = triaged_ids | active_ids
    return bool(open_review_ids(state) - known)
```

The `TriageSnapshot` builder will use this corrected logic for `new_since_triage_ids`.

**Files to modify**:
- `desloppify/engine/_plan/policy/stale.py` -- update `compute_new_issue_ids()` and `is_triage_stale()` to subtract active triage IDs
- `desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/triage.py` -- update `_new_review_ids_since_triage()` to match (it has its own inline version)

**Tests to update**:
- `desloppify/tests/plan/test_stale_policy.py` -- add test case: mid-cycle active IDs are not "new"
- `desloppify/tests/plan/test_epic_triage.py` -- verify sync behavior with active set

### Step 4: Thread `TriageSnapshot` through all consumers

Replace the independent computations at each call site with `build_triage_snapshot()`.

**4a. Guardrails** (`guardrails.py`):

`triage_guardrail_status()` currently calls `is_triage_stale()` + `compute_new_issue_ids()` independently. Replace with:

```python
def triage_guardrail_status(*, plan=None, state=None) -> TriageGuardrailResult:
    resolved_plan = plan if isinstance(plan, dict) else load_plan()
    snapshot = build_triage_snapshot(resolved_plan, state or {})
    return TriageGuardrailResult(
        is_stale=snapshot.is_triage_stale,
        new_ids=set(snapshot.new_since_triage_ids),
        _plan=resolved_plan,
        _snapshot=snapshot,
    )
```

Add `_snapshot` field to `TriageGuardrailResult` so downstream consumers can reuse it.

`triage_guardrail_messages()` currently calls `triage_phase_banner()` independently. Rewrite `triage_phase_banner()` to accept an optional `TriageSnapshot` and derive its text from it, or build its own if none passed.

**4b. Triage dashboard** (`dashboard.py`):

`cmd_triage_dashboard()` builds plan+state+stages manually. Add:
```python
snapshot = build_triage_snapshot(plan, state)
```
Pass `snapshot` to `print_dashboard_header()`, `print_action_guidance()`, and `print_progress()`.

`print_stage_progress()` switches from inline TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES check to consuming `snapshot.stages`.

`print_action_guidance()` currently re-derives "triage_has_run" and "has_new_issues" from meta -- replace with `snapshot.triage_has_completed` and `bool(snapshot.new_since_triage_ids)`.

**4c. Queue render** (`queue_render.py`):

`_print_queue_header()` manually builds a partial `QueueBreakdown`. It also calls `compute_new_issue_ids()` directly. Replace the `new_count` computation with snapshot access.

**4d. `triage_phase_banner()`** (`engine/plan_triage.py`):

Rewrite to accept optional `TriageSnapshot`. Currently it independently checks `queue_order`, `undispositioned_issue_count`, `triage_recommended`, and `has_objective_backlog`. All of these are derivable from the snapshot. Keep the signature backward-compatible by building the snapshot internally if not passed:

```python
def triage_phase_banner(
    plan: PlanModel,
    state: dict | None = None,
    *,
    snapshot: TriageSnapshot | None = None,
) -> str:
    if snapshot is None:
        snapshot = build_triage_snapshot(plan, state or {})
    # derive banner from snapshot fields
```

**4e. Enrich stage** (`enrich.py`):

Line 261 calls `active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)` directly. Replace with snapshot access if a snapshot is available in context, or keep the direct call (it's fine since snapshot uses the same underlying function).

### Step 5: Add integration test for snapshot consistency

**New file**: `desloppify/tests/plan/test_triage_snapshot.py`

Test cases:
1. **Empty plan** -- snapshot shows no cycle, no stages, no stale
2. **Mid-cycle with frozen IDs** -- verify `new_since_triage_ids` excludes frozen set
3. **Completed triage + new issues** -- verify `is_triage_stale` is True, `new_since_triage_ids` only contains genuinely new IDs
4. **Stage progression** -- verify `current_stage` advances correctly through the chain, `blocked_reason` reports unconfirmed prerequisite
5. **Snapshot matches old functions** -- for a given plan+state, verify `snapshot.undispositioned_ids` matches `undispositioned_triage_issue_ids()`, `snapshot.organized_count` matches `triage_coverage()[0]`, etc. This is a migration safety net.

---

### Implementation Sequence

1. **Step 3 first** (stale.py fix) -- smallest change, immediately fixes the "new issues" confusion, no API changes. Can land as an independent commit.
2. **Step 2** (unify stage prerequisites) -- refactors playbook.py and stage_policy.py. Required before Step 1 can use the unified definition.
3. **Step 1** (TriageSnapshot) -- builds on unified prerequisites. Pure addition, no consumers yet.
4. **Step 4** (thread snapshot) -- mechanical replacement of independent derivations with snapshot consumption.
5. **Step 5** (integration test) -- validates the whole stack.

Steps 3 and 2 can potentially land in either order since they don't depend on each other.

---

### Risks and Mitigations

**Risk**: `completion_stages.py` uses `complete:organize`, `complete:enrich`, `complete:sense-check` pseudo-flows that encode "prerequisites for completing at stage X." These need to be preserved or replaced with equivalent snapshot queries.

**Mitigation**: The completion gates just need "are all stages up to X recorded?" which is trivially derivable from the snapshot's `stages` tuple. Replace `missing_stage_prerequisite(stages, flow="complete:enrich")` with a helper like `snapshot_missing_prerequisite(snapshot, through_stage="enrich")`.

**Risk**: `_STAGE_PREREQUISITES` has different prerequisites for different flows (e.g., `"enrich"` requires `organize` confirmed, but `"sense-check"` only requires `enrich` confirmed, not organize). The unified definition must preserve these semantics.

**Mitigation**: The canonical `TRIAGE_STAGE_PREREQUISITES` already captures this -- enrich has `StagePrerequisite("organize", require_confirmation=True)`, sense-check has `StagePrerequisite("enrich", require_confirmation=True)`. The `compute_stage_status()` function walks the DAG correctly.

**Risk**: `synthetic.py` uses `TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES` for blocked-by computation in work queue items. Changing to the richer prerequisite model there would be unnecessary complexity.

**Mitigation**: Keep the derived `TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES` dict for this use case. It's a simple projection of the richer model.
